# Krama-sandarbha 11 Canto

## Chapter One

### Text 1

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*kṛtvā daitya-vadhaṁ kṛṣṇaḥ sa-rāmo yadubhir vṛtaḥ*  
*bhuvo 'vatārayad bhāraṁ javiṣṭhaṁ janayan kalim*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Lord Kṛṣṇa, accompanied by Balarāma and surrounded by the Yadu dynasty, killed many demons. Then, producing a quarrel, he quickly relieved the earth of its burden.**

**Комментарий:**

*athaikādaśasandarbhe sandarbhāṇāṁ samāhṛtiḥ*  
*kriyate yannideśena sa me ’nanyagatergatiḥ*

**He by whose order this collection of Sandarbhas is made into the Eleventh Krama Sandarbha is the goal of this person, with no other goal.**

*jayatāṁ mathurā-bhūmau śrīla-rūpa-sanātanau \|*  
*yau vilekhayatas tattvaṁ jñāpakau pustikām imām \|\|*

**Glory to Rūpa and Sanātana, who, revealing the Lord and his associates for the people of the future, had this book written in the land of Mathurā.**

This is the Karma Sandarbha for the Eleventh Canto. First the following should be considered: previously in the Tenth Canto Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes were described. The result of that description is attaining Kṛṣṇa, while attaining bhakti for him. This is indicated at the conclusion of the Tenth Canto:

*itthaṁ parasya nija-vartma-rirakṣayātta-*  
*līlā-tanos tad-anurūpa-viḍambanāni*  
*karmāṇi karma-kaṣaṇāni yadūttamasya*  
*śrūyād amuṣya padayor anuvṛttim icchan*

**To protect the principles of devotional service to himself, Kṛṣṇa, the best of the Yadus, accepts the pastime forms that have been glorified here in the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam. One who desires to faithfully serve his lotus feet should hear of the activities he performs suitable to his human form , that appear like human acts, which make all other actions insignificant.**

*martyas tayānusavam edhitayā mukunda*  
*śrīmat-kathā-śravaṇa-kīrtana-cintayaiti*  
*tad dhāma dustara-kṛtānta-javāpavargaṁ*  
*grāmād vanaṁ kṣiti-bhujo ’pi yayur yad-arthāḥ*

**By regularly hearing, chanting and meditating on the beautiful topics of Mukunda with ever-increasing sincerity, a mortal being will attain the abode of the Lord, where the inviolable power of death holds no sway. For this purpose, many persons, including great kings, abandoned their mundane homes and took to the forest. (SB** [**10.90.49-50**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/90.49)**)**

The pastimes which manifested in Dvārakā and other place on earth, though they appeared to follow material ways, are actually special manifestations of the nitya-siddha spiritual realm. That is explained at the end of the Eleventh Canto:

*dvārakāṁ hariṇā tyaktāṁ samudro ’plāvayat kṣaṇāt*  
*varjayitvā mahā-rāja śrīmad-bhagavad-ālayam*

As soon as Dvārakā was abandoned by the Supreme Lord, the ocean flooded it on all sides, O King, sparing only his palace.

*nityaṁ sannihitas tatra bhagavān madhusūdanaḥ*  
*smṛtyāśeṣāśubha-haraṁ sarva-maṅgala-maṅgalam*

**Lord Madhusūdana, the Supreme Lord, is eternally present in Dvārakā. By remembering that most auspicious of all auspicious places, one destroys all contamination. (SB** [**11.31.23-24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/31.23)**)**

Thus in this volume, to explain this, the entrance of the Lord into his eternal pastimes along with his devotees is described. It is shown in Kṛṣṇa Sandarbha as well as the First and Second Krama Sandarbhas that Kṛṣṇa’s pastime with the iron pestle etc. is an imitation only. In order that the ideas are respected, Kṛṣṇa and Uddhava, to whom no one can compare, present them. The general description is this:

*śayyāsanāṭanālāpa- krīḍā-snānādi-karmasu*  
*na viduḥ santam ātmānaṁ vṛṣṇayaḥ kṛṣṇa-cetasaḥ*

**The Vṛṣṇis were so absorbed in Kṛṣṇa that they forgot their own bodies while sleeping, sitting, walking, conversing, playing, bathing and so on. (SB** [**10.90.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/90.46)**)**

*vasudevaṁ hareḥ sthāna vadanty ānakadundubhim*

**Therefore Vasudeva, who provided the proper place for the appearance Kṛṣṇa, was also known as Ānakadundubhi (sound of drums). (SB** [**9.24.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/24.28)**)**

Uddhava is particularly glorified:

*noddhavo ’ṇv api man-nyūno yad guṇair nārditaḥ prabhuḥ*  
*ato mad-vayunaṁ loka grāhayann iha tiṣṭhatu*

**Uddhava is not less than me, because he is master of** ***māyā*** **and not at all lacking in any spiritual quality. He should remain on this earth, giving knowledge of me to the world. (SB** [**3.4.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/4.31)**)**

Such devotees asking questions is only part of the pastime, to help others. Śukadeva begins speaking to show this.

### Text 2

*ye kopitāḥ su-bahu pāṇḍu-sutāḥ sapatnair*  
*durdyūta-helana-kaca-grahaṇādibhis tān*  
*kṛtvā nimittam itaretarataḥ sametān*  
*hatvā nṛpān niraharat kṣiti-bhāram īśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord made the Pāṇḍavas, enraged by the numerous offenses of their enemies, such as duplicitous gambling, verbal insults, the seizing of Draupadī's hair, the immediate cause, and then had all the assembled kings kill each other, thus relieving the earth of its burden.**

**Комментарий:**

After personally removing burdens to a great degree, he completely removed the burden through the war (*niraharat*). This agrees with Śrīdhara Svāmī. The second meaning explains that he personally killed obvious demons like Pūtanā and others, and then arranged for the two parties to mutually kill each other, and thus removed the burden completely. However that is a general statement since he also killed Śiśupāla later.

### Text 3

*bhū-bhāra-rāja-pṛtanā yadubhir nirasya*  
*guptaiḥ sva-bāhubhir acintayad aprameyaḥ*  
*manye 'vaner nanu gato 'py agataṁ hi bhāraṁ*  
*yad yādavaṁ kulam aho aviṣahyam āste*

**Перевод:**

**After using the Yadu dynasty, which was protected by his arms, to eliminate the kings who with their armies had been the burden of this earth, the unfathomable Lord thought, "Although the earth's burden is now gone, in my opinion it is not yet gone, because there still remains the intolerable burned of Yādu dynasty itself remain."**

**Комментарий:**

**Kṛṣṇa sandarbha 181:**

Since the Yadus are his eternal associates, if the Lord disappeared without them, they would have destroyed the earth by actions impelled by their agitation of separation. They therefore disappeared first. Plural of “arms” does not mean he used four arms, but that he used other persons’ arms. It should not be thought that the Yadus were a burden because of their being adharmic:

*brahmaṇyānāṁ vadānyānāṁ nityaṁ vṛddhopasevinām*  
*vipra-śāpaḥ katham abhūd vṛṣṇīnāṁ kṛṣṇa-cetasām*

**How could the brāhmaṇas curse the Vṛṣṇis, who were always respectful to the brāhmaṇas, who were charitable, who were inclined to serve seniors and whose minds were always absorbed in Kṛṣṇa? (SB** [**11.1.8**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/1.8)**)**

They were famous as the best of devotees:

*śayyāsanāṭanālāpa- krīḍā-snānādi-karmasu*  
*na viduḥ santam ātmānaṁ vṛṣṇayaḥ kṛṣṇa-cetasaḥ*

**The Vṛṣṇis were so absorbed in Kṛṣṇa that they forgot their own bodies while sleeping, sitting, walking, conversing, playing, bathing and so on. (SB** [**10.90.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/90.46)**)**

The burden of the earth was not increased by their profuse numbers since there were also unlimited mountains and oceans that the earth supported.

### Text 4

*naivānyataḥ paribhavo 'sya bhavet kathañcin*  
*mat-saṁśrayasya vibhavonnahanasya nityam*  
*antaḥ kaliṁ yadu-kulasya vidhāya veṇu-*  
*stambasya vahnim iva śāntim upaimi dhāma*

**Перевод:**

**They cannot at all be censured by anyone else since they have unrestricted power, having taken constant shelter of me. Making a quarrel within the Yadu family like a fire in a bamboo grove, and thus destroying them, I will then attain my abode.**

**Комментарий:**

They cannot be conquered by others. They should destroy themselves but this cannot happen by itself. The reason is given. Taking shelter of me constantly, they naturally have unrestricted power. When they have been destroyed, being disinterested, I will attain my eternal abode of Dvārakā. An example is given. (It is like a fire in a bamboo grove.) An imitation destruction and real destruction look alike but the imitation destruction is not an internal destruction. At another time, there will also be an external manifestation again. With this intention, he made arrangements for their disappearance. The fire arises from the mutual friction of the bamboos.

### Text 5

*evaṁ vyavasito rājan satya-saṅkalpa īśvaraḥ*  
*śāpa-vyājena viprāṇāṁ sañjahre sva-kulaṁ vibhuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! When the powerful Lord, whose desire always come true, had thus decided, he withdrew his family from the earth on the pretext of a curse spoken by an assembly of** ***brāhmaṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

In having the brāhmaṇas’ curse, the intention was to show the power of brāhmaṇas. He had the family come to his abode (*sañjahre*).

### Text 6-7

*sva-mūrtyā loka-lāvaṇya- nirmuktyā locanaṁ nṛṇām*  
*gīrbhis tāḥ smaratāṁ cittaṁ padais tān īkṣatāṁ kriyāḥ*  
*ācchidya kīrtiṁ su-ślokāṁ vitatya hy añjasā nu kau*  
*tamo 'nayā tariṣyantīty agāt svaṁ padam īśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Having stolen people’s eyes with my form which makes them reject all other beauty, having stolen their voices and ears by my words, having stolen their minds and having stolen their movements of their bodies by the markings on my footprints, and having distributed my glories which are praised by poets, people of the future will be delivered from** ***samara*** **by these glories. Thinking in this way, the Lord made up his mind.**

**Комментарий:**

The result, attaining the Lord’s abode, along with attaining great bhakti through the pastimes described in the Tenth Canto is described in two verses. People will be delivered from saṁsāra full of ignorance (*tamas*), and will attain his abode result (understood). That is the result for normal jīvas, but not the case of the Yādavas (who were eternal associates), expressed in verse 3.

### Text 8

*śrī-rājovāca*  
*brahmaṇyānāṁ vadānyānāṁ nityaṁ vṛddhopasevinām*  
*vipra-śāpaḥ katham abhūd vṛṣṇīnāṁ kṛṣṇa-cetasām*

**Перевод:**

**King Parīkṣit said: How could the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **curse the Vṛṣṇis, who were always respectful to the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **who were charitable, who were inclined to serve seniors and whose minds were always absorbed in Kṛṣṇa?**

**Комментарий:**

It is not possible that they cursed them, since the Yādavas were without fault. How could brāhmaṇas who were knowledgeable of the fault of insulting the Lord’s associates, curse them? The Yādavas were absorbed in Kṛṣṇa (*kṛṣṇa-cetasām*). Because of this, the curse could have no power.

### Text 9

*yan-nimittaḥ sa vai śāpo yādṛśo dvija-sattama*  
*katham ekātmanāṁ bheda etat sarvaṁ vadasva me*

**Перевод:**

**O purest of the twice-born! What was the curse and what was the motive for this curse, such that disagreement arose among the Yadus, who were all devotees of Kṛṣṇa? Please tell me all these things.**

**Комментарий:**

*Katham* means “such that.” The Yādavas were dearest to Kṛṣṇa (*ekātmanām*), acitng on his behalf only.

### Text 10

*śrī-bādarāyaṇir uvāca*  
*bibhrad vapuḥ sakala-sundara-sanniveśaṁ*  
*karmācaran bhuvi su-maṅgalam āpta-kāmaḥ*  
*āsthāya dhāma ramamāṇa udāra-kīṛtiḥ*  
*saṁhartum aicchata kulaṁ sthita-kṛtya-śeṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Possessing a body composed of all the most beautiful objects, performing most auspicious acts in this world, and spreading his fame for the benefit of future generations, he desired to take his family for enjoyment in the eternal spiritual abode, since those pastimes were incomplete and his nature is to give happiness to his devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Having stated that everything happened by the Lord’s will, other reasons for his desire are given. Manifesting (*bibhrat*) on earth a form endowed with all beautiful objects, performing auspicious actions on earth , having fulfilled his desires (*āpta-kāmaḥ*) by fulfilling the desires of his devotees, and after he desired to take his family completely (*samhartum*) for enjoyment, being situated in his abode manifested beyond the material world.

If he fulfilled his desires, why did he have another desire for pastimes? He has special fixed pastimes with his dear devotees (*sthita-kṛtya-śeṣaḥ*). Thus though his desire for pastimes as avatāra were complete, there were other pastimes. He is famous for giving happiness to his devotees (*udāra-kīrtiḥ*).

### Text 11-12

*karmāni puṇya-nivahāni su-maṅgalāni*  
*gāyaj-jagat-kali-malāpaharāṇi kṛtvā*  
*kālātmanā nivasatā yadu-deva-gehe*  
*piṇḍārakaṁ samagaman munayo nisṛṣṭāḥ*  
*viśvāmitro 'sitaḥ kaṇvo durvāsā bhṛgur aṅgirāḥ*  
*kaśyapo vāmadevo 'trir vasiṣṭho nāradādayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In the home of the chief of the Yadus, Vasudeva, the father of Kṛṣṇa, the sages Viśvāmitra, Asita, Kaṇva, Durvāsā, Bhṛgu, Aṅgirā, Kaśyapa, Vāmadeva, Atri and Vasiṣṭha, along with Nārada and others, once performed rituals that award abundant pious results, bring great happiness and take away the sins of Kali-yuga for the whole world just by being recounted. After Lord Kṛṣṇa, who was staying in Vasudeva's house as time personified, respectfully sent the sages off at the conclusion of the ceremonies, the sages went to the holy place called Piṇḍāraka.**

**Комментарий:**

They were sent off by Kṛṣṇa the form of the antaryāmī (*kālātmanā*). Everything is dependent on his will.

### Text 13-15

*krīḍantas tān upavrajya kumārā yadu-nandanāḥ*  
*upasaṅgṛhya papracchur avinītā vinīta-vat*  
*te veṣayitvā strī-veṣaiḥ sāmbaṁ jāmbavatī-sutam*  
*eṣā pṛcchati vo viprā antarvatny asitekṣaṇā*  
*praṣṭuṁ vilajjatī sākṣāt prabrūtāmogha-darśanāḥ*  
*prasoṣyantī putra-kāmā kiṁ svit sañjanayiṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**To that holy place, the young boys of the Yadu dynasty had brought Sāmba, son of Jāmbavatī, dressed in woman's garb. Playfully approaching the great sages gathered there, the boys grabbed hold of the sages' feet and impudently asked them with feigned humility, "O learned** ***brāhmaṇas*****, this black-eyed pregnant woman has something to ask you. She is too embarrassed to inquire for herself. She is just about to give birth and is very desirous of having a son. Since all of you are great sages with infallible vision, please tell us whether her child will be a boy or a girl."**

**Комментарий:**

Their lack of humility was arranged by the desire of the Lord.

### Text 16

*evaṁ pralabdhā munayas tān ūcuḥ kupitā nṛpa*  
*janayiṣyati vo mandā muṣalaṁ kula-nāśanam*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Thus put to a test, the sages became angry, and told the boys, "Fools! She will bear you an iron club that will destroy your dynasty."**

**Комментарий:**

The sages appeared to be angry since it was caused by the Lord’s desire. She will produce an iron club (*janayiṣati)*.

### Text 17

*tac chrutvā te 'ti-santrastā vimucya sahasodaram*  
*sāmbasya dadṛśus tasmin muṣalaṁ khalv ayasmayam*

**Перевод:**

**Upon hearing the curse of the sages, the terrified boys quickly uncovered the belly of Sāmba and they observed there an iron club.**

**Комментарий:**

They took off the cloth which make the appearance of pregnancy, because they began to worry, since Sāmba began to feel heavy.

## Chapter Two

### Text 1

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*govinda-bhuja-guptāyāṁ dvāravatyāṁ kurūdvaha*  
*avātsīn nārado 'bhīkṣṇaṁ kṛṣṇopāsana-lālasaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: O best of the Kurus! Eager to be near Kṛṣṇa, Nārada Muni stayed constantly in Dvārakā, which was always protected by the arms of Govinda.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to describe the conversation between Vasudeva and Nārada that took place at that time, Nārada lived at Dvārakā by the will of the Lord, though the Lord did not need any assistance to fulfil his goals. The reason is explained. Though sent here and there by the Lord for pastimes of inquiring about the condition of the people, again and again he returned to Dvārakā. Thus he was constantly (*abhīkṣṇam*) there. He had a desire to be situated near Kṛṣṇa (*kṛṣṇopāsana-lālasaḥ*). The place was protected by Kṛṣṇa’s arms. It was thus always happy.

### Text 2

*ko nu rājann indriyavān mukunda-caraṇāmbujam*  
*na bhajet sarvato-mṛtyur upāsyam amarottamaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Who among the conditioned souls with senses, confronted by death at every step of life, would not render service to the lotus feet of Mukunda, who is worshipable even for the greatest** ***devatās*****?**

**Комментарий:**

Kaimutya is used to show why he lived there. Would a being with senses not serve his feet? This shows that even trees and shrubs without senses derive happiness from his worship. Thus all beings naturally have qualification. They all are confronted with death. Ordinary people who worship him do not have this problem. And those who are detached from material enjoyment should worship him (*upāsyam anmarottamaiḥ*). Thus why would the great king of devotees not worship him?

### Text 4

*śrī-vasudeva uvāca*  
*bhagavan bhavato yātrā svastaye sarva-dehinām*  
*kṛpaṇānāṁ yathā pitror uttama-śloka-vartmanām*

**Перевод:**

**Vasudeva said: O Lord! Your visit, like that of a father to his children, is for the benefit of all living beings, for the most wretched among them, and for devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Your visit benefits ordinary beings, the most fallen (*kṛpaṇānām*), and the most excellent (*uttama-śloka-vartamanām*), just as the coming of parents is beneficial for three types of sons.

### Text 5

*bhūtānāṁ deva-caritaṁ duḥkhāya ca sukhāya ca*  
*sukhāyaiva hi sādhūnāṁ tvādṛśām acyutātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**The activities of** ***devatās*** **lead to both misery and happiness for living beings, but the activities of great saints like you, who have accepted the infallible Lord as their very soul, result only in the happiness of all beings.**

**Комментарий:**

This is explained.

### Text 6

*bhajanti ye yathā devān devā api tathaiva tān*  
*chāyeva karma-sacivāḥ sādhavo dīna-vatsalāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who worship the** ***devatās*** **receive reciprocation from the** ***devatās*** **in a way corresponding to the offering. The** ***devatās*** **are like a person's shadow, but devotees, performing acts of worship, are affectionate to the fallen.**

**Комментарий:**

The devotees, associates of activities of worshipping the Lord (*karma-sacivāḥ*), are not subject to karma, but rather act independently, and are merciful to the fallen.

### Text 7

*brahmaṁs tathāpi pṛcchāmo dharmān bhāgavatāṁs tava*  
*yān śrutvā śraddhayā martyo mucyate sarvato bhayāt*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇa*****! Although I am satisfied simply by seeing you, I still wish to inquire about those duties which give pleasure to the Supreme Lord. Any mortal who faithfully hears about them is freed from all kinds of fear.**

**Комментарий:**

“There is no purpose in your asking questions since you are successful.” Hearing about me with faith, mortals become free of fear.

### Text 8

*vahaṁ kila purānantaṁ prajārtho bhuvi mukti-dam*  
*apūjayaṁ na mokṣāya mohito deva-māyayā*

**Перевод:**

**In a previous birth on this earth, I worshiped the Supreme Lord, Ananta, who alone can award liberation, but because I desired to have a child, I did not worship him for liberation, since I was bewildered by the Lord's illusory energy.**

**Комментарий:**

In two verses, he indicates his goal, being pained by obstacles to Kṛṣṇa on hearing the curse of brāhmaṇas. Bewildered by the mercy of the Lord (*deva-māyayā*), controlled by his sweetness, I did not worship him for liberation, what to speak of other things. I worshipped him to attain a son like him.

### Text 9

*yathā vicitra-vyasanād bhavadbhir viśvato-bhayāt*  
*mucyema hy añjasaivāddhā tathā naḥ śādhi su-vrata*

**Перевод:**

**My dear lord, true to your vow! Please instruct me clearly, so that by your mercy I may easily free myself from great danger, from the curse of the sages.**

**Комментарий:**

*Viśvato bhayāt* means from the cause of great fear, the curse of the sages.

### Text 10

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*rājann evaṁ kṛta-praśno vasudevena dhīmatā*  
*prītas tam āha devarṣir hareḥ saṁsmārito guṇaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: O King! Nārada was pleased by the question of the highly intelligent Vasudeva, which reminded Nārada of Kṛṣṇa and his qualities. Thus Nārada replied to Vasudeva as follows.**

**Комментарий:**

He was reminded of the qualities of Kṛṣṇa, which manifested when he saw Vasudeva.

### Text 11

*śrī-nārada uvāca*  
*samyag etad vyavasitaṁ bhavatā sātvatarṣabha*  
*yat pṛcchase bhāgavatān dharmāṁs tvaṁ viśva-bhāvanān*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: O best of the Sātvatas! You have correctly asked about the eternal duty of the living entity toward the Supreme Lord, which purifies the whole universe.**

**Комментарий:**

He will give the answer that is his goal at the end. He now speaks about bhagavad-dharma.

### Text 13

*tvayā parama-kalyāṇaḥ puṇya-śravaṇa-kīrtanaḥ*  
*smārito bhagavān adya devo nārāyaṇo mama*

**Перевод:**

**Today you have made me remember my Lord, the supremely blissful Nārāyaṇa, who is so auspicious that whoever hears and chants about him becomes completely pure.**

**Комментарий:**

Concerning that dharma, I have been made to remember Nārāyaṇa Ṛṣi, a form of my guru. Absorbed in worshipping Kṛṣṇa he had forgotten even his guru.

### Text 14

*atrāpy udāharantīmam itihāsaṁ purātanam*  
*ārṣabhāṇāṁ ca saṁvādaṁ videhasya mahātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**To explain the devotional service of the Lord, sages have related the ancient history of the conversation between the great soul named King Videha and the sons of Ṛṣabha.**

**Комментарий:**

The students remember (*udāharanti*) a story concerning the dharma taught by Nārāyaṇa. This is stated to especially glorify the topic.

### Text 22

*ta ete bhagavad-rūpaṁ viśvaṁ sad-asad-ātmakam*  
*ātmano 'vyatirekeṇa paśyanto vyacaran mahīm*

**Перевод:**

**These sages wandered the earth while seeing the entire universe, with all its gross and subtle objects, as a manifestation of the Supreme Lord and saw themselves in relation to the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

They saw the universe, with its gross and subtle form (*sad-asad-ātmakam*) everything as a manifestation of the Lord (*bhagavad-rūpam*). They saw themselves in relation to that (*ātmano ‘vyatirekena*).

### Text 23

*avyāhateṣṭa-gatayaḥ sura-siddha-sādhya-*  
*gandharva-yakṣa-nara-kinnara-nāga-lokān*  
*muktāś caranti muni-cāraṇa-bhūtanātha-*  
*vidyādhara-dvija-gavāṁ bhuvanāni kāmam*

**Перевод:**

**The nine Yogendras are liberated souls who travel unchecked to the planets of the** ***devatās,*** **the perfected mystics, the Sādhyas, the heavenly musicians, the Yakṣas, the human beings, and the Kinnaras and the serpents. As they wish, they can travel as well to the worlds of the sages, the Cāraṇas, the ghostly followers of Lord Śiva, the Vidyādharas, the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and the cows.**

**Комментарий:**

They can also travel to the worlds of the sages etc.

### Text 28

*śrī-videha uvāca*  
*manye bhagavataḥ sākṣāt pārṣadān vo madhu-dvisaḥ*  
*viṣṇor bhūtāni lokānāṁ pāvanāya caranti hi*

**Перевод:**

**King Nimi said: I think that you must be direct associates of the Supreme Lord, who is famous as the enemy of the demon Madhu. The pure devotees of Lord Viṣṇu wander throughout the universe to purify all the conditioned souls.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who are another form of Viṣṇu, his associates (*viṣṇoḥ bhūtāni*) wander about to purify the worlds.

### Text 30

*ata ātyantikaṁ kṣemaṁ pṛcchāmo bhavato 'naghāḥ*  
*saṁsāre 'smin kṣaṇārdho 'pi sat-saṅgaḥ śevadhir nṛṇām*

**Перевод:**

**O sinless ones! Therefore, I ask you to kindly tell me what is most beneficial. Even half a moment's association with pure devotees within this world of birth and death is a treasure for any man.**

**Комментарий:**

I ask you what is most beneficial so that I am not affected by fear, for a moment’s association of devotees is a treasure that yields all results in saṁsāra.

### Text 31

*dharmān bhāgavatān brūta yadi naḥ śrutaye kṣamam*  
*yaiḥ prasannaḥ prapannāya dāsyaty ātmānam apy ajaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Please speak about how one engages in the devotional service of the Supreme Lord, if you consider me capable of properly hearing these topics. When a living entity offers loving service to the Supreme Lord, the Lord is immediately satisfied, and in return he gives himself to the surrendered soul.**

**Комментарий:**

Please speak about dharmas since that is ultimately the highest benefit. Do not all dharmas lead to the highest good? Speak about dharmas involving the Lord (*bhagavatān*). What is the use of other things, which are obstacles? That ultimate benefit is that by which the Lord gives himself, when pleased with surrender. Present tense is used to show that he will give himself quickly. He will constantly reveal himself.

### Text 32

*śrī-nārada uvāca*  
*evaṁ te niminā pṛṣṭā vasudeva mahattamāḥ*  
*pratipūjyābruvan prītyā sa-sadasyartvijaṁ nṛpam*

**Перевод:**

**Nārada said: O Vasudeva! When Mahārāja Nimi had inquired from the nine Yogendras about devotional service to the Lord, those best of saintly persons sincerely thanked the King for his questions and with affection spoke to him, in the presence of the priests and members of the sacrificial assembly.**

**Комментарий:**

They all spoke to King Nimi. Kavi spoke first.

### Text 33

*śrī-kavir uvāca*  
*manye 'kutaścid-bhayam acyutasya*  
*pādāmbujopāsanam atra nityam*  
*udvigna-buddher asad-ātma-bhāvād*  
*viśvātmanā yatra nivartate bhīḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Kavi said: for one whose intelligence is disturbed by falsely identifying himself with the temporary material world I consider that constant worship of the Lord’s lotus feet, which produces fearlessness from the material world, is the highest goal because Paramātmā destroys all fear by that worship.**

**Комментарий:**

He answers the question concerning the highest benefit. That which produces fearlessness is the highest benefit. That is worship of the Lord’s lotus feet. The feet manifest without interruption (*nityam*). Even at the stage of sādhana gradually fear is destroyed. It is destroyed by the Lord in the world (*viśvātmanā*).

### Text 34

*ye vai bhagavatā proktā upāyā hy ātma-labdhaye*  
*añjaḥ puṁsām aviduṣāṁ viddhi bhāgavatān hi tān*

**Перевод:**

**Even ignorant living entities can very easily come to know the Supreme Lord if they adopt those means prescribed by the Supreme Lord. The process recommended by the Lord is known as** ***bhāgavata-dharma*****.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 216:**

This process is even for ignorant people (*aviduṣām puṁsām*). This is a glorification of bhakti. The sādhanas (*upāyāḥ*) were spoken by the Lord for easily attaining manifestation of Brahman, Paramātmā and Bhagavān, which arise from his own nature (*ātma)*.

*kālena naṣṭā pralaye vāṇīyaṁ veda-saṁjñitā*  
*mayādau brahmaṇe proktā dharmo yasyāṁ mad-ātmakaḥ*

**By the influence of time, the Vedic knowledge was lost at the time of annihilation. Therefore, when the subsequent creation took place, I spoke to Brahmā the Vedic knowledge in which bhakti is the essence. (SB** [**11.14.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.3)**)**

The above verse confirms that the Lord himself spoke about bhakti. Know the methods (*upāyān*) which are *bhāgavatān dharmān*, bhakti to the Lord. The word *hi* indicates that this is well known. Bhakti is called bhāgavata-dharma as in the following:

*etāvān eva loke ’smin puṁsāṁ dharmaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ*  
*bhakti-yogo bhagavati tan-nāma-grahaṇādibhiḥ*

**Pure bhakti to the Supreme Lord, beginning with the chanting of the holy name of the Lord, is the real dharma (*****dharmaḥ paraḥ*****), the supreme object for the human living in this world. (SB** [**6.3.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/3.22)**)**

In the verse *ātma-labdhaye* (for their benefit) represents the taṭastha-lakṣaṇa, for even if one has not attained benefits one still continues the process. The means for attaining the Lord (another meaning of *ātmā-labdhaye)* indicates the svarūpa-lakṣaṇa. Attaining this method, one follows the Lord favorably.

### Text 36

*kāyena vācā manasendriyair vā*  
*buddhyātmanā vānusṛta-svabhāvāt*  
*karoti yad yat sakalaṁ parasmai*  
*nārāyaṇāyeti samarpayet tat*

**Перевод:**

**In accordance with one’s particular nature, whatever one does with body, words, mind, senses, intelligence or purified consciousness one should offer to the Lord, thinking, "This is for the pleasure of Nārāyaṇa."**

**Комментарий:**

What will be spoken by Prabuddha in chapter 3 is indicated in summary here for those who are lethargic.

### Text 37

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**Перевод:**

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

Having established worship of the Lord’s feet (verse 33), he now confirms this.

Since there will be fear because of māyā, the intelligent person will worship the Lord, with body, words and words, at first a little (*iṣāt*), then taking guru as his self, with devotion in the form of bhāgavata-dharma, and then only with devotion (*ekayā*). This is the detailed meaning of *nityam pādāmbujopāsanam* (verse 33).

### Text 38

*avidyamāno 'py avabhāti hi dvayo*  
*dhyātur dhiyā svapna-manorathau yathā*  
*tat karma-saṅkalpa-vikalpakaṁ mano*  
*budho nirundhyād abhayaṁ tataḥ syāt*

**Перевод:**

**The world of enjoyment, even it is not directly present, remains an influence on the** ***jīva*** **who meditates on the enjoyment, just as he does in a dream or in a desire appearing in the mind. Therefore the intelligent person should control the mind which accepts and rejects actions. Then he becomes fearless.**

**Комментарий:**

Accepting bhakti, one engages the body, words and mind. What to do after that is described in two verses.

### Text 39

*śṛṇvan su-bhadrāṇi rathāṅga-pāṇer*  
*janmāni karmāṇi ca yāni loke*  
*gītāni nāmāni tad-arthakāni*  
*gāyan vilajjo vicared asaṅgaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the most auspicious birth and activities of Kṛṣṇa which are sung by all people and singing his names which describe those activities while having no attachment to anything else, a person should without shame wander in the world.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 61:**

“But by controlling the mind--which is part of yoga, one deviates from pure bhakti.” The mind will be controlled automatically by attachment to the Lord through performance of bhakti alone. Thus bhakti is the only process

*Tad arthakānī* means names which describe his birth and activities. “It is impossible to know all the names.” One should hear and sing the names sung in the world, the famous names (*yāniloke gītāni*), and wander in the world without desires (*asaṅgaḥ*).

### Text 40

*evaṁ-vrataḥ sva-priya-nāma-kīrtyā*  
*jātānurāgo druta-citta uccaiḥ*  
*hasaty atho roditi rauti gāyaty*  
*unmāda-van nṛtyati loka-bāhyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Regulated with such devotion, chanting the name of his dear Kṛṣṇa, he develops** ***prema.*** **His heart melts and he laughs loudly, weeps, wails and sings. He dances like a madman without regard for the public.**

**Комментарий:**

Easily, bhakti will show its third result. That is explained. Those who make a vrata to hear etc. as described above get this result. Among all the names he choose to chant those which are his favorites, those which nourish his spiritual desires (*sva-priya-nāma-kīrtya*). He develops anurāga using chanting as the main cause. His heart melts. This is a sign of mahāprema. Because of various bhāvas he laughs, weeps etc. There are infinite causes of laughing and other symptoms according to infinite varieties of bhakti. Chanting the name is the most effective method.

### Text 41

*khaṁ vāyum agniṁ salilaṁ mahīṁ ca*  
*jyotīṁṣi sattvāni diśo drumādīn*  
*sarit-samudrāṁś ca hareḥ śarīraṁ*  
*yat kiṁ ca bhūtaṁ praṇamed ananyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A person devoted only to the Lord should bow down to ether, fire, air, water, earth, the sun and other luminaries, all living beings, the directions, trees and other plants, the rivers and oceans, seeing them as the body of Kṛṣṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing ether, air, etc. living beings (*sattvāni*) as manifestations of the Lord out of great anurāga for the Lord, seeing them as Śyāmasundara with his yellow cloth etc., in the manner that a greedy man sees the world in terms of money and a lusty man, sees the words in terms of beautiful women, he offers respects. This means he becomes submissive with bhakti.

*yā niśā sarva-bhūtānāṁ tasyāṁ jāgarti saṁyamī \|*  
*yasyāṁ jāgrati bhūtāni sā niśā paśyato muneḥ \|\|*

**The** ***sthita-prajña*** **is awake in the night when all other living entities sleep. The night of the observant** ***sthita-prajña*** **is the time when all the living entities are awake*****.*** **(BG** [**2.69**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/2.69)**)**

### Text 42

*bhaktiḥ pareśānubhavo viraktir*  
*anyatra caiṣa trika eka-kālaḥ*  
*prapadyamānasya yathāśnataḥ syus*  
*tuṣṭiḥ puṣṭiḥ kṣud-apāyo 'nu-ghāsam*

**Перевод:**

**Devotion, direct experience of the Supreme Lord, and detachment from other things—these three occur simultaneously for one who has taken shelter of Kṛṣṇa, in the same way that pleasure, fullness of the stomach and relief from hunger are experienced simultaneously, with each bite, for a person engaged in eating.**

**Комментарий:**

“Previously the result, with manifestation of the Lord, was described in verses such as *pādāmbujopāsanam.* Prema was described in verse 40. Detachment was described in verse 40 also. That is the order. But this is not possible, since they are mutually dependent items. And if they appear simultaneously the fault of mutually taking shelter of each other takes place.” An example of the simultaneous appearance is given to allay this doubt.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 340:**

Bhakti is compared to satisfaction in eating, because both give happiness. Realization is compared to full stomach because both fill up the self. Detachment is compared to loss of hunger because it produces peace. That applies to the second verse also.

Though by eating food one loses appetite, by realizing the Lord one loses appetite for anything other than the Lord. That is the difference. The example is understood to be valid in losing taste for objects except the Lord.

### Text 43

*ity acyutāṅghriṁ bhajato 'nuvṛttyā*  
*bhaktir viraktir bhagavat-prabodhaḥ*  
*bhavanti vai bhāgavatasya rājaṁs*  
*tataḥ parāṁ śāntim upaiti sākṣāt*

**Перевод:**

**O King! The devotee who worships the lotus feet of the infallible Lord with constant endeavor achieves unflinching devotion, detachment and experience of the Lord. He achieves supreme happiness.**

**Комментарий:**

He attains the highest benefit (*parām śāntim*).

### Text 44

*śrī-rājovāca*  
*atha bhāgavataṁ brūta yad-dharmo yādṛśo nṛṇām*  
*yathācarati yad brute yair liṅgair bhagavat-priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Mahārāja Nimi said: Now please tell me about the devotees of the Supreme Lord--their natures, the different types, the conduct, and speech by which one can know who is dear to the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 187:**

Those who are accomplished in bhakti are of three types: those who have attained a body as an associate of the Lord; those who have been completely cleansed of contamination (*nirdhūta-kaṣāya*) and those with faint contamination (*mūrcchita-kaṣāya*). The example of the first is Nārada. An example of the second type is Śukadeva. An example of the third type is Nārada in his previous life.

(Example of the first type with spiritual body):

*prayujyamāne mayi tāṁ śuddhāṁ bhāgavatīṁ tanum \|*  
*ārabdha-karma-nirvāṇo nyapatat pāñca-bhautikaḥ \|\|*

**Having been awarded a transcendental body befitting an associate of the Lord, Nārada’s body made of five material elements, with karmas relating to the present body, fell away. (SB** [**1.6.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/6.28)**)**

(Example of second type with completely purified body in this world):

*sva-sukha-nibhṛta-cetās tad-vyudastānya-bhāvo*  
*’py ajita-rucira-līlākṛṣṭa-sāras tadīyam*  
*vyatanuta kṛpayā yas tattva-dīpaṁ purāṇaṁ*  
*tam akhila-vṛjina-ghnaṁ vyāsa-sūnuṁ nato ’smi*

**Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto the son of Vyāsadeva, Śukadeva Gosvāmī, who destroyed all contamination, and who, though fixed in his own bliss with no distractions, became attracted to the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa and mercifully spread the Bhāgavatam which describes Kṛṣṇa and reveals the rasa of his pastimes. (SB** [**12.12.69**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/12.69)**)**

(Example of third type, slight contamination in the present body):

*hantāsmin janmani bhavān mā māṁ draṣṭum ihārhati \|*  
*avipakva-kaṣāyāṇāṁ durdarśo ’haṁ kuyoginām \|\|*

**Oh! In this body you will not be able to see me again. Lax practitioners who still have some contamination cannot see me. (SB** [**1.6.21**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/6.21)**)**

Though he had some contamination in his previous birth, it is described that Nārada had prema:

*premātibhara-nirbhinna- pulakāṅgo ’tinirvṛtaḥ \|*  
*ānanda-samplave līno nāpaśyam ubhayaṁ mune \|\|*

**My limbs covered in distinct goose bumps out of excessive prema, filled with delight, I fainted out of bliss, and could not see myself or the Lord. (SB** [**1.6.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/6.17)**)**

Bharata is also an example. He had a deep contamination of sattva, supported by his prārabdha-karmas, in the form of desire to protect living entities. He is also described as having prema. Though all three types of siddhas have prema, the first type with spiritual body has the best prema. The siddha with all contamination cleared is second best, and then the type with slight contamination is third.

If one sees a person in his material body with transformations of prema (symptoms) which are very great, by the amount of symptoms one can infer the amount of prema. There is difference in the essential nature of prema by difference in the object of worship--whether it is an expansion of the Lord or the aṁśī, Kṛṣṇa, and by difference in the relationship such as dāsya, sakhya etc.

There is difference in the quantity of prema by the development of prema as a sprout (bhāva) or full prema. This will be shown in Prīti Sandarbha.

Though the goal is directly meeting the Lord, in that meeting there is excellence according to the realization of the Lord’s quality of affection. Without realization of the quality of affection in the shelter of causeless affection, there is no meeting with the Lord, even if one sees him. The afflicted tongue does not really taste the sweetness of sugar candy though it is on the tongue. Thus Ṛṣabha says:

*evaṁ manaḥ karma-vaśaṁ prayuṅkte*  
*avidyayātmany upadhīyamāne*  
*prītir na yāvan mayi vāsudeve*  
*na mucyate deha-yogena tāvat*

**Thus, karmas make the mind fixed upon further action. Since the jīva is engaged in action by ignorance, having accepted a subtle body, as long as he does not have devotion for me, Vāsudeva, he will not be liberated from the body. (SB** [**5.5.6**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/5.6)**)**

Thus the greatness of the devotee is according to the amount of prema. That is expressed in *mayīśe kṛta-sauhṛdārthāḥ*: the devotees’ goal is prema for me, the Lord. (SB [5.5.3](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/5.3)) The greatest devotee is he who has great prema, has a meeting with the Lord and no contamination. When any of these three items are deficient, the devotee is of lower status. Thus SB quoted above speaks of devotees who have not attained spiritual bodies and thus possibly have some deep impressions of material objects, though they practice renunciation of material life. This will be dealt with in another section.

After this, speak of the devotee *(bhāgavatam*), in order that I can understand. Describe his nature (*dharmaḥ*). Speak of his conduct, how he speaks (*yad brute*). He asks about the devotee’s mind, body and words.

In previous verses those qualities were described by Kavi. (SB [11.2.39](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.39)) That is true. But again describe the devotee, considering which among his characteristics determine different levels of uttama, madhyama and kaniṣṭha.

### Text 45

*śrī-havir uvāca*  
*sarva-bhūteṣu yaḥ paśyed bhagavad-bhāvam ātmanaḥ*  
*bhūtāni bhagavaty ātmany eṣa bhāgavatottamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Havi said: The most advanced devotee sees Supreme Lord, one’s object of worship, within everything, and sees everything in the Supreme Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 188:**

Here the mahā-bhāgavata is described in terms of mental qualities which are understood by external symptoms. These symptoms, produced by anurāga, were described by Kavi:

*evaṁ-vrataḥ sva-priya-nāma-kīrtyā*  
*jātānurāgo druta-citta uccaiḥ*  
*hasaty atho roditi rauti gāyaty*  
*unmāda-van nṛtyati loka-bāhyaḥ*

**Regulated with such devotion, chanting the name of his dear Kṛṣṇa, he develops prema. His heart melts and he laughs loudly, weeps, wails and sings. He dances like a madman without regard for the public. (SB** [**11.2.40**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.40)**)**

He experiences the manifestation of the Lord (*bhagavad-bhāvaṁ paśyati*) which he cherishes (*ātmanaḥ*) in all conscious and unconscious objects *(sarva-bhūteṣu*) as described in the following:

*khaṁ vāyum agniṁ salilaṁ mahīṁ ca*  
*jyotīṁṣi sattvāni diśo drumādīn*  
*sarit-samudrāṁś ca hareḥ śarīraṁ*  
*yat kiṁ ca bhūtaṁ praṇamed ananyaḥ*

**A person devoted only to the Lord should bow down to ether, fire, air, water, earth, the sun and other luminaries, all living beings, the directions, trees and other plants, the rivers and oceans, seeing them as the body of Kṛṣṇa. (SB** [**11.2.41**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.41)**)**

He also experiences all entities in the Lord who appears in the devotee’s heart, since the Lord is their shelter. This is the uttama devotee. The gopīs are described in this way:

*vana-latās tarava ātmani viṣṇuṁ*  
*vyañjayantya iva puṣpa-phalāḍhyāḥ*  
*praṇata-bhāra-viṭapā madhu-dhārāḥ*  
*prema-hṛṣṭa-tanavo vavṛṣuḥ sma*

**Kṛṣṇa moves about the forest in the company of his friends, who vividly chant the glories of his magnificent deeds. He thus appears as the most outstanding person, exhibiting his inexhaustible sweetness. When the cows wander onto the mountainsides and Kṛṣṇa calls out to them with the sound of his flute, the trees and creepers in the forest respond by becoming so luxuriant with fruits and flowers that they seem to be manifesting Kṛṣṇa, spread everywhere, within their hearts. As their branches bend low with the weight, the filaments on their trunks and vines stand erect out of prema, and both the trees and the creepers shower sweet sap. (SB** [**10.35.9**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/35.8)**)**

Or the uttama devotee sees his prema for the Lord in all entities conscious and unconscious. The rest is the same. Therefore he offers respect to all entities with devotion because he thinks that they are all devotees as explained in SB [11.2.40](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.40). Thus it is said:

*nadyas tadā tad upadhārya mukunda-gītam*  
*āvarta-lakṣita-manobhava-bhagna-vegāḥ*  
*āliṅgana-sthagitam ūrmi-bhujair murārer*  
*gṛhṇanti pāda-yugalaṁ kamalopahārāḥ*

**When the rivers hear the flute-song of Kṛṣṇa, the flow of their currents is broken and their waters are agitated out of desire, moving around in whirlpools. Presenting offerings of lotus flowers with the arms of their waves, the rivers grasp Murāri’s lotus feet, covering them in their embrace. (SB** [**10.21.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/21.15)**)**

The queens also say:

*kurari vilapasi tvaṁ vīta-nidrā na śeṣe*  
*svapiti jagati rātryām īśvaro gupta-bodhaḥ*  
*vayam iva sakhi kaccid gāḍha-nirviddha-cetā*  
*nalina-nayana-hāsodāra-līlekṣitena*

**Okurarī bird, you are lamenting. Now it is night, and somewhere in this world our husband is asleep in a hidden place. But you are wide awake, O friend, unable to fall asleep. Is it that, like us, you have had your heart pierced to the core by the lotus-eyed Lord’s munificent, playful smiling glances? (SB** [**10.90.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/90.15)**)**

They are not described as brahma-jñānīs (though it is said they see the Lord everywhere) since that jñāna and the results of that jñāna are despised by the devotees and it is contrary to being a devotee because then there would be no difference between the jīva and the Lord. This would contradict the position of the uttama expressed in the following description of aikāntika-bhakti:

*lakṣaṇaṁ bhakti-yogasya nirguṇasya hy udāhṛtam*  
*ahaituky avyavahitā yā bhaktiḥ puruṣottame*

**That quality of bhakti beyond the guṇas is that bhakti which is devoid of results other than bhakti and is unobstructed by other processes. (SB** [**3.29.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/29.11)**)**

Nor does the definition represent realization of a Lord without form, as this would contradict the highest position summarized in the conclusion of the section:

*visṛjati hṛdayaṁ na yasya sākṣād*  
*dharir avaśābhihito ’py aghaugha-nāśaḥ*  
*praṇaya-rasanayā dhṛtāṅghri-padmaḥ*  
*sa bhavati bhāgavata-pradhāna uktaḥ*

**He who binds the lotus feet of the Lord by ropes of love, and whose heart the Lord, destroyer of heaps of sin, does not leave, even if beckoned accidentally, is called the best of devotees. (SB** [**11.2.55**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.55)**)**

### Text 46

*īsvare tad-adhīneṣu bāliśeṣu dviṣatsu ca*  
*prema-maitrī-kṛpopekṣā yaḥ karoti sa madhyamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**An intermediate or second-class devotee, called** ***madhyama-adhikārī,*** **offers his love to the Supreme Lord, is a sincere friend to all the devotees of the Lord, shows mercy to ignorant people who are innocent, and disregards those who are envious of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 189:**

The madhyama-bhāgavata is characterized by special mental qualities. He has prema for the Lord. This means he has bhakti for the Lord (since he has not developed prema). He is friendly towards devotees *(tad-adhīneṣu)*. He is merciful to those who do not know about bhakti or who are indifferent. Prahlāda says:

*śoce tato vimukha-cetasa indriyārtha-*  
*māyā-sukhāya bharam udvahato vimūḍhān*

**I lament for the fools, averse to you, who carry the burden of material responsibilities for gaining material happiness from sense objects. (SB** [**7.9.43**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/9.43)**)**

He ignores those who are inimical to himself, not being disturbed by their enmity to him since he has some compassion for them as ignorant souls. Prahlāda had this attitude towards his father. Though he is disturbed when persons are inimical to the Lord and other devotees, he is not overcome by that. He manifests mercy to the innocent and indifference to those who hate the Lord. Unlike the uttama, he does not see everything in terms of prema. Therefore he is in the category of madhyama.

The uttama devotee however does show great bliss on seeing the devotees, taking them as a manifestation of the Lord. Great friendliness to the devotee is not forbidden for the uttama, but he has such an attitude for all beings. Śiva expresses his friendliness to devotees:

*kṣaṇārdhenāpi tulayena svargaṁ nāpunar-bhavam*  
*bhagavat-saṅgi-saṅgasyamartyānāṁ kim utāśiṣaḥ*

**What to speak of any blessings on this earth, or on Svarga, even the blessing of liberation cannot compare with even half a moment’s association with the devotee of the Lord. (SB** [**4.24.57**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/24.57)**)**

*atha bhāgavatā yūyaṁ priyāḥ stha bhagavān yathā*  
*na mad bhāgavatānāṁ ca preyān anyo ’sti karhicit*

**You devotees are dear to me just as the Supreme Lord is dear to me. The devotees hold me dear, just as they hold the Lord dear. (SB** [**4.24.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/24.30)**)**

Sūta says:

*harer guṇākṣipta-matir bhagavān bādarāyaṇiḥ \|*  
*adhyagān mahad ākhyānaṁ nityaṁ viṣṇu-jana-priyaḥ \|\|*

**Śukadeva, the son of Vyāsa, whose mind became distracted from trance by the qualities of Kṛṣṇa, and who became attracted to the Vaiṣṇavas, studied this great Bhāgavatam. (SB** [**1.7.11**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/7.11)**)**

Śukadeva (uttama) shows hatred for Kaṁsa. But this hatred does not manifest constantly as in the case of madhyamas. In the uttamas the sphūrti of the Lord (seeing him everywhere) is not obstructed by this hatred since they see the Lord in the demons, as the punisher of the demons. Uddhava offers respect to Duryodhana by seeing the Lord in this way.

Śiva says (concerning the Lord appearing within):

*sattvaṁ viśuddhaṁ vasudeva-śabditaṁ*  
*yad īyate tatra pumān apāvṛtaḥ*  
*sattve ca tasmin bhagavān vāsudevo*  
*hy adhokṣajo me namasā vidhīyate*

**The pure antaḥkaraṇa is called Vasudeva, because there the Supreme Lord appears without coverings. In that antaḥkaraṇa, I worshipped the Supreme Lord Vāsudeva, beyond the material senses, by offering respects. (SB** [**4.3.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/3.23)**)**

Thus, in the kidnapping of Lakṣmaṇā, Uddhava offered respects to Duryodhana.

*so ’bhivandyāmbikā-putraṁ bhīṣmaṁ droṇaṁ ca bāhlikam*  
*duryodhanaṁ ca vidhi-vad rāmam āgataṁ abravīt*

**After he had offered proper respects to Dhṛtarāṣṭra and to Bhīṣma, Droṇa, Bāhlika and Duryodhana, Uddhava informed them that Balarāma had arrived. (SB** [**10.68.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/68.17)**)**

Or the uttamas, who see everything in terms of their prema, see that same prema manifested in the demons. This is not unsuitable, for these devotees who are particles of pollen at the lotus feet of the Lord worshipped by millions of their life airs become agitated on seeing the bad behavior of the demons. According to their bhāva they then think, “What kind of person is this who does not have love for our dear one, the supreme Lord, with sweet pastimes benefiting all beings, who is ornamented with gems of good qualities and is compassionate to all people, who is the shelter of the highest, unconditional prema, who is the culmination of all bliss? The cause of hatred for the Lord is beyond my comprehension. All beings from Brahmā down to immovable beings, good or evil, are attracted to the Lord.”

Śukadeva says:

*govinda-bhuja-guptāyāṁ dvāravatyāṁ kurūdvaha*  
*avātsīn nārado ’bhīkṣṇaṁ kṛṣṇopāsana-lālasaḥ*

**O best of the Kurus! Eager to engage in the worship of Kṛṣṇa, Nārada Muni stayed constantly in Dvārakā, which was always protected by the arms of Govinda.**

*ko nu rājann indriyavān mukunda-caraṇāmbujam*  
*na bhajet sarvato-mṛtyur upāsyam amarottamaiḥ*

**O King! Who among the conditioned souls with senses, confronted by death at every step of life, would not render service to the lotus feet of Mukunda, who is worshipable even for the greatest devatās? (SB** [**11.2.1-2**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.1)**)**

### Text 47

*arcāyām eva haraye pūjāṁ yaḥ śraddhayehate*  
*na tad-bhakteṣu cānyeṣu sa bhaktaḥ prākṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee who faithfully engages in the worship of the deity in the temple but does not behave properly toward other devotees or people in general is called an unrefined devotee.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 190:**

The kaniṣṭḥa devotee is described in terms of body and somewhat the mind in relation to conduct related to the Lord.

He has faith in the deity (*arcāyām*) but not in the devotees or others since he does not have prema for the Lord, lacks knowledge of the glories of the devotees and has not manifested the devotee’s quality of respecting all beings. This devotee is a person who has begun bhakti (*prākṛtaḥ).* His faith has not arisen from understanding the meaning of scriptures.

*yasyātma-buddhiḥ kuṇape tri-dhātuke*  
*sva-dhīḥ kalatrādiṣu bhauma ijya-dhīḥ*  
*yat-tīrtha-buddhiḥ salile na karhicij*  
*janeṣv abhijñeṣu sa eva go-kharaḥ*

**One who identifies his self as the inert body composed of mucus, bile and air, who assumes his wife and family are permanently his own, who thinks an earthen image or the land of his birth is worshipable, or who sees a place of pilgrimage as merely the water there, but who never identifies himself with, feels kinship with, worships or even visits those who are wise in spiritual truth—such a person is no better than a cow or an ass. (SB** [**10.84.13**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/84.13)**)**

This condition arises from not understanding scriptural statements like the verse above. This faith is based on tradition only. Thus the category of kaniṣṭha sādhaka has faith that is not based on scripture and has not developed prema.

### Text 48

*gṛhītvāpīndriyair arthān yo na dveṣṭi na hṛṣyati*  
*viṣṇor māyām idaṁ paśyan sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Even while engaging his senses in contact with their objects, one who sees this whole world as the energy of Viṣṇu and is neither repelled nor elated is the greatest among devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 191:**

As was previously explained his mind is not overwhelmed. Though accepting objects with his senses, he does not hate or rejoice. He sees the world *(idam*) as disgusting since it is the play of the external energy (*māyām*). This is a mixture of bodily and mental qualities.

### Text 49

*dehendriya-prāṇa-mano-dhiyāṁ yo*  
*janmāpyaya-kṣud-bhaya-tarṣa-kṛcchraiḥ*  
*saṁsāra-dharmair avimuhyamānaḥ*  
*smṛtyā harer bhāgavata-pradhānaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A person who, by remembering the Lord, is not bewildered by the qualities of** ***saṁsāra,*** **such as birth and death of the body, hunger of the** ***prāṇas,*** **fear in the mind, longing in the intelligence and fatigue in the senses, is the best among the devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 192:**

Qualities of the mind only are described for the rest of the section.

He who is not bewildered by the qualities of saṁsāra, by birth, death, hunger, fear, longing and fatigue of the body, senses, prāṇa, mind and intelligence is the best devotee. Gītā says:

*yeṣāṁ tv anta-gataṁ pāpaṁ janānāṁ puṇya-karmaṇām \|*  
*te dvandva-moha-nirmuktā bhajante māṁ dṛḍha-vratāḥ \|\|*

**But those whose sins have been destroyed by actions pleasing to the great souls, who are free of this ignorance arising from duality, worship me with determination. (BG** [**7.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/7.28)**)**

### Text 50

*na kāma-karma-bījānāṁ yasya cetasi sambhavaḥ*  
*vāsudevaika-nilayaḥ sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The person completely surrendered to the Lord, in whose mind no impressions, desires or actions arise, is considered to be the best of devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

*Bījāni* means impressions. Vāsudeva is his shelter.

### Text 51

*na yasya janma-karmabhyāṁ na varṇāśrama-jātibhiḥ*  
*sajjate 'sminn ahaṁ-bhāvo dehe vai sa hareḥ priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He whose body does not give rise to false identity with good family, pious actions, or caste considerations is dear to the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bahkti Sandarbha 194:**

That person in whose body false identity does not adhere by good family *(janma*), pious acts like austerity, by anuloma, mixed caste etc. (*jātibhiḥ*), but who is attached to the body useful for serving the Lord is dear to the Lord. He is the best devotee. *Bhagavatottamaḥ* is understood from the previous verse since the subjects is the uttama devotee. *Hareḥ priyaḥ* only indicates that he is a devotee.

### Text 52

*na yasya svaḥ para iti vitteṣv ātmani vā bhidā*  
*sarva-bhūta-samaḥ śāntaḥ sa vai bhāgavatottamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who does not think that wealth is his, who does not think in terms of self and other, who is equal to all beings and peaceful, is the best devotee.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 195:**

Wealth, the object of possessiveness, does not belong to himself or others. He does not think of himself in terms of self and others. He does not distinguish difference between self and others, siding with himself.

### Text 53

*tri-bhuvana-vibhava-hetave 'py akuṇṭha-*  
*smṛtir ajitātma-surādibhir vimṛgyāt*  
*na calati bhagavat-padāravindāl*  
*lava-nimiṣārdham api yaḥ sa vaiṣṇavāgryaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He, whose remembrance is not lured by dominion over the three worlds, and who does move for half a minute or half a second from the Lord’s lotus feet which are sought by the** ***devatās*** **who also meditate on the Lord, is the best of devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

He is not lured by dominion over the three worlds, what to speak of their cause *(hetave*). Dvandva compounds can be in the singular. Does he waiver? No, but rather his remembrance is without decrease in the Lord’s feet (*akuṇṭha smṛtiḥ*). He is absorbed in the Lord. This is rare for the devatās like Brahmā and Śiva, who also meditate on the Lord (*ajita).*

### Text 54

*bhagavata uru-vikramāṅghri-śākhā-*  
*nakha-maṇi-candrikayā nirasta-tāpe*  
*hṛdi katham upasīdatāṁ punaḥ sa*  
*prabhavati candra ivodite 'rka-tāpaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**How can the heat of material existence again arise in the hearts of those who worship the Lord, since the heat of lust is destroyed by the Lord’s heroic lotus feet, his toes, his toenails, his toe jewels and the soothing light from his feet? When the moon rises, the heat of lust is dissipated.**

**Комментарий:**

An example illustrates this. Heat is extinguished by the light from the nails of the toes of his heroic feet. The moon light destroys the heat of lust.

### Text 55

*visṛjati hṛdayaṁ na yasya sākṣād*  
*dharir avaśābhihito 'py aghaugha-nāśaḥ*  
*praṇaya-rasanayā dhṛtāṅghri-padmaḥ*  
*sa bhavati bhāgavata-pradhāna uktaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who binds the lotus feet of the Lord by ropes of love, and whose heart the Lord, destroyer of heaps of sin, does not leave, even if beckoned accidentally, is called the best of devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 198:**

Material desire is not possible in this devotee because the Lord will personally (*sākṣāt*) become present. Being glorified accidently the Lord destroys sin. How much more then, being glorified by the devotee with full absorption at all times, will the Lord destroy sins! In either case, the impressions of sin cannot remain.

*etan nirvidyamānānām icchatām akuto-bhayam \|*  
*yogināṁ nṛpa nirṇītaṁ harer nāmānukīrtanam \|\|*

**O King! Continuous chanting of the name of the Lord has been prescribed for devotees, for those with desires for liberation and material elevation, and for those satisfied with the self. (SB** [**2.1.11**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/1.11)**)**

It was asked “What does the devotee speak?” (SB [11.2.42](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.42)) This verse answers by saying the devotee glorifies the Lord.

## Chapter Three

### Text 1

*śrī-rājovāca*  
*parasya viṣṇor īśasya māyinām api mohinīm*  
*māyāṁ veditum icchāmo bhagavanto bruvantu naḥ*

**Перевод:**

**King Nimi said: Now we wish to learn about the illusory potency of the Supreme Lord, which bewilders even great mystics. My lords, please speak to us about this subject.**

### Text 2

*nānutṛpye juṣan yuṣmad- vaco hari-kathāmṛtam*  
*saṁsāra-tāpa-nistapto martyas tat-tāpa-bheṣajam*

**Перевод:**

**Although I am suffering in material existence and am drinking the nectar of your statements about the Lord’s glories which is the remedy for those suffering material existence, my thirst is not yet satiated.**

**Комментарий:**

Even though you describe māyā, your words will be mainly about the Lord, for that is your nature.

### Text 3

*ebhir bhūtāni bhūtātmā mahā-bhūtair mahā-bhuja*  
*sasarjoccāvacāny ādyaḥ sva-mātrātma-prasiddhaye*

**Перевод:**

**Antarīkṣa said: O mighty-armed King! By activating the material elements, the primary soul of all creation has created all living beings in higher and lower species so that these conditioned souls can cultivate either sense gratification or ultimate liberation, according to their desire.**

**Комментарий:**

Till verse 17 he describes various steps in creation. By these elements like mahattattva (*ebhiḥ*) the Lord created all beings. The main goal in creation is for bhakti (*ātma-prasiddhaye*). That is the real purpose. Other goals are secondary.

### Text 4

*evaṁ sṛṣṭāni bhūtāni praviṣṭaḥ pañca-dhātubhiḥ*  
*ekadhā daśadhātmānaṁ vibhajan juṣate guṇān*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord, having entered the material bodies created by the five elements, divides himself into the mind and ten senses and enjoys the various aspects of his devotees’ prema.**

**Комментарий:**

*eta uddhava te praśnāḥ sarve sādhu nirūpitāḥ*  
*kiṁ varṇitena bahunā lakṣaṇaṁ guṇa-doṣayoḥ*  
*guṇa-doṣa-dṛśir doṣo guṇas tūbhaya-varjitaḥ*

**Thus, Uddhava, I have elucidated all of the matters of inquiry, which are suitable for liberation. There is no need for a more elaborate description of good and bad qualities, since to see good and bad is itself a bad quality. The best quality is to transcend seeing good and bad qualities. (SB** [**11.19.45**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.40)**)**

*na mayy ekānta-bhaktānāṁ guṇa-doṣodbhavā guṇāḥ*  
*sādhūnāṁ sama-cittānāṁ buddheḥ param upeyuṣām*

**The fully dedicated devotees who see equally everywhere and who have achieved the Lord who is superior to** ***prakṛti*** **do not have the** ***guṇas*** **which give rise to qualities of good and bad. (SB** [**11.20.36**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.36)**)**

The Lord enjoys the various aspects of prema of his devotees (*juṣate guṇān*).

### Text 5

*guṇair guṇān sa bhuñjāna ātma-pradyotitaiḥ prabhuḥ*  
*manyamāna idaṁ sṛṣṭam ātmānam iha sajjate*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***jīva,*** **enjoying the objects of the senses by the senses which are impelled by the Lord and thinking himself to be that created body, takes repeated births in different bodies and becomes entangled in those bodies.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva is called *prabhuḥ* because he is an aṁśa of the Lord, endowed with some of his jñāna-śakti etc. The reason for his existence in saṁsāra has been explained:

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.*** **(SB** [**11.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37)**)**

Or, tasting (*bhuñjāna*) the pastimes of the devotee’s prema (*gunān*) arising from qualities like mercy (*guṇaiḥ*) revealed by himself, thinking the devotee’s body (*idam sṛtam*) as his body, the Lord becomes attached to that devotee’s body (*iha*).

*sādhavo hṛdayaṁ mahyaṁ sādhūnāṁ hṛdayaṁ tv aham*  
*mad-anyat te na jānanti nāhaṁ tebhyo manāg api*

**The pure devotee is my very heart: what pains him pains me. I am the heart of the pure devotee: what pleases him pleases me. My devotees do not know anything else but me, and I do not know more than they do. (SB** [**9.4.68**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/4.68)**)**

### Text 6

*karmāṇi karmabhiḥ kurvan sa-nimittāni deha-bhṛt*  
*tat tat karma-phalaṁ gṛhṇan bhramatīha sukhetaram*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***jīva*** **engages his active sense organs in activities accompanied by impressions, and, accepting the** ***karma*** **in the form of enjoyment and suffering, wanders in this world.**

**Комментарий:**

The destination of the ordinary jīva is described.

### Text 11

*saṁvartako megha-gaṇo varṣati sma śataṁ samāḥ*  
*dhārābhir hasti-hastābhir līyate salile virāṭ*

**Перевод:**

**Masses of clouds causing destruction, pouring torrents of rain as long as elephants’ trunks for one hundred years, will drown the universe in water.**

**Комментарий:**

The clouds are produced by water drawn up the sun and by the fire and smoke.

### Text 12

*tato virājam utsṛjy vairājaḥ puruṣo nṛpa*  
*avyaktaṁ viśate sūkṣmaṁ nirindhana ivānalaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Then Vairāja Brahmā gives up his universal body, O King, and enters into the subtle** ***prakṛti,*** **like a fire that has run out of fuel.**

**Комментарий:**

Previous to this the individual (*vyaṣṭi*) jīvas merge with Vairāja. Thus Vairāja is the totality of jīvas (*samaṣṭi-jīvaḥ*).

### Text 13

*vāyunā hṛta-gandhā bhūḥ salilatvāya kalpate*  
*salilaṁ tad-dhṛta-rasaṁ jyotiṣṭvāyopakalpate*

**Перевод:**

**Deprived of its quality of aroma by the wind, the element earth is transformed into water; and water, deprived of its taste by that same wind, is merged into fire.**

**Комментарий:**

The fragrance of earth is destroyed by the air, which is the universal covering. Air has the distinguishing quality of touch, and thus being hard, can remove the fragrance. The air enters the earth to do this.

### Text 14

*hṛta-rūpaṁ tu tamasā vāyau jyotiḥ pralīyate*  
*hṛta-sparśo 'vakāśena vāyur nabhasi līyate*  
*kālātmanā hṛta-guṇaṁ nabha ātmani līyate*

**Перевод:**

**Fire, deprived of its form by the wind, dissolves into the element air. When the air loses its quality of touch by the influence of ether, the air merges into that ether. When ether is deprived of sound by time, ether merges into false ego in ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

Vāyu is here called tamas because in the layers of the universal covering, starting with the air covering, the covering of prakṛti (*tamas*) pervades all the layers.

### Text 16

*eṣā māyā bhagavataḥ sarga-sthity-anta-kāriṇī*  
*tri-varṇā varṇitāsmābhiḥ kiṁ bhūyaḥ śrotum icchasi*

**Перевод:**

**I have now described** ***māyā,*** **the illusory energy of the Supreme Lord, which consists of the three modes of material nature, and causes creation, maintenance and annihilation of the material universe. Now, what more do you wish to hear?**

**Комментарий:**

I have described māyā, the effect of the Lord (*eśāḥ*).

### Text 17

*śrī-rājovāca*  
*yathaitām aiśvarīṁ māyā dustarām akṛtātmabhiḥ*  
*taranty añjaḥ sthūla-dhiyo maharṣa idam ucyatām*

**Перевод:**

**King Nimi said: O great sage! You should explain how even a foolish materialist can easily cross over the illusory energy of the Supreme Lord, which is always insurmountable for those who are not accomplished.**

**Комментарий:**

Hearing we have understood the method with verses like SB [11.2.37](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37). Explain how those whose intelligence is fixed in karmas like horse sacrifices (*sthūla-dhiyaḥ*) can easily cross māyā.

### Text 18

*śrī-prabuddha uvāca*  
*karmāṇy ārabhamāṇānāṁ duḥkha-hatyai sukhāya ca*  
*paśyet pāka-viparyāsaṁ mithunī-cāriṇāṁ nṛṇām*

**Перевод:**

**Prabuddha said: One should see that the activities of those who endeavor for destruction of sorrow and creation of happiness by sex life achieve the opposite results.**

**Комментарий:**

First he makes them develop disgust with those actions in three verses. They achieve the opposite result (*pāka-viparyāsam*).

### Text 21

*tasmād guruṁ prapadyeta jijñāsuḥ śreya uttamam*  
*śābde pare ca niṣṇātaṁ brahmaṇy upaśamāśrayam*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one who is inquisitive about the highest truth should surrender to a** ***guru*** **who is skillful in the scriptures and fixed in realization, while free of anger and greed.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowing all this, they should then surrender to guru.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 202:**

The guru should be fixed in the Vedas (*śābde brahmaṇi*) by dedication to deliberation and should be fixed in the manifestation of a form of the Lord (*pare brahmaṇi*) by direct experience.

Nārada says in summarizing the story of Purañjana:

*sa vai priyatamaś cātmāyato na bhayam aṇv api*  
*iti veda sa vai vidvānyo vidvān sa gurur hariḥ*

**The Lord is most beloved, though the jīva is also dear, because from worshipping the Lord there is not the least fear or suffering. He who knows the two ātmās is learned. Such a learned person should be considered your guru and should be worshipped like the Lord. (SB** [**4.29.51**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/29.51)**)**

### Text 22

*tatra bhāgavatān dharmān śikṣed gurv-ātma-daivataḥ*  
*amāyayānuvṛttyā yais tuṣyed ātmātma-do hariḥ*

**Перевод:**

**He who holds the** ***guru*** **dear and worthy of worship should learn** ***bhakti*** **from the** ***guru,*** **by which the Lord, who gives himself in the deity form, is satisfied.**

**Комментарий:**

Eight verses describe what the disciple learns from the guru. The Lord is called ātmā because he is the root form of everything. He has a form of unparalleled bliss. *Ānandād imāni bhūtāni jāyante*: living beings arose from the blissful Lord. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad). The Lord gives himself to the devotees (*ātmadaḥ*). He respects the devotees.

### Text 23

*sarvato manaso 'saṅgam ādau saṅgaṁ ca sādhuṣu*  
*dayāṁ maitrīṁ praśrayaṁ ca bhūteṣv addhā yathocitam*

**Перевод:**

**The disciple should in the beginning learn detachment of the mind from all things of this world, while showing mercy to the inferior, friendship to equals and respect to superiors.**

**Комментарий:**

Fearing lack of faith, he speaks of what the disciple should do to develop faith, in verse 23 etc.

### Text 27-28

*śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ dhyānaṁ harer adbhuta-karmaṇaḥ*  
*janma-karma-guṇānāṁ ca tad-arthe 'khila-ceṣṭitam*  
*iṣṭaṁ dattaṁ tapo japtaṁ vṛttaṁ yac cātmanaḥ priyam*  
*dārān sutān gṛhān prāṇān yat parasmai nivedanam*

**Перевод:**

**The disciple should learn hearing, chanting, and meditation concerning the qualities, activities and birth of the Lord who performs astonishing acts, and should learn to offer all actions to him. He should learn performance of sacrifice to Viṣṇu, charity to Viṣṇu and devotees, austerities such as Ekadaśī, and proper conduct. He should to offer what he treasures to the Lord and to engage wife, sons and house in the service of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

When their antaḥkaraṇa is pure, he then speaks about bhakti directly to the disciples having faith. “If a person previous did sacrifices and has wife and assets, what should he do?” The sacrifice should be offered to Viṣṇu (verse 28).

### Text 29

*evaṁ kṛṣṇātma-nātheṣu manuṣyeṣu ca sauhṛdam*  
*paricaryāṁ cobhayatra mahatsu nṛṣu sādhuṣu*

**Перевод:**

**One should learn to have friendship with people who have devoted their lives to Kṛṣṇa and to serve Kṛṣṇa, the devotees, those of higher status and those of equal status in the proper manner.**

**Комментарий:**

Serving moving and non-moving beings in his previous state, he should learn to serve Kṛṣṇa and the devotees, the best among humans, according to their status. He should learn to have friendship with the special devotees who have made Kṛṣṇa the object of their prema. He should particularly search for them and respect them in his mind.

### Text 31

*smarantaḥ smārayantaś ca mitho 'ghaugha-haraṁ harim*  
*bhaktyā sañjātayā bhaktyā bibhraty utpulakāṁ tanum*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering and inspiring other devotees to remember the Lord who destroys all sins, the devotees will develop hairs standing on end in ecstasy by** ***prema-bhakti*** **produced from** ***sādhana-bhakti*****.**

**Комментарий:**

The direct results of bhakti are described in two verses.

### Text 33

*iti bhāgavatān dharmān śikṣan bhaktyā tad-utthayā*  
*nārāyaṇa-paro māyām añjas tarati dustarām*

**Перевод:**

**Having learned** ***bhakti*** **and being dedicated to Nārāyaṇa, one will easily cross insurmountable** ***māyā*** **by** ***prema-bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

This is a secondary result.

### Text 34

*śrī-rājovāca*  
*nārāyaṇābhidhānasya brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ*  
*niṣṭhām arhatha no vaktuṁ yūyaṁ hi brahma-vittamāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**King Nimi said: You, being most knowledgeable of the Lord, should speak about the** ***svarūpa*** **of Bhagavān, Nārāyaṇa, who is also Brahman and Paramātmā.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhagavat Sandarbha 2:**

*Narāyaṇabhidhānasya* refers to Bhagavān since the other two members of the group of three (Brahman and Paramātmā) are mentioned here. Bhagavān is equated with Nārāyaṇa in the following:

*bhūtair yadā pañcabhir ātma-sṛṣṭaiḥ*  
*puraṁ virājaṁ viracayya tasmin*  
*svāṁśena viṣṭaḥ puruṣābhidhānam*  
*avāpa nārāyaṇa ādi-devaḥ*

**When the primeval Nārāyaṇa accepted the form of the puruṣa, he created the universe from the five elements produced from himself and then entered within the universe by his own portion. (SB** [**11.4.3**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/4.3)**)**

*nārāyaṇas tvaṁ na hi sarva-dehinām*  
*ātmāsy adhīśākhila-loka-sākṣī*  
*nārāyaṇo ’ṅgaṁ nara-bhū-jalāyanāt*  
*tac cāpi satyaṁ na tavaiva māyā*

**O Lord, of whom Mahā-viṣṇu is your expansion! Nārāyaṇa, Mahā-viṣṇu, the source of the other puruṣas is not you. Nor is the third puruṣa, dwelling within all beings, nor is the second puruṣa, the witness of all planets. Mahā-viṣṇu, called Nārāyaṇa because he is the shelter of the Kāraṇa Ocean, which gives rise to material creation, is simply your expansion. That expansion of yours is real, not illusory. (SB** [**10.14.14**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.14)**)**

*nārāyaṇe turīyākhye bhagavac-chabda-śabdite*  
*mano mayy ādadhad yogī mad-dharmā vaśitām iyāt*

**The yogī who concentrates his mind on my form of Nārāyaṇa, known as the fourth factor, Bhagavān, obtains the mystic perfection called** ***vaśitā—*****not being influenced by the guṇas,** **since he possesses my qualities. (SB** [**11.15.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/15.16)**)**

You should describe to us the true nature or tattva (*niṣṭhām*) of these three.

### Text 35

*śrī-pippalāyana uvāca*  
*sthity-udbhava-pralaya-hetur ahetur asya*  
*yat svapna-jāgara-suṣuptiṣu sad bahiś ca*  
*dehendriyāsu-hṛdayāni caranti yena*  
*sañjīvitāni tad avehi paraṁ narendra*

**Перевод:**

**Pippalāyana said: One should know Nārāyaṇa who is the cause of creation, maintenance and destruction of the universe and is without cause, who remains present during the conditions of dream, waking and deep sleep, and beyond those states as well, and who makes the body, senses, life airs and mind move and come to life.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhagavat Sandarbha 2:**

According to the order mentioned in the question, Pippalāyana answers by first describing Bhagavān. He is the cause of creation, maintenance and destruction and is also not the cause. He is beyond the waking, dreaming and deep sleep states. By him the bodies, senses and prāṇas are brought to life and move. Know this form of Nārāyaṇa alone (according to the order of the words in the question) to be supreme tattva (*param*). In order to make the meaning of Brahman clear, Bhagavān is explained first.

Since this tattva has been explained as one only, there are differences in the names of the manifestations of Bhagavān and Brahman with differences of quality and no quality manifest only because of the different realizations of the respective worshippers of these forms.

This should be said. Bhagavān is called “not being the cause (*ahetu*)” because he remains indifferent to the creation by the actions of his svarūpa-śakti. He is the cause of creation, maintenance and destruction through his form as Paramātmā who stimulates prakṛti and jīva into different conditions. Know that form of Bhagavān. He is established as Bhagavān by full manifestation of his śaktis.

The condition of Paramātmā is described. By him everything is brought to life by his entering everything in the form of the jīvas who are his aṁśas. Being impelled by him, the body (with pradhāna and all elements) and senses are made to perform their various functions. Know this to be the form of Paramātmā.

Varuṇa praises Kṛṣṇa:

*namas tubhyaṁ bhagavate brahmaṇe paramātmane*  
*na yatra śrūyate māyā loka-sṛṣṭi-vikalpanā*

**My obeisances unto Bhagavān, Paramātmā and Brahman within whom there is no trace of the illusory energy, which orchestrates the creation of this world. (SB** [**10.28.6**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/28.6)**)**

The commentary says, “Paramātmā means the monitor of all jīvas.” He is called Paramātmā because the jīva’s nature as ātmā depends upon him. Paramātmā means that he assists the jīva.

Then Brahman, without qualities, is described. Know Brahman as that entity which exists in waking, dreaming and deep sleep and in samādhi (*tad-bahiḥ*) as cin-mātra, pure consciousness alone, without qualities.

Similarly speaking of the jīva it is said:

*jāgrat svapnaḥ suṣuptaṁ ca guṇato buddhi-vṛttayaḥ*  
*tāsāṁ vilakṣaṇo jīvaḥ sākṣitvena viniścitaḥ*

**Waking, sleeping and deep sleep, the three functions of the intelligence, are caused by the three guṇas. The jīva is ascertained to be different from these three states since he is only the witness of them. (SB** [**11.13.27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/13.27)**)**

The pure jīva is described in the above verse. However, this not only describes a jīva but implies the full form of consciousness (Brahman) which includes jīva-śakti. Since it is not possible to describe the full form, it is useful to indicate a small portion, just as a person points a finger at a portion of the ocean and says “This is the ocean.” One can understand Brahman by understanding the jīva as non-different from it. That is why the two are equated in the above verse. The jīva and Brahman are considered in this way in other places also. The difference is made known by stating that the jīva exists in states of waking, dreaming and deep sleep and that the pure jīva exists beyond these states (whereas Brahman never has waking, dreaming or deep sleep). The word *ca* indicates that existence beyond the three states.

Know this supreme tattva which manifests in the three forms mentioned. Nārada speaks.

### Text 36

*naitan mano viśati vāg uta cakṣur ātmā*  
*prāṇendriyāṇi ca yathānalam arciṣaḥ svāḥ*  
*śabdo 'pi bodhaka-niṣedhatayātma-mūlam*  
*arthoktam āha yad-ṛte na niṣedha-siddhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Brahman cannot be understood by the mind, what to speak of words, the eye, the** ***jīva*****, the life airs or the senses, just as sparks arising from fire cannot reveal the fire. The wise say that Brahman, though expressed in the Vedas, is not subject to understanding. However, without the existence of Brahman, these negative statements concerning Brahman would have no meaning.**

**Комментарий:**

His supreme position is described. He cannot be understood by material words (*vak*).

Though described in the Vedas (*śabdaḥ*) by statements of direct meaning, those statements forbid understanding by the mind and senses (*bodhaka-niṣedhatayā*). Thus even those words do not describe him, since he has unlimited non-material form and qualities. But he is not without forms and qualities. He is the cause of himself (*ātma-mūlam*). If he speaks something, how can forbiddance have effect?

### Text 37

*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti tri-vṛd ekam ādau*  
*sūtraṁ mahān aham iti pravadanti jīvam*  
*jñāna-kriyārtha-phala-rūpatayoru-śakti*  
*brahmaiva bhāti sad asac ca tayoḥ paraṁ yat*
