*uddāma-bhāva-piśunāmala-valgu-hāsa-*  
*vrīḍāvaloka-nihato madano ’pi yāsām \|*  
*sammuhya cāpam ajahāt pramadottamās tā*  
*yasyendriyaṁ vimathituṁ kuhakair na śekuḥ \|\|*

**Although the queens’ beautiful smiles and furtive glances were all spotless and exciting, and signified deep love, and although they could conquer Cupid himself by making him give up his bow in frustration, those who approached him with false smiles and glances could not agitate the senses of the Lord. (SB** [**1.11.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/11.37)**)**

### Text 19

*vibhvyas tavāmṛta-kathoda-vahās tri-lokyāḥ*  
*pādāvane-ja-saritaḥ śamalāni hantum*  
*ānuśravaṁ śrutibhir aṅghri-jam aṅga-saṅgais*  
*tīrtha-dvayaṁ śuci-ṣadas ta upaspṛśanti*

**Перевод:**

**The nectar-bearing rivers of discussions about you, and also the holy rivers generated from the bathing of your lotus feet, are able to destroy all contamination within the three worlds. But even those who are already pure associate with the holy narrations of your glories by hearing them with their ears and they associate with the holy rivers flowing from your lotus feet by physically bathing in them.**

**Комментарий:**

What more can one say about your actions? This is enough. Even topics about you destroy impurities like lust. *Udavaha* means river by convention, but by etymology means “carrying water.” This meaning then criticizes the sweetness of water in comparison to the sweet topics. The water generated from bathing your feet can also destroy impurities. But what can be said? Even those with pure hearts (*śuci-ṣadaḥ*) constantly serve the two tīrthas to attain special happiness.

### Text 21

*śrī-brahmovāca*  
*bhūmer bhārāvatārāya purā vijñāpitaḥ prabho*  
*tvam asmābhir aśeṣātman tat tathaivopapāditam*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā said: O Lord! Previously we requested you to remove the burden of the earth. O unlimited Lord! That request has certainly been fulfilled.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse is joined with the next. *Tvayā* in verse 22 is joined with *upapāditam* (fulfilled by you.)

### Text 26-27

*nādhunā te ’khilādhāra deva-kāryāvaśeṣitam*  
*kulaṁ ca vipra-śāpena naṣṭa-prāyam abhūd idam*  
*tataḥ sva-dhāma paramaṁ viśasva yadi manyase*  
*sa-lokāl loka-pālān naḥ pāhi vaikuṇṭha-kiṅkarān*

**Перевод:**

**Support of all things! There is nothing remaining at this time for you to do on behalf of the** ***devatās.*** **You have almost withdrawn your dynasty by the curse of the** ***brāhmaṇas.*** **If you so desire, kindly return now to your supreme abode. At the same time, we humbly beg that you always protect us and the inhabitants of the planets since we are your servants.**

**Комментарий:**

You have made your dynasty as if invisible (*naṣṭa-prāyam*). Enter as Kṛṣṇa into your special manifestation of Dvārakā, not visible to the material world (*sva-dhāma*). In your form as Viṣṇu protect us, the protectors of the worlds and protect the servants in Vaikuṇṭha in the various forms of Lord of Vaikuṇṭha, since you appeared in this world with all your aṁśas.

### Text 29

*tad idaṁ yādava-kulaṁ vīrya-śaurya-śriyoddhatam*  
*lokaṁ jighṛkṣad ruddhaṁ me velayeva mahārṇavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Yādava dynasty which has increased in power, courage, and wealth, threatening to spread over the whole universe, has been confined to Dvārakā by me, just as ocean is confined by the shore.**

**Комментарий:**

The dynasty has increased since it cannot be destroyed and desires to spread through the universe since it is unlimited.

### Text 30

*yady asaṁhṛtya dṛptānāṁ yadūnāṁ vipulaṁ kulam*  
*gantāsmy anena loko ’yam udvelena vinaṅkṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**If I were to leave this world without withdrawing the overly proud members of the Yadu dynasty, the whole world would be destroyed by their insane actions.**

**Комментарий:**

It would be auspicious if they spread everywhere since they serve the elders, are generous and kind to brāhmaṇas. In my absence they would destroy the world by their insane actions (*udvelena*).

### Text 31

*idānīṁ nāśa ārabdhaḥ kulasya dvija-śāpa-jaḥ*  
*yāsyāmi bhavanaṁ brahmann etad-ante tavānagha*

**Перевод:**

**Now due to the** ***brāhmaṇa’s*** **curse, the disappearance of my family has already begun. O sinless Brahmā, when my family disappears. I will pay a visit to your abode.**

**Комментарий:**

The disappearance from view (*nāśaḥ*), entering into hidden Dvārakā has begun already. After that entry into invisible Dvārakā, I will go to your abode made by the son of Vikuṇṭhā, having also gone to Vaikuṇṭha above that.

### Text 33

*atha tasyāṁ mahotpātān dvāravatyāṁ samutthitān*  
*vilokya bhagavān āha yadu-vṛddhān samāgatān*

**Перевод:**

**Thereafter, observing that tremendous disturbances were taking place in the city of Dvārakā, the Lord spoke to the assembled senior members of the Yadu dynasty.**

**Комментарий:**

The disturbances took place by the will of the Lord since it was impossible otherwise.

*ity aṅgopadiśanty eke vismṛtya prāg udāhṛtam*  
*muni-vāsa-nivāse kiṁ ghaṭetāriṣṭa-darśanam*

**Some men proposed that the troubles were due to Akrūra’s absence, but they had forgotten the glories of the Supreme Lord, which they themselves had so often described. Indeed, how can calamities occur in a place where the Lord, the residence of all the sages, resides? (SB** [**10.57.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/57.31)**)**

### Text 35

*na vastavyam ihāsmābhir jijīviṣubhir āryakāḥ*  
*prabhāsaṁ su-mahat-puṇyaṁ yāsyāmo ’dyaiva mā ciram*

**Перевод:**

**My dear respected elders! We must not remain any longer in this place if we wish to keep our lives intact. Let us go today to the most pure place Prabhāsa. We should not delay.**

**Комментарий:**

His order indicates that they are equal to him. They should all go. They should go if they desire to avoid disturbance, as he himself desires. Going to Prabhāsa is a pretext only. There should be no semblance of inauspiciousness in his city, the very form of auspiciousness. “Desiring to live” is a statement imitating ordinary humans. It would otherwise contradict the power of accepting the Lord.

### Text 36

*yatra snātvā dakṣa-śāpād gṛhīto yakṣmaṇodu-rāṭ*  
*vimuktaḥ kilbiṣāt sadyo bheje bhūyaḥ kalodayam*

**Перевод:**

**Once, the moon was afflicted with consumption because of the curse of Dakṣa, but just by taking bath at Prabhāsa, the moon was immediately freed from his suffering and again resumed the waxing of his phases.**

**Комментарий:**

The tīrtha is shown to be capable of counteracting the brāhmaṇas’ curse. But if they acted otherwise, the plan of the Lord would be thwarted.

### Text 37-38

*vayaṁ ca tasminn āplutya tarpayitvā pitṝn surān*  
*bhojayitvoṣijo viprān nānā-guṇavatāndhasā*  
*teṣu dānāni pātreṣu śraddhayoptvā mahānti vai*  
*vṛjināni tariṣyāmo dānair naubhir ivārṇavam*

**Перевод:**

**By bathing at Prabhāsa, by offering** ***tarpanas*** **to the Pitṛs and** ***devatās,*** **by feeding the worshipable** ***brāhmaṇas*** **with various delicious foodstuffs and by bestowing gifts upon them as the most suitable candidates for charity, like sowing seeds, we will certainly cross over these terrible dangers through such acts of charity, just as one can cross over a great ocean in a suitable boat.**

**Комментарий:**

By the example of the boat he reinforces his position. One should see the explanations of these statements in Kṛṣṇa Sandarbha 117-134.

### Text 40-41

*tan nirīkṣyoddhavo rājan śrutvā bhagavatoditam*  
*dṛṣṭvāriṣṭāni ghorāṇi nityaṁ kṛṣṇam anuvrataḥ*  
*vivikta upasaṅgamya jagatām īśvareśvaram*  
*praṇamya śirisā pādau prāñjalis tam abhāṣata*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Uddhava, the constant follower of Lord Kṛṣṇa, upon seeing the imminent departure of the Yādavas, hearing from them of the Lord’s instructions, and taking note of the fearful omens, approached the Lord in a private place. He bowed down with his head at the lotus feet of the supreme controller of the universe and with folded hands addressed him as follows.**

**Комментарий:**

First he saw the Yādavas starting their journey to Prabhāsa. Later, he heard that the Lord had given instructions to them. Then he heard from them what he had said. He saw the omens that had been predicted. Actually there was no disturbance, but he determined that there would be an external appearance of disturbance.

### Text 42

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*deva-deveśa yogeśa puṇya-śravaṇa-kīrtana*  
*saṁhṛtyaitat kulaṁ nūnaṁ lokaṁ santyakṣyate bhavān*  
*vipra-śāpaṁ samartho ’pi pratyahan na yad īśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Lord of the** ***devatās!*** **O Lord of** ***yoga-māyā*****! You create purity simply by our hearing and chanting your glories! My Lord! It appears that you will now withdraw your dynasty and you will give up this world. Although you are fully capable of counteracting the** ***brāhmaṇas’*** **curse, you do not do so.**

**Комментарий:**

It seems (*nūnam*) that having brought (*saṁhṛtya*) the family to your beautiful body with a semblance of destruction, you will disappear.

### Text 43

*nāhaṁ tavāṅghri-kamalaṁ kṣaṇārdham api keśava*  
*tyaktuṁ samutsahe nātha sva-dhāma naya mām api*

**Перевод:**

**O Keśava! O master! I cannot tolerate giving up your lotus feet even for a fraction of a moment. Take me along with you to your own abode.**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing that he was not being brought for protection, he prays. Take me to your abode of Dvārakā, the invisible manifestation on earth. As you have taken the Yādavas, take me also. The word *api* would otherwise be meaningless.

### Text 45

*śayyāsanāṭana-sthāna- snāna-krīḍāśanādiṣu*  
*kathaṁ tvāṁ priyam ātmānaṁ vayaṁ bhaktās tyajema hi*

**Перевод:**

**We are your devotees. How can we reject you, our dear self, since we serve you while you lie down, sit, walk, stand, bathe, enjoy recreation, or eat?**

**Комментарий:**

He explains his position.

### Text 46

*tvayopabhukta-srag-gandha- vāso-’laṅkāra-carcitāḥ*  
*ucchiṣṭa-bhojino dāsās tava māyāṁ jayema hi*

**Перевод:**

**Simply by decorating ourselves with the garlands, fragrant oils, clothes and ornaments that you have already enjoyed, and by eating the remnants of your meals, we, your servants, can indeed conquer your illusory energy.**

**Комментарий:**

Not being able to give up the Lord, I pray. It is not from fear of māyā. Even sādhaka devotees surpass māyā and attain happiness by a relationship with something related to you. Since the sādhakas have similar desires, Uddhava takes them as non-different from himself, and using the Yādavas as the subject, speaks about the sādhakas. Even if we have not seen you enjoy the items, we accept them and can conquer māyā.

### Text 48-49

*vayaṁ tv iha mahā-yogin bhramantaḥ karma-vartmasu*  
*tvad-vārtayā tariṣyāmas tāvakair dustaraṁ tamaḥ*  
*smarantaḥ kīrtayantas te kṛtāni gaditāni ca*  
*gaty-utsmitekṣaṇa-kṣveli yan nṛ-loka-viḍambanam*

**Перевод:**

**O great** ***yogī*****! We, on the other hand, though wandering in this world on the path of** ***karma,*** **will overcome insurmountable ignorance along with your devotees, by talking about you, while remembering and glorifying your deeds, words, movements, smiling glances and amorous joking which appear to be human.**

**Комментарий:**

We can overcome (*tarayiṣyāmaḥ*) ignorance.

## Chapter Seven

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*yad āttha māṁ mahā-bhāga tac-cikīrṣitam eva me*  
*brahmā bhavo loka-pālāḥ svar-vāsaṁ me ’bhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O greatly fortunate Uddhava! You have achieved what you desire. Thus Brahmā, Śiva and all other planetary rulers are now praying for me to resume my residence in Vaikuṇṭha. (SB** [**11.7.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/7.1)**)**

**Комментарий:**

You have also achieved what you desire, attaining my abode when the dynasty is withdrawn.

### Text 2

*mayā niṣpāditaṁ hy atra deva-kāryam aśeṣataḥ*  
*yad-artham avatīrṇo ’ham aṁśena brahmaṇārthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Answering the prayer of Brahmā, I descended within this world along with my portion, Baladeva, and performed various activities on behalf of the** ***devatās.*** **I have now completed my mission here.**

**Комментарий:**

*Aṁśena* means “with Balarāma.”

### Text 3

*kulaṁ vai śāpa-nirdagdhaṁ naṅkṣyaty anyonya-vigrahāt*  
*samudraḥ saptame hy enāṁ purīṁ ca plāvayiṣyati*

**Перевод:**

**Now, due to the** ***brāhmaṇas*****’ curse, the Yadu dynasty will certainly perish by fighting among themselves; and on the seventh day from today the ocean will rise up and inundate this city of Dvārakā.**

**Комментарий:**

There would be an imitation of being destroyed by the brāhmaṇa’s curse (*śapa-nirdagdham*). But actually they would simply disappear, by which the curse would be completely counteracted (*nirdagdham*). The ocean will surrounded Dvārakā on all side, and not cover it.

*dvārakāṁ hariṇā tyaktāṁ samudro ’plāvayat kṣaṇāt*  
*varjayitvā mahā-rāja śrīmad-bhagavad-ālayam*

**As soon as Dvārakā was abandoned by the Supreme Lord, the ocean flooded it on all sides, O King, sparing only his palace. (SB** [**11.31.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/31.23)**)**

### Text 4

*yarhy evāyaṁ mayā tyakto loko ’yaṁ naṣṭa-maṅgalaḥ*  
*bhaviṣyaty acirāt sādho kalināpi nirākṛtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O saintly Uddhava! In the near future I will abandon this earth. Then, being overwhelmed by the age of Kali, the earth will be bereft of all auspiciousness.**

**Комментарий:**

I will take you to my abode after some delay because I will establish you now among the sages so that they can receive my instructions. Thus he begins to give instructions.

*noddhavo ’ṇv api man-nyūno*  
*yad guṇair nārditaḥ prabhuḥ*  
*ato mad-vayunaṁ lokaṁ*  
*grāhayann iha tiṣṭhatu*

**Uddhava is not less than me, because he is master of** ***māyā*** **and not at all lacking in any spiritual quality. He should remain on this earth, giving knowledge of me to the world. (SB** [**3.4.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/4.31)**)**

### Text 6

*tvaṁ tu sarvaṁ parityajya snehaṁ sva-jana-bandhuṣu*  
*mayy āveśya manaḥ saṁyak sama-dṛg vicarasva gām*

**Перевод:**

**Now you should completely give up all attachment to your personal friends and relatives and fix your mind on me. Being always conscious of me, observing all things with equal vision, wander throughout the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 66:**

Using Uddhava whom Kṛṣṇa accepted as being as good as himself (SB [3.4.31](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/4.31)), through him, Kṛṣṇa gives instructions to others in this verse. This should be understood in other situations also. The order should be interpreted to mean, “The devotee, following your path, should wander the earth.” The devotee should have equal vision, seeing nothing except the Lord. Thus he does not see good and bad at all. The word *tu* indicates rejection of everything material.

### Text 7

*yad idaṁ manasā vaṁśa cakṣurbhyāṁ śravaṇādibhiḥ*  
*naśvaraṁ gṛhyamāṇaṁ ca viddhi māyā-mano-mayam*

**Перевод:**

**The material universe that you perceive through your mind, speech, eyes, ears and other senses is a temporary creation that is equivalent to what is made of** ***māyā*** **when you perceive in the waking state and to what is made of the mind when you perceive in the dream state.**

**Комментарий:**

Know that all you perceive in waking state predominated by māyā and in dreaming state predominated by mind *(māyā-mana-mayam*) is all temporary.

### Text 8

*puṁso ’yuktasya nānārtho bhramaḥ sa guṇa-doṣa-bhāk*  
*karmākarma-vikarmeti guṇa-doṣa-dhiyo bhidā*

**Перевод:**

**An ignorant person perceives many illusory meanings with conceptions of good and bad. Thinking in terms of good and bad, the ignorant person sees differences in terms of prescribed actions, neglected actions and forbidden actions.**

**Комментарий:**

A person with uncontrolled mind (*ayuktasya*) has illusions and consequently many anārthas. Thus seeing in terms of good and bad, he sees differences in action.

### Text 10

*jñāna-vijñāna-saṁyukta ātma-bhūtaḥ śarīriṇām*  
*atmānubhava-tuṣṭātmā nāntarāyair vihanyase*

**Перевод:**

**Being fully endowed with the conclusion of the Vedas and its realization, satisfied by realization of ā Brahman*****,*** **being the object of affection by all beings, you will not be checked by any obstacles.**

**Комментарий:**

*Ātmā* means Brahman.

### Text 13

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*ity ādiṣṭo bhagavatā mahā-bhāgavato nṛpa*  
*uddhavaḥ praṇipatyāha tattvaṁ jijñāsur acyutam*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmi said: O King! The Supreme Lord thus instructed his pure devotee Uddhava, who was eager to receive knowledge from the Lord. Uddhava then offered obeisances to the Lord and spoke as follows.**

**Комментарий:**

Though he is a great devotee, Uddhava appears to inquire about the highest truth with respect for the Lord’s teachings, and like a low person out of humility, though he has great qualification, speaks, because he is being left by the Lord.

### Text 14

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*yogeśa yoga-vinyāsa yogātman yoga-sambhava*  
*niḥśreyasāya me proktas tyāgaḥ sannyāsa-lakṣaṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O master of all types of yoga! O teacher of** ***jñāna-yoga*****! O embodiment of all** ***yogas*****! O Lord, you make your appearance through** ***bhakti-yoga*****! You have explained renunciation performed by** ***sannyāsīs*** **for the highest benefit.**

**Комментарий:**

He speaks in three verses to make his request.

### Text 18

*tasmād bhavantam anavadyam ananta-pāraṁ*  
*sarva-jñam īśvaram akuṇṭha-vikuṇṭha-dhiṣṇyam*  
*nirviṇṇa-dhīr aham u he vṛjinābhitapto*  
*nārāyaṇaṁ nara-sakhaṁ śaraṇaṁ prapadye*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Feeling disgust with material life because of being afflicted by suffering, I now surrender unto you, the perfect, unlimited, omniscient Lord, whose spiritual abode in Vaikuṇṭha is free from all disturbances. I surrender to you, the shelter of Mahā-viṣṇu, the true friend of all living beings.**

**Комментарий:**

I surrender to the supreme shelter of the great puruṣa who creates mahat-tattva (*nārāyaṇam*) who has appeared as the friend of Nara to show mercy to the condition of mankind.

### Text 19

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*prāyeṇa manujā loke loka-tattva-vicakṣaṇāḥ*  
*samuddharanti hy ātmānam ātmanaivāśubhāśayāt*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Generally those human beings who understand the actual truth are able deliver themselves from the desire for sense objects by their intelligence.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord speaks according to what Uddhava requested.

### Text 20

*ātmano gurur ātmaiva puruṣasya viśeṣataḥ*  
*yat pratyakṣānumānābhyāṁ śreyo ’sāv anuvindate*

**Перевод:**

**An intelligent person, acting as** ***guru*** **instructs, can achieve great benefit by perception and logic.**

**Комментарий:**

*Āmanah guruḥ* means “he who acts according to the guru’s instructions.”

### Text 21

*puruṣatve ca māṁ dhīrāḥ sāṅkhya-yoga-viśāradāḥ*  
*āvistarāṁ prapaśyanti sarva-śakty-upabṛṁhitam*

**Перевод:**

**In the human form of life, those who have great knowledge and are expert in scriptures can directly see me endowed with all of my energies.**

**Комментарий:**

Attaining a human form (*puruṣatve*) persons who have great knowledge (*āvistarām*) and are knowledgeable of scriptures (*dhīrāḥ*) see me.

### Text 23

*atra māṁ mṛgayanty addhā yuktā hetubhir īśvaram*  
*gṛhyamāṇair guṇair liṅgair agrāhyam anumānataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Engaged in** ***bhakti,*** **they search for me, the Lord, directly by processes like hearing and chanting. But I am not attained by inference using indicators and intelligence.**

**Комментарий:**

They search for the Lord, seeing him as different from the jīva since the jīva is not independent (*īśvaram*). He is not subject to anumāna but can be known if one follows scripture.

### Text 27

*prāyo dharmārtha-kāmeṣu vivitsāyāṁ ca mānavāḥ*  
*hetunaiva samīhanta āyuṣo yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Generally human beings work hard to cultivate religiosity, economic development, sense gratification and also inquiry, to increase the duration of their lives, acquire fame and enjoy wealth.**

**Комментарий:**

H\[U1\]umans engage in inquiry (*vivitsāyām*).

### Text 50

*guṇair guṇān upādatte yathā-kālaṁ vimuñcati*  
*na teṣu yujyate yogī gobhir gā iva go-patiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***yogī*** **accepts objects by his senses and at an appropriate time gives them away without attachment, just as the sun draws water by its rays and then disperses it without attachment.**

**Комментарий:**

At the appropriate time, the yogī accepts objects when he is hungry and then rejects them.

### Text 51

*budhyate svena bhedena vyakti-stha iva tad-gataḥ*  
*lakṣyate sthūla-matibhir ātmā cāvasthito ’rka-vat*

**Перевод:**

**Paramātmā is understood to be situated along with his** ***śaktis,*** **just as the sun is situated with its** ***śaktis*****. However unintelligent people consider the Paramātmā to be identical to his** ***śaktis.***

**Комментарий:**

The ātmā or Paramātmā is understood to be situated with difference, as śakti and śaktimān and is seen by the unintelligent to be situated in upādhis (*vyakti-sthaḥ*) as if attached (*tad-gataḥ),* just as the sun, endowed with various palaces, is seen as different from its disk and rays.

## Chapter Eight

### Text 5

*muniḥ prasanna-gambhīro durvigāhyo duratyayaḥ*  
*ananta-pāro hy akṣobhyaḥ stimitoda ivārṇavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A sage is happy but inscrutable, impossible to understand and impossible to surpass. He does not reveal his inner nature and is without agitation, like the calm ocean.**

**Комментарий:**

The ocean, though appearing peaceful and satisfied, is immeasurably deep. The sage, though satisfied in his heart, does not reveal his secret deity of worship. Both are impossible to judge, difficult to understand (*durvigāhyaḥ*). Thus it is impossible to defeat the ocean since has plentiful water and impossible to defeat the sage because his power is so great (*duratyayaḥ*). The ocean and the sage are without limit, because they cannot be divided into parts by their nature. They are undisturbed since the great water body is fixed and the sage cannot be disturbed by sense objects since he has no attachment.

### Text 10

*aṇubhyaś ca mahadbhyaś ca śāstrebhyaḥ kuśalo naraḥ*  
*sarvataḥ sāram ādadyāt puṣpebhya iva ṣaṭpadaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the honeybee takes nectar from all flowers, big and small, an intelligent human being should take the essence from all religious scriptures.**

**Комментарий:**

There are three types of honey collectors: the bee, the makṣikā which does not store honey and the makṣikā that stores it. These three types are used to illustrate the conduct of the sage.

### Text 16

*su-duḥkhopārjitair vittair āśāsānāṁ gṛhāśiṣaḥ*  
*madhu-hevāgrato bhuṅkte yatir vai gṛha-medhinām*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a hunter takes away the honey laboriously produced by the honeybees, similarly, saintly mendicants such as** ***brahmacārīs*** **and** ***sannyāsīs*** **are entitled to enjoy the property painstakingly accumulated by householders dedicated to family enjoyment.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse describes the person from the place called Svaśa who collects the bee’s honey. The sannyāsī enjoys first, before others, the objects like food, belonging to householders who have prospered by wealth (*gṛhāśisaḥ*), who are full of desire (*āśāsānām*).

### Text 21

*tāvaj jitendriyo na syād vijitānyendriyaḥ pumān*  
*na jayed rasanaṁ yāvaj jitaṁ sarvaṁ jite rase*

**Перевод:**

**Although one may conquer all of the other senses, as long as the tongue is not conquered it cannot be said that one has controlled his senses. However, if one is able to control the tongue, then one is understood to be in full control of all the senses.**

**Комментарий:**

Conquering the tongue, one conquers everything. This happens automatically if one can control the tongue.

### Text 22

*piṅgalā nāma veśyāsīd videha-nagare purā*  
*tasyā me śikṣitaṁ kiñcin nibodha nṛpa-nandana*

**Перевод:**

**O son of kings! Previously in the city of Videha there dwelled a prostitute named Piṅgalā. Now please hear what I have learned from that lady.**

**Комментарий:**

Though this section concerns jñāna, he shows the highest bhakti in the story to be narrated.

### Text 31

*santaṁ samīpe ramaṇaṁ rati-pradaṁ*  
*vitta-pradaṁ nityam imaṁ vihāya*  
*akāma-daṁ duḥkha-bhayādhi-śoka-*  
*moha-pradaṁ tuccham ahaṁ bhaje ’jñā*

**Перевод:**

**Giving up the eternal Lord situated within me, the Lord who accepts love and gives love and wealth, foolishly I serve insignificant men who give dissatisfaction, sorrow, fear, worry, lamentation and illusion.**

**Комментарий:**

By her previously described saṁskāras or by an appearance of the Lord by the power of Dattātreya’s mercy, the Lord existed within her (*santam samīpe*). The Lord was the giver of a particular type of prīti (*rati-pradam*) and fulfilled all desires as with Kubjā (*vitta-pradam*).

### Text 35

*suhṛt preṣṭhatamo nātha ātmā cāyaṁ śarīriṇām*  
*taṁ vikrīyātmanaivāhaṁ rame ’nena yathā ramā*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is the dearest for all living beings because he is everyone’s well-wisher and the soul situated in everyone’s heart. Therefore I will now pay the price of complete surrender, and, thus purchasing the Lord, I will enjoy with him just like Lakṣmī.**

**Комментарий:**

I pay the price in a special way, by giving myself.

### Text 41

*saṁsāra-kūpe patitaṁ viṣayair muṣitekṣaṇam*  
*grastaṁ kālāhinātmānaṁ ko ’nyas trātum adhīśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Who else but the Supreme Lord can save the living entity who has fallen into the well of** ***saṁsāra*****, has become blinded by sense objects and has been devoured by the snake of time?**

**Комментарий:**

I surrender to him alone, since no one else can save the living entity.

### Text 42

*ātmaiva hy ātmano goptā nirvidyeta yadākhilāt*  
*apramatta idaṁ paśyed grastaṁ kālāhinā jagat*

**Перевод:**

**The living entity sees that the entire universe has been seized by the serpent of time, when, becoming sober, he detaches himself from all material enjoyment. The Lord is his protector.**

**Комментарий:**

Paramātmā is the protector of the jīva. When ātmā, being sober (*apramattaḥ*), becomes detached from everything other than Paramātmā, he sees the world devoured by time.

### Text 44

*āśā hi paramaṁ duḥkhaṁ nairāśyaṁ paramaṁ sukham*  
*yathā sañchidya kāntāśāṁ sukhaṁ suṣvāpa piṅgalā*

**Перевод:**

**Material desire is undoubtedly the cause of the greatest unhappiness, and freedom from such desire is the cause of the greatest happiness. Therefore, completely cutting off her desire to enjoy lovers, Piṅgalā happily went to sleep.**

**Комментарий:**

She became blissful by taking shelter of the Lord.

## Chapter Nine

### Text 7

*sā taj jugupsitaṁ matvā mahatī vṛīḍitā tataḥ*  
*babhañjaikaikaśaḥ śaṅkhān dvau dvau pāṇyor aśeṣayat*

**Перевод:**

**The young girl feared that the men would consider her family to be poor because their daughter was engaged in husking rice. Being very intelligent, the shy girl broke the shell bracelets from her arms, leaving just two on each wrist.**

**Комментарий:**

They saw she had many bangles on her hands and if there were no noise from husking, it would mean that some other person was husking the rice.

### Text 14

*eka-cāry aniketaḥ syād apramatto guhāśayaḥ*  
*alakṣyamāṇa ācārair munir eko ’lpa-bhāṣaṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A saintly person should remain alone and constantly travel without any fixed residence. Being attentive, he should remain secluded and should act in such a way that no one knows what is doing. Moving without companions, he should not speak more than required.**

**Комментарий:**

He should be attentive to his acts and conduct (*apramatta*ḥ). He should behave in such a way that no one knows what he is doing (*alakṣyamānaḥ*).

### Text 16

*eko nārāyaṇo devaḥ pūrva-sṛṣṭaṁ sva-māyayā*  
*saṁhṛtya kāla-kalayā kalpānta idam īśvaraḥ*  
*eka evādvitīyo ’bhūd ātmādhāro ’khilāśrayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The one form of Viṣṇu withdrew the universe previously created by his** ***māyā*** **through his time** ***śakti*** **at the end of Brahmā’s life, and remained alone, the support of himself and shelter of all his** ***śaktis*****.**

**Комментарий:**

The lord is Nārāyaṇa, called the creator of mahat-tattva. He is the one antaryāmī of the whole universe. He is the one person. There is no other creator with his powers (*ekaḥ*). At the beginning of the kalpa, the creation took place by māyā, his śakti, whose portion is time. At the end of the kalpa he withdraws the universe: everything takes shelter of him, acting as the place of destruction. There are no jīvas differentiated at that time with separate consciousness (*ekaḥ*). There is no differentiation of pradhāna, which is very different from him (*advitīyaḥ*). He was the container of all the lords of Mahā-vaikuṇṭha (*ātmādharaḥ*). He had entered the spiritual world.

### Text 17-18

*kālenātmānubhāvena sāmyaṁ nītāsu śaktiṣu*  
*sattvādiṣv ādi-puruṣaḥ pradhāna-puruṣeśvaraḥ*  
*parāvarāṇāṁ parama āste kaivalya-saṁjñitaḥ*  
*kevalānubhavānanda- sandoho nirupādhikaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When he brought his energies such as** ***sattva*** **to equilibrium by his powerful energy of time, the Lord, who enjoys in himself, who is the controller of** ***prakṛti*** **and the** ***jīvas,*** **who is worshipped by liberated and conditioned** ***jīvas*****, remained in the form of complete bliss, without** ***māyā.***

**Комментарий:**

The first puruṣa (*ādi-puruṣaḥ*), the creator of mahat-tattva, the controller of jīvas and prakṛti (*pradhāna-puruṣeśvaraḥ*) by time, representing his power *(ātmānubhāvena*), brought his energies to equilibrium. He is the supreme shelter of his svāṁśa forms (*para*) and the jīvas (*avara*) and is called *kaivalya*. He alone remained. *Kaivalya* means *kevala.* In that oneness or purity his internal difference is shown. He has a variegated form of various types of bliss and actions (*anubhāva*) arising from his svarūpa, which are pure (*kevala*), because they are variety arising from his svarūpa-śakti. That *kevala* nature is expressed: he is without upādhis.

*sa vā eṣa tadā draṣṭā nāpaśyad dṛśyam ekarāṭ*  
*mene ’santam ivātmānaṁ supta-śaktir asupta-dṛk*

**The one** ***puruṣa*****, who glances over** ***prakṛti,*** **at the beginning of creation, could not see her. He, the possessor of** ***māyā*****, who was sleeping, and the possessor of spiritual consorts, who were awake, considered himself non-existent without her. (SB** [**3.5.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/5.24)**)**

### Text 19

*kevalātmānubhāvena sva-māyāṁ tri-guṇātmikām*  
*saṅkṣobhayan sṛjaty ādau tayā sūtram arindama*

**Перевод:**

**O subduer of the enemies! At the time of creation the Lord by the power of his** ***cit-śakti*** **agitates** ***prakṛti*** **made of the** ***guṇas*** **and creates** ***mahat-tattva (sūtra).***

**Комментарий:**

Establishing his svarūpa-śakti, the creation is then described. At the time of creation, appearing from the lord of Vaikuṇṭha, agitating his māyā, pradhāna with the three guṇas, the puruṣa created sūtra by power of his pure form (*kevalānubhāvena*).

### Text 21

*yathorṇanābhir hṛdayād ūrṇāṁ santatya vaktrataḥ*  
*tayā vihṛtya bhūyas tāṁ grasaty evaṁ maheśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as from within himself the spider expands thread through his mouth, plays with it for some time and eventually swallows it, similarly, the Supreme Lord produces the universe and then withdraws it.**

**Комментарий:**

The spider is taken as guru to illustrate a particular feature. The spider produces a thin thread from its mouth and then withdraws it.

### Text 24

*evaṁ gurubhya etebhya eṣā me śikṣitā matiḥ*  
*svātmopaśikṣitāṁ buddhiṁ śṛṇu me vadataḥ prabho*

**Перевод:**

**O King, from all these spiritual masters I have acquired great wisdom. Now please listen as I explain what I learned from my own body.**

**Комментарий:**

*Matiḥ* is in the singular to express a type.

### Text 25

*deho gurur mama virakti-viveka-hetur*  
*bibhrat sma sattva-nidhanaṁ satatārty-udarkam*  
*tattvāny anena vimṛśāmi yathā tathāpi*  
*pārakyam ity avasito vicarāmy asaṅgaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My body is a** ***guru*** **since it teaches detachment and discrimination. The body supports creation and destruction and its final result is suffering. As much as I discern truth by the body with its senses, I engage in** ***bhakti*** **using the body and senses. But convinced that the body is fit for the dogs and jackals, I wander in the world without attachment to the body.**

**Комментарий:**

The body is the human body. By that body I discern the truth. In non-human bodies such intelligence is lacking.

### Text 31

*na hy ekasmād guror jñāna su-sthiraṁ syāt su-puṣkalam*  
*brahmaitad advitīyaṁ vai gīyate bahudharṣibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Although the Lord is one without a second, the sages have described him in many different ways. Therefore one may not be able to acquire firm and complete knowledge from one** ***guru.***

**Комментарий:**

“One should have one qualified guru. From him one will attain knowledge with all its parts.” Or “Other opinions will be presented by others. What is the use of using material objects as gurus?” Steady, complete knowledge cannot be attained from the mouth of one guru. Why? There are many different ideas about Brahman. To strengthen the ideas presented by the mahāguru, and to destroy contrary ideas, in order to contemplate properly with one’s intelligence, ordinary objects can be considered gurus. But one should not accept persons like Kapila with contrary ideas.

## Chapter Ten

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*mayoditeṣv avahitaḥ sva-dharmeṣu mad-āśrayaḥ*  
*varṇāśrama-kulācāram akāmātmā samācaret*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Taking full shelter in me through** ***bhakti*** **as explained by me, while being without material desire, one should practice** ***varṇāśrama.***

**Комментарий:**

Using Uddhava, the Lord gave teachings. He here clearly teaches others.

### Text 3

*suptasya viṣayāloko dhyāyato vā manorathaḥ*  
*nānātmakatvād viphalas tathā bhedātma-dhīr guṇaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as objects seen in dreams or in the waking mind yield no spiritual results since they depend on material objects, intelligence depending on material objects using the material senses yields no spiritual results.**

**Комментарий:**

Material intelligence seeing external differences with the senses does not become spiritual intelligence or bhakti, perceiving spiritual difference, since spiritual intelligence is one with the form of the Lord, its object of perception.

### Text 4

*nivṛttaṁ karma seveta pravṛttaṁ mat-paras tyajet*  
*jijñāsāyāṁ sampravṛtto nādriyet karma-codanām*

**Перевод:**

**The person dedicated to me engages in** ***karmas*** **without desire and gives up** ***karmas*** **for material results. The person completely fixed in** ***yogārūḍha*** **does not care for daily or periodic** ***karmas.***

**Комментарий:**

The person dedicated to me gives up karmas for material results, performs karmas without desire, and if fixed in me completely, gives up karmas completely. Without taking shelter of me, all three actions become useless. The affix *sam* indicates complete renunciation of matter (*saṁpravṛttaḥ*). Gītā says:

*ārurukṣor muner yogaṁ karma kāraṇam ucyate \|*  
*yogārūḍhasya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇam ucyate \|\|*

**For the sage wishing to attain** ***yoga*****, action is said to be the cause of elevation. For a person who has already attained** ***yoga*****, cessation of action is said to be the cause of maintaining that state.**

*yadā hi nendriyārtheṣu na karmasv anuṣajjate \|*  
*sarva-saṅkalpa-sannyāsī yogārūḍhas tadocyate \|\|*

**When a person has no attachment to the sense objects or to the actions to attain them, having renounced all desires, he is called** ***yogārūḍha*****. (BG** [**6.3-4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/6.3)**)**

### Text 5

*yamān abhīkṣṇaṁ seveta niyamān mat-paraḥ kvacit*  
*mad-abhijñaṁ guruṁ śāntam upāsīta mad-ātmakam*

**Перевод:**

**The person dedicated to me should respect the prohibitions such as non-violence and the rules such as cleanliness. He should serve the peaceful** ***guru*** **who knows me, who is not different from me.**

**Комментарий:**

The last line describes guru, as does the next verse.

### Text 7

*jāyāpatya-gṛha-kṣetra- svajana-draviṇādiṣu*  
*udāsīnaḥ samaṁ paśyan sarveṣv artham ivātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One should see one’s real self-interest in life in all circumstances and should therefore remain detached from wife, children, home, land, relatives, friends, wealth and other things.**

**Комментарий:**

He should not have extreme attachment to wife etc. because he desires the happiness and destruction of suffering for all jīvas as if it were his own happiness, and thus sees all equally.

### Text 8

*vilakṣaṇaḥ sthūla-sūkṣmād dehād ātmekṣitā sva-dṛk*  
*yathāgnir dāruṇo dāhyād dāhako ’nyaḥ prakāśakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***ātmā*****, self-revealing, the observer, is different from the subtle and gross bodies, just as fire, the burning agent and illuminator, is different from wood which is burned, since the revealer is different from the revealed.**

**Комментарий:**

“Because there will be extreme possessiveness because of intense relationships with wife etc., it is unavoidable to seek out their special benefit.” The relationship is rejected by kaimutya in four verses.

### Text 12

*ācāryo ’raṇir ādyaḥ syād ante-vāsy uttarāraṇiḥ*  
*tat-sandhānaṁ pravacanaṁ vidyā-sandhiḥ sukhāvahaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***guru*** **is the wood base, the student is the wood on top, the teachings are the kindling stick, and knowledge, which brings bliss, is the fire.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 208:**

The guru is the base. The teachings (*pravacanam*) are the kindling stick between (*sandhānam*) the bottom (guru) and top (student). *Vidyā* means knowledge arising from scriptures. This is like fire arising by the combination of the three pieces of wood. Śruti says *tad-vijñānārthaṁ sa gurum evābhigacched*: to attain that knowledge one must approach a guru. (Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad [1.1.12](https://vedarama.com/en/books/mundaka/1.1.12)) *ācāryavān puruṣo veda*: accepting a guru one can attain knowledge. (Chāndogya Upaniṣad [6.14.2](https://vedarama.com/en/books/chandogya/6.14.2)) *naiṣā tarkeṇa matir apaneyā proktānyenaiva sujñānāya preṣṭhā*: this knowledge cannot be achieved by logic. O dear one! It should be taught by another person in order to be understood properly. (Kaṭha Upaniṣad [1.2.9](https://vedarama.com/en/books/katha-bs/1.2.9))

### Text 13

*vaiśāradī sāti-viśuddha-buddhir*  
*dhunoti māyāṁ guṇa-samprasūtām*  
*gunāṁś ca sandahya yad-ātmam etat*  
*svayaṁ ca śāṁyaty asamid yathāgniḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Pure** ***vidyā*** **arising from the Lord destroys** ***avidyā*** **in the form of the gross and subtle bodies, which arise from the** ***guṇas.*** **After destroying** ***avidyā*** **and the** ***guṇas,*** ***vidyā*** **itself is destroyed, just as a fire goes out when deprived of fuel.**

**Комментарий:**

The fire goes out when deprived of fuel. It remains in a subtle form but cannot be seen clearly.

*na hi draṣṭur dṛṣṭer viparilopo vidyate’vināśitvān*

**The seeing capacity of the seer is not lost since it is indestructible. (Bṛhad-āraṇyaka Upaniṣad** [**4.3.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bau/4.3.23)**)**

### Text 14-16

*athaiṣām karma-kartṝṇāṁ bhoktṝṇāṁ sukha-duḥkhayoḥ*  
*nānātvam atha nityatvaṁ loka-kālāgamātmanām*  
*manyase sarva-bhāvānāṁ saṁsthā hy autpattikī yathā*  
*tat-tad-ākṛti-bhedena jāyate bhidyate ca dhīḥ*  
*evam apy aṅga sarveṣāṁ dehināṁ deha-yogataḥ*  
*kālāvayavataḥ santi bhāvā janmādayo ’sakṛt*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! If you consider the varieties of places, time, scriptures and bodies, as well as the pleasure and pain achieved by the** ***jīvas*** **who perform** ***karmas*** **to be eternal, and consider all objects to be naturally permanent and real, with distinct forms and without a temporary nature, then repeated birth will still continue because all j*****īvas*** **are connected with bodies and are subject to the divisions of time.**

**Комментарий:**

Some consider this to be the self, but actually it is the result of the subtle body covering the jīva. It is more and more clear that the instructions are for others, not Uddhava. Uddhava is not an atheist. Śrīdhara Svāmī gives an example. The sun, being covered by smoke, an upādhi, transforms into a cloud by the rays of the sun, but remains at all times.

### Text 17

*tatrāpi karmaṇāṁ kartur asvātantryaṁ ca lakṣyate*  
*bhoktuś ca duḥkha-sukhayoḥ ko nv artho vivaśaṁ bhajet*

**Перевод:**

**It can be seen that the performer of** ***karmas*** **is powerless since he experiences both happiness and suffering. What puruṣārtha would make a person helplessness?**

**Комментарий:**

What puruṣārtha would make a person without control, completely dependent? It should make him steady.

### Text 18

*na dehināṁ sukhaṁ kiñcid vidyate viduṣām api*  
*tathā ca duḥkhaṁ mūḍhānāṁ vṛthāhaṅkaraṇaṁ param*

**Перевод:**

**The learned person is not always happy and the fool is not always suffering. Only persons with false pride will claim that one can always be happy by performing** ***karmas.***

**Комментарий:**

The wise person is not always happy. This is because of performing faulty karmas from carelessness. The fool is not always suffering. This is because of some puṇya generated from relationship to a tīrtha etc.

### Text 31

*guṇāḥ sṛjanti karmāṇi guṇo ’nusṛjate guṇān*  
*jīvas tu guṇa-saṁyukto bhuṅkte karma-phalāny asau*

**Перевод:**

**The material senses create material activities with results, either pious or sinful, and the modes of nature set the material senses into motion. The living entity, being fully engaged by the material senses and modes of nature, experiences the various results of action.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhāgavatam is supported by śruti which says:

*bhīṣā’smād vātaḥ pavate, bhīṣodeti sūryaḥ \|*  
*bhīṣā’smād agniś cendraś ca, mṛtyur dhāvati pañcamaḥ \|\|*

**In fear of the Lord the wind blows, and the sun rises. Out of fear of the Lord, Agni, Indra and death flee. (Taittirīya Upanisad** [**2.8.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/taittiriya/2.8.1)**)**

The Lord does not do this personally. Other controllers are rejected in four verses.

The Lord inspires the actions by proximity like a magnet. Matter does not act on its own. Or the verse negates the idea that the jīva by himself performs actions and enjoys the results, by supplying the Sāṅkhya idea. The guṇas are the cause of the jīva’s acting in a particular

### Text 32

*yāvat syād guṇa-vaiṣamyaṁ tāvan nānātvam ātmanaḥ*  
*nānātvam ātmano yāvat pāratantryaṁ tadaiva hi*

**Перевод:**

**As long as there is disturbance of the guṇas there will be variety for the ātmā. As long as variety exists, ātmā will be dependent on** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

Variety is negated according to the advayavādī. The ideas of the advayavādī are presented. There is only one ātmā, whose svarūpa is consciousness and nothing else. By beginningless avidyā, it produces māyā made of the guṇas and its effects generated its disturbance in the ātmā. Like a dream it produces many ideas of you and one idea of myself. This identity of I is its svarūpa, and is the puruṣaḥ. “You” is of three types: other puruṣas, unconscious things, and a special puruṣa called īśvara. When ignorance is gone, because there is no variety, īśvara does not exist. What to speak of a jīva being dependent on īśvara. What to speak of fearing him. What to speak of being dependent on others.

The proposition that the universe which is dissimilar does not exist at any time and that knowledge does not eternally exist are refuted by the two ideas explained, since they show the creation and destruction of upādhis and non-destruction of knowledge.

### Text 33

*yāvad asyāsvatantratvaṁ tāvad īśvarato bhayam*  
*ya etat samupāsīraṁs te muhyanti śucārpitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**As long as the** ***jīva*** **is dependent on** ***karma,*** **he will fear the Lord*****.*** **Those who devote themselves to these ideas are bewildered and subject to lamentation.**

**Комментарий:**

The three statements just explained are criticized in the last line.

### Text 34

*kāla ātmāgamo lokaḥ svabhāvo dharma eva ca*  
*iti māṁ bahudhā prāhur guṇa-vyatikare sati*

**Перевод:**

**When there is agitation and interaction of the material modes of nature, I take on various forms of** ***māyā*****: as the time for enjoyment, the body suitable for enjoyment, as the scriptures proclaiming** ***karma*****, as the places giving enjoyment, as causes of transformation, and as rules for creating enjoyment.**

**Комментарий:**

The first four according to Jaimini are the causes of accomplishing action in the world.

Svabhāva refers to the various causes of unique effects from the guṇas etc. according to Sāṅkhya. Dharma is existence itself according to advaya-vāda. Karma, called apūrva, should also have been mentioned since is the cause of experiencing happiness and distress. They call me these many forms, each acting as a cause and also together acting as a cause, though they are not the whole cause. They call me these things.

*ye ’py anya-devatā-bhaktā yajante śraddhayānvitāḥ \|*  
*te ’pi mām eva kaunteya yajanty avidhi-pūrvakam \|\|*

**Those who are devoted to other gods and with faith worship them, worship me by the wrong method, O son of Kuntī.**

*ahaṁ hi sarva-yajñānāṁ bhoktā ca prabhur eva ca \|*  
*na tu mām abhijānanti tattvenātaś cyavanti te \|\|*

**I am the enjoyer and master of all sacrifices. Those who do not know me as such continue to take birth. (BG** [**9.23-24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.23)**)**

The cause is the association with and defeat by the guṇas (*guṇa-vyatikare*). Conquered by the guṇas, people become atheists, opposing logic, śruti and smṛti. These people call me by these names (time, etc.). But for the theists, these things do not contaminate the Lord.

*dehendriya-prāṇa-mano-dhiyo 'mī*  
*yad-aṁśa-viddhāḥ pracaranti karmasu*  
*naivānyadā lauham ivāprataptaṁ*  
*sthāneṣu tad draṣṭrapadeśam eti*

**Just as unheated iron cannot burn, the body, senses, life airs and intelligence move to their objects only when endowed with consciousness by the Lord. The Lord allows the** ***jīva,*** **who can see in** **waking and dream states, to see him--by endowing him with** ***cit-śakti.*** **(SB** [**6.16.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/16.24)**)**

Parmātmā, the Lord’s aṁśa, stimulates these unconscious components. The doership and enjoyment of the jīvas, conscious particles, which are svarūpa-siddha, does not become apparent without upādhis. By the Lord’s proximity, from the upādhis, jīva’s doership and ability to enjoy arise. Jīva does not have a direct relationship with these, but by ignorance as mentioned in verse 14.

Let there be variety with all the jīvas but there is oneness in the form of Paramātmā. Let there be permanence in the form of the cause of the world and its protectors, called avyakta (prakṛti) and impermanence in the gross form of the universe. There is no permanence at all in the upādhi aspect of the ātmās, but permanence in their svarūpa (as ātmā). Let there be permanence of time in the form of the Lord’s power and permanence of scriptures as the form of the breathing of the Lord, where it does not spread during pralaya. Let the cause of transformation be the guṇas or the ātmā covered by ignorance, by possessing the power of the Lord who is the final cause. Since it is proved by śruti, smṛti and the realization of the wise, that everything takes shelter of the Lord, persons attached to material enjoyment will have fear of the Lord, who actually exists. It is best to become detached. This has been stated to defeat Jaimini’s philosophy. Elsewhere it is stated with the same conclusion, to defeat other opinions:

*ye ’nye ’ravindākṣa vimukta-māninas*  
*tvayy asta-bhāvād aviśuddha-buddhayaḥ*  
*āruhya kṛcchreṇa paraṁ padaṁ tataḥ*  
*patanty adho ’nādṛta-yuṣmad-aṅghrayaḥ*

**O lotus-eyed Lord, although nondevotees, having impure intelligence because of not having devotion to you, think that they are liberated and become** ***jīvanmuktas*** **after severe austerities and penances, they fall down from their position of imagined superiority because they have no regard for the lotus feet of you and your devotees. (SB** [**10.2.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/2.32)**)**

### Text 35

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*guṇeṣu vartamāno ’pi deha-jeṣv anapāvṛtaḥ*  
*guṇair na badhyate dehī badhyate vā kathaṁ vibho*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Lord! The** ***jīva*** **in his uncovered state exists in the** ***guṇas*** **created by his body, but is he not bound up by the** ***guṇas.*** **Or is he bound up?**

**Комментарий:**

Though the jīva exists uncovered by the guṇas (*deha-jeṣu*), does he not become bound up? Or if he exists uncovered, does he become bound up? In the absence of the guṇas the jīva would be liberated. The guṇas would by their nature cover him if present.

## Chapter Eleven

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*baddho mukta iti vyākhyā guṇato me na vastutaḥ*  
*guṇasya māyā-mūlatvān na me mokṣo na bandhanam*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: One cannot say that there is bondage or liberation due to the** ***guṇas*** **for me, since bondage arises from the guṇas, controlled by me. Because the relationship with the** ***guṇas*** **arises only by** ***avidyā*** **or ignorance, there is no real bondage, and consequently no liberation for me.**

**Комментарий:**

A false relationship with the guṇas arises by māyā. There is no bondage or liberation for me, since that arises from dependence on the guṇas, whereas I am the controller of the guṇas.

### Text 3

*vidyāvidye mama tanū viddhy uddhava śarīriṇām*  
*mokṣa-bandha-karī ādye māyayā me vinirmite*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Understand that** ***vidyā*** **and** ***avidyā*** **are my** ***śaktis.*** **They are created by my** ***māyā,*** **are without beginning, and create liberation and bondage for the living beings.**

**Комментарий:**

How ignorance takes place is explained. Though I am liberated eternally, I appear to be bound up. How can both states occur simultaneously? That is explained. Vidyā and avidyā, without beginning (*ādye*), māyā’s own expanded forms (which demonstrate her nature) which spread out (*vinirmite*) by my māyā-śakti, are situated as causes of liberation and bondage for the jīvas (*śārīrīnām*). Māyā is a devotee like Yamarāja.

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.*** **(SB** [**11.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37)**)**

When a jīva is opposed to me, ignorance spreads out and causes bondage while covering jīva’s svarūpa. When a jīva becomes favorable to me, vidyā manifests the svarūpa of the jīva, and causes liberation, while discarding the covering of guṇas. Māyā is the controller of vidyā and avidyā, but operates because of me. *Bandha-moksā-kārī* is formed according to the rule *supāṁ suluk pūrva-savarṇa*. (Pāninī 7.1.39)

### Text 4

*ekasyaiva mamāṁśasya jīvasyaiva mahā-mate*  
*bandho ’syāvidyayānādir vidyayā ca tathetaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O intelligent Uddhava! The bondage of the** ***jīva,*** **who is my one part or** ***taṭastha-śakti,*** **is created by** ***avidyā*** **and is without beginning. By** ***vidyā*****, he achieves liberation which has a beginning.**

**Комментарий:**

The subjective bondage of the jīvas takes place. The condition of each one is described. The jīva is my āṁśa, like a particle in the ray of the sun. The jīva alone becomes bound up, and not me, like the sun. This happens to one type of jīva, not to all.

### Text 6

*suparṇāv etau sadṛśau sakhāyau*  
*yadṛcchayaitau kṛta-nīḍau ca vṛkṣe*  
*ekas tayoḥ khādati pippalānnam*  
*anyo niranno ’pi balena bhūyān*

**Перевод:**

**By chance, two birds of similar nature, who are friends, have made a nest in the same tree. One of them, however, is eating the fruits of the tree, whereas the other, superior in strength, does not eat the fruits.**

**Комментарий:**

The difference is shown by disparate qualities in two verses. Sometimes they are spoken of as non-different (*sadṛśau*). The śruti uses the word *sayujā,* which means “same.” The jīva has a form which is conscious like the Lord.

### Text 7

*ātmānam anyaṁ ca sa veda vidvān*  
*apippalādo na tu pippalādaḥ*  
*yo ’vidyayā yuk sa tu nitya-baddho*  
*vidyā-mayo yaḥ sa tu nitya-muktaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Paramātmā, who does not eat the fruit, knows himself and the** ***jīva.*** **The** ***jīva*** **does not know himself or Paramātmā. The** ***jīva*****, endowed with** ***avidyā,*** **is eternally bound. The Lord, who is endowed with** ***cit-śakti,*** **is eternally liberated.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is *vidyāmāyaḥ,* having vidyā which manifests from his svarūpa-śakti, not from māyā-śakti. This is explained in Akrūra’s prayers.

### Text 18

*śabda-brahmaṇi niṣṇāto na niṣṇāyāt pare yadi*  
*śramas tasya śrama-phalo hy adhenum iva rakṣataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**If one is skillful in knowing Vedic scriptures and Brahman, but not skillful in devotion to the Supreme Lord, the result of one’s effort is nullified. He is like a person who, desiring milk, maintains a cow which does not bear calves.**

**Комментарий:**

Previously jñāna-yoga was discussed. Now bhakti-yoga is discussed.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 67:**

*Pare* means the highest tattva without making distinction between Brahman and Bhagavān since everywhere the same one entity is spread.

### Text 19

*gāṁ dugdha-dohām asatīṁ ca bhāryāṁ*  
*dehaṁ parādhīnam asat-prajāṁ ca*  
*vittaṁ tv atīrthī-kṛtam aṅga vācaṁ*  
*hīnāṁ mayā rakṣati duḥkha-duḥkhī*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! That man is certainly most miserable who takes care of a barren cow, an unchaste wife, a body totally dependent on others, useless children or wealth not utilized for the right purpose. Similarly, one who studies Vedic knowledge devoid of my glories is also most miserable.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 67-68:**

The goal of study of śabda-brahma (scriptures) is being fixed in Parabrahman. Though in all parts of the Vedas the scriptures indicate Parabrahman, still steadiness in Parabrahman does not arise by millions of deliberations on the Vedas. But study of those portions which describe the pastimes of Parabrahman with the form of Bhagavān produces steadiness in the form of Bhagavān. It is said:

*saṁsāra-sindhum ati-dustaram uttitīrṣor*  
*nānyaḥ plavo bhagavataḥ puruṣottamasya*  
*līlā-kathā-rasa-niṣevaṇam antareṇa*  
*puṁso bhaved vividha-duḥkha-davārditasya*

**For a person who is suffering in the fire of countless miseries and even for person who desires to cross the insurmountable ocean of material existence, there is no suitable boat except cultivating the nectar of the narrations of the Supreme Lord's pastimes. (SB** [**12.4.40**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/4.40)**)**

*śreyaḥ-sṛtiṁ bhaktim udasya te vibho*  
*kliśyanti ye kevala-bodha-labdhaye*  
*teṣām asau kleśala eva śiṣyate*  
*nānyad yathā sthūla-tuṣāvaghātinām*

**O Lord! If fools give up bhakti, the all-inclusive path, and suffer to attain realization of ātmā without bhakti, they simply attain suffering and nothing else. They are like fools who beat empty husks. (SB** [**10.14.4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.4)**)**

One should not study Vedic scriptures devoid of the Lord’s pastimes. *Mayā hīnām vācam* means “statements devoid of my pastimes.”

### Text 20

*yasyāṁ na me pāvanam aṅga karma*  
*sthity-udbhava-prāṇa-nirodham asya*  
*līlāvatārepsita-janma vā syād*  
*vandhyāṁ giraṁ tāṁ bibhṛyān na dhīraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! The wise do not accept useless scriptures in which there are no descriptions of how I create, maintain and destroy the universe, and in which there are no descriptions of my auspicious birth as Kṛṣṇa among all the** ***līlāvatāras.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 69:**

The wise man does not accept useless Vedic words (*giram*) in which there are no description of my pastimes (*karma*) which purify (*pāvanam*) the universe—pastimes of creation, maintenance and destruction. Superior to that however are descriptions of the birth of Kṛṣṇa and Rāma among the avatāras, the shelters of prema for the universe *(īpsitam*). Nārada has said:

*idaṁ hi puṁsas tapasaḥ śrutasya vā*  
*sviṣṭasya sūktasya ca buddhi-dattayoḥ \|*  
*avicyuto ’rthaḥ kavibhir nirūpito*  
*yad-uttamaśloka-guṇānuvarṇanam \|\|*

**The unfailing cause of perfection from men’s austerity, study of the Vedas, performance of sacrifices, chanting of mantras, practicing jñāna and giving charity is defined by the wise as glorifying the qualities of the Lord. (SB** [**1.5.22**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/5.22)**)**

Lord Caitanya, the purifying avatāra for Kali-yuga says:

*śrutam apy aupaniṣadaṁ dūre hari-kathāmṛtam \|*  
*yan na santi dravac-citta-kampāśru-pulakādayam \|\|*

**Upaniṣads which, though heard, do not produce a melted heart, trembling of the body, tears and hairs standing on end are far inferior to the nectar of the Lord’s pastimes. (Padyāvalī 39)**

### Text 21

*evaṁ jijñāsayāpohya nānātva-bhramam ātmani*  
*upārameta virajaṁ mano mayy arpya sarva-ge*

**Перевод:**

**Rejecting the misconception of variety concerning** ***ātmā*** **by deliberation, fixing the mind devoid of** ***māyā*** **upon me, the Lord who pervades everywhere, a person will attain** ***sāyujya.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 70:**

Having stated that jñāna is perfected by bhakti, jñāna is summarized.

The deliberation in this verse was described in the first verse:

*baddho mukta iti vyākhyā guṇato me na vastutaḥ*  
*guṇasya māyā-mūlatvān na me mokṣo na bandhanam*

**One cannot actually say that there is bondage or liberation due to the guṇas controlled by me. Because the relationship with the guṇas arises only by avidyā or ignorance, there is no real bondage, and consequently no liberation. That is my opinion. (SB** [**11.11.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.1)**)**

*Ātmani* means “in the pure jīva.” Rejecting the illusion of variety constituted of devatās and humans in the *ātmā,* concentrating (*arpya*) the mind on me, the all-pervading *(sarva-ge*) Brahman, by hearing my pastimes, one should become peaceful.

### Text 22

*yady anīśo dhārayituṁ mano brahmaṇi niścalam*  
*mayi sarvāṇi karmāṇi nirapekṣaḥ samācara*

**Перевод:**

**If you are not able to free your mind from all material disturbances and cannot absorb it completely in Brahman, then perform all your activities as an offering to me, without enjoying the fruits.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 71:**

Having taught jñāna-miśra-bhakti, he teaches pure bhakti with jñāna as its by-product in four verses while discarding jñāna. *Yadi* means “certainly” as in SB [11.4.10](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/4.10). The verses are spoken to Uddhava while Kṛṣṇa thinks that he is desirous of jñāna (though he is not). As stated in SB [10.14.4](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/14.4), certainly *(yadi*) you cannot concentrate the mind on Brahman steadily. You should take shelter of bhakti-yoga which is served by all qualities like jñāna by its nature. Kṛṣṇa first describes a gradual method: offer all your activities to me. If, because of lack of strength of previous bhakti, one desires brahma-jñāna, but the mind cannot co

### Text 23-24

*śraddhālur mat-kathāḥ śṛṇvan su-bhadrā loka-pāvanīḥ*  
*gāyann anusmaran karma janma cābhinayan muhuḥ*  
*mad-arthe dharma-kāmārthān ācaran mad-apāśrayaḥ*  
*labhate niścalāṁ bhaktiṁ mayy uddhava sanātane*

**Перевод:**

**A person with natural faith should constantly hear topics about me, should sing and remember my topics which purify the world, and enact my exploits and birth. He should perform** ***dharma, kāma*** **and** ***artha*** **as service to me. Having taken shelter of me, he will attain permanent** ***bhakti*** **to me, whose form is permanent.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 72:**

One should personally imitate pastimes with devotees having one’s particular devotional bhāva among the pastimes of the Lord’s birth and activities and have others enact pastimes of the Lord and pastimes of other types of devotees. One should perform acts of dharma like giving cows in charity for me as part of Janmāstamī festival. One should perform acts of kāma like building huge palaces on my behalf. One can make temples. One can perform acts of artha such as collecting money, using it for the Lord’s service. In this way one should serve (*ācaran*). Having no other shelter (*mad-apāśrayaḥ*) one attains bhakti by hearing the Lord’s topics, bhakti which is fixed in three phases of time (*niścalām*), since one has no interest in impersonal liberation because of the happiness of bhakti. One should not think that because the object of worship is temporary, that the bhakti is temporary. The Lord is permanent (*sanātane*).

### Text 25

*sat-saṅga-labdhayā bhaktyā mayi māṁ sa upāsitā*  
*sa vai me darśitaṁ sadbhir añjasā vindate padam*

**Перевод:**

**One who has obtained pure devotional service by association with my devotees always worships me. Thus he easily goes to my abode, which is revealed by my devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 73:**

“How does the inclination for the path of bhakti become steady?” The cause is explained. The devotee (*saḥ*) worships me with a taste for bhakti (*bhaktyā*), attained through association. The devotee (*saḥ*) worships me with a taste for bhakti (*bhaktyā*), attained through association. Easily he attains all svarūpa-jñāna of the Lord as Brahman and Bhagavān (*padam*). *Añjasā* means “as a byproduct of bhakti.” *Padam* means *svarūpam*.

### Text 26-27

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*sādhus tavottama-śloka mataḥ kīdṛg-vidhaḥ prabho*  
*bhaktis tvayy upayujyeta kīdṛśī sadbhir ādṛtā*  
*etan me puruṣādhyakṣa lokādhyakṣa jagat-prabho*  
*praṇatāyānuraktāya prapannāya ca kathyatām*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O master, worthy of praise! What type of person do you consider to be a true devotee, and what type of devotional service, approved by great devotees, should be offered to you? Ruler of the** ***devatās*****! O Lord of Vaikuṇṭha! O Lord of the universe! Since I am surrendered to you, am attached to you and am offering respects to you, please explain this to me.**

**Комментарий:**

What type of bhakti, revered by the devotees, should offered to you intently (*upa*)?

You know very well the glory of your devotees. He asks about the real nature of bhakti and the devotees. O avatāra who manifests mahat-tattva (*puruṣādhyakṣa*)! O ruler of Vaikuṇṭha (*lokākhyakṣa)* and the unlimited universes (*jagat-prabho*)! Explain to one who is a devotee (*praṇatāya*), who is affectionate to you (*anuraktāya*), who surrenders only to you (*prapannāya*).

### Text 28

*tvaṁ brahma paramaṁ vyoma puruṣaḥ prakṛteḥ paraḥ*  
*avatīrno ’si bhagavan svecchopātta-pṛthag-vapuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**As the Supreme Brahman, you are unattached like the sky, since you are superior to material nature. Still, by the desire of your devotees, you produce many forms of devotees to spread** ***bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

**Kṛṣṇa sandarbha 90:**

You, directly Bhagavān, have appeared. The powers of Bhagavān are described. You are Brahman. You are the spiritual sky called Vaikuṇṭha. You are the puruṣa superior to prakṛti. Though you are this Bhagavān, you have appeared in this world. You appear in various forms (*prthag-vapu*) to fulfill the desires of your devotees.

Jāmbavān speaks to Kṛṣṇa:

*yasyeṣad-utkalita-roṣa-kaṭākṣa-mokṣair*  
*vartmādiśat kṣubhita-nakra-timiṅgalo ’bdhiḥ*  
*setuḥ kṛtaḥ sva-yaśa ujjvalitā ca laṅkā*  
*rakṣaḥ-śirāṁsi bhuvi petur iṣu-kṣatāni*

**You are the Lord who impelled the ocean to give way when his sidelong glances, slightly manifesting his anger, disturbed the crocodiles and** ***timiṅgila*** **fish within the watery depths. You are the Lord who built a great bridge to establish his fame, who burned down the city of Laṅkā, and whose arrows severed the heads of Rāvaṇa, which then fell to the ground. (SB** [**10.56.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/56.28)**)**

The devatās speak to Kṛṣṇa:

*ketus tri-vikrama-yutas tri-patat-patāko*  
*yas te bhayābhaya-karo ’sura-deva-camvoḥ*  
*svargāya sādhuṣu khaleṣv itarāya bhūman*  
*padaḥ punātu bhagavan bhajatām aghaṁ naḥ*

**O powerful Lord! May that foot which is like a victory flag, endowed with great power during your appearance as Vāmana, with banners falling upon the three worlds like the Gaṅgā River, bestowing fear in the demon troops to drive them to hell, and bestowing fearlessness to the devatā** **armies for taking them to Svarga, purify us of our sins! (SB** [**11.6.13**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/6.13)**)**

Akrūra speaks:

*adbhutānīha yāvanti bhūmau viyati vā jale*  
*tvayi viśvātmake tāni kiṁ me ’dṛṣṭaṁ vipaśyataḥ*

**Whatever wonderful things the earth, sky or water contain, all exist in you. Since you encompass everything, when I am seeing you, what have I not seen? (SB** [**10.41.4**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/41.4)**)**

Therefore it is said:

*govinda-bhuja-guptāyāṁ dvāravatyāṁ kurūdvaha*  
*avātsīn nārado ’bhīkṣṇaṁ kṛṣṇopāsana-lālasaḥ*

**O best of the Kurus! Eager to engage in the worship of Kṛṣṇa, Nārada Muni stayed constantly in Dvārakā, which was always protected by the arms of Govinda. (SB** [**11.2.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.1)**)**

### Text 29-32

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*kṛpālur akṛta-drohas titikṣuḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*satya-sāro ’navadyātmā samaḥ sarvopakārakaḥ*  
*kāmair ahata-dhīr dānto mṛduḥ śucir akiñcanaḥ*  
*anīho mita-bhuk śāntaḥ sthiro mac-charaṇo muniḥ*  
*apramatto gabhīrātmā dhṛtimāñ jita-ṣaḍ-guṇaḥ*  
*amānī māna-daḥ kalyo maitraḥ kāruṇikaḥ kaviḥ*  
*ājñāyaivaṁ guṇān doṣān mayādiṣṭān api svakān*  
*dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvān māṁ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O Uddhava, a saintly person is merciful and never injures others. He is tolerant toward all living entities. He is most truthful. He is free from all envy and jealousy, and his mind is equal in material happiness and distress. He dedicates his time to work for the welfare of all others. His intelligence is never bewildered by material desires and he has controlled his senses. His behavior is always pleasing and exemplary and he is free from possessiveness. He never endeavors in worldly activities and he controls his eating. He has** ***śānti-rati*** **and performs his duties until completion. A saintly person is contemplative and accepts me as his only shelter. Such a person is alert and inscrutable and not subject to changes. He has conquered over the six material qualities—namely hunger, thirst, lamentation, illusion, old age and death. He is free from all desire for prestige and offers honor to others. He is expert in understanding others and is free from cheating. He is compassionate and understands bondage and liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

A person who, understanding good and bad aspects of *dharma* as taught by me gives up all his duties and simply worships me is the best of all.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 199:**

The difference in the devotee according to the difference in sādhana is now explained in five verses of which three verses describe inferior, mixed bhakti. The main item is “taking shelter of me.” In the next verse it says *māṁ bhajeta sa ca sattamaḥ*: the person who worships me is the best. The word *ca* indicates that also what was previously stated: the person who takes shelter of me is the best.

Śrīdhara Svāmī says that *sthiraḥ* means steady in his sva-dharma. Verse 32 however states that one should give up all sva-dharmas. This establishes a different level of devotee since it includes all dharmas, not just contradictory dharmas.

The next higher or madhyama stage, unmixed direct bhakti is described. Giving up dharmas is expressed in the following:

*ye tyakta-loka-dharmārthā viṣṇu-bhakti-vaśaṁ gatāḥ \|*  
*dhyāyanti paramātmānaṁ tebhyo ’pīha namo namaḥ \|\|*

**I offer repeated respects to those who having given up goals of material dharma, and dedicating themselves to Viṣṇu, meditate on Paramātmā. (Hāyaśīrṣa-pañcarātra)**

The verse may be explained as follows also. If one lacks all the qualities mentioned above, but understands good qualities like compassion and their opposites (*doṣān*) as well, determining good and bad among those qualities, and if one gives up all dharma prescribed by varṇāśrama (*svākān dharmān)*, including jñāna, with all one’s nitya and naimittika duties, which were instructed by me, because they are obstacle to bhakti, that person who worships me is the best. The word *ca* indicates that he who lacks the qualities mentioned in the previous verses, but takes shelter of me is also the best, as if he had all the qualities mentioned. And the person who attains those qualities, but worships only me without dharma and jñāna, he is even better than the devotee just mentioned. One should also see the list of qualities of the devotee mentioned in Bhagavad-gītā, chapter twelve (BG [12.12](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/12.12)).

Thus here the lesser devotee (mixed devotee described in verses 29-31), the better (sattara who rejects mixture of karma and jñāna) and the very best (sattama who rejects karma and jñāna, but is endowed with the favorable qualities mentioned in verses 29-31) are described.

This devotee is called sattama because he behaves properly. Though a devotee may perform sinful acts he is still considered proper (sādhu has the same meaning as sattva) because he accepts only the Lord as the object of worship. This is explained in BG [9.30](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/9.30) with *api cet sudrācāraḥ. Sādhur eva sa mantavyaḥ.* However such a devotee with bad conduct is not mentioned when speaking of association of devotees (even though he is sādhu) because, by that association, bhakti does not suitably develop. Thus Prahlāda says:

*guru-śuśrūṣayā bhaktyā sarva-labdhārpaṇena ca*  
*saṅgena sādhu-bhaktānām īśvarārādhanena ca*  
*śraddhayā tat-kathāyāṁ ca kīrtanair guṇa-karmaṇām*  
*tat-pādāmburuha-dhyānāt tal-liṅgekṣārhaṇādibhiḥ*

**One performs bhakti-yoga by service to guru, offering him all that one possesses, by association with devotees of good conduct, by worship of the Lord, by faith in topics of the Lord, by chanting about the Lord’s qualities and activities, by meditation on the Lord’s lotus feet, and by seeing the deity and making offerings to the deity. (SB** [**7.7.30-31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/7.30)**)**

In this verse the word *sādhu* means proper behavior.

### Text 33

*jñātvājñātvātha ye vai māṁ yāvān yaś cāsmi yādṛśaḥ*  
*bhajanty ananya-bhāvena te me bhaktatamā matāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who may or may not know the extent of my powers, my forms and my nature, but who worship me with great possessiveness, I consider to be the best of devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 201:**

On the path of arcana there are three types of devotees according to Padma Purāṇa. The best devotee has the five saṁskāras, navejyā karma and artha-pañcaka.

The madhyama is described as follows:

*tāpaṁ puṇḍraṁ tathā nāma mantro yāgaś ca pañcamaḥ \|*  
*amī pañcaiva saṁskārāḥ paramaikānti-hetavaḥ \|\| ity atra \|*

**The five saṁskāras of branding, tilaka, accepting a name, mantra and worship are the causes of being the best devotee.**

The kaniṣṭha is described as follows:

*śaṅkha-cakrādy-ūrdhva-puṇḍra-dhāraṇādy-ātma-lakṣaṇam \|*  
*tan-namaskaraṇaṁ caiva vaiṣṇavatvam ihocyate \|\|*

**One should offer respects to a person with branding of conch and cakra and tilaka as a Vaiṣṇava.**

The ananya-bhakti with pure dāsya, sakhya etc. is the best among all.

Those who worship me, the soul of all beings (*yaḥ*), a form of eternity, knowledge and bliss, (*yādṛśaḥ)* at all times and places *(yāvāt*), knowing or not knowing me, with ananya-bhāva--one bhāva among dāsya, sakhya, vātsalya and mādhurya, for the son of Nanda-- they are considered the best devotees by me.

(In contrast) the great yogīs say:

*preyān na te ’nyo ’sty amutas tvayi prabho*  
*viśvātmanīkṣen na pṛthag ya ātmanaḥ*  
*athāpi bhaktyeśa tayopadhāvatām*  
*ananya-vṛttyānugṛhāṇa vatsala*

**O Lord! There is no one dearer to you than those who see the jīvas as non-different from you, the soul of the universe. However, O Lord, most affectionate to the devotees, please bestow pure bhakti on those serving you! (SB** [**4.7.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/7.38)**)**

In the verse under discussion (SB [11.11.33](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.33)) however, knowing or not knowing the Lord should not be thought to be good or bad. This devotee is the best (*bhaktatamaḥ)*. This devotee is superior to the devotee called *sattama* (SB [11.11.32](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.29)). Use of the word *bhakta* indicates that the svarūpa of bhakti is greater in this devotee. The Lord says, “This is my opinion. I agree with this.” He has not said this previously about any other devotee. Though the rest of the section referred to the devotee in the singular, in this verse the devotees are mentioned in the plural out of respect. How much more respect will be given to devotees with prema in a particular bhāva!

### Text 34

*mal-liṅga-mad-bhakta-jana- darśana-sparśanārcanam*  
*paricaryā stutiḥ prahva- guṇa-karmānukīrtanam*

**Перевод:**

**One should see, touch, worship serve, praise, offer respects and glorify the qualities and activities of my deity form and my devotees*****.***

**Комментарий:**

*Mal-liṅga-mad-bhakta-jana* should properly be in the genitive case. *Prahva* means offering respects.

### Text 35

*mat-kathā-śravaṇe śraddhā mad-anudhyānam uddhava*  
*sarva-lābhopaharaṇaṁ dāsyenātma-nivedanam*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! One should have faith in hearing topics about me. One should meditate upon me. One should offer valued objects. One should offer oneself, since one is a servant.**

**Комментарий:**

Offering oneself as a servant is predominant in the dāsya of vaidhi-bhakti.

### Text 39

*sammārjanopalepābhyāṁ seka-maṇḍala-vartanaiḥ*  
*gṛha-śuśrūṣaṇaṁ mahyaṁ dāsa-vad yad amāyayā*

**Перевод:**

**One should sincerely serve my temple by cleaning, anointing, sprinkling with flowers, and drawing designs on the floor, just as a servant takes care of the house of a king.**

**Комментарий:**

He acts like an ordinary servant. This means the actions must be done, devoid of deceit for taking money or unsteady heart (*amāyay*ā).

### Text 40

*amānitvam adambhitvaṁ kṛtasyāparikīrtanam*  
*api dīpāvalokaṁ me nopayuñjyān niveditam*

**Перевод:**

**One should not have pride or make a false show of** ***bhakti*****. One should not glorify one’s accomplishments. One should not accept articles such as a lamp offered to the Lord until they are first offered to other devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

One should not announce one’s devotion falsely in front of others (*adambhitvam*), and act for one’s own benefit (*nopayuñyāt*). One should not accept ordinary articles offered to oneself by others. They should be offered to the Lord. If one is worshipping in a temple in a huge forest, it may not be possible to offer to others first. The objects should be fixed, since it is suitable to offer to the Lord something with permanence. One should accept the lamp with devotion. If the object is not ordinary and not fixed, according to one’s ability and one’s desire one offers the article.

### Text 41

*yad yad iṣṭatamaṁ loke yac cāti-priyam ātmanaḥ*  
*tat tan nivedayen mahyaṁ tad ānantyāya kalpate*

**Перевод:**

**If one offers to me what is considered best according to the scriptures and to the people, and also according to one’s own preference and the Lord’s, one becomes qualified for eternal life.**

**Комментарий:**

One should offer what is best according to one’s preference and the Lords (*ca*).

### Text 42

*sūryo ’gnir brāhmaṇā gāvo vaiṣṇavaḥ khaṁ maruj jalam*  
*bhūr ātmā sarva-bhūtāni bhadra pūjā-padāni me*

**Перевод:**

**O saintly Uddhava! Please know that you may worship me in the sun, fire,** ***brāhmaṇas*****, cows, Vaiṣṇavas, sky, wind, water, earth, the individual soul and all living entities.**

**Комментарий:**

The objects listed are not normally grouped together, but seen as abodes of the Lord they are mentioned. Suitable objects of worship are listed.

### Text 43

*sūrye tu vidyayā trayyā haviṣāgnau yajeta mām*  
*ātithyena tu viprāgrye goṣv aṅga yavasādinā*

**Перевод:**

**One worships me in the sun by reciting verses and performing specific Vedic rituals. One worships me in the fire by offering oblations. One worships me in the** ***brāhmana*** **by giving him proper reception. One worships me in the cow by feeding her grass and tickling her.**

**Комментарий:**

One should recite Vedic verses to satisfying the antaryāmī of the sun. One should receive the best brāhmaṇa, as one received guests daily who come to the house.

The best brāhmaṇa should be knowledgeable of devotional scriptures and endowed with the twelve qualities to a greater extent than others. (see SB [7.9.10](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/9.10)) One should show general respect to other brāhmaṇas. If he is a Vaiṣṇava but not a brāhmaṇa, one should worship him equal to the best brāhmaṇa, not as one gives preferential treatment to the best among brāhmaṇas.

### Text 44

*vaiṣṇave bandhu-sat-kṛtyā hṛdi khe dhyāna-niṣṭhayā*  
*vāyau mukhya-dhiyā toye dravyais toya-puraḥsaraiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One worships the Lord in the devotees by respecting them as great friends. One worships the Lord in ether by meditating on the space of the heart. One worships the Lord in air by thinking that air is the chief** ***prāṇa.*** **One worships the Lord in water by offering items starting with water.**

**Комментарий:**

Air is the cause of all life, which derives is power from antaryāmī. One thinks of the antaryāmī of air. Tejas was not mentioned since that was included in sun and fire.

### Text 45

*sthaṇḍile mantra-hṛdayair bhogair ātmānam ātmani*  
*kṣetra-jñaṁ sarva-bhūteṣu samatvena yajeta mām*

**Перевод:**

**One worships me in earth by uttering secret** ***mantras*** **on purified ground. One worships me in the** ***ātmā*** **by offering food to the** ***ātmā*** **in the body. One worships me in all beings the** ***antaryāmī*** **of all beings, seeing their happiness and distress as one’s own.**

**Комментарий:**

Thinking that I reside in the ātmā, one should offer food. One should worship me as antaryāmī in all beings, seeing their happiness and distress as one’s own.

### Text 46

*dhiṣṇyeṣv ity eṣu mad-rūpaṁ śaṅkha-cakra-gadāmbujaiḥ*  
*yuktaṁ catur-bhujaṁ śāntaṁ dhyāyann arcet samāhitaḥ*

**Перевод:**
