The benediction was realized by Brahmā:

*na bhāratī me ’ṅga mṛṣopalakṣyate*  
*na vai kvacin me manaso mṛṣā gatiḥ \|*  
*na me hṛṣīkāṇi patanty asat-pathe*  
*yan me hṛdautkaṇṭhyavatā dhṛto hariḥ \|\|*

**O Nārada! My words are never false. The working of the mind is never false. My senses do not fall onto the wrong path. This is because I hold the Lord in my heart, filled great zeal. (SB** [**2.6.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/6.34)**)**

The Kumāras had also spoken to the Lord:

*yo ’ntarhito hṛdi gato ’pi durātmanāṁ tvaṁ*  
*so ’dyaiva no nayana-mūlam ananta rāddhaḥ*  
*yarhy eva karṇa-vivareṇa guhāṁ gato naḥ*  
*pitrānuvarṇita-rahā bhavad-udbhavena*

**O lord of unlimited qualities! He who resides in the heart, invisible to the sinful, today has been seen by our eyes. When your secret nature was described previously by Brahmā who was born from you, it entered our intelligence through the ears. (SB** [**3.15.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.46)**)**

Still Brahmā could not understand ātmā, because he was preoccupied with activity. This was the will of the Lord to reveal the Lord’s greatness, just as the powers of Vāyu and others were stopped in the story told in the teachings of Haṁsa.

### Text 19

*sa mām acintayad devaḥ praśna-pāra-titīrṣayā*  
*tasyāhaṁ haṁsa-rūpeṇa sakāśam agamaṁ tadā*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Brahmā, desiring the answer to the question, fixed his mind on me. At that time, in my form of Haṁsa, I became visible to Lord Brahmā.**

**Комментарий:**

I appeared in the form of Haṁsa.

### Text 20

*dṛṣṭvā mām ta upavrajya kṛtva pādābhivandanam*  
*brahmāṇam agrataḥ kṛtvā papracchuḥ ko bhavān iti*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing me, the sages, placing Brahmā in the lead, came forward and worshiped my lotus feet. Then they asked, “Who are you?”**

**Комментарий:**

Since they offered respects, their question was not out of ignorance of the Lord but to know his intention.

### Text 21

*ity ahaṁ munibhiḥ pṛṣṭas tattva-jijñāsubhis tadā*  
*yad avocam ahaṁ tebhyas tad uddhava nibodha me*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! The sages, desiring to know the goal of** ***yoga,*** **thus inquired from me who I was. Now please hear as I, master of those with false identity, explain what I spoke to them.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus they are described as inquisitive to know the truth (*tattva-jijñāsubhiḥ*).

### Text 22

*vastuno yady anānātva ātmanaḥ praśna īdṛśaḥ*  
*kathaṁ ghaṭeta vo viprā vaktur vā me ka āśrayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇas*****! If, when asking me who I am, you believe that I am also a** ***jīva,*** **this question cannot occur if there is no plurality of** ***ātmās*** **in the absolute. I would have to answer in terms of plurality of qualities and type.**

**Комментарий:**

Did you ask who I was, understanding that I was a jīva? Or that I was a material body? Or that I was the supreme Lord? He criticizes the question if they think he is a jīva. If the absolute *(vastunaḥ*) has no variety of jīvas (*ātmanaḥ*), it is improper to make distinctions of particular ātmās. How could you ask such a question? *Yadi* (if) indicates certainty. Therefore, O brāhmaṇas, who is the shelter of me, the speaker, and you, the questioners? Who or what is the source which produces your particular identities as brāhmaṇas etc.? I should answer that.

**Paramātmā Sandarbha 34:**

In this statement Haṁsa, taking the role of a regular jīva, takes the jīvas as being only one entity, imitating a common jñānī guru from a material viewpoint. Though the jīvas are all separate aṁśas, not considering this, he speaks in terms of one jīva, suitable for jñāna, for the Kumāras who desire jñāna.

### Text 23

*pañcātmakeṣu bhūteṣu samāneṣu ca vastutaḥ*  
*ko bhavān iti vaḥ praśno vācārambho hy anarthakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**If you ask “Who are you?” in terms of the five elements, which are actually one entity spread everywhere, your question should still not be asked. My use of words, following your example, is also meaningless.**

**Комментарий:**

He responds if they think he is a material body. Thinking in terms of the absolute (*vastutaḥ*), asking “Who are you?” is meaningless, since you think in terms of the five material elements making up the bodies. If you consider that there is oneness of all bodies, just as all ātmās are one by being similar in nature, the question is still meaningless. “But even the wise speak in this way. You also spoke of us brāhmaṇas and yourself.” This speaking was done to remove these misconceptions (*vācārambhaḥ*). If you like me know the truth, then why ask the truth?

### Text 24

*manasā vacasā dṛṣṭyā gṛhyate ’nyair apīndriyaiḥ*  
*aham eva na matto ’nyad iti budhyadhvam añjasā*

**Перевод:**

**Within this world, whatever is perceived by the mind, speech, eyes or other senses is me alone and nothing besides me. All of you please understand this as it is.**

**Комментарий:**

If you think I am the Lord, this is the response. Whatever is perceived by the mind and senses is only me, since there is nothing other than me, the supreme cause. Without me, nothing exists. Thus the question cannot be asked.

### Text 25

*guṇeṣv āviśate ceto guṇāś cetasi ca prajāḥ*  
*jīvasya deha ubhayaṁ guṇāś ceto mad-ātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My dear sons! The mind has a natural proclivity to enter into the sense objects, and similarly the sense objects enter into the mind; but both this material mind and the sense objects are merely designations that cover the spirit soul, related to Paramātmā.**

**Комментарий:**

“If that is so, the senses and mind mutual adhere and remain in that state, joined together. What happens to the jīva? Our question has produced more questions.” The Lord shows the unnatural relationship between the jīva and the senses and mind.

The senses and mind are real. But their relationship with the jīva is due only the covering of the body. This has a relationship with the jīva which is my aṁśa (*mad-ātmanaḥ*). Thus it is not related to me. Just as there is no natural relationship of the clouds arising from the effects of the sun, with the sun’s rays, aṁśas of the sun, and the sun remains far away, so the jīvas, my aṁśas, are related to me.

### Text 26

*guṇeṣu cāviśac cittam abhīkṣṇaṁ guṇa-sevayā*  
*guṇāś ca citta-prabhavā mad-rūpa ubhayaṁ tyajet*

**Перевод:**

**The consciousness remains forever in the sense objects by continual service to them. The sense objects remain strongly in the consciousness. One who absorbs himself in me can give up both.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva cannot reject the sense objects by his own power. He does this by a portion of the Lord’s śakti obtained by worship, considering himself non-different from the Lord. Absorbed in thoughts of non-difference from me (mad-*rūpaḥ*), which is described in the methods of jñāna, one can give up the sense objects. It can also mean worship through bhakti, in which he meditates on my form.

### Text 27

*jāgrat svapnaḥ suṣuptaṁ ca guṇato buddhi-vṛttayaḥ*  
*tāsāṁ vilakṣaṇo jīvaḥ sākṣitvena viniścitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Waking, sleeping and deep sleep, the three functions of the intelligence, are caused by the three** ***guṇas*****. The** ***jīva is*** **ascertained to be different from these three states since it is only the witness of them.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva does not have an inherent relationship with the guṇas etc.

### Text 28

*yarhi saṁsṛti-bandho ’yam ātmano guṇa-vṛtti-daḥ*  
*mayi turye sthito jahyāt tyāgas tad guṇa-cetasām*

**Перевод:**

**When bondage in** ***saṁsāra*** **or identity with the body occurs, it produces the influence of the sense objects. When one gives up** ***saṁsāra*** **by concentration on me, one gives up sense objects and material consciousness completely.**

**Комментарий:**

The inability of the jīva to disengage on his own is confirmed. The Lord is identified with the fourth state (*mayi turye*).

*virāṭ hiraṇyagarbhaś ca kāraṇaṁ cety upādhayaḥ*  
*īśasya yat tribhir hīnaṁ turīyaṁ tat padaṁ viduḥ*

**When the Lord is devoid of the three forms of Kṣīrodakaśāyī, Garbhodakaśāyī and Karaṇodakaśāyī, he is known as the fourth form (Vāsudeva). Śrīdhara Svāmī commentary on (SB** [**11.15.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/15.16)**)**

The answer to the main question is given. The unconscious sense objects and the jīva mutually reject each other by concentration on the Lord.

### Text 29

*ahaṅkāra-kṛtaṁ bandham ātmano ’rtha-viparyayam*  
*vidvān nirvidya saṁsāra- cintāṁ turye sthitas tyajet*

**Перевод:**

**The false identity with the body produces bondage and obstacles to the** ***ātmā.*** **Therefore, an intelligent person, by being situated in the Lord, gives up fear of** ***saṁsāra.***

**Комментарий:**

Again he teaches rejection of the body etc. One who is situated in the Lord (*turye*) gives up thoughts of saṁsāra.

### Text 33

*evaṁ vimṛśya guṇato manasas try-avasthā*  
*man-māyayā mayi kṛtā iti niścitārthāḥ*  
*sañchidya hārdam anumāna-sad-ukti-tīkṣṇa*  
*jñānāsinā bhajata mākhila-saṁśayādhim*

**Перевод:**

**Considering that the three states of the intelligence arising from the** ***guṇas*** **are created by my** ***avidyā*** **within me, being firmly convinced of your nature as** ***ātmā,*** **and cutting the three states by the sharp sword of knowledge, by inference and by scriptural statements, worship me, the destroyer of all doubts.**

**Комментарий:**

It was previously stated:

*tasmāt tvam uddhavotsṛjya codanāṁ praticodanām*  
*pravṛttiṁ ca nivṛttiṁ ca śrotavyaṁ śrutam eva ca*  
*mām ekam eva śaraṇam ātmānaṁ sarva-dehinām*  
*yāhi sarvātma-bhāvena mayā syā hy akuto-bhayaḥ*

**O Uddhava! Give up duties and forbidden acts, the path of enjoyment and the path of renunciation, desire to hear more about** ***dharma,*** **and what you have already heard about** ***dharma.*** **Surrender to me alone, the soul of all beings, using all methods, with** ***bhāva*** **in your mind. You will be fearless by my mercy. (SB** [**11.12.14-15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.14)**)**

*evaṁ gurūpāsanayaika-bhaktyā*  
*vidyā-kuṭhāreṇa śitena dhīraḥ*  
*vivṛścya jīvāśayam apramattaḥ*  
*sampadya cātmānam atha tyajāstram*

**Thus, by worshipping** ***guru,*** **by the axe of** ***jñāna*** **made sharp by** ***bhakti*** **even in a secondary role, by being steady, destroy the subtle body, and with attention attain Paramātmā. Then give up the weapon of** ***jñāna.*** **(SB** [**11.12.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.24)**)**

This is confirmed by the statement of Haṁsa. Contemplating in this way, you should meditate on me.

### Text 34

*īkṣeta vibhramam idaṁ manaso vilāsaṁ*  
*dṛṣṭaṁ vinaṣṭam ati-lolam alāta-cakram*  
*vijñānam ekam urudheva vibhāti māyā*  
*svapnas tridhā guṇa-visarga-kṛto vikalpaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One should understand that this world is a misconception, a diversion for the mind, visible but temporary, and flickering like a fire brand. Brahman is one consciousness but manifest as many. The three states arising by the** ***guṇas*** **create various transformations, but these are temporary like a dream.**

**Комментарий:**

The method of producing jñāna is described. One should see the form of the world in which there is a mistake with particulars (*vibhramam*), since it is seen as a composition of mental conceptions. It should be seen as temporary like a pot. It should be seen as continually moving about like a fire brand, since everything is actually the consciousness of Paramātmā. It appears as many by māyā. Māyā is not independent but derives strength from proximity to Paramātmā. Thus the three states produced by transformations of the guṇas are manufactured like a dream. They are manifestations produced by māyā.

### Text 36

*dehaṁ ca naśvaram avasthitam utthitaṁ vā*  
*siddho na paśyati yato ’dhyagamat svarūpam*  
*daivād apetam atha daiva-vaśād upetaṁ*  
*vāso yathā parikṛtaṁ madirā-madāndhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The perfected sage is not aware whether he is seated or standing while situated in the temporary body, since he has realized Brahman. He is like a drunken man who is not aware of receiving, putting on or taking off his clothing.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīvanmukta is described in two verses.

### Text 38

*mayaitad uktaṁ vo viprā guhyaṁ yat sāṅkhya-yogayoḥ*  
*jānīta māgataṁ yajña yuṣmad-dharma-vivakṣayā*

**Перевод:**

**O** ***brāhmaṇas*****! Know that I am Viṣṇu who has come with a desire to teach you** ***dharma.*** **I have spoken the confidential knowledge of Sāṅkhya and** ***aṣṭāṅga-yoga.***

**Комментарий:**

He gives respect to his own words. He reveals himself with a particular purpose. He identifies himself as Viṣṇu (*yajñaḥ*), not identifying Śiva or others as the Supreme Lord. He has taught dharma.

### Text 39

*ahaṁ yogasya sāṅkhyasya satyasyartasya tejasaḥ*  
*parāyaṇaṁ dvija-śreṣṭhāḥ śriyaḥ kīrter damasya ca*

**Перевод:**

**O best of the** ***brāhmaṇas*****! Please know that I am the supreme shelter of the** ***yoga*** **system, analytic philosophy, seeing equally, speaking pleasantly, influence, beauty, fame and self-control.**

**Комментарий:**

He shows that knowing him, everything becomes known and that he is the most excellent.

### Text 40

*māṁ bhajanti guṇāḥ sarve nirguṇaṁ nirapekṣakam*  
*suhṛdaṁ priyam ātmānaṁ sāmyāsaṅgādayo ’guṇāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**All superior qualities, which are eternal, such as equality to all beings and attachment to my devotee, reside in me. I am beyond the material** ***guṇas,*** **not dependent on material qualities, and am the devotees’ friend, giving love to them.**

**Комментарий:**

He teaches knowledge of himself. Those who are not affected by the guṇas (*aguṇāḥ*), who have unlimited qualities such as equality and attachment to devotees (*sāmyāsaṅgādayaḥ*), described in the earth’s statements in the First Canto, worship me since I am the shelter of all beings (*ātmānam*) and thus most excellent. How could they worship anyone else? Because he has this nature, he is beyond the guṇas, not bound to the guṇas like the jīvas by ignorance. By his will, he is not bound up *(nirapekṣakam*). Since they are also beyond the guṇas, they do not worship anyone except me. Being of this nature, I am give benefit to everyone, without motivation (*suhṛdam*) and the abode of unconditional prema for all beings. It is thus proper that such qualities reside in me. The goal is prema bhakti for me, the most excellent being.

### Text 41

*iti me chinna-sandehā munayaḥ sanakādayaḥ*  
*sabhājayitvā parayā bhaktyāgṛṇata saṁstavaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Thus all of the doubts of the sages headed by Sanaka were destroyed by my words. Worshiping me with transcendental devotion, they chanted my glories with excellent hymns.**

**Комментарий:**

They gave up their doubts and attained bhakti to me. They worshipped me with prema bhakti (*parayā bhaktyā*).

## Chapter Fourteen

### Text 1

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*vadanti kṛṣṇa śreyāṁsi bahūni brahma-vādinaḥ*  
*teṣāṁ vikalpa-prādhānyam utāho eka-mukhyatā*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Kṛṣṇa! The learned sages recommend various excellent** ***sādhanas*** **for perfecting one’s life. Among them are some superior? Is one among them the best?**

**Комментарий:**

Previously, bhakti was said to be the practice, without considering other processes. (SB [11.12.14](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.14)) Uddhava inquires in order to confirm this, rejecting other opinions.

### Text 2

*bhavatodāhṛtaḥ svāmin bhakti-yogo ’napekṣitaḥ*  
*nirasya sarvataḥ saṅgaṁ yena tvayy āviśen manaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! You have explained the process of unalloyed** ***bhakti*****, by which a devotee removes all material association from his life and is able to fix his mind on you.**

**Комментарий:**

He rejects dependence on other results (*nirasya*).

### Text 3

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*kālena naṣṭā pralaye vāṇīyaṁ veda-saṁjñitā*  
*mayādau brahmaṇe proktā dharmo yasyāṁ mad-ātmakaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: By the influence of time, the Vedic knowledge was lost at the time of annihilation. Therefore, when the subsequent creation took place, I spoke to Brahmā the Vedic knowledge in which** ***bhakti*** **is the essence.**

**Комментарий:**

The Vedas were lost by time, at the pralaya, at the end of the kalpa. The variety of the Vedas was lost. The Vedas (*iyam*) explained what you have just explained to me. Results other than absorption in me, such as jñāna or bhakti mixed with jñāna are not what I explained. At the beginning of Brahmā’s first day (*ādau*) the message, in which there is dharma arising from my svarūpa (*mad-ātmakaḥ*)—since it is the essence of hlādinī--was spoken by me to Brahmā.

### Text 5-7

*tebhyaḥ pitṛbhyas tat-putrā deva-dānava-guhyakāḥ*  
*manuṣyāḥ siddha-gandharvāḥ sa-vidyādhara-cāraṇāḥ*  
*kindevāḥ kinnarā nāgā rakṣaḥ-kimpuruṣādayaḥ*  
*bahvyas teṣāṁ prakṛtayo rajaḥ-sattva-tamo-bhuvaḥ*  
*yābhir bhūtāni bhidyante bhūtānāṁ patayas tathā*  
*yathā-prakṛti sarveṣāṁ citrā vācaḥ sravanti hi*

**Перевод:**

**From the forefathers headed by Bhṛgu and other sons of Brahmā appeared many children and descendants, who assumed different forms as** ***devatās*****, demons, human beings, Guhyakas, Siddhas, Gandharvas, Vidyādharas, Cāraṇas, Kindevas, Kinnaras, Nāgas, Kimpuruṣas, and so on. All of the many species, along with their respective leaders, appeared with different desires generated from the three modes of material nature. Therefore, because of the different characteristics of the living entities within the universe, there are many explanations of the meaning of the Vedas.**

**Комментарий:**

From the sons of Brahmā (*pitṛbhyah*) and others (*tebhyaḥ*) many descendants appeared.

### Text 10

*dharmam eke yaśaś cānye kāmaṁ satyaṁ damaṁ śamam*  
*anye vadanti svārthaṁ vā aiśvaryaṁ tyāga-bhojanam*  
*kecid yajñaṁ tapo dānaṁ vratāni niyamān yamān*

**Перевод:**

**Various people claim various paths:** ***karma-mīmāṁsa*****, fame, sense enjoyment, truthfulness, control of the senses, control of the mind, wealth for one’s own use, renunciation, eating, sacrifice, penance, charity, vows,** ***niyamas*** **or** ***yamas.***

**Комментарий:**

Since they did not accept the Lord, persons like Jaimini propagated other ideas. Some taught sva-dharma composed of nitya and naimittika rules. Advaita-yogīs, giving up karma, teach truth, sama and dama. Some, dedicated to karma, teach gain for the self (*svārtham*), with a method and result, but all for the self. In that, some teach enjoyment, wealth, and renunciation (for their gain). The rest of the list are methods.

### Text 11

*ādy-anta-vanta evaiṣāṁ lokāḥ karma-vinirmitāḥ*  
*duḥkhodarkās tamo-niṣṭhāḥ kṣudrā mandāḥ śucārpitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The results of all the processes mentioned are meager, weak, have a beginning and end, are produced by** ***karma,*** **are full of lamentation and end in misery and bewilderment.**

**Комментарий:**

Among them those that act by Vedic rules of action and produce happiness as a result *(lokāḥ*), have a beginning and end. Those which are against the Vedas produce hell.

### Text 12

*mayy arpitātmanaḥ sabhya nirapekṣasya sarvataḥ*  
*mayātmanā sukhaṁ yat tat kutaḥ syād viṣayātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**O learned Uddhava! Those who fix their consciousness on me, giving up all material desires, attain happiness because of me. This cannot possibly be experienced by those engaged in sense gratification.**

**Комментарий:**

They attain happiness by me, the Paramātmā, the dearest form, having eternity, knowledge and bliss (*ātmanā*), by me (*mayā*), manifesting my form with most astonishing form and qualities. How can this happen for those who desire goals other than me (*viṣayātmanām*)?

### Text 13

*akiñcanasya dāntasya śāntasya sama-cetasaḥ*  
*mayā santuṣṭa-manasaḥ sarvāḥ sukha-mayā diśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who does not desire anything within this world, who has controlled his senses, who has fixed his intelligence on me, who regards heaven and hell equally, and whose mind is completely satisfied in me finds only happiness wherever he goes.**

**Комментарий:**

He shows the greatness of happiness from experiencing the Lord in two verses.

### Text 14

*na pārameṣṭhyaṁ na mahendra-dhiṣṇyaṁ*  
*na sārvabhaumaṁ na rasādhipatyam*  
*na yoga-siddhīr apunar-bhavaṁ vā*  
*mayy arpitātmecchati mad vinānyat*

**Перевод:**

**One who has fixed his consciousness on me desires neither the position or abode of Brahmā or Indra, nor an empire on the earth, nor sovereignty in the lower planetary systems, nor the eightfold perfection of** ***yoga*****, nor liberation. Such a person desires me alone.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 132:**

Bhakti is the cause of good qualities like jñāna and vairāgya.

*yasyāsti bhaktir bhagavaty akiñcanā*  
*sarvair guṇais tatra samāsate surāḥ*  
*harāv abhaktasya kuto mahad-guṇā*  
*manorathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ*

**The devatās constantly dwell with all good qualities in that person who has pure bhakti for the Lord. There are no good qualities in the non-devotee who chases after temporary material objects with desire for material pleasure. (SB** [**5.18.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/18.12)**)**

*yat karmabhir yat tapasā jñāna-vairāgyataś ca yat*  
*yogena dāna-dharmeṇa śreyobhir itarair api*  
*sarvaṁ mad-bhakti-yogena mad-bhakto labhate ’ñjasā*  
*svargāpavargaṁ mad-dhāma kathañcid yadi vāñchati*

**Everything that can be achieved by karma, penance, jñāna, vairāgya, mystic yoga, charity, dharma and all other auspicious means of perfecting life is easily achieved by my devotee through bhakti. If somehow or other my devotee desires Svarga, liberation, or residence in my abode, he easily achieves such benedictions. (SB** [**11.20.32-33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.32)**)**

Since bhakti gives the highest happiness by its nature, it makes all practice and results of karma, jñāna and other sādhanas detestable.

*Rasādhipatyam* means ruling the lower planets. *Apunar bhavam* is personal liberation. The devotee who has offered his ātmā to me does not desire any object produced in these places. He desires me, the result of sādhana-bhakti, greater than all puruṣārthas.

### Text 15

*na tathā me priyatama ātma-yonir na śaṅkaraḥ*  
*na ca saṅkarṣaṇo na śrīr naivātmā ca yathā bhavān*

**Перевод:**

**Neither Brahmā, Śiva, Saṅkarṣaṇa, the goddess of fortune nor even my deity are as dear to me as you are.**

**Комментарий:**

Using Uddhava as an example, he shows the excellence of bhakti. Brahmā was a son. Śiva was an assistant, giving happiness (*sam*). Saṅkarṣaṇa was a brother, who was taken from the womb. Śrī was his wife, a shelter of great beauty. Ātma refers to the deity form. They are not pleasing because they are my son, assistant, brother or wife. They are pleasing because of their bhakti. They are not as dear as you, since you have greater bhakti. This shows his great affection for the devotee.

### Text 16

*nirapekṣaṁ muniṁ śāntaṁ nirvairaṁ sama-darśanam*  
*anuvrajāmy ahaṁ nityaṁ pūyeyety aṅghri-reṇubhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I always follow the footsteps of my pure devotees, who are free from all personal desire, are rapt in thought of my pastimes, are fixed in me, without any feelings of enmity, and are equal to all conditions of the world. Let me be purified by the dust from their feet!**

**Комментарий:**

Having praised such devotees, objects of his special prema, he praises special devotees, objects of his special respect. *Nirapekṣam* means a devotee with no desires except for the Lord. This devotee is without agitation (*śāntam).* Thus he has no enemies anywhere. He is devoid of thinking of objects as good or bad (*sama-darśanam*). I follow a devotee such as Nārada, because by such good conduct filled with pure bhakti, I develop excellent bhakti for him. How can it be hidden? I should be purified by the dust from their feet. I will be purified from the fault of not responding fully to his bhakti.

### Text 18

*bādhyamāno ’pi mad-bhakto viṣayair ajitendriyaḥ*  
*prāyaḥ pragalbhayā bhaktyā viṣayair nābhibhūyate*

**Перевод:**

**If my devotee has not fully conquered his senses, he may be harassed by material desires, but because of his generally strong** ***bhakti*****, he will not be defeated by sense gratification.**

**Комментарий:**

He is generally not defeated by enjoyment since the Lord is touched by his humble requests to the Lord to interfere. The Lord intervenes because of the great ability of bhakti, composed of full surrender.

### Text 19

*yathāgniḥ su-samṛddhārciḥ karoty edhāṁsi bhasmasāt*  
*tathā mad-viṣayā bhaktir uddhavaināṁsi kṛtsnaśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Just as a blazing fire turns firewood into ashes, similarly, devotion to me completely burns to ashes sins committed by my devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

He praises all the types of devotees in eight verses. Bhakti with hearing burns all sins to ashes completely.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 125:**

It is also said:

*yathāgniḥ su-samṛddhārciḥ karoty edhāṁsi bhasmasāt \|*  
*pāpāni bhagavad-bhaktis tathā dahati tat-kṣaṇāt \|\|*  
*Just as fire turns wood to ashes, bhakti to the Lord burns up sins immediately. (Padma Purāṇa 5.85.31)*  
*patitaḥ skhalito bhagnaḥ sandaṣṭas tapta āhataḥ*  
*harir ity avaśenāha pumān nārhati yātanāḥ*

**A person who chants the name of the Lord while falling from a cliff, stumbling on the road, receiving injury his body, being bitten by snakes, tormented with fever or being attacked with sticks will not suffer hellish punishment. (SB** [**6.2.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/2.15)**)**

Though the potential mood or imperative mood is not used in the verb, the statement can still be regarded as an injunction similar to the statement *pūṣāpraviṣṭa-bhāgo yad āgneyāṣṭā-kapālo bhavati*: the portion of Pūṣā should be divided into eight portions of Agni. This statement is also not expressed as an injunction but acts as such.

Direct injunctions are also given:

*tasmād bhārata sarvātmā bhagavān īśvaro hariḥ \|*  
*śrotavyaḥ kīrtitavyaś ca smartavyaś cecchatābhayam \|\|*

**The Lord who is Paramātmā, Bhagavān and Lord of power, should be heard about, glorified and remembered by those desiring freedom from fear. (SB** [**2.1.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/1.5)**)**

The word *tasmāt* indicates a reason and thus indicates the fault of not hearing and glorifying the Lord. Just as fire burns by its nature, bhakti also manifests effect without dependence on rules.

*yān āsthāya naro rājan na pramādyeta karhicit*  
*dhāvan nimīlya vā netre na skhalen na pated iha*

**O King! One who accepts this process of devotional service will never be affected by pride. Even while running with eyes closed, he will never trip or fall. (SB** [**11.2.35**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.35)**)**

The word *su-samṛddhārciḥ* (blazing fire) in the Padma Purāṇa quotation refutes the notions that bhakti is dependent on other sādhana, that it is hard to perfect, and that its results are slow. Thus Padma Purāṇa says the effect is immediate (*tat-kṣaṇāt*).

### Text 20

*na sādhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava*  
*na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! I am attained by unmixed** ***bhakti.*** **I cannot be attained by mystic** ***yoga*****, Sāṅkhya philosophy,** ***dharma,*** **Vedic study, jñāna or sannyāsa.**

**Комментарий:**

Yoga etc. do not control me (*na* *sādhayati*) or draw my attention as much as strong sādhana-bhakti, unmixed with jñāna or karma.

### Text 21

*bhaktyāham ekayā grāhyaḥ śraddhayātmā priyaḥ satām*  
*bhaktiḥ punāti man-niṣṭhā śva-pākān api sambhavāt*

**Перевод:**

**Only by** ***bhakti*** **with full faith can the devotees obtain me, Paramātmā, the object of love.** ***Bhakti*** **fixed in me purifies even a dog eater of his low birth.**

**Комментарий:**

I am attained, and gradually controlled by pure bhakti, preceded by faith. That bhakti is fixed firmly in me (*man-niṣṭhā*).

### Text 22

*dharmaḥ satya-dayopeto vidyā vā tapasānvitā*  
*mad-bhaktyāpetam ātmānaṁ na samyak prapunāti hi*

**Перевод:**

**Neither** ***dharma*** **endowed with honesty and mercy nor knowledge obtained with great penance can completely purify one’s consciousness if they are bereft of loving service to me.**

**Комментарий:**

Dharma without desire and brahma-jñāna according to scriptures (*vidyā*) endowed with concentration of mind (*tapasā*) cannot purify the mind devoid of bhakti—desire to please me (*mad-bhaktyā*)-- of desires for material enjoyment and liberation.

### Text 23

*kathaṁ vinā roma-harṣaṁ dravatā cetasā vinā*  
*vinānandāśru-kalayā śudhyed bhaktyā vināśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Without** ***bhakti*** **how can the heart melt? Without melting of the heart, how can one’s hairs stand on end and tears come to the eyes? Without hair standing on end and tears in the eyes how can the consciousness become purified?**

**Комментарий:**

This is a statement in the negative. Hairs standing on end is a sign of melting of the heart. Tears of bliss are special signs of the melted heart. Melting of the heart is the interior or essential nature of bhakti. Without those signs, how can one understand the heart has melted? Without the melted heart, how could bhakti appear? Without bhakti how could the heart be purified?

### Text 24

*vāg gadgadā dravate yasya cittaṁ*  
*rudaty abhīkṣṇaṁ hasati kvacic ca*  
*vilajja udgāyati nṛtyate ca*  
*mad-bhakti-yukto bhuvanaṁ punāti*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee whose speech is choked up, whose heart is melted, who cries continually and sometimes laughs, sometimes feels ashamed, sometimes sings loudly and sometimes dances—a devotee thus fixed in loving service to me purifies the entire universe.**

**Комментарий:**

A person endowed with bhakti, if he is the object of my prema, purifies the world, what to speak of his own heart, his associates, his village, his state, his country, continent and the whole Jambudvīpa.

### Text 25

*yathāgninā hema malaṁ jahāti*  
*dhmātaṁ punaḥ svaṁ bhajate ca rūpam*  
*ātmā ca karmānuśayaṁ vidhūya*  
*mad-bhakti-yogena bhajaty atho mām*

**Перевод:**

**Just as gold, when smelted in fire, gives up its impurities and attains its pure state, similarly the** ***ātmā*** **becomes purified of all contamination caused by impressions of** ***karma*** **by** ***bhakti-yoga*** **and then serves me directly.**

**Комментарий:**

By his prīti he eventually attains great prema. An example is given. By bhakti or affection for me, he purifies the impressions of karma and then worships me fully. He attains full service because of the manifestation of great prema.

### Text 26

*yathā yathātmā parimṛjyate ’sau*  
*mat-puṇya-gāthā-śravaṇābhidhānaiḥ*  
*tathā tathā paśyati vastu sūkṣmaṁ*  
*cakṣur yathaivāñjana-samprayuktam*

**Перевод:**

**To the degree that the** ***ātmā*** **becomes purified by hearing and chanting my glories, a person is able to perceive my real form and qualities and experience their sweetness, just as the eye when smeared with special ointment is able to see finer objects.**

**Комментарий:**

The progress starting with the first worship is described. With the appearance of great prema, he becomes indifferent to matter. As he becomes purified, he sees my form, qualities and activities as they are (*vastu sūkṣmam*). By seeing me continually, great prema arises.

### Text 27

*viṣayān dhyāyataś citta viṣayeṣu viṣajjate*  
*mām anusmarataś citta mayy eva pravilīyate*

**Перевод:**

**The mind of a person meditating upon the sense objects becomes attached to those objects. The mind of a person who constantly remembers me becomes absorbed in me.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus one should practice bhakti. Two verses summarize.

### Text 29

*strīṇāṁ strī-saṅgināṁ saṅgaṁ tyaktvā dūrata ātmavān*  
*kṣeme vivikta āsīnaś cintayen mām atandritaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Being determined, one should completely give up association with women and people intimately associated with women. Sitting in a solitary, secure place, one should concentrate the mind on me with great attention.**

**Комментарий:**

The preliminary actions are described.

### Text 30

*na tathāsya bhavet kleśo bandhaś cānya-prasaṅgataḥ*  
*yoṣit-saṅgād yathā puṁso yathā tat-saṅgi-saṅgataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Of all kinds of suffering and bondage arising from various attachments, none is greater than the suffering and bondage arising from attachment to women and intimate contact with people attached to women.**

**Комментарий:**

Suffering and bondage both arise from association with women etc. The cause is ultimately association with people who associate with women.

### Text 31

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*yathā tvām aravindākṣa yādṛśaṁ vā yad-ātmakam*  
*dhyāyen mumukṣur etan me dhyānaṁ tvaṁ vaktum arhasi*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: My dear lotus-eyed Kṛṣṇa, by what process should one who desires liberation meditate upon you? What are the details of the object of meditation and what is the object of meditation? Kindly explain to me this topic of meditation.**

**Комментарий:**

Uddhava inquires about the method for persons desiring liberation. This is a question meant for others. By knowledge of the hierarchy of all paths, there will be most excellent knowledge of bhakti. This applies elsewhere also.

### Text 32-33

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*sama āsana āsīnaḥ sama-kāyo yathā-sukham*  
*hastāv utsaṅga ādhāya sva-nāsāgra-kṛtekṣaṇaḥ*  
*prāṇasya śodhayen mārgaṁ pūra-kumbhaka-recakaiḥ*  
*viparyayeṇāpi śanair abhyasen nirjitendriyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Sitting on a level seat that is not too high or too low, keeping the body straight and erect yet comfortable, placing the two hands on one’s lap and focusing the eyes on the tip of one’s nose, one should purify the pathways of** ***prāṇa*** **by practicing the exercises of** ***pūraka, kumbhaka*** **and** ***recaka.*** **Having fully controlled the senses, then one should gradually alter the practice to** ***recaka, pūraka, kumbhaka***

**Комментарий:**

One should sit comfortably choosing one āsana among the many, such as svastikāsana. The process of breathing should be repeated.

### Text 34

*hṛdy avicchinam oṁkāraṁ ghaṇṭā-nādaṁ bisorṇa-vat*  
*prāṇenodīrya tatrātha punaḥ saṁveśayet svaram*

**Перевод:**

**Beginning from the** ***mūlādhāra-cakra,*** **one should move the sound** ***oṁ,*** **fine as the fiber in a lotus stem, which has the sound of an uninterrupted bell, upward to the heart by means of the** ***prāṇa*** **and make its steady there.**

**Комментарий:**

One should move the sound to the anāhata-cakra.

### Text 44

*tatra labdha-padaṁ cittam ākṛṣya vyomni dhārayet*  
*tac ca tyaktvā mad-āroho na kiñcid api cintayet*

**Перевод:**

**Being established in meditation on the Lord’s face, one should then withdraw the consciousness and fix it in space. Then, giving up the mind, one should become established in me as Brahman and not think of anything.**

**Комментарий:**

This description is for the desirer of liberation. Or after attaining Brahman, with the desire to destroy the last traces of māyā, one destroys that and the Lord manifests, as in *brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā*. (BG [18.54](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.54))

### Text 45

*evaṁ samāhita-matir mām evātmānam ātmani*  
*vicaṣṭe mayi sarvātman jyotir jyotiṣi saṁyutam*

**Перевод:**

**One who is completely fixed in** ***samādhi*** **should see Brahman in the** ***jīva*** **and the** ***jīva*** **joined with Brahman, just a particle of light joins with the light.**

**Комментарий:**

The ray of light joins with the mass of light or constantly takes shelter of the mass of light.

## Chapter Fifteen

### Text 3

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*siddhayo ’ṣṭādaśa proktā dhāraṇā yoga-pāra-gaiḥ*  
*tāsām aṣṭau mat-pradhānā daśaiva guṇa-hetavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: The masters of the** ***yoga*** **system have declared that there are eighteen types of** ***siddhi*** **and meditation, of which eight are primary, having their shelter in me, and ten are secondary, appearing from the material mode of goodness.**

**Комментарий:**

The eight siddhis are manifested from portions of the svarūpa-śakti, like the Lord’s associates. Thus they are called *mat-pradhānā* (my major features). When manifested in others, they are ābhāsas only. The ten appear in material people as portions of those powers, as their effects, worshipable in relation to me.

### Text 4-5

*aṇimā mahimā mūrter laghimā prāptir indriyaiḥ*  
*prākāmyaṁ śruta-dṛṣṭeṣu śakti-preraṇam īśitā*  
*guṇeṣv asaṅgo vaśitā yat-kāmas tad avasyati*  
*etā me siddhayaḥ saumya aṣṭāv autpattikā matāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Among the eight primary** ***siddhis*****, the three by which one transforms one’s body are** ***aṇimā*****, becoming smaller than the smallest; ma*****himā,*** **becoming greater than the greatest; and** ***laghimā,*** **becoming lighter than the lightest. Through the perfection of** ***prāpti*** **one acquires whatever one desires by one’s senses, and through** ***prākāmya-siddhi*** **one experiences any enjoyable object, seen or unseen. Through** ***iśitā-siddhi*** **one can spread ones powers to other** ***jīvas,*** **and through the controlling potency called** ***vaśitā-siddhi*** **one becomes unattached to material enjoyment. One who has acquired** ***kāmāvasāyitā-siddhi*** **can obtain anything from anywhere, to the highest possible limit. O gentle Uddhava! These eight mystic perfections of mine are considered to be natural and unexcelled.**

**Комментарий:**

By anīmā one becomes small, able to enter stone. By mahimā one becomes large, pervading everywhere. By laghimā one becomes light, able to go to the sun planet on the ray of the sun. By prapti one can touch the moon with the tip of one’s finger. Prākāmya means to have one’s desires unobstructed. One can sink or rise from the earth as if it were water. By iśitā one can control other beings or matter, lessening their powers. Vaśitā refers to vairagya, detachment what is seen or heard, with stages like yatamāna and saṁjñā. Kāmavaśitā means to manifest what one desires. These definitions are found in Sāṅkhya-kaumudī of Vācaspati Miśra. However the definitions of the Lord differ. There are material and spiritual siddhis.

### Text 6-7

*anūrmimattvaṁ dehe ’smin dūra-śravaṇa-darśanam*  
*mano-javaḥ kāma-rūpaṁ para-kāya-praveśanam*  
*svacchanda-mṛtyur devānāṁ saha-krīḍānudarśanam*  
*yathā-saṅkalpa-saṁsiddhir ājñāpratihatā gatiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The ten** ***siddhis*** **arising from the modes of nature are the powers of freeing oneself from hunger and thirst and other bodily disturbances, hearing and seeing things far away, moving the body at the speed of the mind, assuming any form one desires, entering the bodies of others, dying when one desires, witnessing the pastimes between the** ***devatās*** **and Apsarās, attaining what one desires, and having one’s commands or goals unimpeded.**

**Комментарий:**

Three and a half verses describe the siddhis.

### Text 10

*bhūta-sūkṣmātmani mayi tan-mātraṁ dhārayen manaḥ*  
*aṇimānam avāpnoti tan-mātropāsako mama*

**Перевод:**

**The worshipper of** ***tan-mātras*** **should concentrate the mind, the form of the** ***tan-mātras,*** **on me who represent the** ***tan-mātras.*** **He will attain me in the form of the smallest particle, by which he will also attain a small nature.**

**Комментарий:**

*Bhūta-sūkṣma* refers to the tanmātras of Vaikuṇṭha, arising from the svarūpa-śakti, just like the spiritual siddhis described in verse 3. The word *mama* indicates that the aṇimā attained by the yogī is only a portion of the Lord’s *aṇimā.* *jagad-vypapara-varjam*: the perfected jīva attains powers, but not the power to create a universe. (Brahma-sūtra [4.4.17](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/4.4.17)) The jīva cannot attain the same powers as the Lord.

### Text 11

*mahat-tattvātmani mayi yathā-saṁsthaṁ mano dadhat*  
*mahimānam avāpnoti bhūtānāṁ ca pṛthak pṛthak*

**Перевод:**

**One who absorbs his mind in me, representing** ***mahat-tattva,*** **attains my form as the largest object by which he is able to pervade everywhere. By concentration on each element one can attain those elements.**

**Комментарий:**

The yogī attains a form decorated with the śakti of mahimā (*mahimānam*). By meditating on mahat-tattva for mahimā, he gains the qualities of other elements secondarily.

### Text 12

*paramāṇu-maye citta bhūtānāṁ mayi rañjayan*  
*kāla-sūkṣmārthatāṁ yogī laghimānam avāpnuyāt*

**Перевод:**

**By attaching his mind to me as the atoms of matter, the** ***yogī*** **may achieve the perfection called** ***laghimā,*** **a form which is as small as the smallest portion of time.**

**Комментарий:**

The yogī concentrates on me, as my upādhi, the smallest portion of matter devoid of weight (*paramāṇu*) and attains weightlessness (*laghimā).* The smallest portion of ether is like the smallest division of time called paramāṇu.

### Text 13

*dhārayan mayy ahaṁ-tattve mano vaikārike ’khilam*  
*sarvendriyāṇām ātmatvaṁ prāptiṁ prāpnoti man-manāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Fixing his mind with concentration on me represented by** ***ahaṅkāra*** **in** ***sattva,*** **the** ***yogī*** **obtains the power of** ***prāpti,*** **by which he becomes the proprietor of the senses of all living entities, because he is absorbed in me.**

**Комментарий:**

*Vaikārike* means śuddha-sattva.

### Text 14

*mahaty ātmani yaḥ sūtre dhārayen mayi mānasam*  
*prākāmyaṁ pārameṣṭhyaṁ me vindate ’vyakta-janmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who concentrates all mental activities on me represented by** ***sūtra*** **of the** ***mahat-tattva*** **obtains** ***prākāmya*****, the excellence known as** ***sūtra*** **belonging to me, who manifests in the universe.**

**Комментарий:**

He attains the excellence belonging to me, who manifests beyond the senses or who manifests within the universe.

### Text 17

*nirguṇe brahmaṇi mayi dhārayan viśadaṁ manaḥ*  
*paramānandam āpnoti yatra kāmo ’vasīyate*

**Перевод:**

**One who fixes his pure mind on me as the impersonal Brahman obtains the greatest happiness, wherein all his desires are completely fulfilled.**

**Комментарий:**

Merging in Brahman is considered a siddhi.

### Text 18

*śvetadvīpa-patau citta śuddhe dharma-maye mayi*  
*dhārayañ chvetatāṁ yāti ṣaḍ-ūrmi-rahito naraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A human being who concentrates on me as** ***dharma,*** **purity and the Lord of Śvetadvīpa, obtains a pure existence in which he is freed from the six waves of material disturbance, namely hunger, thirst, decay, death, grief and illusion.**

**Комментарий:**

The siddhis arising from the guṇas are described. The Lord is worshipped through his upādhi of material dharma. The person meditates on the Lord beyond the guṇas (*śuddhe*), who has the upādhi of dharma, since he is the presiding deity of sattvika-dharma. He attains sattva devoid of rajas and tamas (*śuddhatām*).

### Text 19

*mayy ākāśātmani prāṇe manasā ghoṣam udvahan*  
*tatropalabdhā bhūtānāṁ haṁso vācaḥ śṛṇoty asau*

**Перевод:**

**The purified living entity who fixes his mind on sound occurring within me who represent the ether and the total life air is then able to perceive within the sky the speaking of all living entities.**

**Комментарий:**

He hears various voices as if from all beings.

### Text 21

*mano mayi su-saṁyojya dehaṁ tad-anuvāyunā*  
*mad-dhāraṇānubhāvena tatrātmā yatra vai manaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the** ***yogī*** **merges his mind in me and his body in the air which accompanies the mind, which is within me, by the power of that mediation on me, his body goes wherever his mind goes.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord is the presiding deity of the mind (*mayi*).

### Text 23

*para-kāyaṁ viśan siddhas ātmānaṁ tatra bhāvayet*  
*piṇḍaṁ hitvā viśet prāṇo vāyu-bhūtaḥ ṣaḍaṅghri-vat*

**Перевод:**

**When a perfect** ***yogī*** **desires to enter another’s body, he should meditate upon himself here I am situated, and then, giving up his own gross body, he should enter the other’s body by identifying with his subtle body and using the external air, just as a bee leaves one flower and flies into another.**

**Комментарий:**

The yogī meditates on the upādhi of prāṇa etc. where the Lord is situated (*ātmānam*).

### Text 25

*vihariṣyan surākrīḍe mat-sthaṁ sattvaṁ vibhāvayet*  
*vimānenopatiṣṭhanti sattva-vṛttīḥ sura-striyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***yogī*** **who desires to enjoy in the pleasure gardens of the** ***devatās*** **should meditate on** ***sattva*** **in the heart, where I reside, and then the heavenly women, generated from** ***sattva*****, will approach him in airplanes.**

**Комментарий:**

He should meditate on sattva which takes shelter of me (*mat-stham*). He meditates on my upādhi called sattva which causes appearance of devatās and their wives (*sattva-vṛttiḥ*).

### Text 26

*yathā saṅkalpayed buddhyā yadā vā mat-paraḥ pumān*  
*mayi satye mano yuñjaṁs tathā tat samupāśnute*

**Перевод:**

**A** ***yogī*** **who has faith in me, absorbing his mind in me, the fulfiller of all desires, will achieve his desired object by that very means.**

**Комментарий:**

Another version has *yathā vā* instead of *yadā vā.*

### Text 27

*yo vai mad-bhāvam āpanna īśitur vaśituḥ pumān*  
*kutaścin na vihanyeta tasya cājñā yathā mama*

**Перевод:**

**A person who attains power to control or rule from me, the supreme ruler and controller, never has his orders disobeyed by any means.**

**Комментарий:**

I am the controller of saṁsāra and independent of it (*īśituḥ vaśituḥ*). He attains upādhis of those qualities.

### Text 28

*mad-bhaktyā śuddha-sattvasya yogino dhāraṇā-vidaḥ*  
*tasya trai-kālikī buddhir janma-mṛtyūpabṛṁhitā*

**Перевод:**

**A** ***yogī*** **who has purified his existence by devotion to me and who knows the process of meditation for such powers obtains knowledge of events of past, present and future, which increase concerns for birth and death.**

**Комментарий:**

Now very minor powers are described. He meditates on the Lord who knows time and the creation, maintenance and destruction of the universe. But he does not become free of birth and death.

### Text 30

*mad-vibhūtīr abhidhyāyan śrīvatsāstra-vibhūṣitāḥ*  
*dhvajātapatra-vyajanaiḥ sa bhaved aparājitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***yogī*** **becomes unconquerable and becomes endowed with imperial paraphernalia such as flags, ornamental umbrellas and fans by meditating on my incarnations, which are decorated with Śrīvatsa and various weapons.**

**Комментарий:**

Among the vibhūtis, he meditates on one form of the Lord whose upādhi is conquest of the world.

### Text 32

*jitendriyasya dāntasya jita-śvāsātmano muneḥ*  
*mad-dhāraṇāṁ dhārayataḥ kā sā siddhiḥ su-durlabhā*

**Перевод:**

**For a sage who has conquered his senses, his mind, his breathing and his nature, and who is always absorbed in meditation on me, what mystic perfection could possibly be difficult to achieve?**

**Комментарий:**

The sage meditates on me, who am actually free of the upādhis. Since all siddhis are by nature in the Lord, all siddhis will manifest just by the Lord’s desire from that form.

### Text 33

*antarāyān vadanty etā yuñjato yogam uttamam*  
*mayā sampadyamānasya kāla-kṣapaṇa-hetavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Learned experts state that the** ***siddhis*** **of** ***yoga*** **are actually impediments. For one who is practicing the supreme** ***yoga*****, who is absorbed in attaining me, the** ***siddhis*** **are a waste of time.**

**Комментарий:**

The person who desires the wealth of my form (*māya sampadyamānasya*) considers the siddhis a waste of time. Thus merging in Brahman is also worthless, since it is a siddhi. *Uttamam yogam* means bhakti yoga without material desires.

### Text 34

*janmauṣadhi-tapo-mantrair yāvatīr iha siddhayaḥ*  
*yogenāpnoti tāḥ sarvā nānyair yoga-gatiṁ vrajet*

**Перевод:**

**Whatever** ***siddhis*** **can be achieved by good birth, herbs, austerities and** ***mantras*** **can all be achieved by** ***yoga*** **as described above. One cannot achieve liberation by any other means.**

**Комментарий:**

The meditation is superior to other methods. One can choose one of the meditations (*yogena*) to achieve the siddhis available by other means.

### Text 35

*sarvāsām api siddhīnāṁ hetuḥ patir ahaṁ prabhuḥ*  
*ahaṁ yogasya sāṅkhyasya dharmasya brahma-vādinām*

**Перевод:**

**I am the cause, the maintainer and the master of all** ***siddhis*** **of the** ***yoga*** **system, of sāṅkhya, jñāna-sādhana,** **and of the searchers for Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

If one has full faith in me, why desire siddhis? One should not meditate on me as the upādhis. Since I am the shelter of everything, one should meditate on me.

This is expressed in two verses. *Dharmasya* means “of jñāna-sādhana.”

## Chapter Sixteen

### Text 1

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*tvaṁ brahma paramaṁ sākṣād anādy-antam apāvṛtam*  
*sarveṣām api bhāvānāṁ trāṇa-sthity-apyayodbhavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: You are without beginning or end, superior to Brahman. You are Svayam Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa, unlimited by anything. You are the protection from danger, the giver of maintenance, the destroyer and the creator.**

**Комментарий:**

The following is Śrīdhara Svāmī’s analysis of the Eleventh Canto starting from Chapter 7.

In nine chapters starting with the seventh, eight questions are answered. Chapters 7 to 10 deal with Brahman. Chapter 11 deals with bondage and liberation, as well as devotees and bhakti (separate topic). Chapter 12 deals with karma. Chapter 13 deals with the steadiness in yoga through the story of Haṁsa. Chapter 14 deals with meditation, as well the best sādhana (separate topic). Chapter 15 deals with different types of dhāraṇā and siddhis. Chapter 16 deals with the Lord’s answer to Uddhava’s questions and vibhūtis. Chapters 17 and 18 deal with varṇāśrama vratas. Chapter 19 deals with jñāna as well as yamas (separate topic). Chapter 20 deals with qualifications for three types of yoga. Chapter 21 deals with puruṣa and prakṛti in sattva-sāṅkhya philosophy. Chapter 22 deals with types of birth and death. Chapters 23-26 deal with tolerating other’s offenses. Chapter 27 deals with kriya-yoga (deity worship). Chapter 28 deals with jñāna and yoga. Chapter 29 deals with saṁsāra. Chapter 30 deals with bhakti.

The eight questions are answered by the end of chapter 15. The other chapters deal with twelve more topics. Thus there are twenty topics.

It was already stated that the Lord is the source of all powers:

*sarvāsām api siddhīnāṁ hetuḥ patir ahaṁ prabhuḥ*  
*ahaṁ yogasya sāṅkhyasya dharmasya brahma-vādinām*

I am the cause, the maintainer and the master of all *siddhis* of the *yoga* system, of *jñāna,* of *niṣkāma-karma,* and of the searchers for Brahman. (SB [11.5.35](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/5.35))

Hearing that, Uddhava describes the Lord as the shelter of everything in order to know through his question about Lord as the source of all material and spiritual powers. You are Brahman, the greatest, since you are the cause of everything. And you are supreme (*paramam*) since you have the form of Bhagavān. Moreover you are directly supreme (*sākṣāt*) since you are Svayam Bhagavān. Thus you are the shelter of unlimited spiritual powers. You are without beginning and end (*anādy-antam*). You are not limited in power like ordinary persons. You are not covered by māyā like the jīvas (*apāvṛtam*). You are the shelter of material powers. You are the puruṣa who gives rise to creation, maintenance and destruction of mahat-tattva etc. (*sarveśām bhāvānām*). You are the aṁśī of the puruṣas:

*yasyāṁśāṁśāṁśa-bhāgena viśvotpatti-layodayāḥ*  
*bhavanti kila viśvātmaṁs taṁ tvādyāhaṁ gatiṁ gatā*

**O source of the universe! The creation, maintenance and destruction of the universe are all carried out by a fraction of an expansion of an expansion of your expansion. Today I have come to take shelter of you, the Supreme Lord. (SB** [**10.85.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/85.31)**)**

### Text 2

*uccāvaceṣu bhūteṣu durjñeyam akṛtātmabhiḥ*  
*upāsate tvāṁ bhagavan yāthā-tathyena brāhmaṇāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Although it is difficult for the impious to understand that you are situated in all superior and inferior creations, the knowers of the Vedas worship you in those forms.**

**Комментарий:**

He asks about the material vibhūtis. The knowers of the Vedas worship you, the form of the guṇas in equilibrium within the effects of pradhāna (*bhūteṣu*).

### Text 3

*yeṣu yeṣu ca bhūteṣu bhaktyā tvāṁ paramarṣayaḥ*  
*upāsīnāḥ prapadyante saṁsiddhiṁ tad vadasva me*

**Перевод:**

**Please tell me the various forms in which the greatest sages worship you with devotion and attain perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

He asks about the spiritual vibhūtis. They worship you, form of Vāsudeva etc. in spiritual forms such as those of Bhagavān, the great being.

*tasya vā etasya mahato bhūtasya niḥśvasitam yad ṛg-vedo yajur-vedaḥ*

**The Ṛg and Yajur Vedas arose as the breathing of the great being. (Bṛhad-āraṇyaka Upaniṣad** [**2.4.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bau/2.4.10)**)**

### Text 4

*gūḍhaś carasi bhūtātmā bhūtānāṁ bhūta-bhāvana*  
*na tvāṁ paśyanti bhūtāni paśyantaṁ mohitāni te*

**Перевод:**

**O maintainer of all beings! Although you are the** ***antaryāmī*** **of the living entities and the benefactor of all beings, you move about hidden from them. Thus being bewildered by you, the living entities cannot see you, although you are seeing them.**

**Комментарий:**

You move about, sometimes in a direct form and sometimes as a śaktyāveśa form.

### Text 5

*yāḥ kāś ca bhūmau divi vai rasāyāṁ*  
*vibhūtayo dikṣu mahā-vibhūte*  
*tā mahyam ākhyāhy anubhāvitās te*  
*namāmi te tīrtha-padāṅghri-padmam*

**Перевод:**

**O supremely potent Lord! Please explain to me your innumerable** ***vibhūtis*** **which you manifest on the earth, in heaven, in hell and in all directions. I offer my humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which are the shelter of all holy places.**

**Комментарий:**

Earth, heaven and hell refer to the lower, middle and upper planets and material and spiritual worlds as well. Tell me about the vibhūtis which attain their greatness (*anubhāvitāḥ*) by you alone, Svayam Bhagavān (*te*). I offer respects to you, like an ocean for rivers, whose lotus feet are the shelter of all guru-paramparas (*tīrtha*).

### Text 6

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*evam etad ahaṁ pṛṣṭaḥ praśnaṁ praśna-vidāṁ vara*  
*yuyutsunā vinaśane sapatnair arjunena vai*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O best of those who know what to inquire! On the Battlefield of Kurukṣetra, Arjuna, desiring to fight with his rivals, asked me the same question.**

**Комментарий:**

He praises the question by referring with astonishment to the discussion between two friends.

### Text 9

*aham ātmoddhavāmīṣāṁ bhūtānāṁ suhṛd īśvaraḥ*  
*ahaṁ sarvāṇi bhūtāni teṣāṁ sthity-udbhavāpyayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! I am the Supersoul of all living entities, and therefore I am naturally their well-wisher and supreme controller. I am the creator, maintainer and annihilator of all entities.**

**Комментарий:**

I am the spiritual vibhūti among the material vibhūtis. I am the ātmā, Paramātmā. I am the benefactor by nature (*suhṛt)*. I am the stimulator of everything (*īśvaraḥ*). He speaks of the material vibhūtis. I am all material things. Among material things, I am certain objects because they are realized as my special vibhūtis by convention. He mentions the spiritual vibhūtis. I am the puruṣa who creates mahat-tattva etc. I am the cause of everything. Other statements should be understood similarly.

### Text 10

*ahaṁ gatir gatimatāṁ kālaḥ kalayatām aham*  
*gunāṇāṁ cāpy ahaṁ sāmyaṁ guṇiny autpattiko guṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I am the result of all those seeking results, and I am time among controllers. I am** ***prakṛti*** **for the** ***guṇas*****, and I am the natural quality in an object having qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

*Gatiḥ* means result or surrender (*śaranāgatiḥ*).

### Text 11

*guṇinām apy ahaṁ sūtraṁ mahatāṁ ca mahān aham*  
*sūkṣmāṇām apy ahaṁ jīvo durjayānām ahaṁ manaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Among material things I am** ***sūtra.*** **In all** ***antaḥkaraṇas,*** **possessing** ***mahat-tattva*****, I am** ***citta.*** **Among subtle things I am the** ***jīva,*** **and of things that are difficult to conquer, I am the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

**Paramātmā Sandarbha 33:**

This means that the jīva is the limit of smallness. It is not subtle in the sense of being difficult to know. *Mahatāṁ ca mahān ahaṁ sūkṣmāṇaṁ apy aham jīvaḥ:* I am the greatest of the great and the smallest of the small. (SB [11.16.11](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/16.11)) To interpret *sūkṣma* to mean “difficult to know” in this verse would go against the sense of the paired statements. (The Lord speaks this verse.) Mahat-tattva is called *mahat* because it is the cause of all elements in the world. It spreads everywhere. The word *mahat* does not mean that it is easy to understand in comparison to earth or other elements. When the jīva is called *sūkṣma* it thus means “very small.” Śrutis also say *eṣo ‘ṇūr ātmā cetasā veditavyo yasmin prāṇaḥ pañcadhā saṁviveṣa:* the jīva is atomic, understood by consciousness; in it the prāṇa of five types rests. (Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad [3.1.9](https://vedarama.com/en/books/mundaka/3.1.9))

*bālāgra-śata-bhāgasya śatadhā kaliptasya ca*  
*bhāgo jīvaḥ savijñehaḥ*

**The jīva should be understood to be the size of a hair tip divided ten thousand times. (Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad** [**5.9**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sveta-bs/5.9)**)**

*ārāgra-mātro hy aparo ‘pi dṛṣṭaḥ*

**The jīva is the size of the tip of a spoke. (Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad** [**5.8**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sveta-bs/5.8)**)**

### Text 24

*yogānām ātma-saṁrodho mantro ’smi vijigīṣatām*  
*ānvīkṣikī kauśalānāṁ vikalpaḥ khyāti-vādinām*

**Перевод:**

**Among the eight** ***aṅgas*** **of** ***yoga,*** **I am the final stage,** ***samādhi*****. Among those desiring victory, I am counsel advocating war, and among processes of expert discrimination, I am the distinction of** ***ātmā*** **from non-ātmā. Among all speculative philosophers, I am diversity of views.**

**Комментарий:**

The various khyātis (theories of error) are explained.

*ātma-khyātir asat-khyātir akhyātiḥ khyatir anyathā \|*  
*tathānirvacana-khyātir ity etat khyāti-pañcakam \|*  
*vijñāna-śūnya-mīmāṁsā-tarkādvaita-vidāṁ matam \|\|*

**There are five philosophiespropounding** ***ātmā-khyāti, asat-khyāti, akhyāti, anyathā-khyāti,*** **and** ***nirvacana-khyāti*** **(theories of error) found in followers of** ***vijñana, śūnyavada*****, Mīmāṁsa, logic and** ***advaita-vāda.***

External objects appear only by the functions of vijñāna (mind is real—ātmā-khyāti), like objects in a dream. This is the theory of vijñāna-vādīs (Yogācāra Buddhists). In perceiving an object, there is only a projection of consciousness. Though one has awareness of the object, such as silver, it is like a dream, and finally there is no awareness of distinctions before the distinction of silver and shell. (The error is in the subject or consciousness.)

Since everything is void, everything manifests by ignorance. That is the theory of śūnya-vādīs (Mādhyamika Buddhists). They think that neither shell or silver exists. It is all a manifestation of non-existing things (*asat-khyāti*). Just as void manifests as shell, it appears as silver. Where the silver is not functional at all (by conventional illusion), it is considered to be a false object (though actually everything is false).

The mīmāṁsakas claim that there is perception of real shell and real silver. Both are not false. They consider that seeing silver in shell to be non-apprehension or akhyāti. The error arises by the combination of two pieces of knowledge. There is no proper discrimination of perceptions and memories. Shell is accepted as silver and silver is accepted as the present perception by a fault of the mind.

Tārkikas (nyāya) say that substances arise by combination of two or more atoms. They consider perceiving shell as silver to be something other than what is seen--anyathā-khyāti. The combination of the two perceptions to produce something other than what they appear is called (*anyathā*). Shell does not have the qualities of real silver. Projecting the qualities of real silver on the shell is called anyathā-khyāti. This contrary knowledge is produced by a function of the mind according to the ancient tārkikas.

All duality (false) is indescribable. This is the claim of the advaita-vādīs. They say that seeing the shell as silver is an indescribable error. Since the silver is both real and false the resulting knowledge which is neither true nor false is called anirvacanīya-khyāti. When the illusion of silver is destroyed by knowledge, silver becomes unreal. When there is perception of the silver, its nonexistence is negated. Thus knowledge of something neither real nor unreal is called anirvacanīya-khyāti.

Here is the Lord’s opinion. These opinions arise by my śakti. Thus they do not mutually destroy each other even now. He shows that there is an inconceivable aspect to the śakti he described. This is called sarvatrācintya-khyāti since it is the Lord himself.

### Text 27

*saṁvatsaro ’smy animiṣām ṛtūnāṁ madhu-mādhavau*  
*māsānāṁ mārgaśīrṣo ’haṁ nakṣatrāṇāṁ tathābhijit*

**Перевод:**

**Among measures of time, I am the year, and among seasons, I am spring. Among months, I am Mārgaśīrṣa, and among constellations, I am the auspicious Abhijit.**

**Комментарий:**

*Abhijit* is the quarter of a constellation shared by Uttarāṣaḍha and Śravana.

### Text 28

*ahaṁ yugānāṁ ca kṛtaṁ dhīrāṇāṁ devalo ’sitaḥ*  
*dvaipāyano ’smi vyāsānāṁ kavīnāṁ kāvya ātmavān*

**Перевод:**

**Among y*****ugas,*** **I am Satya-yuga, and among steady sages, I am Devala and Asita. Among those who have divided the Vedas, I am Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana Vedavyāsa, and among learned scholars, I am the wisest, the knower of** ***ātmā*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Among those who know scriptures from daṇḍa and nīti to ātma-jñāna (*kavīnām*), I am wisest (*kāvyaḥ*) because I know how to discern the ātmā by myself.

### Text 29

*vāsudevo bhagavatāṁ tvaṁ tu bhāgavateṣv aham*  
*kimpuruṣānāṁ hanuman vidyādhrāṇāṁ sudarśanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Among those entitled to the name Bhagavān, I am Vāsudeva, and indeed, you, Uddhava, represent me among the devotees. I am Hanumān among the Kimpuruṣas, and among the Vidyādharas, I am Sudarśana.**

**Комментарий:**

Among the forms of the Lord endowed with six powers, I am Vāsudeva, the first of the catur-vyūha. By this he shows his supreme position.

### Text 30

*ratnānāṁ padma-rāgo ’smi padma-kośaḥ su-peśasām*  
*kuśo ’smi darbha-jātīnāṁ gavyam ājyaṁ haviḥṣv aham*

**Перевод:**

**Among jewels, I am the ruby, and among beautiful things, I am the lotus calyx. Among all types of grass, I am the sacred** ***kuśa,*** **and among oblations, I am ghee and other ingredients obtained from the cow.**

**Комментарий:**

I am the ruby among gems. This refers to the Kaustubha. Krama-dīpikā shows the method of worshipping it. Also one can see the Nārāyaṇa-vyūha-stava.

### Text 32

*ojaḥ saho balavatāṁ karmāhaṁ viddhi sātvatām*  
*sātvatāṁ nava-mūrtīnām ādi-mūrtir ahaṁ parā*

**Перевод:**

**Know that of the powerful, I am strength of the senses and mind, and I am the devotional activities of my devotees. My devotees worship me in nine different forms, among which I am the principal form Vāsudeva.**

**Комментарий:**

Among the nine forms that they worship—Vāsudeva, Saṅkarṣaṇa, Pradyumna, Aniruddha, Nārāyaṇa, Hayagrīva, Varāha, Nṛsiṁha, and Brahmā--I am the principal form Vāsudeva. Just as Viṣṇu sometimes becomes Indra as in Svāyambhuva-manvantara, when Yajña became Indra, so in some *mahā-kalpas*, Viṣṇu becomes Brahmā.

### Text 33

*viśvāvasuḥ pūrvacittir gandharvāpsarasām aham*  
*bhūdharāṇām ahaṁ sthairyaṁ gandha-mātram ahaṁ bhuvaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Among the Gandharvas, I am Viśvāvasu, and I am Pūrvacitti among the heavenly Apsarās. I am the steadiness of mountains and the** ***tan-mātra*** **known as fragrance in the earth.**

**Комментарий:**

I am fragrance, but only the praiseworthy portion (*gandha-mātram*). Gītā says *punya- gandhaḥ pṛthivyām*: I am the pure fragrance of earth (BG [7.9](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/7.9)).The same applies to the next verse with “I am the taste of water.

### Text 36

*gaty-ukty-utsargopādānam ānanda-sparśa-lakṣanam*  
*āsvāda-śruty-avaghrāṇam ahaṁ sarvendriyendriyam*

**Перевод:**

**I am the functions of the ten senses: walking, speaking, evacuation, accepting and pleasure, touching, seeing, tasting, and hearing. I am also the potency by which each of the senses experiences its particular sense object.**

**Комментарий:**

I am the cause of power in all the senses (*sarvendriyendriyam*) as in the śruti statement *cakṣusaś cakṣur*: I am the eye of the eye. (Kena Upaniṣad).

### Text 37

*pṛthivī vāyur ākāśa āpo jyotir ahaṁ mahān*  
*vikāraḥ puruṣo ’vyaktaṁ rajaḥ sattvaṁ tamaḥ param*  
*aham etat prasaṅkhyānaṁ jñānaṁ tattva-viniścayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I am form, taste, aroma, touch and sound;** ***ahaṅkāra*****; the** ***mahat-tattva*****; earth, water, fire, air and sky; the eleven senses; the living entity;** ***prakṛti*****; the modes of goodness, passion and ignorance; and Brahman. I am all of these items, along with knowledge of them, and the determination of truth resulting from that knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

I am earth etc. and I am Brahman (*param*). This shows that Brahman is the Lord’s vibhūti. That is suitable. Bhagavān is the dharmī, manifesting with his qualities. Brahman is dharma, manifesting without qualities. He is like the sun in relation to its rays.

It is said in Viṣṇu Purāṇa:

*śubhāśrayaḥ sa cittasya sarvagasya tathātmanaḥ.*

**The Lord is the shelter of Brahman.**

This is explained by Śrīdhara Svāmī. “The Lord is the shelter (*āśrayaḥ*), or the *pratiṣṭhā*, of the supreme Brahman (*sarvagasya ātmanaḥ*).” Thus the Lord says *brahmaṇo hi pratiṣṭhāham*. Śrīdhara Svāmī explains, “This means I am Brahman which is condensed (*ghanī-bhūtaḥ*), just as the sun globe is a condensed manifestation.”

Matsya says:

*madīyaṁ mahimānaṁ ca paraṁ brahmeti śabditam*  
*vetsyasy anugṛhītaṁ me sampraśnair vivṛtaṁ hṛdi*

**By my mercy, you will realize my power known as the impersonal Brahman, which will be disclosed in your heart through questions and answers. (SB** [**8.24.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-8/24.38)**)**

Brahmā says:

*yasya prabhā prabhavato jagad-aṇḍa-koṭi-*  
*koṭiṣv aśeṣa-vasudhādi-vibhūti-bhinnam \|*  
*tad brahma niṣkalam anantam aśeṣa-bhūtaṁ*  
*govindam ādi-puruṣaṁ tam ahaṁ bhajāmi \|\|*

**I worship the Supreme Lord Govinda, whose powerful form radiates an effulgence known as the undifferentiated, unlimited, all-encompassing Brahman*****,*** **which is completely distinct from its powers displayed in unlimited planets throughout billions of universes. (Brahma-saṁhitā** [**5.40**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bs/40)**)**

Yamunācārya says:

*yad aṇḍa-maṇḍāntara-gocaraṁ ca*  
*yad daśottarāṇy avaraṇāni yāni ca \|*  
*guṇāḥ pradhānaṁ puruṣaḥ paraṁ padaṁ*  
*parātparaṁ brahma te vibhūtayaḥ \|\|*

**Your manifestations of powers consist of whatever is visible within the universe, the coverings of the universe, each ten times thicker than the previous layer, the guṇas, pradhāna, the puruṣa, the spiritual world and the impersonal Brahman (paraṁ brahma). (Stotra-ratnam 14)**

Dhruva says:

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-*  
*dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt*  
*sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt*  
*kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt*

**The bliss for your servant available from meditating on your lotus feet or from hearing about your pastimes from the devotees is not available in your form of greatness known as Brahman, what to speak of the happiness for those who fall from the pleasures of Svarga. (SB** [**4.9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.10)**)**

Even the ātmārāms become attracted to the qualities of Kṛṣṇa.

*ātmārāmāś ca munayo nirgranthā apy urukrame \|*  
*kurvanty ahaitukīṁ bhaktim ittham-bhūta-guṇo hariḥ \|\|*

**Some sages who are** ***ātmārāmas*****, beyond the scriptures, false ego and rules, also practice unmotivated, pure** ***bhakti*** **to the master of pure** ***bhakti,*** **Kṛṣṇa, since he possesses qualities attractive to even them. (SB** [**1.7.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/7.10)**)**

*sva-sukha-nibhṛta-cetās tad-vyudastānya-bhāvo*  
*’py ajita-rucira-līlākṛṣṭa-sāras tadīyam*  
*vyatanuta kṛpayā yas tattva-dīpaṁ purāṇaṁ*  
*tam akhila-vṛjina-ghnaṁ vyāsa-sūnuṁ nato ’smi*

**Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto the son of Vyāsadeva, Śukadeva Gosvāmī, who destroyed all contamination, and who, though fixed in his own bliss with no distractions, became attracted to the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa and mercifully spread the Bhāgavatam which describes Kṛṣṇa and reveals the rasa of his pastimes. (SB** [**12.12.69**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/12.69)**)**

Details are given in the discussion between Vyāsa and Nārada. One should consult Bhagavat Sandarbha.

### Text 38

*mayeśvareṇa jīvena guṇena guṇinā vinā*  
*sarvātmanāpi sarveṇa na bhāvo vidyate kvacit*

**Перевод:**

**Without the Lord and the** ***jīva*** **there is no spiritual existence. Without the** ***guṇas*** **and their causes, there is no material existence. With the totality of** ***jīvas*** **and individual** ***jīvas*** **and their coverings there is no combination of matter and spirit. Without me, nothing exists.**

**Комментарий:**

Without me, no objects exist at all. Without me, the cause of everything, nothing exists. Everything is like horns of a rabbit. “The forms of the Lord like the puruṣa, creator of mahat-tattva, are the cause of everything. Is this all accomplished by such forms?” Without me (*mayā*), taking the form of the puruṣa, nothing exists. A particular cause has a particular effect. His position is shown to be superior to the creators of unlimited universe. This is stated in Gītā. *īśvaraḥ sarva-bhūtānāṁ*: I am the lord of all beings (BG [18.61](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.61))

### Text 39

*saṅkhyānaṁ paramāṇūnāṁ kālena kriyate mayā*  
*na tathā me vibhūtīnāṁ sṛjato ’ṇḍāni koṭiśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Over all time I can count all the atoms of the universe. I could not count all of my** ***vibhūtis*** **since I create millions of universes.**

**Комментарий:**

Over all time, by me, the antaryāmī of all beings, is known the number of atoms. My omniscience is without fail. But I cannot count my vibhūtis since they are unlimited. The verb is understood in the second statement. Though I am the Lord of Mahā-vaikuṇtḥa and the creator of millions of universes, I cannot count. This is like enumerating a thousand names of the Lord.

### Text 41

*etās te kīrtitāḥ sarvāḥ saṅkṣepeṇa vibhūtayaḥ*  
*mano-vikārā evaite yathā vācābhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**I have briefly described to you all my spiritual** ***vibhūtis.*** **But ordinary objects are also designated in the same way with words because of sentiments in the mind.**

**Комментарий:**

All the vibhūtis have been described, but they arise through mental operations. In this meaning, the statement is general since some vibhūtis are directly the Lord and do not arise by mental operations.

Or these vibhūtis have been glorified briefly, but all are glorified with general and particular qualities. Those like Vāsudeva and Nārāyaṇa, forms of parabhraman, beyond the proof of humans, have been glorified. But all the vibhūtis which are subject to human proof are transformations of the mind. They are conceived by the mind alone, as having ābhāsas of a small portion of my śakti, just as a king’s power is described by words indirectly, by describing objects which take shelter of the king. They are not direct forms like Paramātmā.

Or “How can the vibhūtis cause the highest good since they are transformations of the mind?” Just as my qualities are accomplished by words in the form of scriptures, similarly the vibhūtis are understood by scriptures, not external vision.

### Text 42

*vācaṁ yaccha mano yaccha prāṇān yacchedriyāṇi ca*  
*ātmānam ātmanā yaccha na bhūyaḥ kalpase ’dhvane*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, control your speaking, subdue the mind, conquer the life air, and regulate the senses concerning these objects. Through purified intelligence bring your intelligence under control. In this way you will never again fall onto the path of material existence.**

**Комментарий:**

External vision is rejected completely in two verses. Thus, control words subject to mental operations. Control intelligence by sattvika intelligence (*ātmanā*). You will not again be qualified for the path of saṁsāra (*adhvane*).

### Text 44

*tasmād vaco manaḥ prāṇān niyacchen mat-parāyaṇaḥ*  
*mad-bhakti-yuktayā buddhyā tataḥ parisamāpyate*

**Перевод:**

**Through intelligence endowed with faith in me, understanding me as the supreme shelter of words and mind, control words mind and prāṇas. One will accomplish the goal.**

**Комментарий:**

Making this clear, he concludes. By intelligence endowed with devotion for the form of Kṛṣṇa or Paramātmā, taking me as the supreme shelter of words and mind (*mat-parāyaṇaḥ*), control material words, mind and prāṇa. There is nothing beyond that, such as jñāna etc.

## Chapter Seventeen

### Text 1-2

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*yas tvayābhihitaḥ pūrvaṁ dharmas tvad-bhakti-lakṣaṇaḥ*  
*varṇāśamācāravatāṁ sarveṣāṁ dvi-padām api*  
*yathānuṣṭhīyamānena tvayi bhaktir nṛṇāṁ bhavet*  
*sva-dharmeṇāravindākṣa tan mamākhyātum arhasi*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O lotus-eyed Lord! Previously you described** ***dharma*** **of** ***bhakti*** **arising in** **followers of** ***varṇāśrama*** **and even ordinary human beings. Now you should explain to me how all human beings can achieve mixed or secondary** ***bhakti*** **for you by the execution of their prescribed duties.**

**Комментарий:**

The dharma of the varṇas spoken by you, should become bhakti to you for any human by being performed as one’s dharma. You should explain to me (*mama*) how that can take place. In verse 7, dharma is equated with bhakti. The sādhana of bhakti was described previously with arcana as the recommended process or action:

*ojaḥ saho balavatāṁ karmāhaṁ viddhi sātvatāṁ*  
*sātvatāṁ nava-mūrtīnām ādi-mūrtir ahaṁ parā*

**Know that of the powerful, I am strength of the senses and mind, and I am the devotional activities of my devotees. My devotees worship me in nine different forms, among which I am the principal form Vāsudeva. (SB** [**11.16.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/16.37)**)**

### Text 3-4

*purā kila mahā-bāho dharmaṁ paramakaṁ prabho*  
*yat tena haṁsa-rūpeṇa brahmaṇe ’bhyāttha mādhava*  
*sa idānīṁ su-mahatā kālenāmitra-karśana*  
*na prāyo bhavitā martya- loke prāg anuśāsitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! O mighty-armed one! Previously in your form of Haṁsa you spoke to Brahmā about** ***dharma*** **that brings supreme happiness to the practitioner. O Mādhava! O subduer of the enemy! Now much time has passed and that which you previously instructed will soon practically cease to exist.**

**Комментарий:**

What the Lord spoke to Brahmā is described in Viṣṇu-dharmottara.

### Text 7

*tat tvaṁ naḥ sarva-dharma-jña dharmas tvad-bhakti-lakṣaṇaḥ*  
*yathā yasya vidhīyeta tathā varṇaya me prabho*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, my Lord, since you are the knower of all religious principles, please describe to me how the human beings may execute** ***dharma*** **to produce** ***bhakti*** **for you.**

**Комментарий:**

Please explain to us, your devotees (*naḥ*), dharma which has bhakti as its character.

### Text 10

*ādau kṛta-yuge varṇo nṛṇāṁ haṁsa iti smṛtaḥ*  
*kṛta-kṛtyāḥ prajā jātyā tasmāt kṛta-yugaṁ viduḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In the beginning, in Satya-yuga, there is only one social class, called** ***haṁsa*****, to which all human beings belong. In that age all people had accomplished their goal from birth, and thus learned scholars call this the age of accomplishment, Kṛta-yuga.**

**Комментарий:**

In Brahma-kalpa (*ādau*) of Brahmā’s life, the tendencies of varṇāśrama in Satya and Tretā yugas are described in five verses. Where there are descriptions of all four varṇas from the beginning, it is from a different kalpa.

### Text 11

*vedaḥ praṇava evāgre dharmo ’haṁ vṛṣa-rūpa-dhṛk*  
*upāsate tapo-niṣṭhā haṁsaṁ māṁ mukta-kilbiṣāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In Satya-yuga the undivided Veda is expressed by the syllable** ***oṁ,*** **and I am the only object of mental activities. I become manifest as the bull of religion with four legs intact, and thus the inhabitants of Satya-yuga, fixed in austerity and free from all sins, worship me as Haṁsa.**

**Комментарий:**

There was one Veda. Since there was absence of rules for action, at that time, various Vedas were not manifested.

### Text 12

*tretā-mukhe mahā-bhāga prāṇān me hṛdayāt trayī*  
*vidyā prādurabhūt tasyā aham āsaṁ tri-vṛn makhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O greatly fortunate Uddhava! In the beginning of Tretā-yuga, Vedic knowledge appeared from my heart, via the** ***prāṇa,*** **in three divisions—as Ṛg, Sāma and Yajur. Then, from that knowledge I appeared as threefold sacrifice.**

**Комментарий:**

Gradually other Vedas were manifested and those qualified for them manifested. This is described in two verses. The Vedas appeared as three from the heart of me in the form of Vairāja Brahmā (universal form). He was the cause of the creation of the universe as a whole. Since the form produced higher and lower humans, it is not suitable that it should be Viṣṇu. *Makha* means the deity in charge of the sacrifice.

### Text 13

*vipra-kṣatriya-viṭ-śūdrā mukha-bāhūru-pāda-jāḥ*  
*vairājāt puruṣāj jātā ya ātmācāra-lakṣaṇāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**In Tretā-yuga, the four social orders became manifest after being created from the universal form. The** ***brāhmaṇas*** **appeared from the Lord’s face, the** ***kṣatriyas*** **from the Lord’s arms, the** ***vaiśyas*** **from the Lord’s thighs and the** ***śūdras*** **from his legs. Each social division was recognized by its particular duties.**

**Комментарий:**

Vairāja is the form of Brahmā, representing the universe.

### Text 14

*gṛhāśramo jaghanato brahmacaryaṁ hṛdo mama*  
*vakṣaḥ-sthalād vane-vāsaḥ sannyāsaḥ śirasi sthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The married order of life appeared from the loins of my universal form, and the celibate students came from my heart. The forest-dwelling retired order of life appeared from my chest, and the renounced order of life was situated within the head.**

**Комментарий:**

According to Śrīdhara Svāmī, the naiṣṭhikī brahmacārī arose from the heart. The brahmacārī who returns home and becomes a householder after studies arose from the thighs.

### Text 16

*śamo damas tapaḥ śaucaṁ santoṣaḥ kṣāntir ārjavam*  
*mad-bhaktiś ca dayā satyaṁ brahma-prakṛtayas tv imāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Control of the mind, control of the senses, austerity, cleanliness, satisfaction, tolerance, simplicity, devotion to me, mercy and truthfulness are the natural qualities of the** ***brāhmaṇas*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses discuss the qualities of the brāhmaṇas, kṣatriyas and vaiśyas. From this, the qualities of the āśramas can be inferred. For instance the qualities of the brāhmaṇa, particularly sama and dama, appear in the brahmacārī who is a brāhmaṇa.

### Text 17

*tejo balaṁ dhṛtiḥ śauryaṁ titikṣaudāryam udyamaḥ*  
*sthairyaṁ brahmanyam aiśvaryaṁ kṣatra-prakṛtayas tv imāḥ*

**Перевод:**
