**Energy, bodily strength, determination, heroism, tolerance, generosity, great endeavor, steadiness, devotion to the** ***brāhmaṇas*** **and leadership are the natural qualities of the** ***kṣatriyas.***

**Комментарий:**

The qualities of a kṣatriya particularly energy, will appear in a brahmacārī who is a kṣatriya.

### Text 18

*āstikyaṁ dāna-niṣṭhā ca adambho brahma-sevanam*  
*atuṣṭir arthopacayair vaiśya-prakṛtayas tv imāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Faith in Vedic civilization, dedication to charity, freedom from hypocrisy, service to the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **dissatisfaction** **and desire to accumulate money are the natural qualities of the** ***vaiśyas.***

**Комментарий:**

Vaiśya qualities, particularly āṣtikya, will appear in brahmacārīs who are vaiśyas.

### Text 36

*evaṁ bṛhad-vrata-dharo brāhmaṇo ’gnir iva jvalan*  
*mad-bhaktas tīvra-tapasā dagdha-karmāśayo ’malaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A** ***brāhmaṇa*** **observing the great vow of celibacy, brilliant like fire, by performing his svadharma intensely, by worshipping me, becomes purified in heart and becomes liberated.**

**Комментарий:**

If that brāhmaṇa is my devotee, then by doing his sva-dharma intensely (*tīvra-tapasā*) as my devotee, his heart becomes pure and he becomes liberated (*dagdha-karmāśayaḥ*).

### Text 38

*gṛhaṁ vanaṁ vopaviśet pravrajed vā dvijottamaḥ*  
*āśramād āśramaṁ gacchen nānyathāmat-paraś caret*

**Перевод:**

**A second born person (*****brahmacārī*****) becomes a householder,** ***vānaprastha*** **or** ***sannyāsī*** **in the case of a** ***brāhmaṇa.*** **He should progressively move through the** ***āśramas.*** **He should not be without an āśrama unless he is a devotee.**

**Комментарий:**

He discusses all the āśramas progressively. He should not be without āśrama. For a person who is a devotee the following is stated:

*jñāna-niṣṭho virakto vā mad-bhakto vānapekṣakaḥ*  
*sa-liṅgān āśramāṁs tyaktvā cared avidhi-gocaraḥ*

**A person fixed in** ***jñāna*** **and detached from external objects, or my devotee who is detached even from desire for liberation—both should reject the** ***āśrama*** **duties based on external rituals or paraphernalia and conduct themselves beyond the range of rules. (SB** [**11.18.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/18.28)**)**

### Text 39

*gṛhārthī sadṛśīṁ bhāryām udvahed ajugupsitām*  
*yavīyasīṁ tu vayasā yāṁ sa-varṇām anu kramāt*

**Перевод:**

**One who desires to establish family life should marry a wife of his own caste, who is beyond reproach and younger in age. If one desires to accept many wives they may be of lower caste than the first wife.**

**Комментарий:**

The brāhmaṇa may marry women of three lower castes.

### Text 44

*samuddharanti ye vipraṁ sīdantaṁ mat-parāyaṇam*  
*tān uddhariṣye na cirād āpadbhyo naur ivārṇavāt*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a ship rescues those who have fallen into the ocean, similarly, I very quickly rescue from all calamities those persons who uplift suffering** ***brāhmaṇas*** **who are my devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

Having described the great result of the impoverished brāhmaṇa, he describes the results for those of other castes who help them. As a ship rescues persons who have fallen in the ocean I rescue persons who aid such brāhmaṇas.

### Text 55

*karmabhir gṛha-medhīyair iṣṭvā mām eva bhaktimān*  
*tiṣṭhed vanaṁ vopaviśet prajāvān vā parivrajet*

**Перевод:**

**A devotional householder who worships me by execution of his family duties may remain at home may take vā*****naprastha*** **or, if he has a responsible son, take** ***sannyāsa.***

**Комментарий:**

Generally if he has a son, he takes sannyāsa. This is the desire of those following Vedānta. For those attached to karmas another path is indicated.

*ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet \|*  
*anapākṛtya mokṣaṁ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ \|\|*

**The mind should reject the three debts and concentrate on liberation. If one does not reject the debts and strives for liberation one falls from his position. (Manu Samhitā)**

## Chapter Eighteen

### Text 9

*evaṁ cīrṇena tapasā munir dhamani-santataḥ*  
*māṁ tapo-mayam ārādhya ṛṣi-lokād upaiti mām*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***vānaprastha,*** **practicing severe penances, becomes so emaciated that he appears to be mere skin and bones. Thus worshiping me who am penance personified, he goes to Maharloka and eventually attains liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī explains as follows. The result of the vānaprastha who is without desire, who desire only worship of the Lord, is described. If he purifies his mind by austerity of satisfying me and performs pure bhakti, he becomes liberated as a vānaprastha. If there are many obstacles, he obtains liberation gradually. Worshipping me through austerity (*mām tapomayam*) he gradually attains me, since he lacks pure bhakti.

### Text 10

*yas tv etat kṛcchrataś cīrṇaṁ tapo niḥśreyasaṁ mahat*  
*kāmāyālpīyase yuñjyād bāliśaḥ ko ’paras tataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One who with difficulty executes this painful but exalted penance for insignificant material pleasure must be considered the greatest fool.**

**Комментарий:**

He criticizes desires for Maharloka etc. The person who practices austerity without the obstacle of material desires for his whole life attains liberation.

### Text 14

*viprasya vai sannyasato devā dārādi-rūpiṇaḥ*  
*vighnān kurvanty ayaṁ hy asmān ākramya samiyāt param*

**Перевод:**

**“This man is going to surpass us and attain Brahma-loka.” Thus thinking, the** ***devatās*** **create stumbling blocks on the path of the** ***sannyāsī*** **by appearing before him in the shape of his former wife or other women and attractive objects.**

**Комментарий:**

*Param* means Brahma-loka.

### Text 15

*bibhṛyāc cen munir vāsaḥ kaupīnācchādanaṁ param*  
*tyaktaṁ na daṇḍa-pātrābhyām anyat kiñcid anāpadi*

**Перевод:**

**If the** ***sannyāsī*** **desires to wear something besides a mere** ***kaupīna,*** **he may use another cloth around his waist to cover the** ***kaupīna.*** **Having taken the** ***sannyāsa*** **vow, if there is no emergency, he should not accept anything besides his stick and water pot.**

**Комментарий:**

Since he has given up everything previously, with his vow he does not accept anything except the stick and pot.

### Text 18

*bhikṣāṁ caturṣu varṇeṣu vigarhyān varjayaṁś caret*  
*saptāgārān asaṅkḷptāṁs tuṣyel labdhena tāvatā*

**Перевод:**

**Rejecting impure houses, he should approach without previous calculation seven houses from any of the four types of brāhmaṇas and be satisfied with that which is obtained there by begging.**

**Комментарий:**

He should choose from four types of brāhmaṇa houses: those who maintain themselves by donations, teaching, performing sacrifice and taking grains from the field or market floor

### Text 19

*bahir jalāśayaṁ gatvā tatropaspṛśya vāg-yataḥ*  
*vibhajya pāvitaṁ śeṣaṁ bhuñjītāśeṣam āhṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**Taking the food gathered through begging, he should leave the populated areas and go to a reservoir of water. There, performing** ***ācamana,*** **in silence he should purify the food and distribute portions. He should eat everything he has begged, leaving no remnants.**

**Комментарий:**

According to Śrīdhara Svāmī the verse concerns begged food. The food may also be offered before one makes an attempt at begging. This may be of four types. Thus there are five types of food.

### Text 20

*ekaś caren mahīm etāṁ niḥsaṅgaḥ saṁyatendriyaḥ*  
*ātma-krīḍa ātma-rata ātma-vān sama-darśanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With no material attachment, with senses fully controlled, fully determined, satisfied in realization of Paramātmā and enjoying pastimes with Paramātmā, he should travel about the earth without attachment, seeing with equal vision everywhere.**

**Комментарий:**

*Niḥsaṅgaḥ caran* means that he should wander about, not attached to any place or person.

### Text 26

*naitad vastutayā paśyed dṛśyamānaṁ vinaśyati*  
*asakta-citto viramed ihāmutra-cikīrṣitāt*

**Перевод:**

**He should never see as ultimate reality those material things which will perish. With consciousness free from attachment to this world and the next, he should withdraw from all activities meant for material progress.**

**Комментарий:**

Previously, in verse 20, detachment was indicated. This is further described in two verses. He should be detached from objects he has not attained. That is expressed in this verse.

### Text 27

*yad etad ātmani jagan mano-vāk-prāṇa-saṁhatam*  
*sarvaṁ māyeti tarkeṇa sva-sthas tyaktvā na tat smaret*

**Перевод:**

**He should logically consider the universe, as well as the mind, speech and life air, to be ultimately products of** ***māyā,*** **situated in the Lord*****.*** **Thus situated in the self, rejecting them all, he should never think of them.**

**Комментарий:**

He should be detached from objects attained in the past or at present. He considers “Is this māyā or not?” Rejecting the effect of mind, speech and life air on the pure ātmā, one should not think of them again.

### Text 28

*jñāna-niṣṭho virakto vā mad-bhakto vānapekṣakaḥ*  
*sa-liṅgān āśramāṁs tyaktvā cared avidhi-gocaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A person fixed in** ***jñāna*** **and detached from external objects, or my devotee who is detached even from desire for liberation—both should reject the** ***āśrama*** **duties based on external rituals or paraphernalia and conduct themselves beyond the range of rules.**

**Комментарий:**

Others act as follows, similar to SB [11.17.33](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/17.33), giving general rules when talking about naiṣtikī brahmacārīs. Śrīdhara Svāmī mentions this verse in relation to chapter 17: *nānyathā mat-paras caret.*

### Text 30

*veda-vāda-rato na syān na pāṣaṇḍī na haitukaḥ*  
*śuṣka-vāda-vivāde na kañcit pakṣaṁ samāśrayet*

**Перевод:**

**A devotee should never engage in the rituals mentioned in the** ***karma-kāṇḍa*** **section of the Vedas, nor should he take up the marks of a Buddhist, or become involved in logic. He should never take any side whatsoever in useless arguments.**

**Комментарий:**

He should not even study the works of the pāṣaṇḍīs in order to get knowledge.

### Text 32

*eka eva paro hy ātmā bhūteṣv ātmany avasthitaḥ*  
*yathendur uda-pātreṣu bhūtāny ekātmakāni ca*

**Перевод:**

**The one Paramātmā is situated within all material bodies and within everyone’s soul, just as the moon is reflected in innumerable reservoirs of water. Every material body is the effect of Paramātmā.**

**Комментарий:**

The Paramātmā (*ātmā*) is situated in all other beings and in one’s own ātmā. The example of the moon reflected in many ponds is used only to illustrate the power of one entity, not to show that jīvas are reflections of Paramātmā (and thus illusions). This is explained in Paramātmā Sandarbha.

### Text 34

*āhārārthaṁ samīheta yuktaṁ tat-prāṇa-dhāraṇam*  
*tattvaṁ vimṛśyate tena tad vijñāya vimucyate*

**Перевод:**

**If required, one should endeavor to get sufficient foodstuffs, because it is always necessary and proper to maintain one life. When one is fit, one can contemplate spiritual truth, and by understanding the truth one is liberated.**

**Комментарий:**

If one becomes anxious, one should gather food, since it is proper for steadiness of the mind and senses (*tat-prāṇa-dhāraṇam*). Otherwise, there will be disturbance because of an improper situation. With that steadiness one can consider the highest truth.

### Text 36

*śaucam ācamanaṁ snānaṁ na tu codanayā caret*  
*anyāṁś ca niyamāñ jñānī yathāhaṁ līlayeśvaraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Just as I, the Supreme Lord, execute regulative duties by my own free will, similarly, the jñānī** **should maintain general cleanliness, perform** ***ācamana,*** **take bath and execute other regulative duties, not by force, but by his own free will.**

**Комментарий:**

Just as I act by my will, not by force of karma, the jñānī should attain a portion of that śakti and act, since the Lord appears in that person (*jñānī*).

### Text 37

*na hi tasya vikalpākhyā yā ca mad-vīkṣayā hatā*  
*ā-dehāntāt kvacit khyātis tataḥ sampadyate mayā*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***jñānī*** **does not experience any other realization, since it is destroyed by realization of me. Sometimes however, before the body is given up, this false perception recurs. But after death he attains wealth similar to mine**

**Комментарий:**

Since he becomes influenced by auspicious and inauspicious circumstances in the material world, should he not be subject to rules? Some realization other than me (*vikalpākhyā*) does not take place. He in the end attains a wealth similar to mine, such as sārṣṭi etc. (*mayā sampadyate*).

### Text 44

*iti māṁ yaḥ sva-dharmeṇa bhajen nityam ananya-bhāk*  
*sarva-bhūteṣu mad-bhāvo mad-bhaktiṁ vindate dṛḍhām*

**Перевод:**

**One who worships me by his prescribed duties, having no other object of worship, and who understands that I am in all beings including the** ***devatās*** **achieves firm** ***bhakti*** **to me.**

**Комментарий:**

He who worships me by his sva-dharma, who performs sva-dharma to please me, and not for any other purpose (*ananya-bhāk*), achieves steadiness. How can worshipping devatās in sva-dharma please you? He understands that all beings are situated in me.

### Text 46

*iti sva-dharma-nirṇikta- sattvo nirjñāta-mad-gatiḥ*  
*jñāna-vijñāna-sampanno na cirāt samupaiti mām*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, one who has purified his existence by execution of his prescribed duties, who fully understands my supreme position and who is endowed with scriptural and realized knowledge, very soon achieves me.**

**Комментарий:**

Attaining me involves attaining knowledge of me along with the gradual development of bhakti. This summarizes the topic. The person fixed in his sva-dharma, who realizes in truth that the goal is bhakti, who is endowed with general and higher knowledge of my svarūpa, form qualities and activities (*jñāna-vijñāna-sampannaḥ*) soon achieves me. Or if he desires merging in Brahman, the steps are described. Understanding completely the goal, thoroughly studying the jñāna scriptures taught by me, he attains me in the form of impersonal Brahman.

### Text 47

*varṇāśramavatāṁ dharma eṣa ācāra-lakṣaṇaḥ*  
*sa eva mad-bhakti-yuto niḥśreyasa-karaḥ paraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Following rules of** ***varṇāśrama*** **with a little mixture of** ***bhakti*** **produces the best result.**

**Комментарий:**

In practicing sva-dharma, the strength of bhakti in all cases is emphasized.

## Chapter Nineteen

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*yo vidyā-śruta-sampannaḥ ātmavān nānumānikaḥ*  
*mayā-mātram idaṁ jñātvā jñānaṁ ca mayi sannyaset*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: A person endowed with** ***jñāna*** **through the scriptures, who consequently understands** ***ātmā,*** **and has realization, on understanding that the world is produced through false identity, gives up the practice of** ***jñāna*** **in order to attain me.**

**Комментарий:**

He understands that the world is perceived in the ātmā (perceived) by māyā, and not that it is illusion. Just as a king gives a weapon to conquer and after conquering one gives the king the weapon, so the person gives that knowledge to me. He worships only me.

### Text 2

*jñāninas tv aham eveṣṭaḥ svārtho hetuś ca sammataḥ*  
*svargaś caivāpavargaś ca nānyo ’rtho mad-ṛte priyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**For the** ***jñānī,*** **I am the object of worship, the expected result, the** ***sādhana,*** **and the means of realization. I am the cause of happiness and destruction of suffering. There is no one dearer than me for the** ***jñānī.***

**Комментарий:**

For the jñānī, when I am understood completely, I become the supreme sādhana and sādhya and manifest myself, not impersonal Brahman. The word *tu* indicates rejection of other options.

### Text 3

*jñāna-vijñāna-saṁsiddhāḥ padaṁ śreṣṭhaṁ vidur mama*  
*jñānī priyatamo ’to me jñānenāsau bibharti mām*

**Перевод:**

**Those who had previously achieved complete perfection through philosophical and realized knowledge recognized my lotus feet to be supreme. Such a** ***jñānī,*** **most dear to me, pleases me by his knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

Evidence for the realization of previous jñānīs is given. The Kumāras and Śukadeva know my louts feet, not Brahman, to be the best.

*tasyāravinda-nayanasya padāravinda-*  
*kiñjalka-miśra-tulasī-makaranda-vāyuḥ*  
*antar-gataḥ sva-vivareṇa cakāra teṣāṁ*  
*saṅkṣobham akṣara-juṣām api citta-tanvoḥ*

**The wind carrying the aroma of** ***tulasī*** **which had touched the filaments of the Lord’s toe nails on his lotus feet entered their minds through the nostrils and began to agitate their minds and bodies, even though they were fixed in Brahman. (SB** [**3.15.43**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.43)**)**

*sva-sukha-nibhṛta-cetās tad-vyudastānya-bhāvo*  
*'py ajita-rucira-līlākṛṣṭa-sāras tadīyam*  
*vyatanuta kṛpayā yas tattva-dīpaṁ purāṇaṁ*  
*tam akhila-vṛjina-ghnaṁ vyāsa-sūnuṁ nato 'smi*

**Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto the son of Vyāsadeva, Śukadeva Gosvāmī, who destroys of all sin, and who, though fixed in his own bliss with no distractions, became attracted to the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa and mercifully spread the** ***Bhāgavatam*** **which describes Kṛṣṇa and reveals the** ***rasa*** **of his pastimes. (SB** [**12.12.69**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-12/12.69)**)**

Because of that, by far the jñānī is dearest and therefore by his knowledge he nourishes me and makes me joyful (*bibharti*). I say in the Gītā:

*teṣāṁ jñānī nitya-yukta eka-bhaktir viśiṣyate \|*  
*priyo hi jñānino ’tyartham ahaṁ sa ca mama priyaḥ \|\|*

**Of** **these four types, the** ***jñānī*****, who is constantly engaged in thinking of me, who is practicing pure** ***bhakti*****, is the best. This** ***jñānī*** **loves only me, and I love only him. (BG** [**7.17**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/7.17)**)**

*udārāḥ sarva evaite jñānī tv ātmaiva me matam \|*  
*āsthitaḥ sa hi yuktātmā mām evānuttamāṁ gatim \|\|*

**They are all exalted, but the** ***jñānī*** **is my soul, because he remains firmly fixed in devotion to me, taking me as the highest goal. That is my opinion. (BG** [**7.18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/7.18)**)**

### Text 4

*tapas tīrthaṁ japo dānaṁ pavitrāṇītarāṇi ca*  
*nālaṁ kurvanti tāṁ siddhiṁ yā jñāna-kalayā kṛtā*

**Перевод:**

**That perfection which is produced by a small fraction of** ***jñāna*** **cannot be duplicated by performing austerities, visiting holy places, chanting silent prayers, giving in charity or engaging in other pious activities.**

**Комментарий:**

The other methods do not produce sufficient perfection in the form of bhakti. Another version has *śuddhim* instead of *siddhim*. The methods do not produce purity necessary for bhakti.

### Text 5

*tasmāj jñānena sahitaṁ jñātvā svātmānam uddhava*  
*jñāna-vijñāna-sampanno bhaja māṁ bhakti-bhāvataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, O Uddhava, knowing your** ***ātmā*** **through** ***jñāna,*** **endowed with knowledge and realization, worship me with devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

He summarizes his intention. Knowing the svarūpa of the jīva (*svātmānam*), endowed with that knowledge and knowledge of Brahman (*vijñāna*), worship me.

### Text 8

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*jñānaṁ viśuddhaṁ vipulaṁ yathaitad*  
*vairāgya-vijñāna-yutaṁ purāṇam*  
*ākhyāhi viśveśvara viśva-mūrte*  
*tvad-bhakti-yogaṁ ca mahad-vimṛgyam*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: Please explain to me more elevated knowledge, greater knowledge, endowed with detachment and realization, which is approved by previous** ***jñānīs.*** **O Lord of the universe! O form of the universe! Teach me about pure b*****hakti*** **which is sought by the great sages.**

**Комментарий:**

In the previous verse, in order to attain quick knowledge the Lord explained about ātmā (*tvam-padārtha*). Not satisfied with that, Uddhava inquires about general and specific knowledge of Brahman (*tat-padārtha*) along with vairagya. But not satisfied with only that, he asks about the final result, bhakti-yoga.

Previously did you speak of pure knowledge? The knowledge should be pure and full (*vipulam*), the greatest, since if concerning tat-padārtha. Is this knowledge according to vivarta-vāda? No. It is ancient (*purāṇam*), based on beginningless knowledge. *Śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt*: knowledge of Brahman comes from scripture. (Brahma-sūtra) After attaining vijñāna, one attains bhakti to you. Vivarta-vāda destroys bhakti. The realization after vijñāna is given proof: it is sought by great souls like the Kumāras (*mahad-vimṛgyam*).

### Text 9

*tāpa-trayeṇābhihatasya ghore*  
*santapyamānasya bhavādhvanīśa*  
*paśyāmi nānyac charaṇaṁ tavāṅghri-*  
*dvandvātapatrād amṛtābhivarṣāt*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! For one who is being tormented on the terrible path of birth and death and is constantly overwhelmed by the threefold miseries, I do not see any shelter other than your two lotus feet, which are like a refreshing umbrella that pours showers of nectar.**

**Комментарий:**

He praises surrender to the Lord since he is the only shelter of all beings, in two verses. This has more bliss (*amṛta*) than Brahman.

*yā nirvṛtis tanu-bhṛtāṁ tava pāda-padma-*  
*dhyānād bhavaj-jana-kathā-śravaṇena vā syāt*  
*sā brahmaṇi sva-mahimany api nātha mā bhūt*  
*kiṁ tv antakāsi-lulitāt patatāṁ vimānāt*

**The bliss for your servant available from meditating on your lotus feet or from hearing about your pastimes from the devotees is not available in your form of greatness known as Brahman, what to speak of the happiness for those who fall from the pleasures of Svarga. (SB** [**4.9.10**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/9.10)**)**

The Lord destroys all suffering for those who surrender and showers them with complete sweetness.

### Text 10

*daṣṭaṁ janaṁ sampatitaṁ bile ’smin*  
*kālāhinā kṣudra-sukhoru-tarṣam*  
*samuddharainaṁ kṛpayāpavargyair*  
*vacobhir āsiñca mahānubhāva*

**Перевод:**

**O almighty Lord! Please be merciful and uplift this hopeless living entity, bitten by the snake of time, who has fallen into the dark hole of material existence, and still intensely hankers for insignificant material happiness. Sprinkle me with your liberating words.**

**Комментарий:**

Sprinkle me with the nectar of your words which explain bhakti (*apavargyaiḥ*).

*yathā-varṇa-vidhānam apavargaś cāpi bhavati*

**By the rules of varṇa,** ***bhakti*** **appears on its own. (SB** [**5.19.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/19.20)**)**

### Text 11

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ittham etat purā raja bhīṣmaṁ dharma-bhṛtāṁ varam*  
*ajāta-śatruḥ papraccha sarveṣāṁ no ’nuśṛṇvatām*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Just as you are now inquiring from me, in the past King Yudhiṣṭhira, who considered no one his enemy, inquired from the greatest of the upholders of religious principles, Bhīṣma, while all of us were carefully listening.**

**Комментарий:**

Thinking of enlarging to topic for the listeners, I will summarily relate this in order to elaborate later. He speaks in three verses.

### Text 15

*etad eva hi vijñānaṁ na tathaikena yena yat*  
*sthity-utpatty-apyayān paśyed bhāvānāṁ tri-guṇātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**This** ***jñāna*** **becomes** ***vijñāna*** **when one does not see all the elements as one with Paramātmā but directly sees Paramātmā and nothing else. At the stage of** ***jñāna*** **however one should see the temporary nature of all the material elements made of the three** ***guṇas*****.**

**Комментарий:**

The previously described jñāna is called vijñāna. When is that? Through scripture there is awareness of an invisible being as the substratum beyond meditation and as the cause of various effects. That is jñāna. When Paramātmā manifests visibly through scriptures or meditation and nothing else manifests, it is called vijñāna.

### Text 16

*ādāv ante ca madhye ca sṛjyāt sṛjyaṁ yad anviyāt*  
*punas tat-pratisaṅkrāme yac chiṣyeta tad eva sat*

**Перевод:**

**That which is present at creation and destruction, which accompanies all the material phases from one creation to another and which remains alone at universal destruction is the one eternal Paramātmā.**

**Комментарий:**

Effect follows effect (*sṛjyāt sṛjyam*). According to *śrutes tu śabda-mūlatvāt*: the supreme entity is known by scriptures, pariṇāma (transformation into the created world by the Lord’s śakti) is accepted and not vivarta (an illusion of the created world). It does not say something imagined comes from something else imaged.

### Text 17

*śrutiḥ pratyakṣam aitihyam anumānaṁ catuṣṭayam*  
*pramāṇeṣv anavasthānād vikalpāt sa virajyate*

**Перевод:**

**From the four types of evidence—Vedic knowledge, direct experience, traditional wisdom and logical induction—one can understand the temporary nature of the material world, by which one will become detached from the duality of this world.**

**Комментарий:**

This verse forbids a variety of opinions. The word *vikalpa* means doubt. From the four types of knowledge one becomes free of doubt, and becomes steady in the real substance.

Or since one cannot understand the relationship by these proofs. And since it is said that the ātmā is imagined in terms of oneself (*vikalpāt*), one becomes detached. Śruti says *asaṅgo* *hy ayaṁ puruṣaḥ*: the puruṣa is detached from matter. (Bṛhad-ārāṇyaka upaniṣad) *Pratyakṣa* is useless because all awareness arises from difference between the revealer and the revealed. *Aitihyam* is known knowledge handed down from great sages, which is mentioned in scriptures. *Anumāna* is inference. Since wood is burned by fire, on seeing burned wood one infers fire. Sometimes objects perceived are false, as in mistaking shell for silver. In deep sleep there is no perception of the world at all.

Sometimes logic is employed in scriptures:

*ādāv ante ca madhye ca sṛjyāt sṛjyaṁ yad anviyāt*  
*punas tat-pratisaṅkrāme yac chiṣyeta tad eva sat*

**That which is present at creation and destruction, which accompanies all the material phases from one creation to another and which remains alone at universal destruction is the one eternal Paramātmā. (SB** [**11.19.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.16)**)**

### Text 18

*karmaṇāṁ pariṇāmitvād ā-viriñcyād amaṅgalam*  
*vipaścin naśvaraṁ paśyed adṛṣṭam api dṛṣṭa-vat*

**Перевод:**

**The wise man should see that, because of transformations of** ***karma,*** **even the happiness on the planet of Brahma, what to speak of that experienced on earth, is temporary and inauspicious.**

**Комментарий:**

Up to Brahmā everything is seen to be temporary. However, this is not the case of Vaikuṇṭha.

### Text 19

*bhakti-yogaḥ puraivoktaḥ prīyamāṇāya te ’nagha*  
*punaś ca kathayiṣyāmi mad-bhakteḥ kāraṇaṁ paraṁ*

**Перевод:**

**O sinless Uddhava! I previously explained to you** ***bhakti-yoga.*** **Now I will again explain the auspicious cause of** ***bhakti*** **dedicated to me, in order to increase your love.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhakti to me, the method of attaining prema, was previously described. I will again describe the method or the cause of prema (*mad-bhakteḥ kāraṇam*).

### Text 20-24

*śraddhāmṛta-kathāyāṁ me śaśvan mad-anukīrtanam*  
*pariniṣṭhā ca pūjāyāṁ stutibhiḥ stavanaṁ mama*  
*ādaraḥ paricaryāyāṁ sarvāṅgair abhivandanam*  
*mad-bhakta-pūjābhyadhikā sarva-bhūteṣu man-matiḥ*  
*mad-artheṣv aṅga-ceṣṭā ca vacasā mad-guṇeraṇam*  
*mayy arpaṇaṁ ca manasaḥ sarva-kāma-vivarjanam*  
*mad-arthe ’rtha-parityāgo bhogasya ca sukhasya ca*  
*iṣṭaṁ dattaṁ hutaṁ japtaṁ mad-arthaṁ yad vrataṁ tapaḥ*  
*evaṁ dharmair manuṣyāṇām uddhavātma-nivedinām*  
*mayi sañjāyate bhaktiḥ ko ’nyo ’rtho ’syāvaśiṣyate*

**Firm faith in the narration of my sweet pastimes, constant chanting of my glories, unwavering attachment to my worship, praising me through verses, great respect for serving the deity, offering obeisances with the entire body, performing extensive worship of my devotees, consciousness of me in all living entities, offering of ordinary bodily activities in my service, use of words to describe my qualities, offering the mind to me, rejection of all material desires, giving up wealth for my sake, renouncing material sense gratification and happiness, performing acts of charity, offering** ***homa,*** **and chanting** ***japa,*** **performing vows like Ekādaśī as austerity with the purpose of achieving me—by these those human beings who have surrendered themselves to me develop** ***bhakti*** **for me. What other result could remain for my devotee?**

One should worship the devotees more than one should worship me *(pūjābhyadhikā*) since that satisfies me particularly. One should see me in all beings (*man-matiḥ*). Rejecting all desires is caused by becoming steady in hearing about the Lord. What other result remains for the devotee, since all happiness is included in the supreme Lord, the highest puruṣārtha?

### Text 25

*yadātmany arpitaṁ citta śāntaṁ sattvopabṛṁhitam*  
*dharmaṁ jñānaṁ sa vairāgyam aiśvaryaṁ cābhipadyate*

**Перевод:**

**When one’s peaceful consciousness, strengthened by** ***sattva*****, is fixed on Paramātmā, one achieves** ***dharma, jñāna, vairāgya*** **and** ***aiśvarya.***

**Комментарий:**

Secondary characteristics are shown. Dharma, which is in pursuance of bhakti, manifests.

*yasyāsti bhaktir bhagavaty akiñcanā*  
*sarvair guṇais tatra samāsate surāḥ*  
*harāv abhaktasya kuto mahad-guṇā*  
*manorathenāsati dhāvato bahiḥ*

**The** ***devatās*** **constantly dwell with all good qualities in that person who has pure** ***bhakti*** **for the Lord. There are no good qualities in the non-devotee who chases after temporary material objects with desire for material pleasure. (SB** [**5.18.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/18.12)**)**

### Text 26

*yad arpitaṁ tad vikalpe indriyaiḥ paridhāvati*  
*rajas-valaṁ cāsan-niṣṭhaṁ cittaṁ viddhi viparyayam*

**Перевод:**

**When consciousness, fixed on the material body and home, pursues objects in the mode of** ***rajas*** **with the senses, one becomes attached to forbidden objects. Know that such a mind achieves opposite results.**

**Комментарий:**

The opposite is shown. When the consciousness, fixed and body and home (*vikalpe*), pursues objects in rajas and becomes attached to them, know that this is devoid of prema (*viparyayam*).

### Text 27

*dharmo mad-bhakti-kṛt prokto jñānaṁ caikātmya-darśanam*  
*guṇesv asaṅgo vairāgyam aiśvaryaṁ cāṇimādayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

***Dharma*** **means what produces** ***bhakti.*** **Real** ***jñāna*** **is seeing Paramātmā as the ātmā of all beings. Real** ***vairāgya*** **is complete disinterest in sense objects. Real** ***aiśvarya*** **is acquisition of the** ***siddhis.***

**Комментарий:**

Dharma is accurately said to be what produces bhakti and nothing else. That is the meaning of the word *dharma* by mukhya-vṛtti. Dharma means bhakti. This refers to verse 24 as well. Jñāna means seeing that Svayam Bhagavān is the ātmā of all beings (*ekātmya-darśanam*).

### Text 33-35

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ahiṁsā satyam asteyam asaṅgo hrīr asañcayaḥ*  
*āstikyaṁ brahmacaryaṁ ca maunaṁ sthairyaṁ kṣamābhayam*  
*śaucaṁ japas tapo homaḥ śraddhātithyaṁ mad-arcanam*  
*tīrthāṭanaṁ parārthehā tuṣṭir ācārya-sevanam*  
*ete yamāḥ sa-niyamā ubhayor dvādaśa smṛtāḥ*  
*puṁsām upāsitās tāta yathā-kāmaṁ duhanti hi*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Nonviolence, truthfulness, not stealing the property of others, detachment, humility, freedom from possessiveness, acceptance of scripture, celibacy, silence, steadiness, forgiveness and fearlessness are the twelve** ***yamas*****. Internal cleanliness, external cleanliness, chanting the holy names of the Lord, austerity, sacrifice, faith, hospitality, worship of me, visiting holy places, acting and desiring only for the supreme interest, satisfaction, and service to** ***guru*** **are the twelve** ***niyamas.*** **These twenty-four elements bestow all desired benedictions upon those persons who devotedly cultivate them.**

**Комментарий:**

Yamas and niyamas both are twelve in number (*ubhayoḥ*).

### Text 36

*śamo man-niṣṭhatā buddher dama indriya-saṁyamaḥ*  
*titikṣā duḥkha-sammarṣo jihvopastha-jayo dhṛtiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Absorbing the intelligence in me constitutes** ***śama*****, and complete discipline of the senses is** ***dama.*** **Tolerance means patiently enduring suffering, and steadfastness means conquering the tongue and genitals.**

**Комментарий:**

Śama means steadiness of the antaḥkaraṇa. I consider the yamas to be rules related to the senses which are dependent on the antaḥkaraṇa.

### Text 37

*daṇḍa-nyāsaḥ paraṁ dānaṁ kāma-tyāgas tapaḥ smṛtam*  
*svabhāva-vijayaḥ śauryaṁ satyaṁ ca sama-darśanam*

**Перевод:**

**Supreme charity is to give up aggression toward others and real austerity is renunciation of enjoyment. Real heroism is to conquer one’s natural tendency to enjoy material life and truth is seeing all beings as one’s self in happiness or distress.**

**Комментарий:**

*Sama-darśanam* is according to the following:

*nārāyaṇa-parāḥ sarve na kutaścana bibhyati*  
*svargāpavarga-narakeṣv api tulyārtha-darśinaḥ*

**Devotees solely engaged in the service of Nārāyaṇa never fear any condition of life. For them the heavenly planets, liberation and the hellish planets are all the same. (SB** [**6.17.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/17.28)**)**

### Text 39

*dharma iṣṭaṁ dhanaṁ nṝṇāṁ yajño ’haṁ bhagavattamaḥ*  
*dakṣiṇā jñāna-sandeśaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ paraṁ balam*

**Перевод:**

**The respectable wealth for human beings is possessing** ***dharma,*** **and I, Svayam Bhagavān, am sacrifice. Religious remuneration (*****dakṣiṇā*****) is informing friends after a festival of one’s realization of bliss, and the greatest strength is the** ***prāṇāyāma*** **system.**

**Комментарий:**

The best gift one can receive is obtaining instructions of knowledge. The cause of attaining such instructions is one’s great devotion to the ācārya (who consequently gives the instructions). The sponsor of a sacrifice does not give instructions on jñāna, but gives gold etc. Or I Kṛṣṇa, Svayam Bhagavān (*bhagavattamaḥ*) am sacrifice, since by knowledge of me one attains the results of sacrifice.

*sarve vedāḥ sarva-vidyāḥ sa-śāstrāḥ*  
*sarve yajñāḥ sarva ījyaś ca kṛṣṇaḥ \|*  
*viduḥ kṛṣṇaṁ brāhmaṇās tattvato ye*  
*teṣāṁ rājan sarva-yajñāḥ samāptāḥ \|\|*

**Kṛṣṇa is all the Vedas, all knowledge all scriptures, all sacrifices, all worship. O king! Brāhmaṇas who know Kṛṣṇa in truth have completed all sacrifices. (Mahābhārata)**

The words following the ācārya or concerning performance of the sacrifice according to his order are the gift, since they satisfy the Lord, since they result ultimately in knowledge of him.

### Text 40

*bhago ma aiśvaro bhāvo lābho mad-bhaktir uttamaḥ*  
*vidyātmani bhidā-bādho jugupsā hrīr akarmasu*

**Перевод:**

**Lordship means my controlling power. Gain means gaining** ***bhakti.*** **Knowledge is nullifying what is not** ***ātmā*** **in the** ***jīva.*** **Real modesty is dislike of sinful activities.**

**Комментарий:**

Knowledge means negating ideas that I am a devatā, I am strong, I am young etc. (*bhidā-bādhaḥ*).

### Text 45

*eta uddhava te praśnāḥ sarve sādhu nirūpitāḥ*  
*kiṁ varṇitena bahunā lakṣaṇaṁ guṇa-doṣayoḥ*  
*guṇa-doṣa-dṛśir doṣo guṇas tūbhaya-varjitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, Uddhava, I have elucidated all of the matters of inquiry, which are suitable for liberation. There is no need for a more elaborate description of good and bad qualities, since to see good and bad is itself a bad quality. The best quality is to transcend seeing good and bad qualities.**

**Комментарий:**

What I have explained in response to your question is not generally so interesting to me. Most interesting are the qualities arising from yama and niyama just described.

The Lord then points out the fault in producing negative results. Why describe more about good and bad qualities? Elaboration comes from discussing unessential matters. By discussion of spiritual matters, the discussion becomes one chief subject. That should be the result of discussing good and bad qualities. Discerning qualities to be cultivated and faults to be avoided is also a fault. It is a good quality to be devoid of seeing good and bad. Transcending seeing good and bad, one cultivates the highest goal naturally, since depending on seeing good and bad is inferior.

## Chapter Twenty

### Text 1

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*vidhiś ca pratiṣedhaś ca nigamo hīśvarasya te*  
*avekṣate ’raviṇḍākṣa guṇaṁ doṣaṁ ca karmaṇām*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O lotus-eyed Kṛṣṇa! You are the Supreme Lord, and thus the Vedic literatures, consisting of positive and negative injunctions, constitute your order. Such literatures focus upon the good and bad qualities of work.**

**Комментарий:**

Not knowing the Lord’s intention, he asks to hear more topics in two verses. One sees good qualities as puṇya and bad qualities as sins.

### Text 3

*guṇa-doṣa-bhidā-dṛṣṭim antareṇa vacas tava*  
*niḥśreyasaṁ kathaṁ nṝṇāṁ niṣedha-vidhi-lakṣaṇam*

**Перевод:**

**Without seeing in terms of good and bad in the Vedas, which are your words, composed of orders and prohibitions, how can man achieve liberation?**

**Комментарий:**

Without seeing good and bad, how will orders and prohibitions in karma-kāṇḍa take place and how will liberation take place for men? Or, without your words of orders and prohibitions, how will liberation take place? Or without seeing good and bad, how will your words describing orders and prohibitions produce truth and liberation? All the Vedas use orders and prohibitions to purify the antaḥkaraṇa to attain liberation.

### Text 4

*pitṛ-deva-manuṣyānāṁ vedaś cakṣus taveśvara*  
*śreyas tv anupalabdhe ’rthe sādhya-sādhanayor api*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord! Your Vedas are the best cause of knowledge for Pitṛs,** ***devatās*** **and humans.**

**Комментарий:**

The Vedas are the means and goal of liberation and Svarga.

The Vedas are the means for and goal of realizing the form and powers of the Lord (*anupalabdhe arthe*).

### Text 5

*guṇa-doṣa-bhidā-dṛṣṭir nigamāt te na hi svataḥ*  
*nigamenāpavādaś ca bhidāyā iti ha bhramaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The distinction observed between piety and sin comes from your own Vedic knowledge and does not arise by itself. By rejecting the Vedas with their injunctions and prohibitions as you seem to be doing now, such distinction between piety and sin will be nullified. I will be bewildered.**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing good and bad is prescribed in scriptures. How can one reject the scriptures concerning piety and sin previously described? Seeing good and bad arises from the Vedas, which are your order. It does not arise by itself since if seeing in this way arose on its own, it would be destroyed, subject to argument. Since it arises from the Vedas, by having meaning and purpose, it will not be destroyed. Confusion will clearly (*ha*) arise by that rejection.

Since by attaining meaning confusion is destroyed, by your words of the Vedas, destroy the confusion.

### Text 6

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*yogās trayo mayā proktā nṝṇāṁ śreyo-vidhitsayā*  
*jñānaṁ karma ca bhaktiś ca nopāyo ’nyo ’sti kutracit*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Because I desire that human beings may achieve perfection, I have presented three methods—the path of** ***jñāna,*** **the path of** ***karma*** **and the path of** ***bhakti*****. Besides these three, there no other means of elevation.**

**Комментарий:**

The Lord first teaches three yogas in order to explain non-contradiction by difference in qualification, in the same topic, piety and sin, previously described. There are plentiful descriptions of piety and sin for persons practicing karma. The most elevated person should not see in terms of piety and sin (SB [11.19.45](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.40)).

**Bhakti Sandarbha 170:**

Akiñcana-bhakti is situated in the highest position. To show fixation in a particular qualification, a new section commences.

Rejecting extraneous elements, one should act to create favorability to the Lord. There are three forms of creating favorability: the form of jñāna, resulting in realizing the Lord’s Brahman aspect with no qualities; the form of bhakti, resulting in realizing Bhagavān, with qualities; and the form of offering karmas, a means to achieve the other two forms. These are established as methods of jñāna, bhakti and karma for people of differing qualifications. There are no other methods. *Yogaḥ* means methods. The methods are explained by me, the source of scripture, with the desire that people attain liberation, artha-dharma-kāma, and prema (*śreyaḥ*). Bhakti is distinct from karma.

### Text 7

*nirviṇṇānāṁ jñāna-yogo nyāsinām iha karmasu*  
*teṣv anirviṇṇa-cittānāṁ karma-yogas tu kāminām*

**Перевод:**

***Jñāna-yoga*** **is recommended for those who are unattached to material life and thus reject material activities.** ***Karma-yoga*** **is recommended for those who are not disgusted with material life and have great attachment to pleasures.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 171:**

Two verses explain the causes of qualification. Among the methods (*iha*) jñāna is for people detached from happiness derived from objects in this and the next life (*nirviṇṇām*) and who have renounced actions both worldly and Vedic as sādhana (*nyāsinām*). This indicates persons who have developed desire for liberation by great determination. Jñāna-yoga gives results (*siddhi-daḥ*) for these people with detachment and renunciation. Karma yoga gives results for persons who are attached to happiness in this world and the next and cannot give up actions to attain them (*anirviṇṇa-cetasām*). Results arise according to the determination of the performer.

### Text 8

*yadṛcchayā mat-kathādau jāta-śraddhas tu yaḥ pumān*  
*na nirviṇṇo nāti-sakto bhakti-yogo ’sya siddhi-daḥ*

**Перевод:**

**If by unexpected association with devotees one develops faith in my topics, that person, being neither very disgusted with nor attached to material life, is qualified for** ***bhakti*** **and will achieve perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 171:**

Bhakti does not depend on caste as karma does:

*te vai vidanty atitaranti ca deva-māyāṁ*  
*strī-śūdra-hūṇa-śabarā api pāpa-jīvāḥ \|*  
*yady adbhuta-krama-parāyaṇa-śīla-śikṣās*  
*tiryag-janā api kim u śruta-dhāraṇā ye \|\|*

**Women*****,*** **śūdras, Hūṇas, Śabaras and other sinfully born persons also certainly cross over the material world and know the Lord if they learn about the qualities of the devotees of the Lord. If even animals can understand, how much more quickly can humans, who hear from the mouth of guru, understand the Lord? (SB** [**2.7.46**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/7.46)**)**

Its cause is faith alone. That is explained in verse SB [11.20.8](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.8) above. Faith arises by auspiciousness arising from the completely independent mercy obtained by associating with devotees (*yadṛcchayā).* It is said:

*śuśrūṣoḥ śraddadhānasya vāsudeva-kathā-ruciḥ \|*  
*syān mahat-sevayā viprāḥ puṇya-tīrtha-niṣevaṇāt \|\|*

**O brāhmaṇas! Attraction for topics concerning Kṛṣṇa will arise by service to the great devotees, followed by faith, by surrender to the feet of the guru*****,*** **and by the desire to hear. (SB** [**1.2.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.16)**)**

### Text 9

*tāvat karmāṇi kurvīta na nirvidyeta yāvatā*  
*mat-kathā-śravaṇādau vā śraddhā yāvan na jāyate*

**Перевод:**

**As long as one does not become detached from daily and periodic duties or has not awakened his faith in hearing topics about me, one must perform one’s prescribed duties of** ***varṇāśrama.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 172:**

The commentary says “Karma means nitya and naimittika karmas.”

There is the following objection:

*śruti-smṛtī mamaivājñe yas te ullaṅghya vartate \|*  
*ājñā-cchedī mama dveṣī mad-bhakto ’pi na vaiṣṇavaḥ \|\|*

**Whoever ignores śruti and smṛti, thus disrespecting me, even if he is my devotee, is not a Vaiṣṇava, breaks my order and hates me.**

However, this fault does not apply, since verse (SB [11.20.9](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.9)) above gives a superior order (one can give up karmas after being fixed in bhakti). Rather one will disobey the Lord’s order if one who has developed detachment from karmas and faith in bhakti still performs those karmas.

*ājñāyaivaṁ guṇān doṣān mayādiṣṭān api svakān*  
*dharmān santyajya yaḥ sarvān māṁ bhajeta sa tu sattamaḥ*

**A person who, understanding good and bad aspects of dharma as taught by me gives up all his duties and simply worships me is the best of all. (SB** [**11.11.32**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/11.29)**)**

The commentary says, “He gives up duties because of strong bhakti, by being qualified with detachment. This detachment is indicated by Karabhājana:

*devarṣi-bhūtāpta-nṛṇāṁ pitṝṇāṁ*  
*na kiṅkaro nāyam ṛṇī ca rājan*  
*sarvātmanā yaḥ śaraṇaṁ śaraṇyaṁ*  
*gato mukundaṁ parihṛtya kartam*

**O King! One who has given up all varṇāśrama duties and has taken full shelter of the lotus feet of Mukunda, who offers shelter to all, is not a debtor to or servant of the devatās, great sages, ordinary living beings, relatives or Pitṛs. (SB** [**11.5.41**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/5.41)**)**

The devotee is not a servant of the devatās or sages but a servant of the Lord. Thus he need not perform actions dedicated to them. He gives up duties (*kartam)*. Or he gives up seeing differences: he does not see the devatās as independent. Thus it is said:

*ayaṁ devo munir vandya eṣa brahmā bṛhaspatiḥ \|*  
*ity ākhyā jāyate tāvad yāvan nārcayate harim \|\|*

**As long as one does not worship Viṣṇu one should offer respects to devatās, sages, Brahmā and Bṛhaspati. (Garuḍa Purāṇa 1.235.20)**

It is not necessary for the devotee to perform atonements for sins since the tendency for sin does not exist in the surrendered person. If a sin sometimes occurs, atonement for the sin occurs as a by-product of remembering the Lord. That is mentioned in a later verse:

*sva-pāda-mūlam bhajataḥ priyasya*  
*tyaktānya-bhāvasya hariḥ pareśaḥ*  
*vikarma yac cotpatitaṁ kathañcid*  
*dhunoti sarvaṁ hṛdi sanniviṣṭaḥ*

**One who has thus given up all other engagements and has taken full shelter at the lotus feet of Supreme Lord is very dear to the Lord. If such a surrendered soul accidentally commits some sinful activity, the Supreme Lord, great because of his affection for the devotee, seated within everyone’s heart, immediately takes away the reaction to such sin. (SB** [**11.5.42**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/5.42)**)**

This person has given up devotion to devatās (*tyaktānya-bhāvasya*). Surrender (*śaraṇa* in SB [11.5.41](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/5.41)) means faith since it is described that the person has given up karma. Faith means conviction in the meaning of scriptures. Scripture is a cause of fear for those who have not surrendered and causes no fear in the surrendered. One who has faith surrenders.

### Text 10

*sva-dharma-stho yajan yajñair anāśīḥ-kāma uddhava*  
*na yāti svarga-narakau yady anyan na samācaret*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! A person who is situated in his prescribed duty, properly worshiping by Vedic sacrifices but not desiring the results will not go to the heavenly planets; similarly, by not performing forbidden activities he will not go to hell.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 174:**

Having explained the qualifications for karma, jñāna and bhakti, the Lord then instructs that even karma should lead to worship of the Lord.

Not desiring results is done with the understanding that this is the Lord’s order. In association with jñānīs, one offers the results to the Lord in this way only. In association with devotees, one then strives to satisfy the Lord with the offering.

### Text 11

*asmiḹ loke vartamānaḥ sva-dharma-stho ’naghaḥ śuciḥ*  
*jñānaṁ viśuddham āpnoti mad-bhaktiṁ vā yadṛcchayā*

**Перевод:**

**One who is situated in his prescribed duty, free from sinful activities and cleansed of material contamination, in this very life obtains transcendental knowledge or, by fortune,** ***bhakti*** **to me.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 175:**

By the good fortune of mercy from association of devotees, such a person attains bhakti.

*etāvān eva yajatām iha niḥśreyasodayaḥ \|*  
*bhagavaty acalo bhāvo yad bhāgavata-saṅgataḥ \|\|*

**Auspiciousness arises for the worshippers of devatās if steady devotion for the Lord arises in them from association with devotees. (SB** [**2.3.11**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/3.11)**)**

### Text 12

*svargiṇo ’py etam icchanti lokaṁ nirayiṇas tathā*  
*sādhakaṁ jñāna-bhaktibhyām ubhayaṁ tad-asādhakam*

**Перевод:**

**The residents of both heaven and hell desire human birth on the earth planet because human life facilitates** ***jñāna*** **and** ***bhakti,*** **whereas neither heavenly nor hellish bodies provide such opportunities.**

**Комментарий:**

Heaven and hell are not suitable for practicing jñāna or bhakti. One becomes absorbed in great enjoyment in Svarga. One becomes absorbed in great suffering in hell. It is not that jñāna and bhakti are forbidden in those places, though they disqualify one from karmas, for it is said:

*tad upary api bādarāyaṇaḥ sambhavāt*

**It should be accepted that devatās, who are above men also worship Brahman, because they have bodies. That is Lord Bādarāyaṇa's opinion. (Brahma-sūtra** [**1.3.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/1.3.26)**)**

### Text 13

*na naraḥ svar-gatiṁ kāṅkṣen nārakīṁ vā vicakṣaṇaḥ*  
*nemaṁ lokaṁ ca kāṅkṣeta dehāveśāt pramādyati*

**Перевод:**

**A human being who is wise should never desire Svarga or hell. A human being should also never desire continuous residence on the earth, for by such absorption in the material body one forgets about** ***jñāna*** **and** ***bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

Therefore, attaining a human body, one should practice jñāna and bhakti and not desire Svarga

### Text 14

*etad vidvān purā mṛtyor abhavāya ghaṭeta saḥ*  
*apramatta idaṁ jñātvā martyam apy artha-siddhi-dam*

**Перевод:**

**A wise person, knowing that the human body, though temporary, allows attainment of perfection, should act for freedom from** ***saṁsāra*** **before death. Knowing the use of the human body, he should not be lazy.**

**Комментарий:**

He describes the path of vijñāna along with vairagya.

### Text 23

*nirviṇṇasya viraktasya puruṣasyokta-vedinaḥ*  
*manas tyajati daurātmyaṁ cintitasyānucintayā*

**Перевод:**

**When a person, considering what has been said, becomes disgusted with the world and detached from it by contemplating the subject with analysis, his mind gives up its false identity with the body.**

**Комментарий:**

Afraid of saṁsāra *(nirvinnasya*) he becomes detached from it (*viraktasya*).

### Text 24

*yamādibhir yoga-pathair ānvīkṣikyā ca vidyayā*  
*mamārcopāsanābhir vā nānyair yogyaṁ smaren manaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**One should remember Paramātmā by following** ***yamas*** **and** ***niyamas*** **in** ***aśṭāṅga-yoga,*** **by analysis of elements, by knowledge, by worship of my form, and not by any other methods.**

**Комментарий:**

He should have no interest in other methods and goals (*na anyaiḥ*).

### Text 25

*yadi kuryāt pramādena yogī karma vigarhitam*  
*yogenaiva dahed aṁho nānyat tatra kadācana*

**Перевод:**

**If, because of inattention, a** ***jñānī*** **accidentally commits an abominable activity, then by the very practice of** ***jñāna,*** **he should burn to ashes the sinful reaction, without employing any other procedure.**

**Комментарий:**

The person qualified for jñāna is not touched by faults or good qualities arising from karma. He practices yoga as an aṅga of jñāna (*yogena*).

### Text 26

*sve sve ’dhikāre yā niṣṭhā sa guṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ*  
*karmaṇāṁ jāty-aśuddhānām anena niyamaḥ kṛtaḥ*  
*guṇa-doṣa-vidhānena saṅgānāṁ tyājanecchayā*

**Перевод:**

**When the** ***jñānī*** **or devotee remains situated according to his qualification it is praised as a good quality. With a desire to give up material enjoyment, there should be restriction on** ***karma*** **for the practitioners of** ***karma*** **who are innately impure, by following the rules and avoiding the prohibitions.**

**Комментарий:**

Thus offering karmas, practicing pure jñāna and pure bhakti are established according to qualification. One should be situated according to one’s qualification. Doing the opposite is a fault.

By the orders and prohibitions mentioned in the karma scriptures the person who is impure should restrict the karmas. Or “How is yoga possible? Or how will material identity be destroyed by karmas?”

He shows that since karmas are by their nature faulty, among them, some may be considered auspicious by quality and indicates that yoga by its nature has good qualities.

There is some very bitter nīma. In the world there are some species which are sweet and some which are bitter. Similarly, some karmas are by their nature impure. By applying rules and prohibitions on these karmas they are restricted, with a desire that the attachments are given up. It is impossible for them to suddenly give up those actions, so it is done gradually.

### Text 27-28

*jāta-śraddho mat-kathāsu nirviṇṇaḥ sarva-karmasu*  
*veda duḥkhātmakān kāmān parityāge ’py anīśvaraḥ*  
*tato bhajeta māṁ prītaḥ śraddhālur dṛḍha-niścayaḥ*  
*juṣamāṇaś ca tān kāmān duḥkhodarkāṁś ca garhayan*

**Перевод:**

**Having developed faith in topics about me and being disgusted with all** ***karmas*****, a devotee knows that all enjoyments are filled with misery. But he is unable to given them up. Still, with affection for me, with faith and determination, he will continue worshipping me, while at the same time partaking of those enjoyments which give rise to suffering and yet condemning them.**

**Комментарий:**

The path of bhakti is described in two verses. One should develop faith (*jāta-śraddhaḥ*) in my topics and other things related to me. One should fear other activities. However, he may not be able to give the results of old pious acts.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 172:**

Thus verse 11.20.28 explains that he is not completely detached and not too attached. This is the devotee’s initial state. This person should worship me (*bhajeta mām*). This means he is qualified for ananya-bhakti, whereas another person is qualified for jñāna by developing vairāgya. This is because bhakti is independent of other factors since it has its own śakti. This is explained later:

*tasmān mad-bhakti-yuktasya yogino vai mad-ātmanaḥ*  
*na jñānaṁ na ca vairāgya prāyaḥ śreyo bhaved iha*

**Therefore, for a devotee engaged in bhakti, with mind fixed on me, the cultivation of knowledge and renunciation is generally not beneficial for bhakti.**

*yat karmabhir yat tapasā jñāna-vairāgyataś ca yat*  
*yogena dāna-dharmeṇa śreyobhir itarair api*  
*sarvaṁ mad-bhakti-yogena mad-bhakto labhate ’ñjasā*  
*svargāpavargaṁ mad-dhāma kathañcid yadi vāñchati*

**Everything that can be achieved by karma, penance, jñāna, vairāgya, mystic yoga, charity, dharma and all other auspicious means of perfecting life is easily achieved by my devotee through bhakti. If somehow or other my devotee desires Svarga, liberation, or residence in my abode, he easily achieves such benedictions. (SB** [**11.20.32-33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.32)**)**

This means that bhakti does not even depend on indifference to karma. That indifference will arise by faith that bhakti is the supreme method. Thus the qualification of being disgusted with karma merely explains this phenomenon. Though jñāna and karma also depend on faith, since, without that, internal and external inclination will not arise, in the case of bhakti faith alone is the cause. That is its specialty. Depending on strong faith, one is able to perform bhakti. Without faith, ananya-bhakti will not occur. And sometimes, if one has slight faith, bhakti can destroyed. One should not be too detached or too attached, but later, when faith in the Lord’s topics develops, karma will be given up.

However, even without faith, bhakti takes place.

*sakṛd api parigītaṁ śraddhayā helayā vā*  
*bhṛguvara nara-mātraṁ tārayet kṛṣṇa-nāma*

**The name of Kṛṣṇa chanted with faith or without faith delivers all men. (Skanda Purāṇa)**

*satāṁ prasaṅgān mama vīrya-saṁvido*  
*bhavanti hṛt-karṇa-rasāyanāḥ kathāḥ*  
*taj-joṣaṇād āśv apavarga-vartmani*  
*śraddhā ratir bhaktir anukramiṣyati*

**From association with the best devotees, topics of my glorious pastimes become directly realized, bringing the devotee to niṣṭhā. Then the topics become an elixir for the heart and ears at the stage of ruci. By taste for these topics, faith (arising after hearing), bhāva and then prema for the Lord who is the destroyer of material life, quickly develop in sequence. (SB** [**3.25.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/25.25)**)**

One sees results even before faith:

*mriyamāṇo harer nāma gṛṇan putropacāritam*  
*ajāmilo ’py agād dhāma kim uta śraddhayā gṛṇan*

**Ajāmila, by chanting the name of the Lord to indicate his son while dying, attained the abode of the Lord. What then will happen if one chants with faith? (SB** [**6.2.49**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/2.49)**)**

Faith is an aṅga of understanding the abhidheya presented in scriptures, since it is a form of conviction. Thus it is not included as an aṅga of the performance of bhakti.

“Bhakti should not depend on rules to produce its results, just as fire does not depend on rules to burn wood since hearing and chanting about the Lord possess the śakti of the Lord’s svarūpa. Why should bhakti depend on faith or anything else? And even without faith, sometimes one sees attainment of perfection in fools. It was said that chanting with or without faith (*śraddhayā helayā vā*) delivers humans. If one acts without thinking intentionally of offense (*helayā*), and without wicked intention, it does not interfere with bhakti. But if one intentionally commits offense, this will interfere with bhakti. For instance, Vena chanted the name of the Lord with envy and bhakti did not appear. In the same way, sometimes the śakti of a substance is impeded. For instance the fire’s power to burn is impeded by damp wood.

*śraddhayopāhṛtaṁ preṣṭhaṁ bhaktena mama vāry api*

**Actually, whatever is offered to me with faith by my devotee—even if only a little water—is most dear to me.**

*bhūry apy abhaktopāhṛtaṁ na me toṣāya kalpate*  
*gandho dhūpaḥ sumanaso dīpo ’nnādyaṁ ca kiṁ punaḥ*

**Even excellent presentations do not satisfy me if they are offered by nondevotees. But I am pleased by any insignificant offering made by devotees, what to speak of presentations of fragrant oil, incense, flowers and palatable foods. (SB** [**11.27.17-18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/27.16)**)**

In the above verses faith and *bhakti* refer to respect (*bhakti* does not refer to the activities of bhakti). This respect destroys the offense which produces disrespect, in order to produce excellent results in the form of the Lord’s satisfaction.

Therefore faith is not an aṅga of bhakti, but is a special qualification for ananya-bhakti, just as knowledge, desire and ability are qualifications for karma. This special qualification is what is designated in *mat-kathādau* *jāta-śraddhas tu* (SB 11.20.8) and *jāta-śraddho mat-kathāsu* (SB [11.20.27](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.27)).

*Tato bhajeta māṁ* (SB [11.20.28](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.27)) means “after developing faith, one should worship me. *Tataḥ* also indicates “no limits to who can perform it.” Thus even ātmārāmas start bhakti. This indicates bhakti’s ruling position. Thus it is said later:

*na kiñcit sādhavo dhīrā bhaktā hy ekāntino mama*  
*vāñchanty api mayā dattaṁ kaivalyam apunar-bhavam*

**But intelligent devotees, completely dedicated to me alone, do not desire liberation even if I offer it to them. (SB** [**11.20.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.34)**)**

To announce bhakti’s ruling position, it is said that karma and jñāna cannot produce results without bhakti.

Having stated that the cause of qualification for ananya-bhakti is faith alone, that person worships the Lord (SB [11.20.28](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.27)) and practices with faith (*śraddhāluḥ)*. He develops āsakti (*dhṛḍha-niṣcayaḥ*) after ruci (*prītaḥ*). He is devoid of interruption in concentrated practice. Because he cannot suddenly give up the desires, he despises them for later they will produce lamentation. In this verse *kāmān* means enjoyments which are not sinful, for in scriptures it is stated that one should not engage in such sinful acts.

### Text 29

*proktena bhakti-yogena bhajato māsakṛn muneḥ*  
*kāmā hṛdayyā naśyanti sarve mayi hṛdi sthite*

**Перевод:**

**When an intelligent person engages constantly in worshiping me through** ***bhakti*****, as previously described by me, all material desires within the heart are destroyed, since I am situated in the heart.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhakti burns up all desires.

### Text 30

*bhidyate hṛdaya-granthiś chidyante sarva-saṁśayāḥ*  
*kṣīyante cāsya karmāṇi mayi dṛṣṭe ’khilātmani*

**Перевод:**

***Ahaṅkāra*** **is cut and all doubts are destroyed. On seeing the Lord in the mind and with the eyes, all** ***karmas*** **are destroyed.**

**Комментарий:**

All doubts including the idea that it is impossible to see the Lord, are ended and karmas are destroyed.

*tad adhigama uttara-pūrvārdhayor aśleṣa-vināśau tad-vyapadeśāt*

**With attainment of Brahman, present sinful actions have no effect and previous karmas are destroyed since that is indicated the śruti. (Brahma-sūtra** [**4.1.13**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/4.1.13)**)**

### Text 31

*tasmān mad-bhakti-yuktasya yogino vai mad-ātmanaḥ*  
*na jñānaṁ na ca vairāgya prāyaḥ śreyo bhaved iha*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, for a devotee engaged in** ***bhakti,*** **with mind fixed on me, the cultivation of knowledge and renunciation is not beneficial for** ***bhakti.***

**Комментарий:**

For persons qualified for bhakti, involvement in karma and jñāna is not approved. Thus that person is not touched by the faults and good qualities that produce them.

Since bhakti destroys ahaṅkāra, the practices of jñāna and vairagya are not beneficial for the devotee, what to speak of karma-yoga, since they create obstacles to bhakti and require great but useless endeavor. The repetition of the negative *na* indicate that these should not be done at all. *Prāyaḥ* indicates deliberation. The practices are not accepted as a rule. For the devotees, there is no use in practicing jñāna and vairagya. Though this is so, just as on the path of direct liberation, some have a tendency for gradual liberation, some devotees may have a tendency for gradual progress in bhakti. This is described in *brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā* (BG [18.54](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.54)). Bhakti has no dependence on jñāna or other processes since in bhakti, prema, the king of results, produces itself.

### Text 32-33

*yat karmabhir yat tapasā jñāna-vairāgyataś ca yat*  
*yogena dāna-dharmeṇa śreyobhir itarair api*  
*sarvaṁ mad-bhakti-yogena mad-bhakto labhate ’ñjasā*  
*svargāpavargaṁ mad-dhāma kathañcid yadi vāñchati*

**Перевод:**

**Everything that can be achieved by** ***karma,*** **penance,** ***jñāna, vairāgya,*** **mystic** ***yoga,*** **charity,** ***dharma*** **and all other auspicious means of perfecting life is easily achieved by my devotee through** ***bhakti.*** **If somehow or other my devotee desires Svarga, liberation, or residence in my abode, he easily achieves such benedictions.**

**Комментарий:**

For that purpose he shows the power of bhakti particularly in two verses.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 84:**

Material happiness and the happiness of liberation are attainable by purifying one’s existence. Happiness surpassing that is available also: Vaikuṇṭha.

All this is available if, somehow or other, some devotee desires it for assisting bhakti. For instance Citraketu desired Svarga and there chanted the glories of the Lord. (SB [6.17.3](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/17.2)) Śukadeva desired liberation. He came out the womb of his mother by his request when Kṛṣṇa prevented māyā from attacking him extending over time which became like a mustard seed on the horn of a cow. This is narrated in Brahma-vaivarta Purāṇa. Liberation can assist bhakti, for Gītā says *brahma-bhūtaḥ prasannātmā:* coming to the level of Brahman one then attains bhakti. One who desires Vaikuṇṭha enters a desired group of the Lord’s attendants. By prema one attains one’s desired situation by the desire to serve directly the lotus feet of the Lord.

*yac ca vrajanty animiṣām ṛṣabhānuvṛttyā*  
*dūre yamā hy upari naḥ spṛhaṇīya-śīlāḥ*  
*bhartur mithaḥ suyaśasaḥ kathanānurāga-*  
*vaiklavya-bāṣpa-kalayā pulakī-kṛtāṅgāḥ*

**Those who are far above rules by following the Lord with dedication, who are superior to us, and whose qualities are desirable by us, and whose bodies develop goose bumps and uncontrolled tears through attraction to discussions of the Lord possessing most excellent qualities, enter Vaikuṇṭha. (SB** [**3.15.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.25)**)**

### Text 35

*nairapekṣyaṁ paraṁ prāhur niḥśreyasam analpakam*  
*tasmān nirāśiṣo bhaktir nirapekṣasya me bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**It is said that the process which is without dependence on other processes or results is the best process. Therefore a person who does not desire any rewards and does not depend on other processes develops** ***bhakti*** **for me.**

**Комментарий:**

The practice of such bhakti is similar.

### Text 36

*na mayy ekānta-bhaktānāṁ guṇa-doṣodbhavā guṇāḥ*  
*sādhūnāṁ sama-cittānāṁ buddheḥ param upeyuṣām*

**Перевод:**

**The fully dedicated devotees who see equally everywhere and who have achieved the Lord who is superior to** ***prakṛti*** **do not have the** ***guṇas*** **which give rise to qualities of good and bad.**

**Комментарий:**

As the person doing niṣkāma-karma is not contaminated by the faults of sakāma-karma, the person qualified for jñāna is not contaminated by the faults of karma. The person qualified for bhakti is not contaminated by the faults of either type of karma or of jñāna. The person having perfection in bhakti is not contaminated by the faults of the previous four persons. The final result is shown in this verse, as was expressed previously:

*eta uddhava te praśnāḥ sarve sādhu nirūpitāḥ*  
*kiṁ varṇitena bahunā lakṣaṇaṁ guṇa-doṣayoḥ*  
*guṇa-doṣa-dṛśir doṣo guṇas tūbhaya-varjitaḥ*

**Thus, Uddhava, I have elucidated all of the matters of inquiry, which are suitable for liberation. There is no need for a more elaborate description of good and bad qualities, since to see good and bad is itself a bad quality. The best quality is to transcend seeing good and bad qualities. (SB** [**11.19.45**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/19.40)**)**

## Chapter Twenty-one

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ya etān mat-patho hitvā bhakti-jñāna-kriyātmakān*  
*kṣudrān kāmāṁś calaiḥ prāṇair juṣantaḥ saṁsaranti te*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Those who give up my methods,** ***bhakti, jñāna*** **and** ***niṣkāma-karma*****, and instead cultivate insignificant sense enjoyment by the fickle senses, undergo the continual cycle of material existence.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī speaks of those who practice niṣkāma-karma. They have not developed a sense of vairagya. Those who purified, fixed in jñāna, have developed vairagya. Those are fixed in bhakti have developed faith. Those who have not come to jñāna or bhakti, and not even to niṣkāma-karma cultivate sense enjoyment (*kṣudrāṇ kāmān*) and have all faults. The desires produced by performance karma, jñāna and bhakti may be material or spiritual.

### Text 2

*sve sve ’dhikāre yā niṣṭhā sa guṇaḥ parikīrtitaḥ*  
*viparyayas tu doṣaḥ syād ubhayor eṣa niścayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Being fixed according to one’s qualification is a good quality, and to be fixed in something not of one’s qualification is a bad quality. This is how good and bad are determined.**

**Комментарий:**

Good and bad qualities are described considering the previous adhikāras in order to determine good and bad objects in the world. Being fixed sakāma-karma, niṣkāma karma, vairagya and śraddhā, and performing the various yogas, is a good quality. Not being fixed is a bad quality. This determines good and bad for both materially and spiritually qualified persons (*ubhayoḥ*). However the material sādhanas produce good qualities whereas the spiritual sādhana (bhakti) produces perfection. Jñāna and karma must have a mixture of bhakti to attain results. Without a portion of bhakti in jñāna and karma, the goal of those sādhanas cannot be achieved. Without bhakti, they fail. Since bhakti does not depend on them and produces all results, if one mixes bhakti with karma or jñāna, bhakti fails.

Thus being fixed in karma with the help of a little bhakti is a good quality for the person qualified for karma. Similarly being fixed in jñāna with a little bhakti is a good quality for the jñānī. But being fixed in pure bhakti, what to speak of bhakti in the form of prema, is the best quality for the devotee. Doing less or more than one’s qualification is a fault. Doing less destroys the lesser method, and doing more than one’s qualification disqualifies one from attaining its goal.

### Text 3

*śuddhy-aśuddhī vidhīyete samāneṣv api vastuṣu*  
*dravyasya vicikitsārthaṁ guṇa-doṣau śubhāśubhau*  
*dharmārthaṁ vyavahārārthaṁ yātrārtham iti cānagha*

**Перевод:**

**O sinless Uddhava! In order to resolve doubts about certain objects among general objects, they are established as good or bad, pure or impure, auspicious or inauspicious. In terms of** ***dharma*** **they are classed as pure or impure. In terms of common dealings they are classed as good and bad, and in terms of circumstance they are classed as auspicious or inauspicious.**

**Комментарий:**

According to different qualifications, good and bad are described in different circumstances. Objects are classes as pure or impure, auspicious or inauspicious, good and bad, according to scripture, to resolve doubts about them. Using objects without consideration of good or bad, but considering pure or impure should still be considered in terms of good or bad. Using objects with consideration of good and bad in terms of function, without considering use for dharma and auspicious or inauspicious, should be considered good or bad. Thus the statements about good and bad are only general. The list of purposes should not be exactly correlated with pure and impure, good and bad and auspicious and inauspicious, as this is not seen anywhere. The usage is according to the spiritual scriptures, not other works.

Dharma and yatra (physical subsistence) involve different motives.

### Text 4

*darśito ’yaṁ mayācāro dharmam udvahatāṁ dhuram*

**Перевод:**

**I have revealed this way of life for persons bearing the burden of** ***dharma.***

**Комментарий:**

The conduct, filled with happiness, is shown by me for persons bearing the weight of bhakti (*dharmam*) since it has arisen by bhakti, the svarūpa of happiness.

### Text 6

*vedena nāma-rūpāṇi viṣamāṇi sameṣv api*  
*dhātuṣūddhava kalpyanta eteṣāṁ svārtha-siddhaye*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Although all material bodies are composed of the same five elements and are thus equal, the Vedic literatures conceive of different names and forms in relation to such bodies so that the living entities may achieve their goal of life.**

**Комментарий:**

Though the same material elements are used, since all beings are constituted of ātmā without guṇas and matter filled with fault, different names such as Brahmā and different forms (higher or lower) are employed to indicate good or bad, like classifying nīma according to taste. The purpose is for jīvas to achieve dharma etc. according to their tendency or qualification. The form limits the tendency. Gradually the jīvas proceed to liberation.

### Text 7

*deśa-kālādi-bhāvānāṁ vastūnāṁ mama sattama*  
*guṇa-doṣau vidhīyete niyamārthaṁ hi karmaṇām*

**Перевод:**

**O saintly Uddhava! In order to restrict materialistic activities, I have established that which is proper and improper among all material things, space and time.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to restrict (*niyamārtham*) activities I have established proper and improper. This is concerning objects not related to the Lord.

*vanaṁ tu sāttviko vāso grāmo rājasa ucyate*  
*tāmasaṁ dyūta-sadanaṁ man-niketaṁ tu nirguṇam*

**Residence in the forest is in the mode of goodness, residence in a town is in the mode of passion, residence in a gambling house displays the quality of ignorance, and residence in a place where I reside is beyond the** ***guṇas.*** **(SB** [**11.25.25**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/25.25)**)**

*sāttvikaḥ kārako ’saṅgī rāgāndho rājasaḥ smṛtaḥ*  
*tāmasaḥ smṛti-vibhraṣṭo nirguṇo mad-apāśrayaḥ*

**A performer of action who is free of attachment is in** ***sattva*****, a performer of action overcome by sense objects is in** ***rajas*****, and a performer of action who is devoid of inquiry is in** ***tamas.*** **But a performer of action who has taken shelter of me alone is beyond the** ***guṇas.*** **(SB** [**11.25.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/25.26)**)**

The shelter of the guṇas is beyond the guṇas. In the form of the Vedas I (*mama*) have established the classification.

### Text 8

*akṛṣṇa-sāro deśānām abrahmaṇyo ’sucir bhavet*  
*kṛṣṇa-sāro ’py asauvīra- kīkaṭāsaṁskṛteriṇam*

**Перевод:**

**Among places, those bereft of the spotted antelope, those devoid of devotion to the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **those possessing spotted antelopes but having unrespectable Kīkaṭas, unclean people or deserts are all considered to be impure.**

**Комментарий:**

Though a place has the spotted antelope but is devoid of qualified persons who respect brāhmaṇas, it is impure even if devoid of Kīkaṭas. If a place has qualified persons who respect brāhmaṇas, it is pure, even if there are Kīkaṭas.

### Text 10

*dravyasya śuddhy-aśuddhī ca dravyeṇa vacanena ca*  
*saṁskāreṇātha kālena mahatvālpatayātha vā*

**Перевод:**

**An object’s purity or impurity is established by application of another object, by words, by cleaning, by the effects of time or according to size.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses describe purity.

### Text 14

*snāna-dāna-tapo-’vasthā- vīrya-saṁskāra-karmabhiḥ*  
*mat-smṛtyā cātmanaḥ śaucaṁ śuddhaḥ karmācared dvijaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The self can be cleansed by bathing, charity, austerity, age, conduct, purification rituals, prescribed duties and, above all, by remembrance of me. The** ***brāhmaṇa*** **and others should be duly purified before performing their specific activities.**

**Комментарий:**

Remembrance of the Lord is an independent item.

*apavitraḥ pavitro vā sarvāvasthāṁ gato ’pi vā*  
*yaḥ smaret puṇḍarīkākṣaṁ sa bāhyābhyantara-śuciḥ*

**Whether one is pure or contaminated, and regardless of one’s external situation, simply, by remembering the lo­tus-eyed Personality of Godhead, one can cleanse one’s in­ter­nal and external existence. (Garuḍa Purāṇa)**

This is mentioned, not for general usage, but for purification of impure items without remedy, since remembrance of the Lord has unfailing effects in all circumstances.

*harir harati pāpāni duṣṭa-cittair api smṛtaḥ*

**The Lord destroys sins. Even sinful persons should remember him.**

### Text 19

*viṣayeṣu guṇādhyāsāt puṁsaḥ saṅgas tato bhavet*  
*saṅgāt tatra bhavet kāmaḥ kāmād eva kalir nṛṇām*

**Перевод:**

**One becomes attached because of thinking that an object is good. From that attachment desire arises. From desire quarrel arises.**

**Комментарий:**

Four verses trace the decline of a person with attachments.

### Text 23

*phala-śrutir iyaṁ nṝṇāṁ na śreyo rocanaṁ param*  
*śreyo-vivakṣayā proktaṁ yathā bhaiṣajya-rocanam*

**Перевод:**

**The rewards promised in scripture are not the ultimate benefit for man but are merely enticements for liberating him. They are like promises of candy spoken to induce a child to take beneficial medicine.**

**Комментарий:**

Having criticized persons desiring material objects, he criticizes persons desiring spiritual goals. The person gives the child medicine by promising something else.

### Text 27

*kāminaḥ kṛpaṇā lubdhāḥ puṣpeṣu phala-buddhayaḥ*  
*agni-mugdhā dhūma-tāntāḥ svaṁ lokaṁ na vidanti te*

**Перевод:**

**Those who are full of lust, avarice and greed mistake mere flowers to be the actual fruits. Bewildered by the glare of fire and fatigued by its smoke, they cannot recognize the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

*Svam lokam* means “one’s shelter.”

### Text 28

*na te mām aṅga jānanti hṛdi-sthaṁ ya idaṁ yataḥ*  
*uktha-śastrā hy asu-tṛpo yathā nīhāra-cakṣuṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Persons dedicated to sense gratification obtained through honoring the Vedic rituals cannot understand that I am situated in everyone’s heart and that the entire universe is non-different from me and emanates from me, because their eyes are covered by ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

They cannot understand me because I am beyond the guṇas.

### Text 35

*vedā brahmātma-viṣayās tri-kāṇḍa-viṣayā ime*  
*parokṣa-vādā ṛṣayaḥ parokṣaṁ mama ca priyam*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas, divided into three divisions, ultimately reveal worship of the Lord. The Vedic sages and** ***mantras*****, however, state this indirectly, since they know that I prefer this indirect method.**

**Комментарий:**

The Vedas deal with the ātmā, the highest root, Bhagavān, who is the greatest (*brahma*). The cause of being invisible to all is given. Indirect expression is dear to me.

*eṣa mohaṁ sṛjamy āśu yo janān mohayiṣyati*  
*tvaṁ ca rudra mahā-bāho moha-śastrāṇi kāraya*  
*atathyāni vitathyāni darśayasva mahā-bhuja*  
*prakāśaṁ kuru cātmānaṁ aprakāśaṁ ca māṁ kuru*

**I will quickly create illusion. O Śiva, you who will bewilder people! Make deceptive scriptures. Reveal what is false. Reveal yourself and hide me. (Varāha Purāṇa)**

He reveals his intention in the following:

*iti te jñānam ākhyātaṁ guhyād guhyataraṁ mayā*  
*vimṛśyaitad aśeṣeṇa yathecchasi tathā kuru \|\|*

**Thus I have explained a scripture more secret than the secret knowledge of** ***mantras*****. Considering this thoroughly, do as you wish to do. (BG** [**18.63**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.63)**)**

He is hidden because all are not qualified:

*rājan patir gurur alaṁ bhavatāṁ yadūnāṁ*  
*daivaṁ priyaḥ kula-patiḥ kva ca kiṅkaro vaḥ*  
*astv evam aṅga bhagavān bhajatāṁ mukundo*  
*muktiṁ dadāti karhicit sma na bhakti-yogam*

**O King Parīkṣit! Kṛṣṇa was the protector, instructor, object of worship, giver of affection, and the protector of both the Yadus and Pāṇḍavas. But he was sometimes your servant. The Lord gives liberation but not even** ***bhāva-bhakti*** **to those who worship him. (SB** [**5.6.28**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-5/16.28)**)**

### Text 36

*śabda-brahma su-durbodhaṁ prāṇendriya-mano-mayam*  
*ananta-pāraṁ gambhīraṁ durvigāhyaṁ samudra-vat*

**Перевод:**

**The sound of the Vedas is very difficult to comprehend and manifests on different levels within the** ***prāṇa*****, mind, intelligence and senses. This Vedic sound is unlimited and deep like the ocean.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together.
