The Vedas are difficult comprehend though they are made of prāṇa, mind and senses, and appear in the form of para, paśyantī and vaikharī, in those places, appearing like material sound, since they are unlimited as sound, deep in terms of meaning, and impossible to enter (*durvigāhyam*). Even if one enters, the meaning cannot be understood.

### Text 37

*mayopabṛṁhitaṁ bhūmnā brahmaṇānanta-śaktinā*  
*bhūteṣu ghoṣa-rūpeṇa biseṣūrṇeva lakṣyate*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas, which are spread everywhere by me because I possess many forms, pervade everywhere as Brahman, and possess unlimited powers, are perceived by the wise as** ***nāda*** **within all beings, subtle as the threads within the lotus stalk.**

**Комментарий:**

If the Vedas have this nature, why do they appear in prāṇa?” They manifest in these places by me (*mayopabṛṁhitam*). “The Vedas manifest in unlimited Vaikuṇṭha and in unlimited universes. How does it manifest from you alone?” I possess unlimited śakti as the greatest being by nature (*brahma*). “That which is spread everywhere should be unlimited. The Vedas do not appear to be unlimited.” They are seen by yogīs as nāda, the invisible form of praṇava. It is said:

*amātro’nanta-mātraś ca dvaitasyopaśamaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*oṅkāro vidito yena sa munir netaro janaḥ \|\|*

**It is without measure and of infinite measure. It is all auspicious, destroying duality. He who knows oṁ is the only wise man. (Māṇḍukya-kārikā 26-29)**

### Text 38-40

*yathorṇanābhir hṛdayād ūrṇām udvamate mukhāt*  
*ākāśād ghoṣavān prāṇo manasā sparśa-rūpiṇā*  
*chando-mayo ’mṛta-mayaḥ sahasra-padavīṁ prabhuḥ*  
*oṁkārād vyañjita-sparśa- svaroṣmāntastha-bhūṣitām*  
*vicitra-bhāṣā-vitatāṁ chandobhiś catur-uttaraiḥ*  
*ananta-pārāṁ bṛhatīṁ sṛjaty ākṣipate svayam*

**Перевод:**

**Just as a spider brings forth from its heart its web and emits it through its mouth, the Supreme Lord, made of supreme bliss, made of the Vedas, taking support of ether, appears as** ***parā*** **sound made of** ***prāṇa,*** **and then creates and destroys, by his mind which produces the consonants and the manifest Vedas--which are unlimited in sound and meaning, decorated with the consonants, vowels, sibilants, and semivowels emanating from the subtle sound** ***oṁ,*** **and which are filled with a variety of languages and meters, each four syllables longer than the previous.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe how the Vedas arise from the Lord in the form of para, paśyantī etc. through prāṇa, the mind and senses. The spider from its heart, through the mouth, emits its web. Similarly the Lord (*prabhuḥ*), my aṁśa Paramātmā, the very form of the highest bliss *(amṛta-mayaḥ*), by his śakti becoming the Vedas (*chando-mayaḥ*) endowed with all knowledge, taking support of ether, manifests in the ādhāra-cakra of Hiraṇyagarbha and creates para sound (*bṛhatīm*). Then becoming slightly audible as nāda (*ghoṣavān*) it manifests as madhyama sound in the navel cakra. Then becoming filled with prāṇa in the heart, from oṁ it attains the form of nāda and manifests as paśyantī by the mind with a touch of awareness of different syllables (*sparśa-rūpiṇā*). Then it manifests as vaikharī, the Vedas with branches (*sahasra-padavīm*), with manifested consonants, vowels, silibants and semivowels a well as meters (*chandobhiḥ*).

At another time, the Vedas enter the Lord. The mātrās are short, long and extended, the svaras are low, middle and high pitch. There are forty-nine consonants with *kṣa* being included in the consonants and sibilants.

### Text 42

*kiṁ vidhatte kim ācaṣṭe kim anūdya vikalpayet*  
*ity asyā hṛdayaṁ loke nānyo mad veda kaścana*

**Перевод:**

**What do the Vedas instruct as action? What is the final meaning of the Vedas? What alternatives do the Vedas raise? No one except me or my dear devotee knows the intended meaning of the Vedas.**

**Комментарий:**

I know the meaning of the Vedas which arise from me.

### Text 43

*māṁ vidhatte ’bhidhatte māṁ vikalpyāpohyate tv aham*  
*etāvān sarva-vedārthaḥ śabda āsthāya māṁ bhidām*  
*māyā-mātram anūdyānte pratiṣidhya prasīdati*

**Перевод:**

**The Vedas indicate** ***bhakti*** **as the action and indicate me as the meaning. I am the meaning of all the Vedas. I, as** ***karma*** **and** ***jñāna,*** **am proposed and rejected as alternatives. The Vedas, taking shelter of me, proposing** ***karma*** **and** ***jñāna*** **and then rejecting them as** ***māyā*****, become happy by giving the devotees bliss.**

**Комментарий:**

I the form of Kṛṣṇa am the ultimate meaning of the Vedas. That is shown in the last two lines.

The Vedas and whatever follows the Vedas (*śabdaḥ*) rejecting the world (*māyā-mātram*), describing my various avatāras (*bhidām*), taking support (*āsthāya*) of me (*mām*) in the form of Kṛṣṇa, become successful (*prasīdati*). Gītā says:

*vedaiś ca sarvair aham eva vedyo vedānta-kṛd veda-vid eva cāham \|\|*

**And I am also known by all the** ***Vedas*****. I have made the** ***Vedānta*** **to explain myself, since I alone know the** ***Vedas.*** **(BG** [**15.15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/15.15)**)**

## Chapter Twenty-two

### Text 1-3

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*kati tattvāni viśveśa saṅkhyātāny ṛṣibhiḥ prabho*  
*navaikādaśa pañca trīṇy āttha tvam iha śuśruma*  
*kecit ṣaḍ-viṁśatiṁ prāhur apare pañca-viṁśatiṁ*  
*saptaike nava ṣaṭ kecic catvāry ekādaśāpare*  
*kecit saptadaśa prāhuḥ ṣoḍaśaike trayodaśa*  
*etāvattvaṁ hi saṅkhyānām ṛṣayo yad-vivakṣayā*  
*gāyanti pṛthag āyuṣmann idaṁ no vaktum arhasi*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava inquired: O Lord! O master of the universe! How many different elements of creation have been enumerated by the great sages? I have heard you personally describe a total of twenty-eight—God, the** ***jīva*****, the** ***mahat-tattva*****, false ego, the five gross elements, the ten senses, the mind, the five subtle objects of perception and the three modes of nature. But some authorities say that there are twenty-six elements, while others cite twenty-five or else seven, nine, six, four or eleven, and even others say that there are seventeen, sixteen or thirteen elements. What did the sages have in mind when they calculated the creative elements in such different ways? O Lord with an eternal form! Kindly explain this to me.**

**Комментарий:**

Having made the goal of karma-kāṇḍa secondary, and determined that jñāna-kāṇḍa up to Svayam Bhagavān is the primary topic, Uddhava inquires in order to discard different arguments in the meaning of jñāna-kāṇḍa. Śrīdhara Svāmī says that people disagree in defining what are the real existing objects as effects. *Āyuṣman* (O ever living person!) indicates that the Lord eternally possesses life.

### Text 4

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*yuktaṁ ca santi sarvatra bhāṣante brāhmaṇā yathā*  
*māyāṁ madīyām udgṛhya vadatāṁ kiṁ nu durghaṭam*

**Перевод:**

**Lord Kṛṣṇa replied: Because all material elements are present in the other elements, all these sages speak the truth. It is not difficult for disputants who have taken shelter of my** ***māyā*** **to argue.**

**Комментарий:**

Presenting his own view through all the other views, he praises them all. The brāhmaṇas, those who know the Vedas, speak the truth (*yuktam*). “If everyone is correct, why do they reject other opinions and establish their individual opinions.”

They take shelter of my māyā. Since the place for illusion of water in the desert is not limited, there can be any number of illusions. Thus, one can establish twenty-eight elements. Māyā is not the acintya-śakti, but rather ignorance which manifests falsity. Since they take shelter of māyā, and māyā takes shelter of me alone, it is proper that they should know it belongs to me alone. They have some truth but my truth manifests everything.

### Text 5

*naitad evaṁ yathāttha tvaṁ yad ahaṁ vacmi tat tathā*  
*evaṁ vivadatāṁ hetuṁ śaktayo me duratyayāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When philosophers argue, “I don’t choose to analyze this particular case in the same way that you have,” the cause of their arguments is my insurmountable** ***śakti*****.**

**Комментарий:**

This is explained. My śaktis in the form of various arguments are the causes.

### Text 6

*yāsāṁ vyatikarād āsīd vikalpo vadatāṁ padam*  
*prāpte śama-dame ’pyeti vādas tam anu śāmyati*

**Перевод:**

**But for those who have fixed their intelligence on me and controlled their senses, differences of perception disappear and consequently the argument ceases.**

**Комментарий:**

Because of the contact with the acintya-śakti, all different opinions (*vikalpaḥ*) of various philosophers have appeared. The opinions disappear when the senses are controlled and the mind is fixed on me (*śama-dame*). After that, naturally, the arguments cease, since they understand the outstanding supreme Lord.

### Text 7

*parasparānupraveśāt tattvānāṁ puruṣarṣabha*  
*paurvāparya-prasaṅkhyānaṁ yathā vaktur vivakṣitam*

**Перевод:**

**O best among men! Because subtle and gross elements mutually enter into one another, philosophers may desire to calculate the number of elements in terms of causes or effects according to their personal desire and thus arrive at different totals.**

**Комментарий:**

According to the desire of the speaker, various numbers of elements arise.

### Text 8

*ekasminn api dṛśyante praviṣṭānītarāṇi ca*  
*pūrvasmin vā parasmin vā tattve tattvāni sarvaśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**All subtle material elements are actually present within their gross effects; similarly, all gross elements are present within their subtle causes. Thus we can find all material elements within any single element.**

**Комментарий:**

All the elements are seen to enter into one element. And the one element enters into all the other elements.

### Text 11

*puruṣeśvarayor atra na vailakṣaṇyam aṇv api*  
*tad-anya-kalpanāpārthā jñānaṁ ca prakṛter guṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**There is little difference between the Lord and the** ***jīva.*** **To think of them as extremely different is useless.** ***Jñāna*** **arises from the** ***guṇas*** **of** ***prakṛti*** **and is thus useless for gaining proper vision.**

**Комментарий:**

The ātmā cannot be understood, what to speak of the Paramātmā. In order to understand Paramātmā or the Lord, some knowers of truth impart knowledge that the Lord is different from the jīva. That Lord is self-revealing knowledge. Others however, not understanding the distinction, think without distinction of a conscious entity and propound that. That is expressed here. According to them, the jīva should not have knowledge. Knowledge is a quality of prakṛti. Since the jīva wanders about in the currents of the guṇas, it should have no relationship with knowledge.

### Text 12

*prakṛtir guṇa-sāmyaṁ vai prakṛter nātmano guṇāḥ*  
*sattvaṁ rajas tama iti sthity-utpatty-anta-hetavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

***Prakṛti*** **is the equilibrium of the** ***guṇas.*** **The** ***guṇas*** **belong to** ***prakṛti*****, not the** ***jīva.*** **These modes—goodness, passion and ignorance—are the causes of the creation, maintenance and destruction of this universe, and thus cannot inhere in the eternal** ***jīva.***

**Комментарий:**

“You maintain twenty-eight elements. How can twenty-six elements be correct?” to erase that doubt, the Lord establishes his previous point. The equilibrium of the guṇas is prakṛti. The guṇas of prakṛti cannot be the ātmā. The guṇas of prakṛti are not the qualities of the jīva (knowledge and bliss), what to speak of being the qualities of the Lord. The guṇas of prakṛti are the cause of material creation and destruction. The word *anta* means atyantika-pralaya. This means liberation. Ignorance is the cause of destruction, since it is caused by karma. Knowledge (*sattva*) is the cause of liberation.

### Text 13

*sattvaṁ jñānaṁ rajaḥ karma tamo ’jñānam ihocyate*  
*guṇa-vyatikaraḥ kālaḥ svabhāvaḥ sūtram eva ca*

**Перевод:**

**In this world** ***sattva*** **is recognized as knowledge,** ***rajas*** **is action and** ***tamas*** **is ignorance. The Lord is identified with time, the agitator of the** ***guṇa*****s.** ***Svabhāva*** **or change arising from action is the same as** ***mahat-tattva.***

**Комментарий:**

The descriptions are indications only and not definitive of their natures. Since all of prakṛti is insentient, and the indication of knowledge (in sattva) is by relationship with a shadow of consciousness, sattva is not a cause of knowledge about the Lord.

When sattva manifests, knowledge of the Lord does not arise. The sun does not manifest from its ābhāsa within a room. The Lord is self-manifesting.

*sa yad ajayā tv ajām anuśayīta guṇāṁś ca juṣan*  
*bhajati sarūpatāṁ tad anu mṛtyum apeta-bhagaḥ*  
*tvam uta jahāsi tām ahir iva tvacam ātta-bhago*  
*mahasi mahīyase ’ṣṭa-guṇite ’parimeya-bhagaḥ*

**The** ***jīva*** **contacts matter by the influence of** ***māyā,*** **takes on a material form due to** ***upādhis,*** **and enjoys material objects. He thus experiences** ***saṁsāra.*** **You, however, avoid the material energy in the same way that a snake abandons its old skin. Glorious in your possession of eight mystic powers, you display unlimited greatness. (SB** [**10.87.38**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/87.38)**)**

According to this, the jīva, by being subject to beginningless ignorance, has his knowledge covered. For liberation he must search out some other entity that gives knowledge. The supreme Lord is thus ascertained. One who hears about him repeats the message. From the Lord comes the jīva’s knowledge.

*parābhidhyānāt tu tirohitaṁ tato hy asya bandha-viparyayau*

**Dreams disappear by the will of the Lord. This is not surprising since the Lord (through māyā) is also the cause of bondage and liberation of the jīva. (Brahma-sūtra** [**3.2.5**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/3.2.5)**)**

This knowledge is accepted by others and should be given to them since it is an aṁśa of the svarūpa-śakti, included in the Lord. The three guṇas are parts of prakṛti, which is composed of them. They are nothing more than that. This is determined by those who enumerate twenty-six elements. Having established the twenty-six elements, the three guṇas of prakṛti are additional.

Limiting the number to that, there are nine, eleven, five and three. We hear that you are these elements. By repeating the question, this is counted as your opinion. The three guṇas are shown first, as causes of having additional elements.

The agitation (*vyatikaraḥ*) of the guṇas arises by time, which is made active by the Lord. Impressions of karma are the cause of the jīva attaining particular bodies. This is called *svabhāva.* By me, made of time, the universe becomes endowed with karma.

Sūtra is the mukhya-prāṇa, composed of kriyā-śakti. Mahat-tattva is connected to sūtra.

### Text 14

*puruṣaḥ prakṛtir vyaktam ahaṅkāro nabho ’nilaḥ*  
*jyotir āpaḥ kṣitir iti tattvāny uktāni me nava*

**Перевод:**

**I have described the nine basic elements as the** ***jīva, prakṛti, mahat-tattva,*** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **ether, air, fire, water and earth.**

**Комментарий:**

He enumerates nine and eleven elements in two and half verses. *Puruṣaḥ* is the jīva, and v*yaktam* is mahat-tattva, filled with jñāna-śakti. The five gross elements are also listed.

### Text 16

*śabdaḥ sparśo raso gandho rūpaṁ cety artha-jātayaḥ*  
*gaty-ukty-utsarga-śilpāni karmāyatana-siddhayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Sound, touch, taste, smell and form are the sense objects of the knowledge-acquiring senses. Movement, speech, excretion of wastes and semen, and manufacture are functions of the working senses, but these are not counted as elements.**

**Комментарий:**

The sense objects are listed. It indicates other items such as the Lord, to make twenty-eight elements. Movement etc. are rejected as elements. Other things are also rejected by this statement.

### Text 17

*sargādau prakṛtir hy asya kārya-kāraṇa-rūpiṇī*  
*sattvādibhir guṇair dhatte puruṣo ’vyakta īkṣate*

**Перевод:**

**Taking the form of the causal and resulting (*****kāraṇa*** **and** ***kārya)*** **elements,** ***prakṛti*** **accepts creation and destruction of the universe through the** ***guṇas*****. The Lord does not undergo transformation but merely remains the witness.**

**Комментарий:**

In two verses, he describes everything that the philosophers enumerate.

### Text 21

*catvāry eveti tatrāpi teja āpo ’nnam ātmanaḥ*  
*jātāni tair idaṁ jātaṁ janmāvayavinaḥ khalu*

**Перевод:**

**Some philosophers propose the existence of four basic elements, of which three—fire, water and earth—emanate from the fourth, the Lord. By these elements the creation of the products in this universe arose.**

**Комментарий:**

According this idea, from ātmā, four elements arise. By these four, the world arises, as the appearance of effects (*avayavinaḥ*). Ether is not considered, as it is invisible. Air is also subtle.

### Text 27

*evaṁ me puṇḍarīkākṣa mahāntaṁ saṁśayaṁ hṛdi*  
*chettum arhasi sarva-jña vacobhir naya-naipuṇaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Kṛṣṇa! O omniscient Lord! Cut this great doubt out of my heart with your words, which exhibit your great skill in reasoning.**

### Text 28

*tvatto jñānaṁ hi jīvānāṁ pramoṣas te ’tra śaktitaḥ*  
*tvam eva hy ātma-māyāyā gatiṁ vettha na cāparaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**From you alone through your energy, the** ***jīvas’*** **knowledge arises and disappears. No one but you can understand the real nature of your illusory potency.**

**Комментарий:**

The difference between the Lord and the jīva was the Lord’s opinion. The Lord as the cause is now shown. Through your śakti, the functions of māyā, the jīva’s knowledge appears and disappears. The Lord’s cruelty through lack of power is refuted. “How can knowledge manifest from me, for jīvas covered by māyā. How does knowledge disappear by māyā for the ātmā which is self-revealing?” In answer to the first question, your śakti removes māyā. By rejecting you, jīva can be defeated. The twenty-eight (with sattva-guṇa giving knowledge) assist the twenty six.

### Text 33

*yo ’sau guṇa-kṣobha-kṛto vikāraḥ*  
*pradhāna-mūlān mahataḥ prasūtaḥ*  
*ahaṁ tri-vṛn moha-vikalpa-hetur*  
*vaikārikas tāmasa aindriyaś ca*

**Перевод:**

***Ahaṅkāra,*** **which arises from** ***mahat-tattva,*** **arising from agitation of** ***prakṛti*****, has three forms:** ***sattva, tamas*** **and** ***rajas.*** **These cause a variety of opinions caused by ignorance.**

### Text 34

*ātmāparijñāna-mayo vivādo*  
*hy astīti nāstīti bhidārtha-niṣṭhaḥ*  
*vyartho ’pi naivoparameta puṁsāṁ*  
*mattaḥ parāvṛtta-dhiyāṁ sva-lokāt*

**Перевод:**

**Those who do not know Paramātmā, who are fixed in goals other than me, engage in arguments concerning the reality or unreality of the world, and do not cease to argue, though they achieve no results. They have turned their thoughts from me, the Lord, who gives regard only to the devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

How to destroy ahaṅkāra? It is said:

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.*** **(SB** [**11.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37)**)**

One should put aside refusing the existence of the Lord and become favorable to the Lord. For persons whose intelligence is turned away from me, their shelter (*sva-lokāt*), there will be arguments between oneself and others about existence or nonexistence of ātmā and the Lord of one’s svarūpa (*ātmā-parijñānam*). Though without meaning, the arguments will never end. As long as the person does not recognize me, he will engage in arguments and not attain knowledge. On accepting me, knowledge will arise as a secondary result. The main result is attaining me. This is the parama-puruṣārtha.

### Text 35-36

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*tvattaḥ parāvṛtta-dhiyaḥ sva-kṛtaiḥ karmabhiḥ prabho*  
*uccāvacān yathā dehān gṛhṇanti visṛjanti ca*  
*tan mamākhyāhi govinda durvibhāvyam anātmabhiḥ*  
*na hy etat prāyaśo loke vidvāṁsaḥ santi vañcitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O supreme master! Please explain to me how persons with diverted intelligence accept superior and inferior bodies by their materialistic activities and then give up such bodies. O Govinda, this topic is very difficult for foolish persons to understand. Bewildered by your** ***māyā,*** **no one generally knows this.**

**Комментарий:**

Two verses go together. How does the inactive ātmā go from one body to another?

### Text 37

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*manaḥ karma-mayaṁ ṇṝṇām indriyaiḥ pañcabhir yutam*  
*lokāl lokaṁ prayāty anya ātmā tad anuvartate*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: The material mind of man, dependent on** ***karma,*** **travels along with the five senses from one material body to another. The** ***ātmā,*** **although different from this mind, follows it.**

**Комментарий:**

The ātmā, riding on the mind, also goes. When the mind moves, the ātmā moves.

### Text 40

*janma tv ātmatayā puṁsaḥ sarva-bhāvena bhūri-da*  
*viṣaya-svīkṛtiṁ prāhur yathā svapna-manorathaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O most charitable Uddhava! What is called birth is simply a person’s total identification with a new body. One accepts the new body just as one completely accepts the experience of a dream or a fantasy as reality.**

**Комментарий:**

In a dream, the past appears like the present.

### Text 46

*mā svasya karma-bījena jāyate so ’py ayaṁ pumān*  
*mriyate vāmaro bhrāntyā yathāgnir dāru-saṁyutaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A person does not actually take birth out of the seed of** ***karma*****, nor, being immortal, does he die. By illusion the living being appears to be born and to die, just as fire in connection with firewood appears to begin and then cease to exist though the element lasts for the lifetime of Brahmā.**

**Комментарий:**

Even for the ignorant people, birth and death do not exist. Their existence is like mistaking a rope for a snake. This is Śrīdhara Svāmī’s explanation.

*nṛtyato gāyataḥ paśyan yathaivānukaroti tān*  
*evaṁ buddhi-guṇān paśyann anīho ‘py anukāryate*

**Just as one may imitate persons whom one sees dancing and singing, similarly the soul, although never the doer of material activities, is thus forced to imitate the qualities of the intelligence. (SB** [**11.22.53**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/22.53)**)**

Because of the qualities of intelligence, a jīva has false knowledge, since the observer and the object observed have different qualities.

### Text 57

*tasmād uddhava mā bhuṅkṣva viṣayān asad-indriyaiḥ*  
*ātmāgrahaṇa-nirbhātaṁ paśya vaikalpikaṁ bhramam*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, O Uddhava, do not try to enjoy sense objects with the material senses. Recognize that ignorance based on false identity with the body, which produces non-attainment of the self.**

**Комментарий:**

Though speaking to Uddhava, he is teaching others. The Lord himself says:

*noddhavo ’ṇv api man-nyūno yad guṇair nārditaḥ prabhuḥ*  
*ato mad-vayunaṁ loka grāhayann iha tiṣṭhatu*

**Uddhava is not less than me, because he is master of** ***māyā*** **and not at all lacking in any spiritual quality. He should remain on this earth, giving knowledge of me to the world. (SB** [**3.4.31**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/4.31)**)**

### Text 61

*su-duḥṣaham imaṁ manya ātmany asad-atikramam*  
*viduṣām api viśvātman prakṛtir hi balīyasī*  
*ṛte tvad-dharma-niratān śāntāṁs te caraṇālayān*

**Перевод:**

**O soul of the universe! It is very difficult for a person to tolerate the offenses committed against him by ignorant people. Even a person who knows the method of toleration has strong intolerance in his nature. Only your devotees, who are fixed in your qualities and who have achieved peace by residing at your lotus feet, are devoid of this intolerance.**

**Комментарий:**

Only persons who engage in hearing about you and glorifying you do not have intolerance. This is not surprising for me. You have given examples of others in this chapter, not the devotees.

## Chapter Twenty-three

### Text 4

*kathayanti mahat puṇyam itihāsam ihoddhava*  
*tam ahaṁ varṇayiṣyāmi nibodha su-samāhitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! In this regard a most purifying story is told, and I shall now describe it to you. Please listen with attention.**

**Комментарий:**

He illustrates with a story.

### Text 10

*tad-avadhyāna-visrasta- puṇya-skandhasya bhūri-da*  
*artho ’py agacchan nidhanaṁ bahv-āyāsa-pariśramaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O magnanimous Uddhava, by his neglect of these** ***devatās*** **he depleted his stock of piety and all his wealth. He had gained this wealth with great fatigue and effort.**

**Комментарий:**

Some portion of wealth only disappeared. Because of good impressions, with a desire for liberation, he offered the remaining portion wealth to the Lord.

### Text 13

*tasyaivaṁ dhyāyato dīrghaṁ naṣṭa-rāyas tapasvinaḥ*  
*khidyato bāṣpa-kaṇṭhasya nirvedaḥ su-mahān abhūt*

**Перевод:**

**Meditating for a long time, having lost his fortune and feeling great pain and lamentation, his throat choked up with tears, he experienced a powerful feeling of renunciation.**

**Комментарий:**

Though miserable, overcoming all offenses, a special impression from long past arose.

### Text 28

*nūnaṁ me bhagavāṁs tuṣṭaḥ sarva-deva-mayo hariḥ*  
*yena nīto daśām etāṁ nirvedaś cātmanaḥ plavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord, who comprises all the** ***devatās,*** **must be satisfied with me. He has brought me to this suffering condition and detachment, which is the boat to carry me over this ocean of material life.**

**Комментарий:**

From his impressions, he realized a relationship with the Lord. Since sacrifices involved the Lord, the Lord’s worship manifested at that time.

### Text 29

*so ’haṁ kālāvaśeṣeṇa śoṣayiṣye ’ṅgam ātmanaḥ*  
*apramatto ’khila-svārthe yadi syāt siddha ātmani*

**Перевод:**

**I will perform austerities and with full attention I shall meditate on the lotus feet of the Lord. If the remainder of my life is like this, I shall reach perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

If the remainder of my life is like this, concentrating with attention on the Lord (*ātmani*) who is highest human goal, having direct vision of him, I will destroy the gross and subtle bodies (*aṅgam*).

### Text 31

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*ity abhipretya manasā hy āvantyo dvija-sattamaḥ*  
*unmucya hṛdaya-granthīn śānto bhikṣur abhūn muniḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord said: His mind thus determined, that most excellent Avantī** ***brāhmaṇa*** **was able to untie the knots of self-interest caused by** ***ahaṅkāra.*** **He then assumed the role of a peaceful and silent** ***sannyāsī*** **mendicant.**

**Комментарий:**

*Bhikṣuḥ* means sannyāsī.

### Text 45

*dānaṁ sva-dharmo niyamo yamaś ca*  
*śrutaṁ ca karmāṇi ca sad-vratāni*  
*sarve mano-nigraha-lakṣaṇāntāḥ*  
*paro hi yogo manasaḥ samādhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Charity, prescribed duties,** ***niyama*** **and** ***yama,*** **hearing from scripture, pious works and purifying vows all have as their final aim the subduing of the mind, since control of the mind is the best** ***yoga.***

**Комментарий:**

Everything aims at controlling all mental functions (*manas*). More than having knowledge in the mind, the jīva must have natural knowledge of the Lord. Without that, the mental functions cannot be controlled.

### Text 50

*janas tu hetuḥ sukha-duḥkhayoś cet*  
*kim ātmanaś cātra hi bhaumayos tat*  
*jihvāṁ kvacit sandaśati sva-dadbhis*  
*tad-vedanāyāṁ katamāya kupyet*

**Перевод:**

**If you say that people are the cause of my happiness and distress, then where is the place for the soul in such a conception? Happiness and distress pertain not to the soul but simply to the interactions of two material bodies. If someone bites his tongue with his own teeth, at whom can he become angry when he suffers?**

**Комментарий:**

Though the jīva is the main factor in all bodies, what is its role if people are the cause of happiness and distress? The same goes for the tongue.

### Text 51

*duḥkhasya hetur yadi devatās tu*  
*kim ātmanas tatra vikārayos tat*  
*yad aṅgam aṅgena nihanyate kvacit*  
*krudhyeta kasmai puruṣaḥ sva-dehe*

**Перевод:**

**If you say that the** ***devatās*** **who rule the bodily senses cause suffering, still, what role does the** ***ātmā*** **have, since two** ***devatās*** **become the causes and objects of infliction? Since the** ***devatās*** **operate all the limbs in all bodies, when one limb of the body attacks another limb in the same body, with whom can the person in that body be angry?**

**Комментарий:**

When someone’s’ hand hits the face, at whom does one become angry? Can one become angry at the devatā? Indra, lord of the hand, is in my body and the other person’s body. How can one become angry at him?

### Text 52

*ātmā yadi syāt sukha-duḥkha-hetuḥ*  
*kim anyatas tatra nija-svabhāvaḥ*  
*na hy ātmano ’nyad yadi tan mṛṣā syāt*  
*krudhyeta kasmān na*

**Перевод:**

**If the** ***jīva*** **were the cause of happiness and distress, because it is conscious, then we could not blame others, since happiness and distress would be simply the nature of the** ***jīva.*** **Only** ***jīva*** **exists anyway, but if you say something else exists, it is a superimposition on the jīva. Since happiness and distress do not actually exist in this concept, why become angry at oneself or others?**

**Комментарий:**

It is superimposed on the jīva by ignorance (*mṛṣā*).

### Text 55

*kālas tu hetuḥ sukha-duḥkhayoś cet*  
*kim ātmanas tatra tad-ātmako ’sau*  
*nāgner hi tāpo na himasya tat syāt*  
*krudhyeta kasmai na parasya dvandvam*

**Перевод:**

**If we accept time as the cause of happiness and distress, that experience still cannot apply to the spirit soul, since time is a manifestation of the Lord’s spiritual potency and the living entities are also expansions of the Lord’s spiritual potency. Fire does not burn its own flames or sparks, nor does the cold harm its own snowflakes. The** ***jīva*** **is transcendental and beyond the experience of material happiness and distress. At whom, therefore, should one become angry?**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva is an aṁśa of the Lord, and time and the Lord are one. Thus the jīva is also similar to time (*tad-ātmakaḥ*).

### Text 56

*na kenacit kvāpi kathañcanāsya*  
*dvandvoparāgaḥ parataḥ parasya*  
*yathāhamaḥ saṁsṛti-rūpiṇaḥ syād*  
*evaṁ prabuddho na bibheti bhūtaiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The influence of happiness and suffering for the** ***jīva,*** **who is superior to all else, cannot arise from any means proposed by anyone. It arises only from the mind, which gives shape to** ***saṁsāra.*** **One who becomes enlightened does not fear, though covered with matter.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva who is by his svarūpa beyond māyā (*parasya*) is superior to other things *(parataḥ*).

### Text 57

*etāṁ sa āsthāya parātma-niṣṭhām*  
*adhyāsitāṁ pūrvatamair maharṣibhiḥ*  
*ahaṁ tariṣyāmi duranta-pāraṁ*  
*tamo mukundāṅghri-niṣevayaiva*

**Перевод:**

**Becoming fixed in** ***ātmā*** **and slightly fixed in Paramātmā, as established by the previous sages, I shall cross over the insurmountable ocean of nescience by service to the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

The mind should be fixed in Paramātmā. Without service to the feet of Mukunda, there will be disturbance. Considering in this way, there are problems in fixing the mind on him. I will take support of service to the Lord. The Lord spoke correctly:

*su-duḥṣaham imaṁ manya ātmany asad-atikramam*  
*viduṣām api viśvātman prakṛtir hi balīyasī*  
*ṛte tvad-dharma-niratān śāntāṁs te caraṇālayān*

**O soul of the universe! It is very difficult for a person to tolerate the offenses committed against him by ignorant people. Even a person who knows the method of toleration has strong intolerance in his nature. Only your devotees, who are fixed in your qualities and who have achieved peace by residing at your lotus feet, are devoid of this intolerance. (SB** [**11.22.61**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/22.61)**)**

### Text 59

*sukha-duḥkha-prado nānyaḥ puruṣasyātma-vibhramaḥ*  
*mitrodāsīna-ripavaḥ saṁsāras tamasaḥ kṛtaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**No other force besides bewilderment of the mind makes the soul experience happiness and distress.** ***Saṁsāra*** **in the form of friends, neutral parties and enemies are caused by ignorance.**

**Комментарий:**

Saṁsāra in the form of friends, neutrals and enemies gives happiness and distress. Saṁsāra is nothing else but this. It is produced by ignorance. It causes bewilderment of the mind (*ātma-vibrhamaḥ*)

## Chapter Twenty-four

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*atha te sampravakṣyāmi sāṅkhyaṁ pūrvair viniścitam*  
*yad vijñāya pumān sadyo jahyād vaikalpikaṁ bhramam*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Now I shall describe to you Sāṅkhya, which has been perfectly established by ancient authorities. By understanding this, a person can immediately give up the illusion of identity with the body.**

**Комментарий:**

In his commentary Śrīdhara Svāmī uses the word *prakṛti*. This is also called *pradhāna.*

### Text 2

*āsīj jñānam atho artha ekam evāvikalpitam*  
*yadā viveka-nipuṇā ādau kṛta-yuge ’yuge*

**Перевод:**

**Originally, during Satya-yuga, when all men were expert in spiritual discrimination, and also during the period of annihilation, the Lord was complete, one alone, without difference.**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī defines Bhagavān from the Third Canto:

*bhagavān eka āsedam agra ātmātmanāṁ vibhuḥ*  
*ātmecchānugatāv ātmā nānā-maty-upalakṣaṇaḥ*

**Bhagavān, who exists in the form of Paramātmā and Brahman according to the viewpoint, alone existed before the creation of the universe, when the desire to create bodies of the** ***jīvas*** **was absent. (SB** [**3.5.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/5.23)**)**

**Paramātmā Sandarbha 50:**

The word Bhagavān is here equivalent to Brahman since they are one entity according to *vadanti tat tat-vidas* (SB [1.2.11](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.11)). At the end of the discussion the Lord says:

*eṣa sāṅkhya-vidhiḥ proktaḥ saṁśaya-granthi-bhedanaḥ*  
*pratilomānulomābhyāṁ parāvara-dṛśa mayā*

**Thus I, the perfect seer of everything material and spiritual, have spoken this knowledge of sāṅkhya, which destroys the illusion of doubt by analysis of creation and annihilation. SB** [**11.24.29**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/24.29)

Thus it is clear that the form of Bhagavān remains, since the Lord is described as the perfect seer of everything material and spiritual (*parāvara-dṛśā māyā*).

When was this condition? The oneness arose when people were very intelligent at the beginning, Satya-yuga, and appears again at the time of pralaya (*ayuge*).

Or jñānā-matra means jīva-tattva.

### Text 3

*tan māyā-phala-rūpeṇa kevalaṁ nirvikalpitam*  
*vāṅ-mano-’gocaraṁ satyaṁ dvidhā samabhavad bṛhat*

**Перевод:**

**That one entity, remaining free from material dualities, divided himself into two categories—*****prakṛti*** **and** ***jīva,*** **which are beyond words and mind and which are real, by his form of māyā**

**Комментарий:**

**Paramātmā Sandarbha 51:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī’s commentary says, “The Brahman *(bṛhat*) became two, in the illusory or playful form of māyā glanced upon by Brahman. That māyā is a special manifestation (*phalam*) of Brahman. This act was beyond words and mind.”

Or later it is said:

*prakṛtir yasyopādānam ādhāraḥ puruṣaḥ paraḥ*  
*sato ’bhivyañjakaḥ kālo brahma tat tritayaṁ tv aham*

***Prakrṭi*** **is the material cause, the** ***puruṣa*** **is the foundational cause. Time, the indirect cause, is the agitator of** ***prakṛti.*** **I am all three. (SB** [**11.24.19**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/24.19)**)**

Brahman has three forms. Situated in that form as the greatest, Brahman, the result of māyā, its specialized śakti, is a distorted form. As that distorted form, māyā, Brahman became two.

### Text 4

*tayor ekataro hy arthaḥ prakṛtiḥ sobhayātmikā*  
*jñānaṁ tv anyatamo bhāvaḥ puruṣaḥ so ’bhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**Of these two categories of manifestation, one is** ***prakṛti,*** **which embodies cause and effect. The other is the conscious living entity, designated as the** ***jīva.***

**Комментарий:**

**Paramātmā Sandarbha 52:**

The commentary of Śrīdhara Svāmī says “Of the two divided parts (*tayoh*) one is the form of cause and effect (*ubhayātmikā*).”

Viṣṇu Purāṇa [1.2.24](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vp/1.2.24) says *purāṇe viṣṇoḥ svarūpāt parato 'hi te 'nye rūpe prādhānaṁ puruṣaś ca vipra:* O brāhmaṇa, from the svarūpa of Viṣṇu appear two other forms: pradhāna and the jīva. The commentary says, “From the svarūpa of Viṣṇu who has no upādhis (*parataḥ*), the two entities pradhāna and puruṣa (*anye*), created by māyā, become manifest.”

### Text 5

*tamo rajaḥ sattvam iti prakṛter abhavan guṇāḥ*  
*mayā prakṣobhyamāṇāyāḥ puruṣānumatena ca*

**Перевод:**

**When material nature was agitated by my glance, the three material modes—goodness, passion and ignorance—became manifest to fulfill the pending desires of the conditioned souls.**

**Комментарий:**

The guṇas (tamas etc.) related to prakṛti, agitated by me, in the form of the assisting puruṣa, the creator of mahat-tattva, who is active as time, keeping in mind the host of devotees, were manifested. And they were especially manifested by the activated svabhāva, the impressions or knowledge (*mata*) related to (*anu*) all the jīvas (*puruṣa*).

### Text 6

*tebhyaḥ samabhavat sūtraṁ mahān sūtreṇa saṁyutaḥ*  
*tato vikurvato jāto yo ’haṅkāro vimohanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**From these modes arose the primeval** ***sūtra.*** ***Mahat-tattva*** **is endowed with** ***sūtra.*** **By the transformation of the** ***mahat-tattva*** **was generated the** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **the cause of the living entities’ bewilderment.**

**Комментарий:**

From the guṇas, mahat-tattva endowed with sūtra arose. Their difference is shown (mahat-tattva is in sattva, and sūtra in rajas). In this way six tattvas have been described: the Lord, the jīva, prakṛti, svabhāva, sūtra and mahat-tattva.

The guṇas are included in prakṛti and the devatās of the senses are included in the elements. The total of ahaṅkāra and other elements is twenty-two. Add to this the six tattvas and the total is twenty-eight.

### Text 7

*vaikārikas taijasaś ca tāmasaś cety ahaṁ tri-vṛt*  
*tan-mātrendriya-manasāṁ kāraṇaṁ cid-acin-mayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**False ego, with qualities of matter and spirit, in the modes of goodness, passion and ignorance is the cause of** ***tan-mātras*****, the senses, and the mind,**

**Комментарий:**

Śrīdhara Svāmī says that the meaning of the śrutis is contained in the Purāṇas. From ahaṅkāra arose ether and mind. The smārtas also have the same idea. That is suitable since it is said:

*vedārthadadhikaṁ manye purāṇārthaṁ varānane!*  
*vedāḥ pratiṣṭhitā devi! purāṇe nātra saṁśayaḥ*  
*purāṇam anyathā kṛtvā tiryyagyonimavāpnuyāt*  
*sudānto ’pi suśānto ’pi/ na gatiṁ prāpnuyāt kvacit*

**O beautiful faced woman! I consider the meaning of the Purāṇas greater than the meaning of the Vedas. The Vedas are established in all the Purāṇas without doubt. If one disrespects the Purāṇas one attains an animal birth. Though controlling the senses and mind, one never attains the spiritual goal. (Nāradīya Purāṇa)**

*yo veda caturo vedān sāṅgopaniṣado dvijāḥ \|*  
*purāṇaṁ naiva jānāti na ca sa syād vicakṣaṇaḥ \|\|*

**The clever person who knows the Vedas and Upaniṣads but does not know the Purāṇas is not knowledgeable. (Skanda Purāṇa)**

*bhārata-vyapadeśena hy āmnāyārthaḥ pradarśitaḥ \|*  
*vedāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ sarve purāṇe nātra saṁśayaḥ \|\|*

**By the teachings of Mahābhārata I have shown the meaning of the Vedas. The Vedas are established in all the Purāṇas without doubt. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa)**

### Text 9

*mayā sañcoditā bhāvāḥ sarve saṁhatya-kāriṇaḥ*  
*aṇḍam utpādayām āsur mamāyatanam uttamam*

**Перевод:**

**Impelled by me, all these elements combined to function in an orderly fashion and together gave birth to the universal egg, which is my excellent place of residence.**

**Комментарий:**

This abode of the Lord is an upādhi (material). It is called excellent because it encompasses everything *(vyasti*).

### Text 10

*tasminn ahaṁ samabhavam aṇḍe salila-saṁsthitau*  
*mama nābhyām abhūt padmaṁ viśvākhyaṁ tatra cātma-bhūḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I appeared within that egg, on the Garbhodaka water, and from my navel arose the universal lotus and Brahmā.**

**Комментарий:**

I manifests in the form of the second puruṣa, within the universe, on its water, called the Garbhodaka Ocean.

### Text 12

*devānām oka āsīt svar bhūtānāṁ ca bhuvaḥ padam*  
*martyādīnāṁ ca bhūr lokaḥ siddhānāṁ tritayāt param*

**Перевод:**

**Heaven was established as the residence of the** ***devatās,*** **Bhuvarloka as that of the ghostly spirits, and the earth system as the place of human beings and other mortal creatures. Those mystics who strive for liberation are promoted beyond these three divisions.**

**Комментарий:**

The siddhas refers to persons who have attained siddhis by yoga.

### Text 13

*adho ’surāṇāṁ nāgānāṁ bhūmer oko ’sṛjat prabhuḥ*  
*tri-lokyāṁ gatayaḥ sarvāḥ karmaṇāṁ tri-guṇātmanām*

**Перевод:**

**Brahmā created the region below the earth for the demons and the Nāga snakes. In this way the destinations of the three worlds were arranged according to** ***karma*** **in the three** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhagavat Sandarbha 63:**

Those who attain siddhis by yoga attain places beyond the three worlds, like Maharloka. Below the earth are places like Atala. The three worlds consist of Bhū (including lowers planets), Bhuvar and Svarga. Karma is for householders and austerity is for brahmacārīs and vānaprasthas. The brahmacārī who later becomes a householder goes to Maharloka and the permanent brahmacārī goes to Janaloka. The vānaprastha goes to Tapaloka. The sannyāsī goes to Brahmaloka. According to the method, they attain different destinations.

The Lord’s abode (*mad-gatiḥ*), Vaikuṇṭha, is attained by bhakti.

*yan na vrajanty agha-bhido racanānuvādāc*  
*chṛṇvanti ye ’nya-viṣayāḥ kukathā mati-ghnīḥ*  
*yās tu śrutā hata-bhagair nṛbhir ātta-sārās*  
*tāṁs tān kṣipanty aśaraṇeṣu tamaḥsu hanta*

**Those who hear topics other than the glorification of Kṛṣṇa, unworthy topics which destroy intelligence, cannot enter Vaikuṇṭha. Topics heard by those unfortunate persons, taking away their good fortune, throw them into hell with no shelter. (SB** [**3.15.23**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.23)**)**

Concerning the planet, Brahmā speaks to the devatās:

*yat saṅkulaṁ hari-padānati-mātra-dṛṣṭair*  
*vaidūrya-mārakata-hema-mayair vimānaiḥ*  
*yeṣāṁ bṛhat-kaṭi-taṭāḥ smita-śobhi-mukhyaḥ*  
*kṛṣṇātmanāṁ na raja ādadhur utsmayādyaiḥ*

**Vaikuṇṭha is filled with airplanes made of gold, vaidūrya and emeralds, obtained solely by worship of the Lord’s lotus feet. It is filled with devotees absorbed in the Lord who cannot be disturbed by the smiles, glances and words of the most beautiful women with broad hips and smiling faces. (SB** [**3.15.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/15.20)**)**

The planes of the devotees are attained by worship of the Lord, not by karma.

*parīkṣya lokān karma-citān brāhmaṇo nirvedam āyān nāstyakṛtaḥ kṛtena*

**After examining all the worlds attained by karmas, the brāhmaṇa should become indifferent. One cannot attain freedom from karma by acts of karma. (Muṇḍaka Upaniṣad** [**1.2.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/mundaka/1.2.12)**)**

*Akṛtaḥ* means the planet with no karma. Gītā also speaks of the Lord’s planet:

*tam eva śaraṇaṁ gaccha sarva-bhāvena bhārata*  
*tat-prasādāt parāṁ śāntiṁ sthānaṁ prāpsyasi śāśvatam \|\|*

**O descendent of Bharata, take refuge in the Paramātmā alone with all your facilities. By his mercy, you will attain the eternal spiritual abode. (BG** [**18.62**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.62)**)**

### Text 15

*mayā kālātmanā dhātrā karma-yuktam idaṁ jagat*  
*guṇa-pravāha etasminn unmajjati nimajjati*

**Перевод:**

**This world, endowed with** ***karma,*** **has been arranged by me, the Supreme Lord who acts as the force of time. Thus one sometimes rises up to Satyaloka and then sinks down to take birth as a plant.**

**Комментарий:**

By me, the doer, this world is joined with the results of karma. *Phalam ata upapatteḥ*: the Lord alone gives the results because that is reasonable. (Brahma-sūtra [3.2.39](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vs/3.2.39))

### Text 18

*yad upādāya pūrvas tu bhāvo vikurute ’param*  
*ādir anto yadā yasya tat satyam abhidhīyate*

**Перевод:**

**Accepting its causal nature, the previous condition transforms into another condition. Since the cause exists in the beginning and end, it is called real.**

**Комментарий:**

It is proper to say that everything is real since all existence is produced by me.

### Text 19

*prakṛtir yasyopādānam ādhāraḥ puruṣaḥ paraḥ*  
*sato ’bhivyañjakaḥ kālo brahma tat tritayaṁ tv aham*

**Перевод:**

***Prakrṭi*** **is the material cause, the** ***puruṣa*** **is the foundational cause. Time, the indirect cause, is the agitator of** ***prakṛti.*** **I am all three.**

**Комментарий:**

This is explained.

### Text 22-27

*anne pralīyate martyam annaṁ dhānāsu līyate*  
*dhānā bhūmau pralīyante bhūmir gandhe pralīyate*  
*apsu pralīyate gandha āpaś ca sva-guṇe rase*  
*līyate jyotiṣi raso jyotī rūpe pralīyate*  
*rūpaṁ vāyau sa ca sparśe līyate so ’pi cāmbare*  
*ambaraṁ śabda-tan-mātra indriyāṇi sva-yoniṣu*  
*yonir vaikārike saumya līyate manasīśvare*  
*śabdo bhūtādim apyeti bhūtādir mahati prabhuḥ*  
*sa līyate mahān sveṣu guṇesu guṇa-vattamaḥ*  
*te ’vyakte sampralīyante tat kāle līyate ’vyaye*  
*kālo māyā-maye jīve jīva ātmani mayy aje*  
*ātmā kevala ātma-stho vikalpāpāya-lakṣaṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**At the time of annihilation, the mortal body of the living being becomes merged into food. Food merges into the grains, and the grains merge back into the earth. The earth merges into its** ***tan-mātra*****, fragrance. Fragrance merges into water, and water further merges into its** ***tan-mātra*****, taste. That taste merges into fire, which merges into form. Form merges into air, and air merges into touch. Touch merges into ether. Ether finally merges into sound. The senses all merge into** ***ahaṅkāra*** **in rajas. O gentle Uddhava! This merge into the controlling mind, which itself merges into** ***ahaṅkāra*** **in the mode of goodness. Sound becomes one with false ego in the mode of ignorance, and all-powerful false ego, the first of all the physical elements, merges into** ***mahat-tattva.*** **The** ***mahat-tattva,*** **possessing qualities, dissolves into the** ***guṇas.*** **These** ***guṇas*** **then merge into the unmanifest** ***prakṛti*** **in due time. Time merges into the** ***jīva*** **covered by** ***māyā.*** **The** ***jīva*** **is embraced by me, Paramātmā, the unborn, who remains alone, without covering, from whom creation and annihilation are manifested.**

**Комментарий:**

Anna or food is mentioned, as it is a portion of earth which produces it. Food merges into grains, its subtle state and causal portion. Grains merges into earth, its shelter. As the elements become suitable for milking by earth through creation, at destruction they become unsuitable for milking (sustaining living beings).

The senses merge into ahaṅkāra in rajas (*sva-yoniṣu*). The tendency of all senses, the mind, merges into ahaṅkāra in sattva (*vaikārike*), the abode of the controlling deity of the mind (*manisīśvare*). Mahat-tattva and sūtra (not mentioned) merge into the guṇas. Prakṛti merges into time, the śakti of the imperishable Lord. Time, which produces sequence of actions, an amazing effect of the Lord, merges into the jīva, who explores māyā (*māyāmaye*). Śrīdhara Svāmī says that māyā means knowledge. The jīva merges into me, Paramātmā. Paramātmā has no destruction (*aje*). Parmātmā, the supreme puruṣa, remains in his svarūpa as the pure ātmā, Bhagavān (*ātma-sthaḥ*), who accepts creation, maintenance and destruction.

### Text 29

*eṣa sāṅkhya-vidhiḥ proktaḥ saṁśaya-granthi-bhedanaḥ*  
*pratilomānulomābhyāṁ parāvara-dṛśā mayā*

**Перевод:**

**Thus I, the perfect seer of everything material and spiritual, have spoken this knowledge of Sāṅkhya, which destroys the illusion of doubt by analysis of creation and annihilation.**

**Комментарий:**

Svayam Bhagavān is established as the Lord in the beginning and end of everything (*parāvara-dṛśā*)

## Chapter Twenty-five

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*guṇānām asammiśrāṇāṁ pumān yena yathā bhavet*  
*tan me puruṣa-varyedam upadhāraya śaṁsataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O best among men, please listen as I describe to you how the living entity attains a particular nature by association with individual** ***guṇas*** **in their unmixed forms*****.***

**Комментарий:**

I will describe a nature by association with guṇas unmixed, and also mixed.

### Text 2

*śamo damas titikṣekṣā tapaḥ satyaṁ dayā smṛtiḥ*  
*tuṣṭis tyāgo ’spṛhā śraddhā hrīr dayādiḥ sva-nirvṛtiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Control of the mind, sense control, tolerance, discrimination, austerity, truthfulness, mercy, careful study of the past and future, satisfaction in any condition, renunciation, detachment, faith, being embarrassed at improper action, charity, simplicity, humbleness and satisfaction within oneself are qualities of the mode of goodness.**

**Комментарий:**

Also the Lord’s six qualities starting with aiśvarya should be included.

### Text 17

*vikurvan kriyayā cā-dhīr anivṛttiś ca cetasām*  
*gātrāsvāsthyaṁ mano bhrāntaṁ raja etair niśāmaya*

**Перевод:**

**You should discern the mode of passion by its symptoms—the agitation of the intelligence because of objects nearby, material thirst in the intelligence and senses, an unhealthy condition of the action senses, and a confused mind.**

**Комментарий:**

Then the intelligence is agitated by the actions (*kriyayā*) of the senses etc. when mind does not stop its attraction for sense objects, when the limbs of the body become agitated and the mind becomes confused, see by this the mode of rajas.

### Text 18

*sīdac cittaṁ vilīyeta cetaso grahaṇe ’kṣamam*  
*mano naṣṭaṁ tamo glānis tamas tad upadhāraya*

**Перевод:**

**When one’s consciousness becomes bewildered and fails to function, when one is not aware of objects, when the mind fails to decide, and when ignorance and torpor are prominent, understand that** ***tamas*** **has become predominant.**

**Комментарий:**

The way in which tamas operates is described. When the antaḥkaraṇa, cause of remembrance, disappears (*sīdāt*), or becomes very weak, when the consciousness is not able to comprehend objects by the power of great remembrance, and the mind disappears, when ignorance and torpor arise, understand that tamas is present.

### Text 19

*edhamāne guṇe sattva devānāṁ balam edhate*  
*asurāṇāṁ ca rajasi tamasy uddhava rakṣasām*

**Перевод:**

**With the increase of the mode of goodness, the strength of the** ***devatās*** **similarly increases. When passion increases, the demonic beings become strong. And with the rise of ignorance, O Uddhava, the strength of the Rākṣasas increases.**

**Комментарий:**

Sattva should be practiced by the person desiring welfare of all.

### Text 20

*sattvāj jāgaraṇaṁ vidyād rajasā svapnam ādiśet*  
*prasvāpaṁ tamasā jantos turīyaṁ triṣu santatam*

**Перевод:**

**It should be understood that wakefulness comes from the mode of goodness, sleep with dreaming from the mode of passion, and deep, dreamless sleep from the mode of ignorance. The fourth state of consciousness extends over the other three.**

**Комментарий:**

The fourth state is not bound up by the guṇas.

### Text 21

*upary upari gacchanti sattvena brāhmaṇā janāḥ*  
*tamasādho ’dha ā-mukhyād rajasāntara-cāriṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Brahminical persons are elevated by the mode of goodness to higher and higher positions. The mode of ignorance, on the other hand, forces one is born as plant life. And by the mode of passion one continues transmigrating through human bodies.**

**Комментарий:**

It is best to perform actions in sattva. Another reading instead of *upari upari* is *ābrahmaṇo janā*: they go up to Brahmaloka by *sattva.* By tamas they go down to ignorance (*āmukhyāt*). By rajas they wander about in Svarga etc.

### Text 22

*sattve pralīnāḥ svar yānti nara-lokaṁ rajo-layāḥ*  
*tamo-layās tu nirayaṁ yānti mām eva nirguṇāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Those who leave this world in** ***sattva*** **go to the heavenly planets, those who pass away in** ***rajas*** **remain in the world of human beings, and those dying in** ***tamas*** **must go to hell. But those who are free from the influence of all modes of nature come to me, even while living.**

**Комментарий:**

Those who die with a prominence of sattva go to Svarga. Those with increase of rajas when they die (*rajolayaḥ*) stay on earth. Those who die with prominence of tamas to go to hell. Those not affected by the guṇas, those who perform pure bhakti go to me.

### Text 23

*mad-arpaṇaṁ niṣphalaṁ vā sāttvikaṁ nija-karma tat*  
*rājasaṁ phala-saṅkalpaṁ hiṁsā-prāyādi tāmasam*

**Перевод:**

**Prescribed duties performed as an offering to me, without consideration of the fruit, is considered to be in** ***sattva.*** **Prescribed actions performed with a desire to enjoy the results but offered to me is in** ***rajas.*** **And work impelled by violence and envy is in** ***tamas.***

**Комментарий:**

*Vā* means “and.” Actions of varṇāśrama performed without desire for results offered to me are sāttvika actions.

### Text 24

*kaivalyaṁ sāttvikaṁ jñāna rajo vaikalpikaṁ ca yat*  
*prākṛtaṁ tāmasaṁ jñāna man-niṣṭhaṁ nirguṇaṁ smṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**Knowledge concerning the** ***jīva*** **apart from the body is in** ***sattva.*** **Knowledge with various sacrifices in** ***rajas*****. Knowledge of material life is in** ***tamas.*** **Knowledge concerning me, however, is understood to be beyond the** ***guṇas*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Having described different actions according to the guṇas, now different types of knowledge according to the guṇas is described.

*Kaivalyam* means knowledge of oneness concerning tattva, according to scripture. This is sattvika-jñāna. Knowledge of material things of the materialists is in ignorance (*prākṛtam*). Knowledge fixed in agniṣtoma sacrifices etc. is in rajas. Knowledge fixed in me produces what is beyond the guṇas (*nirguṇam*).

*nairguṇya-sādhanaṁ yat tat niguṇe parikīrtitam iti*  
*yathā śāstrokta-vijñānaṁ kevalaṁ jñānam ucyate*  
*sva-dṛṣṭa-śāstrānukūlyād adṛṣṭānāṁ tu bhaktitaḥ*  
*guṇānāṁ tu harau bhāvaṁ viniścitya tad āśrayāt*  
*yathā śāstrānusandhānaṁ jñānaṁ ca hari-saṁśrayam*

**Sādhana which is beyond the guṇas is called nirguṇa. Because it is vijñāna according to scriptures is called pure knowledge. That knowledge is beyond the guṇas because of bhakti, following scriptures the person has seen. Knowledge presented in scriptures takes shelter of the Lord. Since it takes shelter of him, that knowledge shows an affectionate relationship with the Lord. (Madhva’s commentary on this verse.)**

### Text 25

*vanaṁ tu sāttviko vāso grāmo rājasa ucyate*  
*tāmasaṁ dyūta-sadanaṁ man-niketaṁ tu nirguṇam*

**Перевод:**

**Residence in the forest is in the mode of goodness, residence in a town is in the mode of passion, residence in a gambling house displays the quality of ignorance, and residence in a place where I reside is beyond the** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 135:**

Having described bhakti as nirguṇa by its jñāna, it is then described in Bhāgavatam as nirguṇa by its kriyā. Because they are related to the Lord, actions like hearing and chanting are under the kriyā-śakti. Living where the Lord resides is given as an example.

Living in the forest refers to the vānaprastha. Living in the town refers to the gṛhastha. Gambling dens are representative of many such places where sinful people reside. The abode of the Lord, where persons perform service to the Lord, is beyond the guṇas.

*Vanam* is in apposition to *vasaḥ* like the phrase “ghee is life” (meaning ghee causes long life). The forest composed of trees has a prominence of rajas and tamas. Sattva becomes a secondary guṇa of the forest, because of the mixture with the two other guṇas which are more prominent, though living a solitary life there is sāttvika. But since staying in the forest arises from sattva and increases sattva, living in the forest is regarded as having a prominence of sattva.

*Grāmyaḥ* means living in the town. Gambling means living in places where there is gambling. Similarly my abode means a place where the Lord lives. That place is nirguṇa because of the power of being related to the Lord, like a touchstone. Thus the eyes and other senses become nirguṇa by contact with the Lord. Thus it is said:

*diviṣṭhās tatra paśyanti sarvān eva caturbhujān*

**The devatās see that persons living in the Lord’s abode of Puri have four arms. (Brahma Purāṇa)**

Śrīdhara comments, “The abode of the Lord is nirguṇa because it is the place of his appearance.”

### Text 26

*sāttvikaḥ kārako ’saṅgī rāgāndho rājasaḥ smṛtaḥ*  
*tāmasaḥ smṛti-vibhraṣṭo nirguṇo mad-apāśrayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A performer of action who is free of attachment is in** ***sattva*****, a performer of action overcome by sense objects is in** ***rajas*****, and a performer of action who is devoid of inquiry is in** ***tamas.*** **But a performer of action who has taken shelter of me alone is beyond the** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 136:**

Having described the Lord’s abode as spiritual, all the activities related to the Lord are described as spiritual. The meaning is that the activities take up the guṇas by the mentality of the agent rather than the objects involved. The body of the person with a mentality of sattva develops sattva, transforming the lower guṇas.

### Text 27

*sāttviky ādhyātmikī śraddhā karma-śraddhā tu rājasī*  
*tāmasy adharme yā śraddhā mat-sevāyāṁ tu nirguṇā*

**Перевод:**

**Faith in** ***ātmā*** **is in** ***sattva*****, faith in prescribed** ***karma*** **is in** ***rajas*****, faith in irreligious activities is in** ***tamas,*** **but faith in my devotional service is beyond the** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 137:**

After explaining that all actions related to the Lord are nirguṇa, the Lord explains that faith, the cause of action, is also nirguṇa if related to the Lord. *Adharma* means “inferior dharma.”

### Text 29

*sāttvikaṁ sukham ātmotthaṁ viṣayotthaṁ tu rājasam*  
*tāmasaṁ moha-dainyotthaṁ nirguṇaṁ mad-apāśrayam*

**Перевод:**

**Happiness arising from knowledge of** ***ātmā*** **is in** ***sattva,*** **happiness arising from sense objects is in** ***rajas*** **and happiness arising from delusion and degradation is in** ***tamas.*** **But that happiness arising from activities related to me is beyond the** ***guṇas.***

**Комментарий:**

He speaks of happiness beyond the guṇas. Bhakti should be understood to be beyond the guṇas with hearing and other activities as the main cause.

*śuśrūṣoḥ śraddadhānasya vāsudeva-kathā-ruciḥ \|*  
*syān mahat-sevayā viprāḥ puṇya-tīrtha-niṣevaṇāt \|\|*

**O** ***brāhmaṇas!*** **Attraction for topics concerning Kṛṣṇa will arise by service to the great devotees, followed by faith, by surrender to the feet of the pure** ***guru*****, and by the desire to hear. (SB** [**1.2.16**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-1/2.16)**)**

The following are all devoid of relationship with the Lord:

*dravyaṁ deśaḥ phalaṁ kālo jñānaṁ karma ca kārakaḥ*  
*śraddhāvasthākṛtir niṣṭhā trai-guṇyaḥ sarva eva hi*

**Therefore food, place, result of activity, time, knowledge, work, the performer of work, faith, state of consciousness, species of life and destination after death are all based on the three** ***guṇas. (*****SB** [**11.2.30**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.30)**)**

### Text 32

*etāḥ saṁsṛtayaḥ puṁso guṇa-karma-nibandhanāḥ*  
*yeneme nirjitāḥ saumya guṇā jīvena citta-jāḥ*  
*bhakti-yogena man-niṣṭho mad-bhāvāya prapadyate*

**Перевод:**

**O gentle Uddhava, all these items related to the** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma*** **are causes of** ***saṁsāra.*** **The living entity conquers these** ***guṇas*****, manifested from the mind, by** ***bhakti-yoga.*** **Dedicated only to me, he surrenders and attains a loving relation to me.**

**Комментарий:**

He surrenders and attains prema for me (*mad-bhāvāya*).

### Text 33

*tasmād deham imaṁ labdhvā jñāna-vijñāna-sambhavam*  
*guṇa-saṅgaṁ vinirdhūya māṁ bhajantu vicakṣaṇāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore, having achieved this human form of life, which allows one to develop knowledge and realization through** ***bhakti,*** **those who are intelligent should free themselves from all contamination of the** ***guṇas*** **and worship me.**

**Комментарий:**

Already it was said:

*tasmāt tvam uddhavotsṛjya codanāṁ praticodanām*  
*pravṛttiṁ ca nivṛttiṁ ca śrotavyaṁ śrutam eva ca*  
*mām ekam eva śaraṇam ātmānaṁ sarva-dehinām*  
*yāhi sarvātma-bhāvena mayā syā hy akuto-bhayaḥ*

**O Uddhava! Give up duties and forbidden acts, the path of enjoyment and the path of renunciation, desire to hear more about** ***dharma,*** **and what you have already heard about** ***dharma.*** **Surrender to me alone, the soul of all beings, using all methods, with** ***bhāva*** **in your mind. You will be fearless by my mercy. (SB** [**11.12.14-15**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/12.14)**)**

Again it is stated that worship of the Lord is the highest process.

### Text 34

*niḥsaṅgo māṁ bhajed vidvān apramatto jitendriyaḥ*  
*rajas tamaś cābhijayet sattva-saṁsevayā muniḥ*

**Перевод:**

**A wise sage, free from all material association and alert, should subdue his senses and worship me. As a preliminary, he should conquer** ***rajas*** **and t*****amas*** **by engaging himself only with things in** ***sattva.***

**Комментарий:**

This is repeated in the first line.

### Text 35

*sattvaṁ cābhijayed yukto nairapekṣyeṇa śānta-dhīḥ*  
*sampadyate guṇair mukto jīvo jīvaṁ vihāya mām*

**Перевод:**

**Then, being fixed in devotional service, the sage should also conquer** ***sattva*** **by indifference toward it, arising from** ***bhakti.*** **Thus pacified within his mind, the** ***jīva,*** **freed from the** ***guṇas,*** **giving up the subtle body, attains me.**

**Комментарий:**

By bhakti, the jīva attains me.

## Chapter Twenty-six

### Text 3

*saṅgaṁ na kuryād asatāṁ śiśnodara-tṛpāṁ kvacit*  
*tasyānugas tamasy andhe pataty andhānugāndha-vat*

**Перевод:**

**One should never associate with materialists, who are dedicated to gratifying their genitals and bellies. By following even one such person, one falls into the deepest pit of darkness, just like a blind man who follows another blind man.**

**Комментарий:**

One should not take association of the materialists.

### Text 6

*kāmān atṛpto ’nujuṣan kṣullakān varṣa-yāminīḥ*  
*na veda yāntīr nāyāntīr urvaśy-ākṛṣṭa-cetanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Although for many years Purūravā had enjoyed sex pleasure in the evening hours, still he was not satisfied by such insignificant enjoyment. His mind was so attracted to Urvaśī that he did not notice how the nights were coming and going.**

**Комментарий:**

This was the condition of her remaining on earth.

### Text 24

*tasmāt saṅgo na kartavyaḥ strīṣu straiṇeṣu cendriyaiḥ*  
*viduṣāṁ cāpy avisrabdhaḥ ṣaḍ-vargaḥ kim u mādṛśām*

**Перевод:**

**Therefore one should never let his senses associate freely with women or with men attached to women. Even those who are highly learned cannot trust the six senses; what to speak, then, of foolish persons like me.**

**Комментарий:**

One should not associate with even one of the senses such as the ear, even if one has knowledge.

### Text 25

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*evaṁ pragāyan nṛpa-deva-devaḥ sa urvaśī-lokam atho vihāya*  
*ātmānam ātmany avagamya māṁ vai upāramaj jñāna-vidhūta-mohaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: Having thus chanted this song, Mahārāja Purūravā, enjoyer among the** ***devatās*** **and human beings, gave up the planet of Urvaśī. His illusion destroyed by knowledge, he realized me by** ***bhakti*** **in his mind and gave up his body.**

**Комментарий:**

His illusion was removed by some previous knowledge about the mind being stolen by a woman. This arose from knowledge of the Lord. The advice given later is suitable:

*tato duḥsaṅgam utsṛjya satsu sajjeta buddhimān*  
*santa evāsya chindanti mano-vyāsaṅgam uktibhiḥ*

**An intelligent person should therefore reject all bad association and instead take up the association of devotees. Only they can destroy the dangerous attachments in the mind by their words. (SB** [**11.26.26**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/26.26)**)**

### Text 26

*tato duḥsaṅgam utsṛjya satsu sajjeta buddhimān*  
*santa evāsya chindanti mano-vyāsaṅgam uktibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**An intelligent person should therefore reject all bad association and instead take up the association of devotees. Only they can destroy the dangerous attachments in the mind by their words.**

**Комментарий:**

Just giving up bad association is nothing. One should associate with devotees.

### Text 27

*santo ’napekṣā mac-cittāḥ praśāntāḥ sama-darśinaḥ*  
*nirmamā nirahaṅkārā nirdvandvā niṣparigrahāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**My devotees fix their minds on me and do not depend upon anything material. They are always peaceful, endowed with equal vision, and free from possessiveness, false ego, duality and attachment.**

**Комментарий:**

Fixing the mind on the Lord is further described. Not depending on anything else, means meditating only on subjects describing the form and qualities of the Lord.

*viṣayān dhyāyataś citta viṣayeṣu viṣajjate*  
*mām anusmarataś citta mayy eva pravilīyate*

**The mind of a person meditating upon the sense objects becomes attached to those objects. The mind of a person who constantly remembers me becomes absorbed in me. (SB** [**11.14.27**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.27)**)**

### Text 28

*teṣu nityaṁ mahā-bhāga mahā-bhāgeṣu mat-kathāḥ*  
*sambhavanti hi tā nṝṇāṁ juṣatāṁ prapunanty agham*

**Перевод:**

**O greatly fortunate Uddhava! In the association of such saintly devotees there arises constant discussion of me, and those topics destroy the sins of the speaker and listener.**

**Комментарий:**

The topics destroy sins completely (*pra*) since they are purified naturally.

### Text 29

*tā ye śṛṇvanti gāyanti hy anumodanti cādṛtāḥ*  
*mat-parāḥ śraddadhānāś ca bhaktiṁ vindanti te mayi*

**Перевод:**

**Whoever attentively hears, chants and relishes these topics about me becomes faithfully dedicated to me and achieves** ***bhakti*** **to me.**

**Комментарий:**

The topics not only purify but bestow bhakti by practice.

### Text 30

*bhaktiṁ labdhavataḥ sādhoḥ kim anyad avaśiṣyate*  
*mayy ananta-guṇe brahmaṇy ānandānubhavātmani*

**Перевод:**

**What more remains to be accomplished for the perfect devotee after achieving devotional service unto me, with unlimited qualities in** ***prema,*** **accompanied by the bliss of liberation in Brahman?**

**Комментарий:**

Bhakti is the result of bhakti. The cause is the Lord (*mayi*) who is the greatest of all (*brahmaṇi*), and thus endowed with unlimited qualities. *Ānandād dhīmāni bhūtāni jāyante*: the living beings were born from bliss. (Taittirīya Upaniṣad) *Ko hy evānyāt kaḥ prāṇyād*: who could breathe without the Lord? (Taittirīya Upaniṣad)

Among his unlimited qualities, he has unlimited sweet qualities. That produces the bliss in his greatness, and makes him the very highest in greatness. Because all of his portions are complete, bhakti is perfect. Because it is full of the same bliss, it should be utilized as the highest puruṣārtha.

### Text 31

*yathopaśrayamāṇasya bhagavantaṁ vibhāvasum*  
*śītaṁ bhayaṁ tamo ’pyeti sādhūn saṁsevatas tathā*

**Перевод:**

**Just as cold, fear and darkness are eradicated for one who has approached a fire, so apathy, fear and ignorance are destroyed for one engaged in serving the devotees of the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

After showing devotion in this chapter, as with previous topics, the main quality of the devotees previously described is discussed in four verses. One who serves fire for sacrifices (*bhagavantam*) has cold etc. eradicated.

### Text 34

*santo diśanti cakṣūṁsi bahir arkaḥ samutthitaḥ*  
*devatā bāndhavāḥ santaḥ santa ātmāham eva ca*

**Перевод:**

**My devotees bestow eyes to others so they can see me and are also give illumination to the eye, like the sun when it has risen in the sky. My devotees are the deities, the real friends, and the object of love. They are non-different from me.**

**Комментарий:**

You give shelter to the suffering people. What is the use of devotees? This verse answers.

### Text 35

*vaitasenas tato ’py evam urvaśyā loka-niṣpṛhaḥ*  
*mukta-saṅgo mahīm etām ātmārāmaś cacāra ha*

**Перевод:**

**Thus losing his desire to see Urvaśī, Mahārāja Purūravā began to wander the earth, free of all material association and completely satisfied within the self.**

**Комментарий:**

Having described what was intended, he concludes the story.

## Chapter Twenty-seven

### Text 1

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*kriyā-yogaṁ samācakṣva bhavad-ārādhanaṁ prabho*  
*yasmāt tvāṁ ye yathārcanti sātvatāḥ sātvatarṣabha*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Lord, O master of the devotees! Please explain your worship called** ***kriyā-yoga,*** **taking shelter of which the devotees worship you in the proper way.**

**Комментарий:**

Having heard the path for persons desiring liberation, Uddhava asks about the highest goal, for those who do not desire liberation.

### Text 2

*etad vadanti munayo muhur niḥśreyasaṁ nṛṇām*  
*nārado bhagavān vyāsa ācāryo ’ṅgirasaḥ sutaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**All the great sages repeatedly declare that such worship brings the greatest benefit in human life. This is the opinion of Nārada Muni, the great Vyāsadeva and my own** ***guru,*** **Bṛhaspati.**

**Комментарий:**

He gives proof of the process of deity worship in two verses. Uddhava accepted Bṛhaspati as his guru since he gave him teachings about the Lord. In the Bhāgavatam he comes in Saṅkarṣaṇa’s sampradāya.

### Text 3-4

*niḥsṛtaṁ te mukhāmbhojād yad āha bhagavān ajaḥ*  
*putrebhyo bhṛgu-mukhyebhyo devyai ca bhagavān bhavaḥ*  
*etad vai sarva-varṇānām āśramāṇāṁ ca sammatam*  
*śreyasām uttamaṁ manye strī-śūdrāṇāṁ ca māna-da*

**Перевод:**

**O most magnanimous Lord, the instructions on deity worship first emanated from your lotus mouth. Then they were spoken by the great Brahmā to his sons, headed by Bhṛgu, and by Śiva to his wife, Pārvatī. This type of worship is approved as the best for all the occupational and spiritual orders of society, even for women and** ***śūdras.***

**Комментарий:**

Remembrance etc. are not only for renounced persons, but for all people. It is approved by the authorities for all.

### Text 5

*etat kamala-patrākṣa karma-bandha-vimocanam*  
*bhaktāya cānuraktāya brūhi viśveśvareśvara*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed one! O Supreme Lord of all lords of the universe! Please explain this means of liberation from the bondage of work for your devotee having material attachments.**

**Комментарий:**

The lord of the puruṣāvataras (*viśvesvāreśvara*) is Svayam Bhagavān.

### Text 6

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*na hy anto ’nanta-pārasya karma-kāṇḍasya coddhava*  
*saṅkṣiptaṁ varṇayiṣyāmi yathāvad anupūrvaśaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O Uddhava! There is no end to the details of deity worship; so I shall explain this topic to you briefly, one step at a time.**

**Комментарий:**

Though deity worship is as you described, I did not speak about it because it is vast. Now, understanding your desire, I will describe it in summary.

### Text 7

*vaidikas tāntriko miśra iti me tri-vidho makhaḥ*  
*trayāṇām īpsitenaiva vidhinā māṁ samarcaret*

**Перевод:**

**One should carefully worship me by selecting one of the three methods of deity worship: Vedic, tantric or mixed.**

**Комментарий:**

One should worship by selecting one method according to one’s qualification and one’s faith. For instance, women and śūdras can only practice Pañcarātrika worship. Others can practice Vedic or mixed worship.

### Text 8

*yadā sva-nigamenoktaṁ dvijatvaṁ prāpya pūruṣaḥ*  
*yathā yajeta māṁ bhaktyā śraddhayā tan nibodha me*

**Перевод:**

**Now please listen faithfully as I explain exactly how a person who has achieved twice-born status through the relevant Vedic prescriptions should worship me with devotion.**

**Комментарий:**

According to one’s desire, being fixed in one’s dharma and having attained twice born status as its chief element, one should perform worship as the Lord instructs.

### Text 9

*arcāyāṁ sthaṇḍile ’gnau vā sūrye vāpsu hṛdi dvijaḥ*  
*dravyeṇa bhakti-yukto ’rcet sva-guruṁ mām amāyayā*

**Перевод:**

**A twice-born person should worship me, his worshipable Lord, without duplicity, offering appropriate paraphernalia in loving devotion to me in the deity form, in the ground, in fire, in the sun, in water or within the worshiper’s own heart.**

**Комментарий:**

According to his capacity, he should make a place for worship. The first option is the deity form, and then if that is not possible, the next option.

### Text 12

*śailī dāru-mayī lauhī lepyā lekhyā ca saikatī*  
*mano-mayī maṇi-mayī pratimāṣṭa-vidhā smṛtā*

**Перевод:**

**The deity form of the Lord is said to appear in eight varieties—stone, wood, metal, clay, paint, sand, the mind or jewels.**

**Комментарий:**

He describes the different types of deity according to what is most common*. Saikatī* means sand. However this form is for persons having material desires, and not for persons desiring prīti, since both maintaining or not maintaining that form are contrary to prīti.

### Text 13

*calācaleti dvi-vidhā pratiṣṭhā jīva-mandiram*  
*udvāsāvāhane na staḥ sthirāyām uddhavārcane*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! The deity form of the Lord, non-different from me, the shelter of all living entities, can be established in two ways: as a movable or immovable form. But a deity, having been installed, is not subject to calling and sending away.**

**Комментарий:**

There are two types of deity, the shelter of all jīvas (*jīva-mandiram*). Those deities which remain a long time are those made of stone etc. The Lord is not called and sent away from these forms, since when installed, the Lord is called to stay permanently.

It will be said that one should perform nyāsa on the deity (verse 24) one should worship the deity and decorate that form devotedly (verse 32). Finally he should place his head at the Lord’s feet (verse 42).

### Text 14

*asthirāyāṁ vikalpaḥ syāt sthaṇḍile tu bhaved dvayam*  
*snapanaṁ tv avilepyāyām anyatra parimārjanam*

**Перевод:**

**The deity made of perishable material can optionally be called forth and sent away, or installed. Bathing should be done with water except if the deity is made of clay, paint or wood, in which cases one wipes the form without using water.**

**Комментарий:**

The impermanent forms like sand, if they are to be destroyed, are installed and sent away. If they are to remain for some days for some reason, they are not sent away. What to speak of permanent forms. Painted forms such those made of wood should not be bathed. However, that is a general rule, since some wooden forms are bathed. Those made of *tinduka-sāra* are bathed. Using the mantras of the deity, worship is done with bathing, offering arghya etc.

### Text 15

*dravyaiḥ prasiddhair mad-yāgaḥ pratimādiṣv amāyinaḥ*  
*bhaktasya ca yathā-labdhair hṛdi bhāvena caiva hi*

**Перевод:**

**One should worship my forms by offering the most excellent paraphernalia. But a devotee completely freed from material desire may worship me with whatever he is able to obtain, and may even worship me within his heart with mental paraphernalia.**

**Комментарий:**

Persons with material desires must worship using excellent materials, since they may become duplicitous (and offer cheap objects out of avarice). This is not necessary as a rule for the devotee, since he does not have duplicity (*amayinaḥ*).

### Text 16-17

*snānālaṅkaraṇaṁ preṣṭham arcāyām eva tūddhava*  
*sthaṇḍile tattva-vinyāso vahnāv ājya-plutaṁ haviḥ*  
*sūrye cābhyarhaṇaṁ preṣṭhaṁ salile salilādibhiḥ*  
*śraddhayopāhṛtaṁ preṣṭhaṁ bhaktena mama vāry api*

**Перевод:**

**In worshiping the deity forms, my dear Uddhava, bathing and decoration are the most pleasing offerings. For the deity traced on sacred ground, the process of** ***tattva-vinyāsa*** **is most dear. Oblations of sesame and barley soaked in ghee are the preferred offering to the sacrificial fire. Worship consisting of offering** ***arghya*** **is preferred for the sun. One should worship me in the form of water by offering water itself. Actually, whatever is offered to me with faith by my devotee—even if only a little water—is most dear to me.**

**Комментарий:**

Bathing and decorating are most pleasing to the devotee and to me.

### Text 18

*bhūry apy abhaktopāhṛtaṁ na me toṣāya kalpate*  
*gandho dhūpaḥ sumanaso dīpo ’nnādyaṁ ca kiṁ punaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Even excellent presentations do not satisfy me if they are offered by nondevotees. But I am pleased by any insignificant offering made by devotees, what to speak of a presentations of fragrant oil, incense, flowers and palatable foods.**

**Комментарий:**

The statement starting with faith (*śraddhayā*) from the previous verse is understood in the second line also. If the devotee is eager to serve me with no other desires, fragrance etc. are dear to me. What more can be said?

### Text 20

*kṛta-nyāsaḥ kṛta-nyāsāṁ mad-arcāṁ pāṇināmṛjet*  
*kalaśaṁ prokṣaṇīyaṁ ca yathāvad upasādhayet*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee should sanctify the various parts of his body by touching them and chanting** ***mantras.*** **He should do the same for my deity forms and then with his hands he should clean the deity of old flowers and the remnants of previous offerings. He should properly prepare the sacred pot and the vessel containing water for sprinkling.**

**Комментарий:**

In apply keśava-nyāsa and other nyāsas to the lower parts of the body, one should not place the devatās there, but simply touch the limbs while meditating on their forms and chanting their mantras. This is not to be done by the devotees. It is a method of purifying the body of material elements. He can think of his body as an associate of the Lord suitable for serving his cherished form of the Lord, according to his goal in service, as is favorable for himself. Where there are prescriptions for meditating on the form of oneself as the form of one’s deity, he meditates on his form as an associate of the Lord, since he is fixed in pure bhakti. Prescriptions for meditating on being one with the Lord are for general worshippers. The associates have forms of śuddha-sattva manifesting from the Lord’s cit-śakti.

### Text 21

*tad-adbhir deva-yajanaṁ dravyāṇy ātmānam eva ca*  
*prokṣya pātrāṇi trīṇy adbhis tais tair dravyaiś ca sādhayet*

**Перевод:**

**Then, with the water of that** ***prokṣaṇīya*** **vessel he should sprinkle the area where the deity is being worshiped, the offerings that are going to be presented, and his own body. Next he should prepare with various auspicious substances and water three vessels.**
