**Комментарий:**

Two verses describe preparatory acts.

### Text 23

*piṇḍe vāyv-agni-saṁśuddhe hṛt-padma-sthāṁ parāṁ mama*  
*aṇvīṁ jīva-kalāṁ dhyāyen nādānte siddha-bhāvitām*

**Перевод:**

**The worshiper should meditate upon my subtle form, whose portion is the** ***jīva*** **which is situated within the worshiper’s own body, now purified by air and fire. This form of the Lord is experienced by self-realized sages in the last part of the vibration of the sacred syllable** ***oṁ.***

**Комментарий:**

*Śruti* says

*yo vedādau svaraḥ prokto vedānte ca pratiṣṭhitaḥ \|*  
*tasya prakṛti-līnasya yaḥ paraḥ sa maheśvaraḥ \|\|*

**The sound pronounced in the beginning and end of the Vedas remains supreme as the ruler when prakṛti merges in the Lord. (Mahā-nārāyaṇa Upaniṣad)**

Since the sound is within pranava, it is without form. It is chanted unlimitedly, since its chanting is the Vedas and concluded the Vedas. The form of prakṛti disappears in the Lord. He remains supreme, the controller.

### Text 24

*tayātma-bhūtayā piṇḍe vyāpte sampūjya tan-mayaḥ*  
*āvāhyārcādiṣu sthāpya nyastāṅgaṁ māṁ prapūjayet*

**Перевод:**

**When Paramātmā pervades the devotee’s body, the devotee, filled with Paramātmā, worships the Paramātmā there, and then being filled with Paramātmā, one calls Paramātmā into the deity form and fixes him there. Performing** ***nyāsa*** **on the deity’s limbs, he then worships that form.**

**Комментарий:**

Filled with the form of Paramātmā (*ātma-bhūtayā*), he calls the Lord. Thinking himself non-different from the qualities of the Lord, he worships the deity.

### Text 25-26

*pādyopasparśārhaṇādīn upacārān prakalpayet*  
*dharmādibhiś ca navabhiḥ kalpayitvāsanaṁ mama*  
*padmam aṣṭa-dalaṁ tatra karṇikā-kesarojjvalam*  
*ubhābhyāṁ veda-tantrābhyāṁ mahyaṁ tūbhaya-siddhaye*

**Перевод:**

**After making my seat in the form of an eight-petalled lotus effulgent with a pericarp and stamens, endowed with the personified deities of religion, knowledge, renunciation and opulence and with my nine spiritual energies, following the regulations of both the Vedas and the** ***tantras,*** **he should offer me water for washing the feet, water for washing the mouth,** ***arghya*** **and other items of worship. By this process he achieves both material enjoyment and liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 286:**

Some explain them in another way according to Nārada-pañcarātra. There it is said *adharmādy-ādya-catuṣkaṁ tu aśreyasi niyojanam*: adharma, ajñāna, avairāgya and anaiśvarya are used in inauspicious events. The meaning is that these items are mentioned with the meaning that the Lord is the antaryāmī śakti in these items.

### Text 28

*nandaṁ sunandaṁ garuḍaṁ pracaṇḍaṁ caṇḍaṁ eva ca*  
*mahābalaṁ balaṁ caiva kumudaṁ kamudekṣaṇam*

**Перевод:**

**One should worship the Lord’s associates Nanda and Sunanda, Garuḍa, Pracaṇḍa and Caṇḍa, Mahābala and Bala, and Kumuda and Kumudekṣaṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 285:**

The devatās such as Durgā and Ganeśa in the worship of the āvaraṇa during worship are seen as the Lord’s eternal servitors in Vaikuṇṭha such as Viṣvaksena. They are not the Ganeśa and Durgā composed of māyā-śakti.

*pravartate yatra rajas tamas tayoḥ*  
*sattvaṁ ca miśraṁ na ca kāla-vikramaḥ \|*  
*na yatra māyā kim utāpare harer*  
*anuvratā yatra surāsurārcitāḥ \|\|*

In Vaikuṇṭha there are no rajas or tamas, and no sattva mixed with rajas and tamas. There is no influence of time. There is no influence of māyā at all, what to speak of its products such as material elements. In Vaikuṇṭha the inhabitants are fully dedicated to the Lord and are worshipable by the devas, asuras and devotees. (SB [2.9.10](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-2/9.10))

Rather these associates are filled with the Lord’s svarūpa-śakti. The controlling position of the special function of the śakti emanating from Kṛṣṇa’s svarūpa is described in the śruti and tantras.

In the discussion of Śruti-vidyā it is said:

*bhaktir bhajana-sampattir bhajate prakṛtiḥ priyam \|*  
*jñāyate ’tyanta-duḥkhena seyaṁ prakṛtir ātmanaḥ \|\|*  
*durgeti gīyate sadbhir akhaṇḍa-rasa-vallabhā \|\|*

**Bhakti, who is known as prakṛti, endowed with a wealth of worship, worships her dear Lord. She, the prakṛti of the Lord, is known with great difficulty. Thus the devotees glorify her, dear to the Lord of rasa, as Durgā (difficult to approach).**

Her non-difference from the Lord is stated in Gautamīya-kalpa:

*yaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ saiva durgā syād yā durgā kṛṣṇa eva saḥ*

**He who is Kṛṣṇa is Durgā. She who is Durgā is Kṛṣṇa.**

*tvam eva parameśāni asyādhiṣṭhātṛ-devatā*

**You are the supreme goddess, the controlling deity.**

This hints at worshipping the Lord and Durgā as non-different, just as worship of the universal form and Mahā-viṣṇu are treated as non-different.

Durgā is a portion of māyā, engaged to serve by protecting the mantra in this world, which is dependent on the Lord. She is the servant of the spiritual Durgā and is not the controller of service in bhakti. Padma Purāṇa describes the āvaraṇa devatās beyond māyā in Vaikuṇṭha:

*satyācyutānanta-durgā-viṣvaksena-gajānanāḥ \|*  
*śaṅkha-padma-nidhī lokāś caturthāvaraṇaṁ śubham \|\|*  
*aindra-pāvaka-yāmyāni nairṛtaṁ vāruṇaṁ tathā \|*  
*vāyavyaṁ saumyam aiśānaṁ saptamaṁ munibhiḥ smṛtam \|\|*  
*sādhyā marud-gaṇāś caiva viśvedevās tathaiva ca \|*  
*nityāḥ sarve pare dhāmni ye cānye ca divaukasaḥ \|\|*  
*te vai prākṛta-loke’sminn anityās tridaśeśvarāḥ \|*  
*te ha nākaṁ mahimānaḥ sacanta iti vai śrutiḥ \|\|*

**Durgā, Viṣvaksena and Ganeśa made of eternity, knowledge and bliss, Śaṅkha-nidhi and Padma-nidhi are in the fourth glorious circle. Indra, Agni, Yama, Nairṛta, Varuṇa, Vāyu, Saumya and Śiva are in the seventh circle. The Sādhyas, Maruts, and Viśvadevas are also there. All the others in the spiritual world are also eternal. Those in the material heavenly planets are temporary devatās.**

Śruti says “The devatās associate with the Lord in the spiritual world.” (Padma Purāṇa 6.228.60, 64-66)

They are aṁśas of the Lord. In describing the different devatās of the eighteen-syllable mantra it is said:

*sarvatra deva-devo ’sau gopa-veśa-dharo hariḥ \|*  
*kevalaṁ rūpa-bhedena nāma-bhedaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\|*

**The Lord, wearing the dress of a cowherd, the Lord of lords, having various names, is everywhere glorified in different forms.**

The ananya-bhaktas should not worry that the spiritual devatās share the same name with their material counterparts. Viṣvaksena and others should be honored since they are engaged in eternal service to the Lord in Vaikuṇṭha. Not worshipping them is considered a fault. It is said “He who never identifies himself with, feels kinship with, worships or even visits those who are wise in spiritual truth (devotees)—such a person is no better than a cow or an ass.” (SB [10.84.13](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/84.13))

*arcayitvā tu govindaṁ tadīyān nārcayet tu yaḥ*  
*na sa bhāgavato jñeyaḥ kevalam dāmbhikaḥ smṛtaḥ*

**He who after worshipping Govinda does not worship his followers is not known as devotee. He is simply a pretender. (Padma Purāṇa 6.253.177)**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 286:**

In the pītha-pūja, worship of the guru’s shoes is prescribed in the Lord’s abode. Just as the Lord exists in the form of guru in an individual form (vyasti-rūpa) as a devotee avatāra, the Lord exists in the pīṭha in his spiritual abode in the form of guru’s shoes as the total form of guru (samaṣṭi-rūpa), being a direct avatāra of the Lord, to the deity’s left side.

### Text 32

*vastropavītābharaṇa- patra-srag-gandha-lepanaiḥ*  
*alaṅkurvīta sa-prema mad-bhakto māṁ yathocitam*

**Перевод:**

**My devotee should then lovingly decorate me with clothing, a sacred thread, various ornaments,** ***tulasī*** **garlands, and he should anoint my body with unguents, all in the prescribed manner.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 286:**

The great devotees see the deity directly as the Supreme Lord since by seeing the deity and Lord as different, their bhakti will be interrupted.

The devotee identifies the deity as me *(mām).* The devotee decorates the deity (*mām*) in prema (*sa-prema*).

Viṣṇu says to Ambarīṣa in Viṣṇu-dharma:

*tasyāṁ cittaṁ samāveśya tyaja cānyān vyāpāśrayān \|*  
*pūjitā saiva te bhaktyā dhyātā caivopakāriṇī \|\|*  
*gacchaṁs tiṣṭhan svapan bhuñjaṁs tām evāgre ca pṛṣṭhataḥ \|*  
*upary-adhas tathā pārśve citnayaṁs tām athātmanaḥ \|\|*

**Concentrating the mind on that form, giving up other thoughts, one should worship and meditate on the form, which is the benefactor. While moving, standing, lying down, and eating, one should think of the deity of the Lord to be in front, behind, above, below and on the side of oneself.**

In worshipping Kṛṣṇa, the sacred thread is offered. Gautamīya-tantra says *yajña-sūtraṁ tato dadyād athavā svarṇa-nirmitam*: one should then offer a sacred thread or one made of gold. Narasiṁha Purāṇa says:

*trivṛc-chuklaṁ ca pītaṁ ca paṭṭa-śūkādi-nirmitam*  
*yajñopavītaṁ govinde dattvā vedāntago bhavet*

**One should offer to Kṛṣṇa a yellow sacred thread of three strands made of silk. One attains the end of the Vedas.**

### Text 34

*guḍa-pāyasa-sarpīṁṣi śaṣkuly-āpūpa-modakān*  
*saṁyāva-dadhi-sūpāṁś ca naivedyaṁ sati kalpayet*

**Перевод:**

**If possible, the devotee should arrange to offer me sugar candy, sweet rice, ghee,** ***śaṣkulī, āpūpa, modaka*****,** ***saṁyāva,*** **yogurt, vegetable soups and other palatable foods.**

**Комментарий:**

*Śaśkuli* is a sweet in the shape of an ear and fried in ghee. *Āpūpa* isknown as *puyā.* It is also called āpūpikam. Āpūpa is poetic license. The naivedya is described in Gautamīya-tantra:

*nivedayed uttamānnaṁ na kadannaṁ kadācana \|*  
*uttamaṁ vidhinā prāptam athavā yad ayācitam \|\|*  
*sva-vittopacitaṁ vātha kṛṣṇāya parikalpayet \|*  
*śūdrāl labdhaṁ chalāl labdham atha vārddhuṣikācitam \|*  
*ity ādy annaṁ kad-annaṁ tu dānān narakam āvahet \|\|*

**One should offer the best food, never something inferior. It should be attained by by śiloñcha, or not by begging. One can obtain by buying with one’s wealth. One should offer it to Kṛṣṇa. Food obtained from a śūdra, by deceit, or from vārddhuṣika.? By offering such food one goes to hell.**

Hārita-smṛti says:

*nābhakṣyaṁ naivedyārthe bhakṣyeṣv apy ājya-mahiṣī-kṣīraṁ pañca-nakhā matsyāś ca*

**The following are not to be offered: goat and buffalo milk, fish, five nailed animals.**

*abhakṣyaṁ cāpy ahṛdyaṁ ca naivedyaṁ na nivedayet \|*  
*keśa-kīṭāvapannaṁ ca tathā cāvihitaṁ ca yat \|\|*

**One should not offer inedible food or unpleasing food. Foods filled with hair or insects are not proper. (Viṣṇu-dharmottara)**

One should see Hari-bhakti-vilāsa for the inedible foods.

### Text 37

*paristīryātha paryukṣed anvādhāya yathā-vidhi*  
*prokṣaṇyāsādya dravyāṇi prokṣyāgnau bhāvayeta mām*

**Перевод:**

**After spreading** ***kuśa*** **grass around the pit and sprinkling water around it, one should offer wood into the fire according to the prescribed rules. Then one should arrange the items to be offered as oblations and should sanctify them with water from the sprinkling vessel. The worshiper should next meditate upon me within the fire.**

**Комментарий:**

One should meditate on me as the antaryāmī in the fire.

### Text 42

*abhyarcyātha namaskṛtya pārṣadebhyo baliṁ haret*  
*mūla-mantraṁ japed brahma smaran nārāyaṇātmakam*

**Перевод:**

**Having thus worshiped the Lord in the sacrificial fire, the devotee should offer his obeisances to the Lord’s personal associates by bowing down and should then present offerings to them. He should then chant quietly the** ***mūla-mantra*** **of the deity of the Lord, remembering Brahman as Nārāyaṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

*punar ācamanaṁ dadyāt karodvartanam eva ca \|*  
*sa-karpūraṁ ca tāmbūlam kuryān nīrājanaṁ tathā \|\|*

**One should again offer ācamana and wipe his hand. One should offer āroti with camphor and offer tāmbūla. (Hari-bhakti-vilāsa)**

*atha bhuktavate dattvā jalaiḥ karpūra-vāsitaiḥ \|*  
*ācamanaṁ ca tāmbūlam candanaiḥ kara-mārjanam \|\|*

**After eating one should offer ācamana using water scented with camphor, as well as tāmbula, and wipe his hand. (Hari-bhakti-vilāsa)**

*sneha-saṁyukta-vipula-vartikābhir anekadhā \|*  
*ārātribhir anekābhiḥ sthāpitābhiḥ prayatnataḥ \|\|*  
*padma-svastika-rūpeṇa haṁsākāreṇa cānagha!*  
*bhrāmayed raghunāthasya purastāt prayato’nvaham \|\|*

**One should offer āroti with attention many times, using thick wicks full of ghee, waving lamps having the shape of a lotus, svastika or swan in front of the Lord daily. (Agastya-saṁhitā)**

### Text 44

*upagāyan gṛṇan nṛtyan karmāṇy abhinayan mama*  
*mat-kathāḥ śrāvayan śṛṇvan muhūrtaṁ kṣaṇiko bhavet*

**Перевод:**

**Singing along with others, chanting loudly and dancing, acting out my transcendental pastimes, and hearing and telling stories about me, the devotee should for some time absorb himself in such festivity.**

**Комментарий:**

He should become absorbed in joy by the singing etc. (*kṣanikaḥ*).

### Text 45

*stavair uccāvacaiḥ stotraiḥ paurāṇaiḥ prākṛtair api*  
*stutvā prasīda bhagavann iti vandeta daṇḍa-vat*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee should offer homage to the Lord with all kinds of hymns and prayers, both from the Purāṇas and from ordinary traditions. Praying, “O Lord, please be merciful to me!” he should fall down flat like a rod to offer his obeisances.**

**Комментарий:**

He should praise the Lord by self-composed verses (*stavaiḥ*) in various ways (*uccāvacaiḥ*). *Stotraiḥ* refers to verses composed by previous sages, from the Purāṇas, tantras and Śrauta works. One should also recite verses from contemporary authors (prākrṭaiḥ).

*yaḥ svayaṁ gadya-padyābhyāṁ ghaṭitābhyāṁ namaskṛtiḥ \|*  
*kriyate bhakti-yuktena vācikas tūttamas tu saḥ \|\|*

**He who with devotion praises the Lord through prose and verses composed by himself is the best. (Kālikā Purāṇa)**

### Text 46

*śiro mat-pādayoḥ kṛtvā bāhubhyāṁ ca parasparam*  
*prapannaṁ pāhi mām īśa bhītaṁ mṛtyu-grahārṇavāt*

**Перевод:**

**Placing his head at the feet of the deity with arms together, he prays, “O my Lord, please protect me, as I am surrendered unto you. I am most fearful of this ocean of material existence, the mouth of death.”**

**Комментарий:**

Offer prayers from far away, he then speaks at a slight distance. It is forbidden to be very close, in the deity room.

*agre pṛṣṭhe vāma-bhāge samīpe garbha-mandire \|*  
*japa-homa-namaskārān na kuryāt keśavālaye \|\|*

**One should not offer respects, japa or homa in front of the deity, behind, to the left side, close, or in the deity room. (Hari-bhakti-vilāsa)**

*padbhyāṁ karābhyāṁ jānubhyām urasā śirasā dṛśā \|*  
*manasā vacasā ceti praṇāmo’ṣṭāṅga īritaḥ \|\|*

**One should offer respects with eight limbs: two feet, two hands, two knees, chest, head, eyes, mind and words. (Hari-bhakti-vilāsa)**

*jānubhyāṁ caiva bāhubhyāṁ śirasā vacasā dhiyā \|*  
*pañcāṅgakaḥ praṇāmaḥ syāt pūjāsu pravarāv imau \|\|*

**One can offer respects with five limbs: two knees, two arms, head, words and intelligence. These two types are best. (Hari-bhakti-vilāsa)**

### Text 47

*iti śeṣāṁ mayā dattāṁ śirasy ādhāya sādaram*  
*udvāsayec ced udvāsyaṁ jyotir jyotiṣi tat punaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Praying in this way, the devotee should respectfully place upon his head the remnants given by me. And if the particular deity is meant to be sent away at the end of the worship, then this should be performed. The devotee once again places the light of the deity’s presence inside the light of the lotus within his own heart.**

**Комментарий:**

He places the light, the form of the Lord, in his heart, like a small flame which has been separated from a big fire.

### Text 48

*arcādiṣu yadā yatra śraddhā māṁ tatra cārcayet*  
*sarva-bhūteṣv ātmani ca sarvātmāham avasthitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Whenever one develops faith in me—in my form as the deity or in other forms—one should worship me in that form. I certainly exist both within all created beings and also separately in my original form, since I am the supreme soul.**

**Комментарий:**

Though the major elements of arcana have been described, faith is the cause of my appearance. Without that, I do not manifest directly. *Viral aviduṣam*: the ignorant saw only a material form. He speaks to indicate the necessity of faith. If there is faith, it is not rare for the Lord to appear. He manifested to Hiraṇyakaśipu from the pillar.

### Text 50

*mad-arcāṁ sampratiṣṭhāpya mandiraṁ kārayed dṛḍham*  
*puṣpodyānāni ramyāṇi pūjā-yātrotsavāśritān*

**Перевод:**

**One should engage religious, wealthy men who take it as their duty to support daily worship, special occasions and festivals, in building temples and attractive flower gardens for the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

The statement means that one should necessarily establish deities.

### Text 53

*mām eva nairapekṣyeṇa bhakti-yogena vindati*  
*bhakti-yogaṁ sa labhata evaṁ yaḥ pūjayeta mām*

**Перевод:**

**But one who simply engages in devotional service with no motives at all attains** ***prema,*** **and by** ***prema*** **attains me.**

**Комментарий:**

One should have no other motive than bhakti (*nairapekṣyeṇa*).

### Text 55

*kartuś ca sārather hetor anumoditur eva ca*  
*karmaṇāṁ bhāginaḥ pretya bhūyo bhūyasi tat-phalam*

**Перевод:**

**Not only the performer of the theft but also anyone who assists him, instigates the crime or applauds it, must also receive this result after death, since they must share the** ***karma.*** **According to the gravity of the crime, they must suffer a proportionate consequence.**

**Комментарий:**

The results accrue not only to the thief (or the donor who took back the gift) but to all involved.

## Chapter Twenty-eight

### Text 1

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*para-svabhāva-karmāṇi na praśaṁsen na garhayet*  
*viśvam ekātmakaṁ paśyan prakṛtyā puruṣeṇa ca*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: One should neither praise nor criticize the conditioned nature and activities of other persons. Rather, one should see this world along with** ***prakṛti*** **and the** ***jīva*** **as one with the Lord.**

**Комментарий:**

He speaks in order to reject external vision in bhakti-yoga, or he speaks jñāna along with its practice as difficult to show the easy and successful path of bhakti. One should see the world as one with the Lord, along with prakṛti and jīva. One should see Parmātmā as the one ātmā:

*ādāv ante janānāṁ sad bahir antaḥ parāvaram*  
*jñānaṁ jñeyaṁ vaco vācyaṁ tamo jyotis tv ayaṁ svayam*

**Brahman is what exists in the beginning and in the end. It is external objects and the** ***jīva*****. It is superior and inferior objects. It is the senses, the sense objects and objects expressed by words. Brahman is the** ***jīva*** **in ignorance and in knowledge. (SB** [**7.15.57**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/15.57)**)**

It will also be said:

*jñānaṁ viveko nigamas tapaś ca*  
*pratyakṣam aitihyam athānumānam*  
*ādy-antayor asya yad eva kevalaṁ*  
*kālaś ca hetuś ca tad eva madhye*

***Jñāna*** **gives power of discrimination. It is produced by knowledge of the Vedas, by following one’s** ***dharma*****, by personal realization, by instructions and by logical analysis. By this one realizes Brahman, the final cause, which reveals everything and which exists at the beginning and end of the universe, and during the interim as well. (SB** [**11.28.18**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/28.18)**)**

### Text 2

*para-svabhāva-karmāṇi yaḥ praśaṁsati nindati*  
*sa āśu bhraśyate svārthād asaty abhiniveśataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***jñānī*** **who indulges in praising or criticizing the qualities and behavior of others will quickly fall from his position of** ***jñāna*** **by his entanglement in illusory dualities.**

**Комментарий:**

He will fall from being absorbed in the supreme. He will become absorbed in varieties, imagined as something other than they really are.

### Text 3

*taijase nidrayāpanne piṇḍa-stho naṣṭa-cetanaḥ*  
*māyāṁ prāpnoti mṛtyuṁ vā tadvan nānārtha-dṛk pumān*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the** ***jīva*** **when dreaming experiences false dreams and when in deep sleep loses consciousness and experiences a deathlike state, so a person experiencing material duality achieves confusion and destruction.**

**Комментарий:**

The different states of consciousness affect him. Without seeing oneness with Paramātmā, man sometimes loses external consciousness and attains a state of dreaming, filled with the Lord’s māyā-śakti, equivalent to the normal māyā in this world. Sometimes he loses external and internal consciousness and attains a state of deep sleep, equivalent to death. Similarly he sometimes attains a state of giving up his dead body and becoming absorbed in his body with birth, which is also caused by māyā, and becomes bewildered.

### Text 4

*kiṁ bhadraṁ kim abhadraṁ vā dvaitasyāvastunaḥ kiyat*  
*vācoditaṁ tad anṛtaṁ manasā dhyātam eva ca*

**Перевод:**

**That which is expressed by material words or contemplated by the mind in duality is false. What, therefore, is actually good or bad within this insubstantial world of duality, and how can the extent of such good and bad be measured?**

**Комментарий:**

It was said:

*ābādhito ’pi hy ābhāso yathā vastutayā smṛtaḥ*  
*durghaṭatvād aindriyakaṁ tadvad artha-vikalpitam*

**Just as illusions, though false, are accepted as real, so objects perceived by the senses are accepted as real, because of difficulty in proving them otherwise. (SB** [**7.15.58**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/15.58)**)**

In six and a half verses he explains how there is no distinction between praise and criticism, since duality of seeing different bodies is caused by concepts of duality. Duality (*dvaitam*) means to see the body as separate from Paramātmā.

### Text 5

*chāyā-pratyāhvayābhāsā hy asanto ’py artha-kāriṇaḥ*  
*evaṁ dehādayo bhāvā yacchanty ā-mṛtyuto bhayam*

**Перевод:**

**Although reflections, echoes and mirages are only illusory reflections of real things, they produce real experiences. In the same way, although the identification of the conditioned soul with the material body, mind and ego is illusory, this identification generates fear within him even up to the moment of death.**

**Комментарий:**

Since Paramātmā alone is the entity everywhere, other things do not exist. Why does man become absorbed in things as false as a rabbit’s horn? Or how does it happen?

### Text 6-7

*ātmaiva tad idaṁ viśvaṁ sṛjyate sṛjati prabhuḥ*  
*trāyate trāti viśvātmā hriyate haratīśvaraḥ*  
*tasmān na hy ātmano ’nyasmād anyo bhāvo nirūpitaḥ*  
*nirūpite ’yaṁ tri-vidhā nirmūla bhātir ātmani*  
*idaṁ guṇa-mayaṁ viddhi tri-vidhaṁ māyayā kṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**The Lord creates the universe and is created. He maintains the universe and is maintained. He destroys the universe and is destroyed. Nothing other than Paramātmā exists but he is different from everything. Perceptions of three types of suffering in Paramātmā are baseless. Know that the universe made of** ***guṇas*** **and three types of suffering are created by my inconceivable energy.**

**Комментарий:**

He explains how the universe is one with Paramātmā in two verses. The master of various śaktis (*prabhuḥ*), ātmā himself, creates this universe as his body. Thus he is personally created. And he maintains it, and is maintained.

Therefore, in this universe there is nothing other than him. Without a cause the effect cannot be accomplished. “Then he becomes transformed.” The verse answers. He is other than everything else, since he is not transformed, through his acintya-śakti. Unintelligent people thinking that he who is pure is subject to the three types of suffering is baseless, since he has no relationship with the world.

### Text 8

*etad vidvān mad-uditaṁ jñāna-vijñāna-naipuṇam*  
*na nindati na ca stauti loke carati sūrya-vat*

**Перевод:**

**One who has completely understood knowledge and specialized knowledge, as described herein by me, does not indulge in material criticism or praise. Like the sun, he acts equally to all.**

**Комментарий:**

How does one see everything as one with Paramātmā? One should follow what was already described.

### Text 9

*pratyakṣeṇānumānena nigamenātma-saṁvidā*  
*ādy-antavad asaj jñātvā niḥsaṅgo vicared iha*

**Перевод:**

**By direct perception, logical deduction, scriptural testimony and personal realization, one should know that this world has a beginning and an end and so is merely temporary. Thus one should live in this world without attachment.**

**Комментарий:**

There is two types of duality: one sees bodies and one imagines the source as something other than Paramātmā. Then one sees that everything is temporary. The ātmā is not attached in either case, since it is different from ātmā. Thus one should give up all attachments. One should know the world has a beginning and end (*ādy-antavat*).

### Text 10

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*naivātmano na dehasya saṁsṛtir draṣṭṛ-dṛśyayoḥ*  
*anātma-sva-dṛśor īśa kasya syād upalabhyate*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Lord! It is not possible for this material existence to be the experience of either the soul, who is the seer, or of the body, which is the seen object, since the soul is innately endowed with knowledge, and on the other hand, the material body is not a conscious, living entity. To whom, then, does this experience of material existence pertain?**

**Комментарий:**

Uddhava has some questions concerning the teachings that have been presented for the jīva destroying saṁsāra.

### Text 12

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*yāvad dehendriya-prāṇair ātmanaḥ sannikarṣaṇam*  
*saṁsāraḥ phalavāṁs tāvad apārtho ’py avivekinaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: As long as the foolish** ***jīva*** **is related to the material body, senses and** ***prāṇa,*** **his material existence continues to flourish, although it is based on illusion.**

**Комментарий:**

As long has the jīva has a relationship (*sannikarṣaṇam*) with the body, senses and prāṇa, saṁsāra will flourish. The cause is lack of discrimination (*avivekinaḥ*):

*bhayaṁ dvitīyābhiniveśataḥ syād*  
*īśād apetasya viparyayo 'smṛtiḥ*  
*tan-māyayāto budha ābhajet taṁ*  
*bhaktyaikayeśaṁ guru-devate ātmā*

**For the** ***jīva*** **averse to the Lord, there will be** ***saṁsāra*** **consisting of identity with body and lack of identity with the soul, because of his absorption in the material coverings on the soul, arising from the Lord’s** ***māyā.*** **Therefore, the intelligent person, taking** ***guru*** **as his Lord and very self, should fully worship the Lord with pure** ***bhakti.*** **(SB** [**11.2.37**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.37)**)**

The condition results from lack of knowledge of ātmā produced by māyā.

### Text 13

*arthe hy avidyamāne ’pi saṁsṛtir na nivartate*  
*dhyāyato viṣayān asya svapne ’narthāgamo yathā*

**Перевод:**

**Though objects are unreal (or temporary),** ***saṁsāra*** **does not cease, just as a person absorbed in unreal dream objects experiences fear.**

**Комментарий:**

Though born of ignorance, the relationship with other things produces obstacles. An example is added.

### Text 14

*yathā hy apratibuddhasya prasvāpo bahv-anartha-bhṛt*  
*sa eva pratibuddhasya na vai mohāya kalpate*

**Перевод:**

**Although while dreaming a person experiences many undesirable things, upon awakening, he is no longer confused by the dream experiences.**

**Комментарий:**

This is shown in reverse.

### Text 15

*śoka-harṣa-bhaya-krodha- lobha-moha-spṛhādayaḥ*  
*ahaṅkārasya dṛśyante janma-mṛtyuś ca nātmanaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Lamentation, elation, fear, anger, greed, confusion and hankering, as well as birth and death, are caused by the** ***ahaṅkāra*** **and not by the** ***ātmā***

**Комментарий:**

The conditions in relation to discerning the truth arise from ahaṅkāra, not from the ātmā. As long as ahaṅkāra produces identity with the two bodies, this condition continues. One does not see lamentation, joy and fear disappear, since one does not see this condition in deep sleep. And for the pure ātmā, there is no birth and death.

### Text 16

*dehendriya-prāṇa-mano-’bhimāno*  
*jīvo ’ntar-ātmā guṇa-karma-mūrtiḥ*  
*sūtraṁ mahān ity urudheva gītaḥ*  
*saṁsāra ādhāvati kāla-tantraḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***ahaṅkāra,*** **which is a covering on the** ***jīva,*** **which assumes its form by the** ***guṇas*** **and** ***karma*****, which is called by many names such as** ***sūtra, mahat-tattva*****, body, senses life air and mind, and which is dependent on the Lord, makes the** ***jīva*** **accept its qualities for continuation of** ***saṁsāra.***

**Комментарий:**

Saṁsāra is related to ahaṅkāra and liberation also is. The termination of ahaṅkāra means liberation. Ahaṅkāra, becoming fixing in its nature and spreading by ignorance, produces complete covering by upādhis. When there is no ahaṅkāra, there is no covering by upādhis. Though the qualities like lamentation are reflections of the ahaṅkāra’s svarūpa, the jīva develops identity with a body because of its relationship with it. Thus the jīva enters saṁsāra. By negation of ahaṅkāra the jīva is liberated.

### Text 17

*amūlam etad bahu-rūpa-rūpitaṁ*  
*mano-vacaḥ-prāṇa-śarīra-karma*  
*jñānāsinopāsanayā śitena*  
*cchittvā munir gāṁ vicaraty atṛṣṇaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Although** ***ahaṅkāra*** **has no factual basis, it is perceived in many forms—as the functions of the mind, speech, life air and bodily faculties. But with the sword of knowledge, sharpened by** ***bhakti*****, a sober sage will cut off** ***ahaṅkāra*** **and live in this world free from all material attachment.**

**Комментарий:**

Because ahaṅkāra is produced by ignorance, it has no basis (*amūlam*).

### Text 18

*jñānaṁ viveko nigamas tapaś ca*  
*pratyakṣam aitihyam athānumānam*  
*ādy-antayor asya yad eva kevalaṁ*  
*kālaś ca hetuś ca tad eva madhye*

**Перевод:**

***Jñāna*** **gives power of discrimination. It is produced by knowledge of the Vedas, by following one’s** ***dharma*****, by personal realization, by instructions and by logical analysis. By this one realizes Brahman, the final cause, which reveals everything and which exists at the beginning and end of the universe, and during the interim as well.**

**Комментарий:**

By five methods, one is taught knowledge and then one realizes Paramātmā. This is realized in two verses. Paramātmā, the real entity, in the form of knowledge, is the efficient and material cause and is the sole cause (*kevalam*) of the beginning and the end, since everything enters into Bhagavān. He possesses power to make everything disappear.

*Jñānam* is the cause of viveka or careful consideration. *Kāla* refers to the efficient cause and *hetuḥ* refers to the material cause.

### Text 19

*yathā hiraṇyaṁ sv-akṛtaṁ purastāt*  
*paścāc ca sarvasya hiraṇ-mayasya*  
*tad eva madhye vyavahāryamāṇaṁ*  
*nānāpadeśair aham asya tadvat*

**Перевод:**

**Gold alone is present before its manufacture into gold products, the gold alone remains after the products’ destruction, and the gold alone is the essential reality while it is utilized under various designations. Similarly, I alone exist before the creation of this universe, after its destruction and during its maintenance.**

**Комментарий:**

Being the supreme object, the Lord is the cause of everything.

### Text 20

*vijñānam etat triy-avastham aṅga*  
*guṇa-trayaṁ kāraṇa-karya-kartṛ*  
*samanvayena vyatirekataś ca*  
*yenaiva turyeṇa tad eva satyam*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Intelligence has three states: waking, dreaming and deep sleep, which arises from the conditions of cause, effect and doer. Everything arises by knowledge which pervades the other states. That fourth state is realized by** ***samādhi*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Because the jīva is connected with the objects of matter and remains there on his own in states of waking and sleeping, distinct from the Lord, upādhis cover the individual jīva, as the illusion of a body of a snake on seeing a rope. However, like the rope, the jīva is real. Paramātmā remains the master of all jīvas and the upādhis. This is explained in two verses.

*Yo vijñāne tiṣṭhan*: the jīva is situated in knowledge. (Bṛhad-ārāṇyaka Upaniṣad) The jīva covered by the upādhis of the three guṇas, develops three states: as effect, cause and doer. Collectively they are called *triy-avastham.* According to grammarians like Vyāḍi and Gālava, the form *triy-avastham* is permissible. It means “that state in which there are three guṇas.”

### Text 21

*na yat purastād uta yan na paścān*  
*madhye ca tan na vyapadeśa-mātram*  
*bhūtaṁ prasiddhaṁ ca pareṇa yad yat*  
*tad eva tat syād iti me manīṣā*

**Перевод:**

**That which did not exist in the past and will not exist in the future also has no existence for the period of its duration, but is only a superficial designation. In my opinion, whatever is created and revealed by a first object is ultimately only that first object.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of being covered by the three guṇas is described.

### Text 22

*avidyamāno ’py avabhāsate yo*  
*vaikāriko rājasa-sarga esaḥ*  
*brahma svayaṁ jyotir ato vibhāti*  
*brahmendriyārthātma-vikāra-citram*

**Перевод:**

**Although not existing previously, what appears to exist now, created by transformation, by** ***rajoguṇa,*** **is the effect of Brahman, which is independent, and the revealer of all else. Thus the universe whose variety is created by the senses, the sense objects, the mind and the elements of physical nature is only Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

The material world does not exist in pure Brahman. It exists as a disturbance caused by his śakti. The world’s variety is described. What manifests is revealed by Brahman.

### Text 23

*evaṁ sphutaṁ brahma-viveka-hetubhiḥ*  
*parāpavādena viśāradena*  
*chittvātma-sandeham upārameta*  
*svānanda-tuṣṭo ’khila-kāmukebhyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Thus, clearly understanding Brahman by discriminating logic, by skillfully rejecting one’s misidentification with matter, one should cut to pieces all doubts about** ***ātmā*** **and, satisfied in the soul’s natural bliss, withdraw from all the senses.**

**Комментарий:**

The conclusion is reached. This is similar to what was previously said:

*evaṁ vimṛśya guṇato manasas try-avasthā*  
*man-māyayā mayi kṛtā iti niścitārthāḥ*  
*sañchidya hārdam anumāna-sad-ukti-tīkṣṇa*  
*jñānāsinā bhajata mākhila-saṁśayādhim*

**Considering that the three states of the intelligence arising from the** ***guṇas*** **are created by my** ***avidyā*** **within me, being firmly convinced of your nature as** ***ātmā,*** **and cutting the three states by the sharp sword of knowledge, by inference and by scriptural statements, worship me, the destroyer of all doubts. (SB** [**11.3.33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/3.33)**)**

### Text 24

*nātmā vapuḥ pārthivam indriyāṇi*  
*devā hy asur vāyur jalam hutāśaḥ*  
*mano ’nna-mātraṁ dhiṣaṇā ca sattvam*  
*ahaṅkṛtiḥ khaṁ kṣitir artha-sāmyam*

**Перевод:**

**The material body made of earth, the senses, their presiding** ***devatās, prāṇa,*** **intelligence, mind,** ***citta*****, and** ***ahaṅkāra*****, are not the** ***ātmā.*** **The external air, water, fire, ether and earth, the** ***tan-mātras*** **and** ***prakṛti*** **are not the** ***ātmā*****.**

**Комментарий:**

He lists various objects which are like rays of the sun, thereby refuting contrary ideas.

### Text 25

*samāhitaiḥ kaḥ karaṇair guṇātmabhir*  
*guṇo bhaven mat-suvivikta-dhāmnaḥ*  
*vikṣipyamāṇair uta kiṁ nu dūṣaṇaṁ*  
*ghanair upetair vigatai raveḥ kim*

**Перевод:**

**For one who has properly realized my form, what credit is there if his senses—mere products of the material modes—are perfectly concentrated in meditation? And on the other hand, what blame is incurred if his senses happen to become agitated? Indeed, what does it mean to the sun if the clouds come and go?**

**Комментарий:**

For the person fixed on the form of the Lord, there is no dependence on purification of the jīva. For the person fixed on the form of the lord, is it good quality to have controlled senses, and a bad quality to have unsteady senses?

### Text 26

*yathā nabho vāyv-analāmbu-bhū-guṇair*  
*gatāgatair vartu-guṇair na sajjate*  
*tathākṣaraṁ sattva-rajas-tamo-malair*  
*ahaṁ-mateḥ saṁsṛti-hetubhiḥ param*

**Перевод:**

**Just as the sky is not affected by the coming and going of the qualities of air, fire, water and earth, or by the seasons, so the jīva fixed in knowledge is not affected by** ***ahaṅkāra*** **or by contamination of** ***sattva, rajas*** **or** ***tamas*****, the causes of material existence.**

**Комментарий:**

For the supreme entity like the sun globe there is no relationship at all with matter. That is shown by an example. Causes of saṁsāra exist only (*param*) for the jīva filled with ahaṅkāra and upādhis (*ahaṁ-mateḥ*) and not for the jīva having fixed jñāna-śakti.

### Text 27

*tathāpi saṅgaḥ parivarjanīyo*  
*guṇeṣu māyā-raciteṣu tāvat*  
*mad-bhakti-yogena dṛḍhena yāvad*  
*rajo nirasyeta manaḥ-kaṣāyaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Nevertheless, until one has completely eliminated from his mind all attraction to sense objects by firmly practicing devotional service to me, one must very carefully avoid associating with the material modes, which are produced by my illusory energy.**

**Комментарий:**

There is limitation for the jñānī not having realization. He must become successful by bhakti.

### Text 28

*yathāmayo ’sādhu cikitsito nṛṇāṁ*  
*punaḥ punaḥ santudati prarohan*  
*evaṁ mano ’pakva-kaṣāya-karma*  
*kuyoginaṁ vidhyati sarva-saṅgam*

**Перевод:**

**Just as an improperly treated disease recurs and gives repeated distress to the patient, the mind that is not completely purified of its perverted tendencies and** ***karma*** **will remain attached to material things and repeatedly torment the imperfect** ***yogī.***

**Комментарий:**

In spite of obstacles, the yogī is not destroyed. Later, it will be said in relation to the imperfect yogī:

*yoga-caryām imāṁ yogī vicaran mad-apāśrayaḥ*  
*nāntarāyair vihanyeta niḥspṛhaḥ sva-sukhānubhūḥ*

**The** ***yogī*** **who has taken shelter of me, free from hankering because he experiences the happiness of the soul within, is never defeated by obstacles while executing this process of** ***yoga.*** **(SB** [**11.28.44**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/28.44)**)**

### Text 30

*karoti karma kriyate ca jantuḥ*  
*kenāpy asau codita ā-nipatāt*  
*na tatra vidvān prakṛtau sthito ’pi*  
*nivṛtta-tṛṣṇaḥ sva-sukhānubhūtyā*

**Перевод:**

**The** ***jīva*** **performs action, inspired by Paramātmā, and is made to take a different body by that action. This cycle continues until universal devastation. The** ***jñānī,*** **however, though situated in his body, having experienced his own constitutional bliss, gives up all material desires and does not engage in action or its effects.**

**Комментарий:**

Three verses describe how the perfect yogī has no destruction.

### Text 33

*pūrvaṁ gṛhītaṁ guṇa-karma-citram*  
*ajñānam ātmany aviviktam aṅga*  
*nivartate tat punar īkṣayaiva*  
*na gṛhyate nāpi visṛyya ātmā*

**Перевод:**

**In the state of bondage, ignorance which made into variety by actions created by the** ***guṇas*** **was accepted as the self, without investigation. O Uddhava! Ignorance is destroyed by** ***jñāna.*** **But** ***ātmā*** **is never accepted or rejected.**

**Комментарий:**

The jīva becomes different because of accepting and rejecting guṇas and karma. The relationship with the guṇas and karmas arises from ignorance.

### Text 34

*yathā hi bhānor udayo nṛ-cakṣuṣāṁ*  
*tamo nihanyān na tu sad vidhatte*  
*evaṁ samīkṣā nipuṇā satī me*  
*hanyāt tamisraṁ puruṣasya buddheḥ*

**Перевод:**

**When the sun rises, it destroys the darkness covering men’s eyes, but it does not create the eye, which in fact was existent all along. Similarly, my** ***vidyā-śakti*** **will destroy the covering on knowledge for a person who has my glance of mercy.**

**Комментарий:**

Revelation for the Lord’s aṁśa arises spontaneously from destruction of ignorance, by the Lord’s mercy. The particle of consciousness is not created. *Me samīkṣa* means (having my glance of mercy).

### Text 35

*eṣa svayaṁ-jyotir ajo ’prameyo*  
*mahānubhūtiḥ sakalānubhūtiḥ*  
*eko ’dvitīyo vacasāṁ virāme*  
*yeneṣitā vāg-asavaś caranti*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord is self-luminous, unborn and immeasurable. He is pure consciousness. Knowing him, everything is known. He is one alone, and without compare. He is beyond words but by him the power of speech and the life airs are set into motion.**

**Комментарий:**

Revelation of *tat* (the Lord) reveals everything on its own, by manifesting to the ātmā. By knowing him, everything is known *(sakalānubhūtiḥ*), since he is all knowledge (*mahānubhūtiḥ*).

### Text 36

*etāvān ātma-sammoho yad vikalpas tu kevale*  
*ātman ṛte svam ātmānam avalambo na yasya hi*

**Перевод:**

**Since only one** ***ātmā*** **exists, seeing duality is one’s own bewilderment. It has no basis except the** ***jīva*** **himself.**

**Комментарий:**

“Duality is produced when illusion takes shelter of the pure Brahman or tat-padārtha. It is said *āśrayatva-viṣayatva-bhāginī nirvibhāga-citir eva kevalā*: the indivisible consciousness divides into āśraya and viṣaya. (Sārīrika-bhāsya). How is it that by your mercy, illusion of another entity takes place. Are you Brahman or the Lord, who creates upādhis resulting in illusion?”

*Vikalpa* means illusion of duality. The support for illusion exists only for the jīva, because there is no support for illusion in that which is devoid of relationship with anything else (*kevale*).

*hlādinī sandhinī saṁvit tvayy ekā sarva-saṁsthitau \|*  
*hlāda-tāpa-kārī miśrā tvayi no guṇa-varjite \|\|*

**In you who are the shelter of all spiritual qualities exist the energies of hlādinī, sandhinī and saṁvit. In you who are devoid of material qualities, the mixture of happiness and distress found in the material world does not exist. (Viṣṇu Purāṇa** [**1.12.68**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/vp/1.12.68)**)**

*hlādinyā saṁvidā śliṣṭaḥ saccidānanda īśvaraḥ \|*  
*svāvidyā-saṁvṛto jīvaḥ saṅkleśa-nikarākaraḥ \|\|*

**The Lord full of eternity, knowledge and bliss is embraced by the hlādinī and saṁvit śaktis. The jīva, the very form of suffering, is surrounded by his own ignorance. (Sarvajña-sūkta)**

### Text 37

*yan nāmākṛtibhir grāhyaṁ pañca-varṇam abādhitam*  
*vyarthenāpy artha-vādo ’yaṁ dvayaṁ paṇḍita-māninām*

**Перевод:**

**The duality of the five material elements is perceived only in terms of names and forms. Those who say this duality is real are pseudo scholars vainly proposing the non-existent.**

**Комментарий:**

“If duality is superimposed upon one’s svarūpa, when knowledge arises by the mercy of the Lord, the illusion should be understood. But it is not so. It is a combination like cooked grains. Why should illusion disappear by knowledge alone?” Some raise this doubt. Though it is imposed by the ignorance of the jīva, duality does not exist in the way that silver exists in the shop of a merchant. Rather without beginning, the previous illusion generates the later imposition. Then what is the Lord? What is his mercy? Others raise this doubt. These doubts are expressed in this verse.

Forms made of the five elements are perceived with names etc. and cannot be annulled by thought, since it is accomplished by the combination of the perceiver and other factors. Some think in this way. They think that objects are words only, without a substantial substance. Only falsely learned persons think of duality, and not the truly learned. Since perceiver and perceived, the source of form and the form, the spiritual and the material, light and darkness are impossible to combine, the perception of duality arises by ignorance alone. The six elements are simply superimposed. It is like the horns of a rabbit, who actually has upraised ears.

## Chapter Twenty-nine

### Text 1

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*su-dustarām imāṁ manye yoga-caryām anātmanaḥ*  
*yathāñjasā pumān siddhyet tan me brūhy añjasācyuta*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O Acyuta, I think that the method of** ***yoga*** **described by you is very difficult for one who cannot control his mind. Therefore please explain to me immediately how someone can more quickly attain perfection.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 326:**

Though the Lord speaks of results generated by bhakti accompanied by jñāna at the end of the previous chapter, Uddhava, the greatest devotee, cannot accept even a little jñāna in bhakti. Because it is difficult in most cases, one will not achieve the final result and because bhakti, which he desired to hear about, is easy to execute, and one will definitely achieve the results, bhakti alone should be practiced. That is the intention of his statement in the two verses.

### Text 2

*prāyaśaḥ puṇdarīkākṣa yuñyanto yogino manaḥ*  
*viṣīdanty asamādhānān mano-nigraha-karśitāḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord! Generally those** ***yogīs*** **who try to absorb their minds in Brahman experience frustration. Because of their inability to perfect** ***samādhi*****, they become fatigued in their attempt to bring the mind under control.**

### Text 3

*athāta ānanda-dughaṁ padāmbujaṁ*  
*haṁsāḥ śrayerann aravinda-locana*  
*sukhaṁ nu viśveśvara yoga-karmabhis*  
*tvan-māyayāmī vihatā na māninaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O lotus-eyed Lord of the universe! Therefore, discerning men joyfully take shelter of your lotus feet, the source of bliss. But those who take pride in their accomplishments in** ***yoga*** **and** ***karma*** **fail to take shelter of you and are defeated by your illusory energy.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 326:**

With no regard for jñāna, practicing bhakti alone, devotees practice bhakti to the form of Kṛṣṇa alone, without giving attention to the results of jñāna and yoga. That is explained in the next four verses.

Because some (*yogīs*) are depressed in their attempts, therefore (*athātaḥ*) those skillful at discerning the essence (*haṁsāḥ*) happily (*sukham*) serve with certainty (*nu*) your lotus feet, filled with all bliss. Since Uddhava was directly seeing Kṛṣṇa’s feet when he was speaking, he does not say “your lotus feet” but simply “the lotus feet.” These pure devotees (*amī*) are not obstructed by activities of yoga, which is your māyā, because of which they do not become proud. In this sādhana they think that they achieve results from the Lord’s unconditional mercy to the fallen, not by their own efforts in yoga.

### Text 4

*kiṁ citram acyuta tavaitad aśeṣa-bandho*  
*dāseṣv ananya-śaraṇesu yad ātma-sāttvam*  
*yo ’rocayat saha mṛgaiḥ svayam īśvarāṇāṁ*  
*śrīmat-kirīṭa-taṭa-pīḍita-pāda-pīṭhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O infallible Lord! O friend of all beings! It is not very astonishing that you are dependent on your servants who have taken exclusive shelter of you. As Rāmacandra, you displayed special affection for monkeys such as Hanumān while** ***devatās*** **shook your foot pedestal with the tips of their crowns,**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 327:**

The devotee receives the results of jñāna and yoga, but not only that, much more. O friend of the completely surrendered (*aśeṣa-bandho*), or friend of all, even the demons! You give benefit to all without discrimination in the form of liberation etc. This is not astonishing for you to do. You are dependent (*ātma-sāttvam*) on your completely surrendered devotees, who are averse to jñāna and yoga, such as Bali. For example you say:

*na sādhayati māṁ yogo na sāṅkhyaṁ dharma uddhava*  
*na svādhyāyas tapas tyāgo yathā bhaktir mamorjitā*

**O Uddhava! I am attained by unmixed bhakti. I cannot be attained by mystic yoga, Sāṅkhya philosophy, dharma, Vedic study, austerity or renunciation. (SB** [**11.14.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/14.20)**)**

You do not show any prejudice for species or qualities among the living beings who surrender. This is seen your intimate pastimes. You enjoyed sakhya-bhāva (*saha*) with the animals of Vṛndāvana. But what is your other nature? Śiva, Brahmā and others (*īśvarānām)* bowed to your foot pedestal. You give liberation, which is the highest fruit for the jñānīs and yogīs, to demons. But you are dependent on your servants like the Pāṇḍavas. You act as their friend, messenger and guard. The intention of Uddhava is to say that bhakti to Kṛṣṇa alone is the process.

### Text 5

*taṁ tvākhilātma-dayiteśvaram āśritānāṁ*  
*sarvārtha-daṁ sva-kṛta-vid visṛjeta ko nu*  
*ko vā bhajet kim api vismṛtaye ’nu bhūtyai*  
*kiṁ vā bhaven na tava pāda-rajo-juṣāṁ naḥ*

**Перевод:**

**What person who knows the mercy that you show to your devotees could reject you, the dearest among all** ***jīvas*****, the Supreme Lord of all, who gives all perfections to the devotees who take shelter of you? Who would reject you and accept something for the sake of material enjoyment or liberation, which simply leads to forgetfulness of you? And what lack is there for us who are engaged in the service of the dust of your lotus feet?**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 328:**

The result is described. What person who knows what favors you have done for him (*sva-kṛta-vit*), having revealed to him your beauty as described in SB [3.28.13](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/28.13), could reject you, as described in the previous line (*tam tvā)*? There are some persons like the following:

*evaṁ harau bhagavati pratilabdha-bhāvo*  
*bhaktyā dravad-dhṛdaya utpulakaḥ pramodāt*  
*autkaṇṭhya-bāṣpa-kalayā muhur ardyamānas*  
*tac cāpi citta-baḍiśaṁ śanakair viyuṅkte*

**The unfortunate yogī who has developed love for the Lord, full of all sweet qualities, whose heart is somewhat soft because of devotion, whose body hairs stand on end in ecstasy, who is constantly overcome with intense tears of joy, gradually withdraws his hook-like mind from the Lord’s form. (SB** [**3.28.34**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-3/28.34)**)**

But no one can give you up: he who gives you up is most ungrateful.

And who are you? You are dearer than a million lives to all beings by your nature (*akhilātma-dayita*) and are the controller *(īśvaram*). You give everything to those who take shelter of you. The word *nu* indicates conjecture. Who would accept devatās or another sādhana, and reject you, for wealth and power (*bhūtyai)* or for liberation (*vismṛtaye)?* No one would do so. All these results are included in bhakti to you that we perform. What is not achieved by those who worship your foot dust? The word *vā* in the last line indicates disinterest in these results.

*yat karmabhir yat tapasā jñāna-vairāgyataś ca yat*  
*yogena dāna-dharmeṇa śreyobhir itarair api*  
*sarvaṁ mad-bhakti-yogena mad-bhakto labhate ’ñjasā*  
*svargāpavargaṁ mad-dhāma kathañcid yadi vāñchati*

**Everything that can be achieved by karma, penance, jñāna, vairāgya, mystic yoga, charity, dharma and all other auspicious means of perfecting life is easily achieved by my devotee through bhakti. If somehow or other my devotee desires Svarga, liberation, or residence in my abode, he easily achieves such benedictions. (SB** [**11.20.32-33**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/20.32)**)**

### Text 6

*naivopayanty apacitiṁ kavayas taveśa*  
*brahmāyuṣāpi kṛtam ṛddha-mudaḥ smarantaḥ*  
*yo ’ntar bahis tanu-bhṛtām aśubhaṁ vidhunvann*  
*ācārya-caittya-vapuṣā sva-gatiṁ vyanakti*

**Перевод:**

**O my Lord! Intelligent persons cannot fully express their indebtedness to you, even if they were endowed with the lifetime of Brahmā, because on remembering what you have done form them, their joy increases even more: appearing externally as** ***guru*** **and internally as Paramātmā, you reveal the goal of** ***prema*** **to the devotees.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 328:**

O Lord! The wise (*kavayaḥ*), even with a life span like Brahmā’s, remembering your assistance (*kṛtam*), becoming ever increasingly blissful (*ṛddha-mudaḥ*) because of bhakti to you, cannot see (*upayanti*) how they can repay you (*apacitim*). Therefore they never give you up. What you have done is described. For some among all embodied souls, those who have received your mercy, you destroy obstacles *(aśubham*) to your bhakti and reveal understanding of yourself (*sva-gatim*) externally through a guru (*ācārya-vapuṣā*) and internally as a form revealed through meditation (*caittya-vapuṣā*).

### Text 7

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*ity uddhavenāty-anurakta-cetasā*  
*pṛṣṭo jagat-krīḍanakaḥ sva-śaktibhiḥ*  
*gṛhīta-mūrti-traya īśvareśvaro*  
*jagāda sa-prema-manohara-smitaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Thus questioned by the most affectionate Uddhava, Kṛṣṇa, the supreme controller of all controllers, who utilizes the universe for his pastimes and assumes three forms by his energies, began to reply, displaying his attractive smile filled with love.**

**Комментарий:**

Without regard for being the lord of lords, Kṛṣṇa spoke with affection. As the lord of lords he has three forms. Through his śakti he has vibhūtis endowed with some of his portions. That is the lowest type. He manifests as Brahmā, Viṣṇu and Śiva. That is the medium type. He manifests as the lord of Mahā-viṣṇu. That is the highest type.

### Text 12

*mām eva sarva-bhūteṣu bahir antar apāvṛtam*  
*īkṣetātmani cātmānaṁ yathā kham amalāśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**With a pure heart the** ***jñānī*** **should see the complete form of Paramātmā, within all beings and also within oneself, present both externally and internally, just like the ether spreads everywhere without being contaminated.**

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 330:**

The Lord then speaks about the greatness of bhakti. To devotees like Uddhava, he speaks of pure bhakti in four verses. Then for others not so inclined, he considers by his mercy other things in order to encourage his worship. Since people are generally inclined to compete with others, he considered that they could only worship his form as antaryāmī as a means of somehow making them favorable. In order to make them quickly give up the competitive mood and turn towards the Lord, he teaches worship of himself in the form of his portion, the antaryāmī.

*atha vā bahunaitena kiṁ jñātena tavārjuna \|*  
*viṣṭabhyāham idaṁ kṛtsnam ekāṁśena sthito jagat \|\|*

**But what is the use of knowing all these individual vibhūtis, Arjuna? Please understand: by just one portion of myself, I am firmly established in this universe. (BG** [**10.42**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/10.42)**)**

Thus the Lord says:

*mām eva sarva-bhūteṣu bahir antar apāvṛtam*  
*īkṣetātmani cātmānaṁ yathā kham amalāśayaḥ*

**With a pure heart one should see me, the complete form of Paramātmā, within all beings and also within oneself, present both externally and internally, just like the ether spreads everywhere without being contaminated. (SB** [**11.29.12**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/29.12)**)**

The commentary says, “In three verses the Lord speaks about essential bhakti. One should see me situated as the Lord (*ātmānam*) in all beings (*ātmani*). That Lord is complete, being outside and inside (*bahiḥ antaḥ*). How can this be? He cannot be covered or restrained (*apāvrṭam*). How is this possible? He is without limbs and spreads everywhere like ether.” One should see only me, the form of Kṛṣṇa, not the form of antaryāmī. In this manner, it becomes essential (antaraṅga) bhakti.

### Text 13-14

*iti sarvāṇi bhūtāni mad-bhāvena mahā-dyute*  
*sabhājayan manyamāno jñānaṁ kevalam āśritaḥ*  
*brāhmaṇe pukkase stene brahmaṇye ’rke sphuliṅgake*  
*akrūre krūrake caiva sama-dṛk paṇḍito mataḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O brilliant Uddhava! The person who respects all beings as Brahman and contemplates this, who sees equally the** ***brāhmaṇa*** **and the outcaste, the thief and the giver of charity to** ***brāhmaṇas*** **, the sun and the sparks of fire, the gentle and the cruel, is a true** ***jñānī.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 331:**

Though taking shelter of the vision of antaryāmī (*kevalaṁ jñānam*), honoring and thinking of all entities as being situated as my form (*mad-bhāvena*) in the manner previously explained, one is considered to be learned. He sees me in the brāhmaṇa and others.

### Text 19

*ayaṁ hi sarva-kalpānāṁ sadhrīcīno mato mama*  
*mad-bhāvaḥ sarva-bhūteṣu mano-vāk-kāya-vṛttibhiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I consider this process—using one’s mind, words and bodily functions for realizing me within all living beings—to be the easiest process of the** ***jñānī*** **to realize Brahman*****.***

**Комментарий:**

**Bhakti Sandarbha 332:**

This type of worship quickly destroys competition. (verse 14). There is no other easy process for attaining Brahman for the *jñānī.* (verse 15) To practice this vision one should offer respects to all beings. (16) The result of this worship is manifestation of Kṛṣṇa everywhere spontaneously. (verse 17) Referring to verse 18 there is a similar usage of the word *Brahman* in the verse where the Lord speaks to the Pracetas:

*navyavad dhṛd aye yaj jño brahmaitad brahma-vādibhiḥ*  
*na muhyanti na śocanti na hṛṣyanti yato gatāḥ*

**Those who have attained my form which is known as Brahman by the followers of Brahman are not bewildered. They do not lament and do not rejoice, since I, full of knowledge, have entered their fresh lotus hearts. (SB** [**4.30.20**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-4/30.20)**)**

The commentary says that Brahman refers to a fresh manifestation of Bhagavān. Thus the result of this worship is seeing Bhagavān everywhere. Or the word Brahman is used in Gopāla-tāpanī to indicate Kṛṣṇa in the following:

*yo ’vatārāṇāṁ madhye śreṣṭho ’vatāraḥ ko bhavati \|*  
*yena lokās tuṣṭā devās tuṣṭā bhavanti \|*  
*yaṁ smṛtvā muktā asmāt saṁsārād bhavanti \|*  
*kathaṁ vāsyāvatārasya brahmatā bhavati \|*

**Brahmā spoke. Who is the best among the avatāras, by whom all people and devatās become satisfied, by whose remembrance people become liberated from saṁsāra? How can this avatāra be Svayam Bhagavān (*****brahmatā*****)? (Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad** [**2.24**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/gopala-tapani/2.24)**)**

Thus the result of the process is to see Parabrahman in human form. After stating this, Kṛṣṇa praises such worship as topmost:

*ayaṁ hi sarva-kalpānāṁ sadhrīcīno mato mama*  
*mad-bhāvaḥ sarva-bhūteṣu mano-vāk-kāya-vṛttibhiḥ*

**I consider this process—using one’s mind, words and bodily functions for realizing me within all living beings—to be the easiest process to realize me*****.*** **(SB** [**11.29.19**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/29.19)**)**

I consider this to be the correct method (*sadhrīcīnaḥ*) among all methods (*sarva-kalānām*): thinking of (*bhāvaḥ*) my form (*mat*) in all beings.

### Text 20

*na hy aṅgopakrame dhvaṁso mad-dharmasyoddhavāṇv api*  
*mayā vyavasitaḥ samyaṅ nirguṇatvād anāśiṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! Because I have personally established it, the process of** ***niṣkāma-bhakti*** **is beyond the** ***guṇas.*** **Thus even by starting and not completing the process, there is no destruction of results.**

**Комментарий:**

There are no obstacles related to my dharma, what to speak of anything having to assist it.

### Text 21

*yo yo mayi pare dharmaḥ kalpyate niṣphalāya cet*  
*tad-āyāso nirarthaḥ syād bhayāder iva sattama*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava, greatest of saints! If** ***bhakti*** **is offered to me without personal motivation, endeavoring for results is useless, just as expressions of fear or lamentation are useless in dangerous situations.**

**Комментарий:**

What to speak of direct bhakti, even indirect bhakti (bhakti by superimposition) is glorified.

**Bhakti Sandarbha 33:**

Dharma prescribed by the Vedas done as an offering to me, if offered without desire for results (*niṣphalāya kalpate*), dharma done with effort, will not be useless (*nirarthaḥ*). When the actions are done without desire for enjoyment, they do not create obstacles for bhakti and are thus not useless. Since acts are offered to Kṛṣṇa favorably, how much more useful they are than acts offered unfavorably, which also give results. This is made clear by an example. The efforts of Kaṁsa and others, related to the Lord by fear or hatred, were not useless since those efforts gave them liberation.

### Text 22

*eṣā buddhimatāṁ buddhir manīṣā ca manīṣiṇām*  
*yat satyam anṛteneha martyenāpnoti māmṛtam*

**Перевод:**

**The devotee is the most intelligent among the intelligent and the most his temporary body and attains me, the only truth, though he is an inconsequential** ***jīva*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Bhakti is justly glorified since it brings about attainment of me, Svayam Bhagavān Kṛṣṇa, the cause of all bliss (*amṛtam*) and the cause of all existence (*satyam*).

### Text 23

*eṣa te ’bhihitaḥ kṛtsno brahma-vādasya saṅgrahaḥ*  
*samāsa-vyāsa-vidhinā devānām api durgamaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Thus have I related to you—both in brief and in detail—a complete survey of Brahman. Even for the** ***devatās,*** **this science is very difficult to comprehend.**

**Комментарий:**

I explained bhakti-yoga (*eṣaḥ*), the means of attaining the Lord, in summarized form (*samāsa-vidhinā*) (SB [10.29.22](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-10/29.21)) and with details (*vyāsa-vidhinā*). (SB [11.7.6](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/7.6)) This is difficult to understand even for the devatās fixed in sattva. It is said in the Sixth Canto:

*devānāṁ śuddha-sattvānām ṛṣīṇām amalātmanām \|*  
*bhaktir mukunda-caraṇe na prāyeṇopajāyate \|\|*

*Bhakti* to the Lord’s lotus feet rarely develops even in the *devatās* with minds possessing *sattva-guṇa* and in pure sages. (SB [6.14.2](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-6/14.2))

### Text 24

*abhīkṣṇaśas te gaditaṁ jñānaṁ vispaṣṭa-yuktimat*  
*etad vijñāya mucyeta puruṣo naṣṭa-saṁśayaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**I have repeatedly spoken this knowledge to you with clear reasoning. Anyone who properly understands it will become free from all doubts and attain liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Jñāna does not directly result in attainment of the Lord, but liberation.

### Text 25

*su-viviktaṁ tava praśnaṁ mayaitad api dhārayet*  
*sanātanaṁ brahma-guhyaṁ paraṁ brahmādhigacchati*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who fixes his attention on these clear answers to your questions will attain the eternal secret of the Vedas—the form of the supreme Brahman.**

**Комментарий:**

Searching through everything from previous to later, the result, attaining me, will happen in any case, through sādhana and siddha bhakti, since you are dedicated to me. One will attain param brahma (*brahma guhyam*), me.

*yūyaṁ nṛ-loke bata bhūri-bhāgā*  
*lokaṁ punānā munayo 'bhiyanti*  
*yeṣāṁ gṛhān āvasatīti sākṣād*  
*gūḍhaṁ paraṁ brahma manuṣya-liṅgam*

**You Pāṇḍavas are most fortunate in this world. Sages who purify the planets come and visit your houses because the Supreme Brahman personally resides in your houses in a human form. (SB** [**7.10.48**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-7/10.48)**)**

### Text 26

*ya etan mama bhakteṣu sampradadyāt su-puṣkalam*  
*tasyāhaṁ brahma-dāyasya dadāmy ātmānam ātmanā*

**Перевод:**

**One who liberally disseminates this knowledge among my devotees is the bestower of Brahman. I give myself to him.**

**Комментарий:**

Meeting my devotee brings this result. What more can be said? I become controlled by that devotee (*dadāmy ātmānām*).

### Text 27

*ya etat samadhīyīta pavitraṁ paramaṁ śuci*  
*sa pūyetāhar ahar māṁ jñāna-dīpena darśayan*

**Перевод:**

**He who loudly recites this supreme knowledge, which is the pure and purifying, becomes purified day by day, by revealing me to others with the lamp of knowledge.**

**Комментарий:**

The result of simply reciting this knowledge, without understanding it, is stated.

This knowledge is without any fault (*śuci*) and removes all faults (*paramam pavitram*) of the hearer and reciter. It is not surprising that the reciter becomes purified. Not only that, it shows me by the lamp of knowledge to those who have heard.

### Text 28

*ya etac chraddhayā nityam avyagraḥ śṛṇuyān naraḥ*  
*mayi bhaktiṁ parāṁ kurvan karmabhir na sa badhyate*

**Перевод:**

**Anyone who regularly listens to this knowledge with faith and attention, while engaging in my pure devotional service, will never become bound by** ***karma.***

**Комментарий:**

The special result for those who constantly hear with faith is described.

### Text 29

*apy uddhava tvayā brahma sakhe samavadhāritam*  
*api te vigato mohaḥ śokaś cāsau mano-bhavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**O Uddhava! O friend! Have you now completely understood Brahman? Are the confusion and lamentation that arose in your mind now dispelled?**

**Комментарий:**

Overcome with mercy, Kṛṣṇa pacifies Uddhava. Uddhava had confusion concerning the unexpected condition of the devotees and lamentation that they would not attain him again. The conversation you have had with me explained the various results from hearing. Do you have any doubt about these results or concerning my associates?

### Text 30

*naitat tvayā dāmbhikāya nāstikāya śaṭhāya ca*  
*aśuśrūṣor abhaktāya durvinītāya dīyatām*

**Перевод:**

**You should not share this instruction with anyone who is hypocritical, atheistic or dishonest, or with anyone who hears without faith, who is not a devotee, or who is not humble.**

**Комментарий:**

Seeing Uddhava’s clear understanding, he then teaches using negative and positive statements in two verses.

### Text 31

*etair doṣair vihīnāya brahmaṇyāya priyāya ca*  
*sādhave śucaye brūyād bhaktiḥ syāc chūdra-yoṣitām*

**Перевод:**

**This knowledge should be taught to one who is free from these bad qualities, who is dedicated to the welfare of the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **and who is kindly disposed, saintly and pure. And if common workers and women are found to have devotion for the Supreme Lord, one should speak it to them.**

**Комментарий:**

If they have bhakti, one should teach this even to śūdras and women. They are also qualified.

### Text 32

*naitad vijñāya jijñāsor jñātavyam avaśiṣyate*  
*pītvā pīyūṣam amṛtaṁ pātavyaṁ nāvaśiṣyate*

**Перевод:**

**When an inquisitive person comes to understand this knowledge, he has nothing further to know. After all, one who has drunk sweet nectar cannot drink anything else.**

**Комментарий:**

Since bhakti is the sādhana and the result, nothing remains to be known. One who has drunk the sweetest nectar cannot drink anything else.

### Text 33

*jñāne karmaṇi yoge ca vārtāyāṁ daṇḍa-dhāraṇe*  
*yāvān artho nṛṇāṁ tāta tāvāṁs te ’haṁ catur-vidhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Through analytical knowledge, ritualistic work, mystic yoga, mundane business and politics, people seek to advance in religiosity, economic development, sense gratification and liberation. Whatever men can accomplish in these multifarious ways I give to my devotee.**

**Комментарий:**

I have taught you in order that you teach others. I am everything, both your sādhana and your sādhya. Jñāna gives liberation, karma gives dharma and kāma. Yoga gives various astonishing siddhis. Occupations and politics give various worldly articles (*artha*). These are the four types mentioned in the verse.

### Text 34

*martyo yadā tyakta-samasta-karmā*  
*niveditātmā vicikīrṣito me*  
*tadāmṛtatvaṁ pratipadyamāno*  
*mayātma-bhūyāya ca kalpate vai*

**Перевод:**

**When a person who gives up all** ***karmas*** **offers himself entirely to** ***guru*** **and eagerly desires to render service to me alone, he achieves freedom from death and, more important, is qualified to be an associate of mine in** ***prema*****.**

**Комментарий:**

Enough of speaking about you. I give the ordinary human the most extraordinary result.

### Text 35

*śrī-śuka uvāca*  
*sa evam ādarśita-yoga-mārgas*  
*tadottamaḥśloka-vaco niśamya*  
*baddhāñjaliḥ prīty-uparuddha-kaṇṭho*  
*na kiñcid ūce ’śru-pariplutākṣaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Hearing these words spoken by Kṛṣṇa, and having thus been shown the entire path of** ***yoga*****, Uddhava folded his hands, but his throat choked up with love and his eyes overflowed with tears. Thus he could say nothing.**

**Комментарий:**

He had been shown the complete path of yoga by Kṛṣṇa (*ādarśita-yoga-mārgaḥ*).

### Text 36

*viṣṭabhya cittaṁ praṇayāvaghūrṇaṁ*  
*dhairyeṇa rājan bahu-manyamānaḥ*  
*kṛtāñjaliḥ prāha yadu-pravīraṁ*  
*śīrṣṇā spṛśaṁs tac-caraṇāravindam*

**Перевод:**

**O King! Steadying his mind, which had become overwhelmed with love, with determined effort, Uddhava, thinking himself successful and touching the Lord’s lotus feet with his head, spoke with folded hands to greatest hero of the Yadu dynasty.**

**Комментарий:**

In order to cover his rising sorrow of separation, he desired to make himself joyful by thinking of his good fortune of having mercifully attained instructions from Kṛṣṇa (*bahu-manyamānaḥ*).

### Text 37

*śrī-uddhava uvāca*  
*vidrāvito moha-mahāndhakāro*  
*ya āśrito me tava sannidhānāt*  
*vibhāvasoḥ kiṁ nu samīpa-gasya*  
*śītaṁ tamo bhīḥ prabhavanty ajādya*

**Перевод:**

**Uddhava said: O unborn, primeval Lord! My great ignorance has now been dispelled by your merciful association. Indeed, how can cold, darkness and fear exert their power over one who has approached the brilliant sun?**

**Комментарий:**

The great darkness concerning the impossibility of attaining you (*moha-mahāndhakāraḥ*) and the confusion which took shelter of me have gone completely because of your nature of being close by, with a sower of your merciful glance, what to speak of your teachings.

### Text 39

*vṛkṇaś ca me su-dṛḍhaḥ sneha-pāśo*  
*dāśārha-vṛṣṇy-andhaka-sātvateṣu*  
*prasāritaḥ sṛṣṭi-vivṛddhaye tvayā*  
*sva-māyayā hy ātma-subodha-hetinā*

**Перевод:**

**The firm rope of affection for the families of the Dāśārhas, Vṛṣṇis, Andhakas and Sātvatas—a rope you originally cast over me by your illusory energy for the purpose of developing your family—is now cut off by the weapon of knowledge of the self.**

**Комментарий:**

The rope of affection that you spread by your māyā to increase your family, has been cut by the weapon of understanding ātmā, spoken by you to me. That rope of affection for them was accepted by me because they were related to you. Or, the rope of affection was cut by me previous to meeting you, but after the killing of Kaṁsa, by your mercy to your deveotees (*sva-māyayā*), in order to increase the affection of the world for you, it spread out. Your mercy took the form of the weapon of realizing Brahman. How then can I reject it?

### Text 40

*namo ’stu te mahā-yogin prapannam anuśādhi mām*  
*yathā tvac-caraṇāmbhoje ratiḥ syād anapāyinī*

**Перевод:**

**Obeisances unto you, O greatest of** ***yogīs*****! Please instruct me, surrendered to you, so that I may have constant attachment to your lotus feet.**

**Комментарий:**

By your mercy my highest desire will be achieved. With pitiful voice he again prays. Please make me surrendered. He has some special desire in the surrender. There should be constant attraction to your lotus feet.

### Text 41

*śrī-bhagavān uvāca*  
*gacchoddhava mayādiṣṭo badary-ākhyaṁ mamāśramam*  
*tatra mat-pāda-tīrthode snānopasparśanaiḥ śuciḥ*

**Перевод:**

**The Supreme Lord said: O Uddhava! On my order, go to Badarikāśrama. Purify yourself by sipping and bathing in the holy waters there, which have emanated from my lotus feet.**

### Text 42

*Īkṣayālakanandāyā vidhūtāśeṣa-kalmaṣaḥ*  
*vasāno valkalāny aṅga vanya-bhuk sukha-niḥspṛhaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**By your glance you should destroy the unlimited sins present in the Alakanandā River. Dress yourself in bark and eat whatever is naturally available in the forest. Be content and free from desire.**

**Комментарий:**

Even though the attachment to my lotus feet that you prayed for has already been achieved, it will increase spontaneously by separation from me. It does not depend on any other practice. You will undergo difficulties by separation from me. To teach the world act according to sādhana, do not undergo fasting etc. On completing what I desire in terms of teaching the world, your desire will be achieved. This is explained in four and half verses.

Go to that place. By going there and by the nature of following the order, your desire will be fulfilled on its own. There, naturally you will not have desires for worldly happiness. The Lord is established in Badarikāśrama. Situated in pain of separation in the place of my constant pastimes, you will experience great difficulties.

By your glance you will purify the Alakananda River of its sorrow arising from the touch of sinful people with unlimited contamination.

### Text 43-44

*titikṣur dvandva-mātrāṇāṁ suśīlaḥ saṁyatendriyaḥ*  
*śāntaḥ samāhita-dhiyā jñāna-vijñāna-saṁyutaḥ*  
*matto ’nuśikṣitaṁ yat te viviktam anubhāvayan*  
*mayy āveśita-vāk-citto mad-dharma-nirato bhava*  
*ativrajya gatīs tisro mām eṣyasi tataḥ param*

**Перевод:**

**Be tolerant of all dualities, good-natured, self-controlled, peaceful and endowed with knowledge and realization. With fixed attention, reveal to the sages these instructions filled with special discrimination that I have imparted to you. Fix your words and thoughts upon me, and always be absorbed in my qualities. Help the sages cross beyond the conditions of the three** ***guṇas*** **and finally you will come to me.**

**Комментарий:**

Reveal these instructions which were well deliberated by me (*viviktam*) as in verse 40.

Crossing over the famous stages of kaniṣṭha, madhyama and uttama (*tisraḥ gatīḥ*), you will attain something superior (*param*) to those (*tataḥ*). What is that? It is me. He says this with mercy. He said to Arjuna:

*man-manā bhava mad-bhakto mad-yājī māṁ namaskuru*  
*mām evaiṣyasi satyaṁ te pratijāne priyo ’si me \|\|*

**Being my devotee, offer your mind to me. Offer articles to me in worship. Offer respects to me. I promise that you will come to me alone without doubt, for you are most dear to me. (BG** [**18.65**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/bg/18.65)**)**

### Text 49

*bhava-bhayam apahantuṁ jñāna-vijñāna-sāraṁ*  
*nigama-kṛd upajahre bhṛṅga-vad veda-sāram*  
*amṛtam udadhitaś cāpāyayad bhṛtya-vargān*  
*puruṣam ṛṣabham ādyaṁ kṛṣṇa-saṁjñaṁ nato ’smi*

**Перевод:**

**Since he is the author of the Vedas, the Lord, like a bee, has extracted the real essence from the Vedas in order to destroy fear of material existence. This essence of all knowledge and self-realization is like the nectar extracted from the milk ocean, which the Lord as Mohinī made his devotees drink while cheating the demons. I offer my obeisances to that Supreme Lord, the original and greatest of all beings, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.**

**Комментарий:**

The one avatāra destroys fear of saṁsāra and gives nectar. Though one should offer respects for these reasons, he is also the first. I offer respects to that person. This shows his great respect. He is also puruṣa, the first of all.

*pūrvam evāham ihāsam iti tat-puruṣasya puruṣatvam*

**It is said “In the beginning I alone existed in this world.” Because the Lord was the only person existing previously, he is called the** ***puruṣa.*** **(Taittirīya Āraṇyaka** [**1.23.1**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/taittiriya/1.23.1)**)**

*Ṛṣabhaḥ* means the best.

**Kṛṣna Sandarbha 38:**

He says *kṛṣṇa-saṁjñam* (he who is called Kṛṣṇa) so that he cannot be mistaken for any other form. By offering respects to that form he indicates that the form is eternal. Śrīdhara Svāmī says the verse means, “I offer respects to the son of Nanda, the highest bliss.”

## Chapter Thirty

### Text 2

*brahma-śāpopasaṁsṛṣṭe sva-kule yādavarṣabhaḥ*  
*preyasīṁ sarva-netrāṇāṁ tanuṁ sa katham atyajat*

**Перевод:**

**After his dynasty met destruction from the curse of the** ***brāhmaṇas,*** **how could the best of the Yadus give up his body, the dearest object of all eyes?**

**Комментарий:**

How could he give up his body? This means he did not give up his body. He makes clear the desire in his heart. The reason he did not give up his body is that he was the highest bliss for all those who experienced prema. This included the ātmārāmas.

### Text 3

*pratyākraṣṭuṁ nayanam abalā yatra lagnaṁ na śekuḥ*  
*karṇāviṣṭaṁ na sarati tato yat satām ātma-lagnam*  
*yac-chrīr vācāṁ janayati ratiṁ kiṁ nu mānaṁ kavīnāṁ*  
*dṛṣṭvā jiṣṇor yudhi ratha-gataṁ yac ca tat-sāmyam īyuḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Once their eyes were fixed upon his transcendental form, women were unable to withdraw them, and once that form had entered the ears of the sages and became fixed in their hearts, it would never depart. What to speak of acquiring fame, the great poets who described the beauty of the Lord’s form would have their words invested with joy. And by seeing that form on Arjuna’s chariot, all the warriors on the battlefield of Kurukṣetra attained the liberation.**

**Комментарий:**

Causes for the impossibility of giving up his body are listed. The respected gopīs, controlled by prema which is desired by all the devotees could not withdraw their eyes from him. How could he make his body do something contrary to this? That would be contrary to the following statement:

*visṛjati hṛdayaṁ na yasya sākṣād*  
*dharir avaśābhihito 'py aghaugha-nāśaḥ*  
*praṇaya-rasanayā dhṛtāṅghri-padmaḥ*  
*sa bhavati bhāgavata-pradhāna uktaḥ*

**He who binds the lotus feet of the Lord by ropes of love, and whose heart the Lord, destroyer of heaps of sin, does not leave, even if beckoned accidentally, is called the best of devotees. (SB** [**11.2.55**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-11/2.55)**)**

And thus the form entered the ears of the best ātmārāmas, the highest of devotees, and never departed. The great poets like Vyāsa had their words invested with joy. And there was a special reason why he could not give up that body (it could not have been material): seeing the form, the warriors achieved liberation. Even his devotees achieve bodies beyond time. Then how was it possible for Kṛṣṇa to have a perishable body?

*mat-sevayā pratītaṁ te sālokyādi-catuṣṭayam*  
*necchanti sevayā pūrṇāḥ kuto 'nyat kāla-viplutam*

**My devotees, who are always satisfied to be engaged in my service, are not interested even in the four principles of liberation \[sālokya, sārūpya, sāmīpya and sārṣṭi\], although these are automatically achieved by their service. What then is to be said of any perishable happiness? (SB** [**9.4.67**](https://vedarama.com/en/books/sb-9/4.67)**)**

### Text 4

*śrī ṛṣir uvāca*  
*divi bhuvy antarikṣe ca mahotpātān samutthitān*  
*dṛṣṭvāsīnān su-dharmāyāṁ kṛṣṇaḥ prāha yadūn idam*

**Перевод:**

**Śukadeva Gosvāmī said: Having observed many disturbing signs in the sky, on the earth and in outer space, Kṛṣṇa addressed the Yadus assembled in the Sudharmā council hall as follows.**

**Комментарий:**

Accepting these points, Śukadeva explains what was questioned.

### Text 9

*vidhir eṣa hy ariṣṭa-ghno maṅgalāyanam uttamam*  
*deva-dvija-gavāṁ pūjā bhūteṣu paramo bhavaḥ*

**Перевод:**

**This is indeed the appropriate process for counteracting our imminent adversity, and it is sure to bring about the highest good fortune. Such worship of the** ***devatās, brāhmaṇas*** **and cows can earn the highest auspiciousness for all living entities.**

**Комментарий:**

Counteracting adversity indicates attaining a manifestation of his spiritual planet.

### Text 12

*tatas tasmin mahā-pānaṁ papur maireyakaṁ madhu*  
*diṣṭa-vibhraṁśita-dhiyo yad-dravair bhraśyate matiḥ*

**Перевод:**

**Then, their intelligence covered by fate, they liberally indulged in drinking sweet liquor, which can completely intoxicate the mind.**

**Комментарий:**
